### [1] [PINN-Slicer: Exploiting Rotational Symmetry to Solve the Photon Orbit Equation Around a Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33953)
*Claire David,Tlotlo Oepeng,Harrison B. Prosper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 PINN-Slicer，利用二阶自治常微分方程对自变量平移的不变性，用单个在薄切片上训练的 PINN 逐片递归外推至全定义域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络（physics-informed neural networks, PINNs）在长时程（long-horizon）定义域上训练困难，且定义域扩大时通常需重新训练网络。

Method: 针对形如 \ddot{u}=G(u,\dot{u}) 的二阶自治常微分方程（second-order autonomous ordinary differential equations），利用其对自变量平移的不变性；将单个 PINN 嵌入显式满足初始条件的解拟设（solution ansatz），在自变量的薄切片上以 Sobol 采样的初始条件训练，再逐片递归部署于整个定义域。

Result: 在描述黑洞周围光子轨道（photon orbits）的非线性方程上验证，相对于有限差分（finite-difference）参考解，u = r_s/r 的逐点均方根误差（root-mean-square error, RMSE）低于 10^{-3}。

Conclusion: 单个训练好的网络无需重训练即可泛化到训练范围内的任意轨道初始条件，并可处理无法先验已知的解定义域。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) typically struggle with long-horizon domains, often requiring the network to be retrained as the domain grows. We present PINN-Slicer, a method for second-order autonomous ordinary differential equations, $\ddot{u} = G(u, \dot{u})$, which exploits the invariance of this class of equations under translations of the independent variable. A single PINN, embedded within a solution ansatz that enforces the initial conditions explicitly, is trained on a thin slice of the independent variable using Sobol-sampled initial conditions. The PINN is then deployed recursively, slice-by-slice, across the full domain. We demonstrate PINN-Slicer on the nonlinear equation that describes photon orbits around a black hole, and reach a point-by-point root-mean-square error (RMSE) below $10^{-3}$ in $u = r_s/r$ (the inverse radial coordinate in units of the Schwarzschild radius) relative to a finite-difference reference solution. A key advantage of PINN-Slicer is that a single trained network generalizes to any orbital initial condition within the training range, without retraining. In addition, PINN-Slicer can cope with solution domains that may not be known a priori.

</details>


### [2] [Verified experience improves materials prediction with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33195)
*Yiming Zhang,Koji Tsuda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出顺序式基准 MatMemBench 与免训练记忆框架 MatRecall，使大语言模型（large language models）能把已验证的预测结果作为“经验片段”存储并在后续任务中召回，从而在材料发现（materials discovery）循环中实现在线自适应与跨任务迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料发现本质上是迭代闭环，实验与计算的结果应反馈到下一轮预测；然而现有预测器多为训练后固定的单任务专用模型（specialized single-task models），大语言模型虽可跨异构任务但性能不及专用模型，检索增强（retrieval-augmented）变体又丢弃自身产出结果，且现有基准孤立评分单个查询，无法检验基于结果的改进。

Method: 构建 MatMemBench——覆盖 22 项材料任务的顺序基准（sequential benchmark），仅在预测完成后揭示结果，用于测试在线自适应（online adaptation）、记忆保持（memory retention）与跨任务迁移（cross-task transfer）；并提出 MatRecall 框架，将带有证据、结果与错误信息的已验证预测以片段（episodes）形式存储，无需重新训练（without retraining）即可召回。

Result: MatRecall 相较无记忆模型（memory-free model）将中位性能提升 62%，在 11 项任务上超过专用模型；在反馈停止后仍保持有效性，并可在相关任务间迁移。

Conclusion: 经核验的经验（verified experience）有望成为可复用资源，支撑自我改进的材料发现循环（self-improving discovery loops）。

Abstract: Materials discovery is an iterative loop: experiments and calculations verify predictions, and each outcome can inform the next. Yet most predictors are specialized single-task models fixed after training. Large language models have recently emerged as predictors across heterogeneous tasks but generally underperform specialized models, and retrieval-augmented variants discard their own outcomes. Benchmarks also score queries in isolation, leaving improvement from outcomes untested. Here we construct MatMemBench, a sequential benchmark of 22 materials tasks that reveals outcomes only after prediction and tests online adaptation, memory retention and cross-task transfer. We present MatRecall, a framework that lets large language models store verified predictions, with evidence, outcome and error, as episodes and recall them without retraining. MatRecall improved median performance by 62% over a memory-free model, exceeded specialized models on 11 tasks, remained useful after feedback stopped and transferred between related tasks. Verified experience could become a reusable resource for self-improving discovery loops.

</details>

### [3] [Noise-induced Extinction in Hierarchical Food Webs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33569)
*Varun Nevash,Eric Forgoston,M. Ani Hsieh*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该文针对任意规模等级食物网（hierarchical food web），建立了由人口噪声（demographic noise）驱动灭绝的大偏差理论（large-deviation theory），利用 WKB 方法导出涨落定点的闭式解与灭绝网络，并给出平均灭绝时间的精确递推关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限种群终将因人口噪声灭绝，即使确定性动力学具有稳定共存态；但以往缺乏对任意规模食物网中这一灭绝过程的一般性定量理论，本文旨在填补该空白。

Method: 构建含 N 个物种的等级食物网（基础物种 X₁ 内禀繁殖、消费者捕食其下所有物种，内蕴杂食性），在单位转化效率与仅基础物种自限条件下分析平均场动力学；对主方程（master equation）作 WKB 处理得到 2N 维哈密顿量，并以闭式求解涨落定点。

Result: 证明被动特征值符号等于灭绝物种在扩增群落中的密度符号，每个含 X₁ 的部分群落均为鞍点；共存态为唯一稳定定点。灭绝网络含 3^{N-1} 个涨落驱动跃迁，粗粒化动力学给出每个平均灭绝时间的精确递推；并以三物种杂食模块作完整分析（含数值瞬子作用量与最可能灭绝路径，经随机模拟验证）。

Conclusion: 该框架为等级食物网的噪声驱动灭绝提供了可解析的定量理论，并显示删除单一营养链接即可重塑灭绝网络，表明网络拓扑对灭绝路径与时间具有关键影响。

Abstract: Every finite population is eventually driven to extinction by demographic noise, even when the deterministic dynamics have a stable coexistence state. We develop a large-deviation theory of this process for hierarchical food webs of arbitrary size. In a community of $N$ species, the basal species $X_1$ reproduces intrinsically and every consumer may prey on all species below it, so that omnivory is built into the interaction graph. For unit conversion efficiency and self-limitation confined to the basal species, the mean-field dynamics have at most $2^{N-1}+1$ feasible fixed points. We prove that the sign of each passive eigenvalue equals the sign of the extinct species' density in the enlarged community, that every partial community containing $X_1$ is a saddle with one unstable direction for each extinct consumer, and that the coexistence state is the only stable fixed point. A WKB treatment of the master equation gives a $2N$-dimensional Hamiltonian whose fluctuational fixed points are obtained in closed form. The resulting extinction network has $3^{N-1}$ fluctuation-driven transitions, and its coarse-grained dynamics yield an exact recursion for every mean extinction time. We analyse the three-species omnivory module in full, including a minimal set of existence conditions, global stability of coexistence, numerical instanton actions for all nine transitions and the most probable extinction route, which we confirm with stochastic simulations. Finally, we show how deleting a single trophic link reshapes the extinction network.

</details>

### [4] [A Nonlocal Allen--Cahn Model for Volume-Constrained Wetting and Its Multi-Structure-Preserving Discretization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33754)
*Yadong Liu,Nan Lu,Zhonghua Qiao,Yuze Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对有限界面厚度下质量守恒相场模型中液滴仍可能收缩/消失的问题，构建了耦合非线性弥散体积约束与动态壁面弛豫的非局部 Allen–Cahn 模型，并提出精确守恒的 CVC 数值格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限界面厚度（finite interface thickness）条件下，即便相场质量守恒，小液滴在润湿弛豫（wetting relaxation）过程中仍可能收缩甚至消失，这与体积守恒的物理直觉不符，因此需要引入体积层面的约束机制。

Method: 基于 Onsager 原理（Onsager's principle）建立非局部 Allen–Cahn 模型，将非线性弥散体积约束（nonlinear diffuse-volume constraint）与动态壁面弛豫（dynamic wall relaxation）耦合；该约束梯度在纯相值处消失且在能量平衡中不做功，保持原有体相与壁面耗散结构。数值上构造相容变分链式法则（Compatible Variational Chain-Rule, CVC）格式，在修正项与体积增量中使用体积密度相同的离散导数。

Result: 该格式精确守恒非线性弥散体积，并对原始离散能量重现体相–壁面耗散恒等式；在矫顽性条件（coercivity condition）下能量给出依赖于网格的最大范数界（maximum-norm bound）。数值实验显示空间与时间均达二阶收敛，平壁测试中小液滴保持性与几何体积保持性改善，并能重现平衡帽形（cap shapes）与接触角（contact angles）；附加测试展示了化学非均匀与粗糙基底上的润湿行为。

Conclusion: 体积约束与壁面弛豫的耦合建模配合 CVC 离散格式，可在保持原始耗散结构与二阶精度的同时改善小液滴保持和体积守恒，并适用于复杂基底润湿模拟。

Abstract: At finite interface thickness, small droplets can shrink or disappear during wetting relaxation even when the phase-field mass is conserved. We develop a nonlocal Allen--Cahn model that couples a nonlinear diffuse-volume constraint to dynamic wall relaxation through Onsager's principle. The constraint gradient vanishes at the pure-phase values and does zero work in the energy balance, preserving the original bulk and wall dissipation structure. We construct a Compatible Variational Chain-Rule (CVC) scheme using the same discrete derivative of the volume density in the correction and in the volume increment. The scheme exactly conserves the nonlinear diffuse volume and reproduces the bulk--wall dissipation identity for the original discrete energy. Under a coercivity condition, the energy also yields a mesh-dependent maximum-norm bound. Numerical tests show second-order convergence in space and time. In flat-wall tests, the proposed model improves small-droplet retention and geometric-volume preservation and reproduces equilibrium cap shapes and contact angles. Additional tests illustrate wetting on chemically heterogeneous and rough substrates.

</details>


### [5] [Simultaneous Inversion of Drift and Potential in the Fractional Magnetic Schrödinger Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34189)
*Yun-Long Liu,Jin Wen,Jun-Liang Fu,Xue-Juan Ren*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文在二元加性框架下研究含漂移项分数阶磁薛定谔方程的Calderón问题，证明当磁势已知时可唯一同时确定漂移与标量势，磁势未知时三重势仅能确定到规范等价类且漂移为规范不变量，并给出数值重构框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对含漂移（drift）项的分数阶磁薛定谔方程（fractional magnetic Schrödinger equation），现有Calderón问题（Calderón problem）的唯一性结果尚不完整，且缺乏相应的数值重构验证。

Method: 在二元加性框架（bivariate additive framework）下，利用外部的Dirichlet-to-Neumann测量，借助Alessandrini恒等式（Alessandrini identity）、Runge逼近（Runge approximation）以及分数阶磁算子的唯一延拓性质（unique continuation property）进行理论证明；数值上结合有限差分离散（finite difference discretization）、径向基函数代理建模（radial basis function surrogate modeling）与平滑TV正则化（smoothed TV regularization）。

Result: 当磁势A已知且固定时，漂移b与标量势q可被同时且唯一确定；当A未知时，三元组(A,b,q)仅能恢复至自然规范等价类（gauge equivalence class），而漂移b为规范不变量（gauge invariant），因而唯一确定。一维与二维数值算例验证了方法的有效性并支持理论唯一性结论。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了含漂移分数阶磁薛定谔方程Calderón问题的唯一性与规范结构刻画，并提供可行的数值重构方案，理论与数值结果相互印证。

Abstract: We study the Calderón problem for the fractional magnetic Schrödinger equation with drift in the bivariate additive framework. When the magnetic potential $A$ is known and fixed, we prove that the drift $b$ and the scalar potential $q$ can be simultaneously and uniquely determined from exterior Dirichlet-to-Neumann measurements. When $A$ is unknown, we prove that the triple $(A,b,q)$ can only be recovered up to a natural gauge equivalence class, and that the drift $b$ is a gauge invariant, hence uniquely determined. The proofs rely on the Alessandrini identity, Runge approximation, and the unique continuation property for fractional magnetic operators. Furthermore, we have developed a numerical reconstruction framework that combines finite difference discretization, radial basis function surrogate modeling, and smoothed TV regularization. One-dimensional and two-dimensional numerical examples have verified the effectiveness of the proposed method and supported the theoretical uniqueness result.

</details>

### [6] [Two Timescales of Forgetting in Fine-tuning of Language Models: Fast Behavioral Suppression and Slow Representational Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33302)
*Yilin Luo,Jin Hwa Lee,Andrew M. Saxe,Antonio Sclocchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在受控的随机层级模型（Random Hierarchy Model, RHM）上研究监督微调（supervised fine-tuning, SFT），发现行为层面的规则抑制与潜在表征（latent representations）的遗忘发生在两个显著分离的时间尺度上，并在自然语言实验中得到类似结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对预训练（pretraining）后经微调（fine-tuning）如何改变模型表征的理解有限，尤其不清楚表层行为的抑制是否伴随支撑该行为的潜在表征被抹除。

Method: 将 Transformer 在具有层级潜在结构的受控上下文无关文法（context-free grammar）RHM 上预训练，再用其生成规则的受限子集进行 SFT，追踪生成行为与内部表征的变化；并在自然语言的真实设定下验证。

Result: 被删除规则的生成在快时间尺度上改变，该尺度与 token–latent 相关性大小成反比，而其表征保持不变；对应隐藏表征在更长的时间尺度上被遗忘，并伴随网络参数的缓慢漂移。自然语言实验观察到类似结果。

Conclusion: 微调中的遗忘存在时间尺度分离（timescale separation），行为抑制先于表征遗忘；该现象可能是语言模型微调的更普遍特性。

Abstract: Today, a core pipeline of artificial intelligence systems is pretraining on a broad range of text corpora and then fine-tuning on specific tasks or desired behaviors. Yet we still have limited understanding of how fine-tuning on a restricted data distribution modifies representations acquired during pretraining. In particular, it remains unclear whether surface behavioral suppression happens together with the erasure of latent representations that support it. In this work, we study supervised fine-tuning (SFT) by pretraining transformers on a controlled context-free grammar with hierarchical latent structure, the Random Hierarchy Model (RHM), and fine-tuning with a restricted subset of its generative rules. While during pretraining the model's ability to generate with the correct grammar rules coincides with building the internal representations of the corresponding latent, we find that SFT exhibits a very different dynamics: (i) the generation of deleted rules changes on a fast timescale inversely proportional to the magnitude of the token-latent correlation; meanwhile, their corresponding representations stay intact; (ii) the hidden representations of the deleted rules become forgotten on a much longer timescale, associated with a slow drift of the network parameters. Finally, we observe analogous results in a realistic setup with natural language, suggesting that this timescale separation in forgetting is a more general phenomenon of fine-tuning language models.

</details>


### [7] [When High Capacity Becomes Undeployable: Pore-Filling Constraints for Multi-Objective Optimization of Direct Air Capture Sorbents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34154)
*Yujie Liu,Xuehua Shen,Zuotai Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将孔体积填充边界编码为显式结果约束，用于直接空气捕集（DAC）固体胺配方的多目标贝叶斯优化（MOBO），揭示无约束搜索会系统性奖励超出孔填充边界、名义容量虚高但不可部署的候选配方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 名义CO2容量最高的胺浸渍吸附剂往往最难部署，因为超出孔体积上限的胺会堵塞活性位点；然而面向DAC的多目标贝叶斯优化通常将配方搜索视为无约束问题，缺乏对孔填充边界的显式建模。

Method: 构建可DOI追溯的618种固体胺配方基准集（来自43篇文献），将孔填充边界编码为显式约束 c(x) = tanh(1 - phi_hat) - tau，并以此为诊断工具比较约束与无约束MOBO（100个随机种子），同时用分层模型协调仪器效应以检验边界。

Result: 无约束MOBO获得更高名义容量（3.639 +/- 0.815 vs. 2.964 +/- 0.728 mmol/g，p = 1.0e-11），但其Pareto最优候选中63.2%违反孔填充边界，约束条件下为39.3%；约束使前沿转向更低负载量（55.8 vs. 65.2 wt%）与更大孔径。协调仪器效应后，77.8%的动力学方差来自实验室间差异，孔填充带来显著动力学惩罚（p = 0.002）；标准超体积（hypervolume）对忽视可行性的方法高估13.9-16.1%（约束方法为11.1%）。

Conclusion: 约束前沿、再生能耗第三目标与协调后动力学三项代理分析一致指向 phi_hat ~ 0.4-0.5（45-55 wt%负载量）的保守可部署窗口，表明显式编码孔填充边界可纠正MOBO的可行性偏差并提升DAC吸附剂筛选的可部署性。

Abstract: Amine-impregnated sorbents with the highest nominal CO2 capacity are frequently the least deployable, as amine beyond the pore-volume limit blocks active sites. Yet multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) for direct air capture (DAC) treats formulation search as unconstrained. We encode the pore-filling boundary as an explicit outcome constraint, c(x) = tanh(1 - phi_hat) - tau, and use it as a diagnostic instrument on a hand-curated, DOI-traceable benchmark of 618 solid-amine formulations from 43 publications. Unconstrained MOBO attains higher nominal capacity (3.639 +/- 0.815 vs. 2.964 +/- 0.728 mmol/g, p = 1.0e-11), but across 100 seeds 63.2% of its Pareto-optimal candidates violate the pore-filling boundary versus 39.3% under the constraint: it is rewarded for proposing candidates outside the pore-confined design regime. The constraint steers the front to lower loadings (55.8 vs. 65.2 wt%) and larger pores. The boundary is corroborated once apparatus effects are harmonized hierarchically: 77.8% of kinetic variance is between-laboratory, and pore filling carries a significant kinetic penalty (p = 0.002). Standard hypervolume over-credits feasibility-agnostic methods by 13.9-16.1% (vs. 11.1% constrained), and three surrogate-based analyses---the constrained front, a regeneration-energy third objective, and harmonized kinetics---consistently point to a conservative deployable window at pore filling phi_hat ~ 0.4-0.5 (45-55 wt% loading). The benchmark and associated analysis outputs accompany this submission.

</details>


### [8] [Interpretable identification of constitutive parameter field for three-dimensional heterogeneous and graded hyperelastic materials from full-field displacements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34310)
*Kanhaiya Lal Chaurasiya,Vaskar Halder,Prajyot Patil,Gaurav Navnath Shinde,Debashish Das,Akshay Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种可解释、高效的反演框架，仅用位移场数据即可预测非均质超弹性材料三维本构参数场的相对空间变化，精度可接受且计算时间显著低于近期深度学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用实验可得的位移场与边界反力识别非均质固体的空间变化材料属性仍具挑战，而表征非均质与梯度材料对功能梯度材料、复合材料设计及生物医学组织表征具有价值；现有研究多集中于均质材料，非均质/梯度材料研究较少，且近期方法多依赖参数繁重的深度学习框架。

Method: 利用超弹性材料（hyperelastic material）反问题的线性特性，在有限元（finite element）层面直接优化本构参数，构建可解释且省时的框架，预测多个本构参数场的三维相对空间变化，且仅使用位移场数据。

Result: 在涉及非均质、梯度（graded）与各向异性（anisotropic）超弹性材料的三维合成基准与二维实验基准上完成验证，取得可接受的预测精度，计算时间显著低于近期报道的深度学习（deep learning）框架。

Conclusion: 该框架为仅基于位移数据的非均质超弹性材料参数场识别提供了一种兼顾可解释性、精度与计算效率的替代方案，相较于参数繁重的深度学习反演方法更具实用性。

Abstract: The inverse problem of identifying spatially varying material properties of heterogeneous solids using experimentally available displacement fields and boundary reaction forces alone remains challenging. Being able to characterize heterogeneous and graded solids would be advantageous in the design and manufacturing of functionally graded materials and composites. Additionally, an efficient technique for identifying heterogeneous material properties from in vivo measurements would be useful in biomedical contexts to characterize heterogeneous biological tissues. Although several techniques developed over the past two decades have addressed inverse identification for homogeneous materials, including linear elastic, hyperelastic, viscoelastic, and elastoplastic solids, comparatively fewer studies have considered heterogeneous and graded materials. Furthermore, several recent approaches to characterizing heterogeneous and graded materials employ parameter-heavy, deep learning-based frameworks trained to enforce force equilibrium at specified collocation points within the material. In this work, we propose an interpretable and time-efficient framework that predicts the three-dimensional relative spatial variation of a constitutive parameter field for multiple parameters of a heterogeneous hyperelastic material, using only displacement-field data. In this work, we validate our framework on 3D synthetic and 2D experimental benchmarks involving heterogeneous, graded, and anisotropic hyperelastic materials. By leveraging the linear nature of the inverse problem for hyperelastic materials and directly optimizing for the constitutive parameters at the finite-element level, we achieve acceptable prediction accuracy with significantly lower computational time compared to recently reported deep-learning-based frameworks.

</details>

### [9] [Genus one mirror symmetry and surfaces with trivial first Chern class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34594)
*Gabriel Abánades-Joglar,Dennis Eriksson*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究零第一陈类光滑射影簇上亏格一 BCOV 型镜像对称（BCOV type mirror symmetry）的推广，证明朴素推广在扭典范丛情形下不成立，并通过 Enriques 曲面与双椭圆曲面的计算给出反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有设想认为亏格一 Gromov-Witten 不变量（Gromov-Witten invariants）可由镜像族的 BCOV 不变量（BCOV invariant）编码，本文检验该期望在 $c_1(X)=0$ 的光滑射影簇上是否可自然推广。

Method: 结合 Oberdieck 对 Enriques 曲面的 Klemm-Mariño 猜想（Klemm-Mariño conjecture）的证明与 Yoshikawa 的等变解析挠率（equivariant analytic torsion）计算，完成对曲面情形这些不变量的研究，并对双椭圆曲面（bielliptic surfaces）作类似分析。

Result: 两类曲面均出现已知平凡典范丛（trivial canonical bundle）情形中所没有的符号问题（sign issue），该符号问题表明镜像对称预言在扭典范丛（torsion canonical bundle）簇上即使相差一个符号也无法成立。

Conclusion: 亏格一 BCOV 型镜像对称的朴素推广在 $c_1(X)=0$ 的射影簇上一般不成立；扭典范丛情形构成本质障碍，而非技术性符号约定问题。

Abstract: The main goal of this paper is to study the extension of genus-one BCOV type mirror symmetry for smooth projective varieties with vanishing $c_1(X)$. We show that the naive generalization of the expectation that one can encode genus one Gromov-Witten invariants in the BCOV invariant of the mirror family cannot hold in this setup. We show this by completing the study of these invariants for surfaces. Progress had been made recently by Oberdieck's proof of the Klemm-Mariño conjecture for Enriques surfaces, which can be combined with computations of equivariant analytic torsion by Yoshikawa. We also perform the analogous study for bielliptic surfaces.
  Both of these cases present a sign issue not present in the known trivial canonical bundle cases, which we show implies that the mirror symmetry predictions cannot hold for varieties with torsion canonical bundle, even up to sign.

</details>


### [10] [Positive Geometry of Oriented Matroid Strata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32956)
*Arne Kuhrs,Artyom Lisitsyn*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 摘要研究以 Plücker 除子为边界的 Grassmannian 的正几何，证明三点秩下 (Gr₃ₙ, Z₃ₙ) 在 n≤9 时为 Sturmfels–Telen 意义下的正排列，并据此判定七、八点时 Cachazo–Early–Zhang 被积函数是否张成对数形式空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是理解边界为 Plücker 除子并集的 Grassmannian 的正几何结构，并将其与广义双伴随振幅（generalized biadjoint amplitudes）被积函数的张成性质联系起来。

Method: 在秩三情形下，利用有限域（finite fields）上构型空间 𝒳₃ₙ 的点数计算组合秩（combinatorial rank），并给出逐点加点的递归以构造更小边界与任意秩的情形。

Result: n≤9 时 (Gr₃ₙ, Z₃ₙ) 为正排列；七点时各区间的典范形式张成全部对数形式（logarithmic forms），八点时不然，因 Möbius–Kantor 构型不可在实数上实现；十点起边界出现 K3 曲面使亏格为正。

Conclusion: 该结果确定了七点与八点时 CEZ 被积函数是否张成对数形式空间，且递归方法可恢复正 Grassmannian，并表明堆叠多胞体（stacked polytopes）的实现空间是组合秩为一的正排列的区域。

Abstract: We study the positive geometry of Grassmannians whose boundary is a union of Plücker divisors. For the full Plücker boundary the regions are closures of realization spaces of uniform oriented matroids. In rank three we show that the pair $(\mathrm{Gr}_{3,n},Z_{3,n})$ is a positive arrangement in the sense of Sturmfels and Telen for $n\le 9$ points and compute its combinatorial rank from point counts of the configuration space $\mathcal{X}_{3,n}$ over finite fields. For seven points the canonical forms of the regions span all logarithmic forms. For eight points they do not, because the Möbius-Kantor configuration is not realizable over the reals. This determines for seven and eight points whether the integrands of Cachazo, Early and Zhang for the generalized biadjoint amplitudes of Cachazo, Early, Guevara and Mizera span the space of logarithmic forms. From ten points on, the genus is positive because a K3 surface appears in the boundary. For smaller boundaries and arbitrary rank we give a recursion that adds one point at a time and preserves the genus and the combinatorial rank. It recovers the positive Grassmannian and shows that realization spaces of stacked polytopes are regions of positive arrangements of combinatorial rank one.

</details>

### [11] [Large-deformation constitutive modeling of viscoelastic foams: Application to an open-cell foam material](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33416)
*Jialiang Tao,Alexander K. Landauer,Zicheng Yan,Xiuqi Li,Christian Franck,David L Henann*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文针对开孔弹性体泡沫（open-cell elastomeric foams）建立并标定了一个大变形黏超弹性唯象本构框架，通过均匀压缩/拉伸实验与应变局部化检测算法验证标定实验有效性，并用非均匀变形实验结合有限元模拟验证了模型的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开孔弹性体泡沫广泛用于冲击防护与缓冲，具有高弹性压缩性、速率敏感性（rate sensitivity）、体积-偏斜耦合（volumetric-distortional coupling）及动态载荷下可能的压实带（compaction banding）现象，需要可标定、可验证的大变形黏弹性本构模型来支撑其力学响应预测。

Method: 采用源于闭孔泡沫的唯象建模框架（phenomenological modeling framework），将总响应分解为平衡态超弹性（hyperelastic）贡献与若干非线性类 Maxwell 贡献，并用对数应变不变量（invariants of the logarithmic strain）描述大变形与强体积-偏斜耦合；基于各向同性开孔黏弹性泡沫的均匀单轴压缩与拉伸实验（含应力-应变与横向-轴向应变数据，覆盖十倍间隔应变率）进行标定，同时开发新的应变局部化检测算法（strain localization detection algorithm）以排查压实带对标定假设的影响。

Result: 在覆盖多应变率与多种变形类型的故意非均匀变形验证实验中，有限元数值模拟（finite-element-based numerical simulations）得到的模型预测与实测响应在多数情况下一致，差异基本处于实验不确定度（experimental uncertainty bounds）范围内。

Conclusion: 所提出并标定的大变形黏弹性唯象模型具备对开孔弹性体泡沫在非均匀变形条件下力学响应的预测能力；应变局部化检测算法为标定实验的有效性提供了保障。

Abstract: Open-cell elastomeric foams are widely utilized for impact protection and cushioning applications. These foams can demonstrate remarkable elastic compressibility and rate sensitivity over a wide range of force and acceleration protection levels. They often exhibit volumetric-distortional coupling in their mechanical response and potentially compaction banding when subjected to dynamic loads. Here, a phenomenological modeling framework, adopted previously for closed-cell foams, is specified using an experimental characterization-based approach for calibrating and validating the large-deformation, viscoelastic material model. Homogeneous simple compression and tension experiments were performed on isotropic, open-cell viscoelastic foam materials, and stress-strain and lateral-axial strain data were used for model calibration. Data were collected at decadal strain rates to inform the rate-sensitive aspects of the model. The modeling approach involved decomposing the total material response into an equilibrium hyperelastic contribution and several nonlinear, Maxwell-like contributions and utilized invariants of the logarithmic strain to account for large deformations and the strong volumetric-distortional coupling effect. A new strain localization detection algorithm was developed and used to check for the formation of compaction bands, which would invalidate modeling assumptions for the calibration experiments. To validate the predictive capability of the constitutive model, experiments were conducted that involved intentionally inhomogeneous deformations over a spectrum of strain rates and deformation types. The consistency between the measured responses from the validation experiments and the model predictions obtained from finite-element-based numerical simulations was largely within the experimental uncertainty bounds, demonstrating the predictive capability of the model.

</details>


### [12] [Dancing dervishes, spinning skirts and whirling waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33429)
*Paul R. Kaneelil,Sifan Yin,L. Mahadevan*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 受旋转托钵僧袍饰与卡塔克舞者长裙图案启发，研究软质重质圆片绕轴稳定旋转的动力学，实验揭示其经历重力主导的垂挂旋转态、展平态与波浪图案逆行旋转的旋舞态三个典型状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转托钵僧（whirling dervish）袍饰与卡塔克舞者（Kathak dancer）长裙的褶皱图案提供了对软质旋转薄片动力学的直观启发，此前缺乏对该类软质重质圆片稳定旋转状态与相变的系统刻画。

Method: 采用实验与理论相结合的方法：通过实验观察软质重质圆片（soft heavy circular sheet）的旋转行为，构建三维相图（three-dimensional phase diagram），并用最小模型（minimal model）分析状态转变，推导描述相界的标度律（scaling laws）并经实验验证。

Result: 实验表明初始垂挂的圆片随转速变化依次呈现三种典型状态（archetypal states）：重力主导的垂挂旋转态（gravity-dominated draped rotation）、展平态（flat state）以及相对旋转参考系出现波浪图案逆行旋转（retrograde rotation）的旋舞态（whirling state）；三维相图刻画了各构型，标度律所预言的相界与实验一致。

Conclusion: 软质重质圆片的稳定旋转存在由重力、惯性等因素竞争决定的多重状态与相界，最小模型与标度律可定量描述其相变，为软质薄片旋转动力学提供了可预测的框架。

Abstract: Inspired by the patterns seen in the tunic of a whirling dervish or the long skirt of a Kathak dancer, we study the dynamics of a \emph{soft} heavy circular sheet spinning steadily around its axis. Using experiments, we show that the initially draped sheet can pass through three archetypal states as it rotates: a gravity-dominated draped rotation state, a flat state, and finally a whirling state marked by retrograde rotation of wavy patterns relative to the rotating frame. We map out these various configurations in a three-dimensional phase diagram, analyze the transitions using a minimal model, and derive scaling laws to describe the phase boundaries which we confirm using experiments.

</details>


### [13] [Soft Matter, Hard Rules: Emulsions Follow the Laws of Granular Suspension Rheology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33526)
*Wenjun Chen,Eric De Giuli,Matthieu Wyart,Yoël Forterre,Jasna Brujić,Bloen Metzger*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 通过将控制变量由体积分数改为渗透压（osmotic pressure）并使用 Capillarytron 仪器测量，发现浓乳液的流变数据在相对于压力依赖的堵塞点（jamming point）归一化后可坍缩为单一幂律发散，从而给出跨牛顿、屈服与剪切变稀区间（regimes）的无参数预测性本构关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浓乳液（dense emulsions）广泛存在于食品、制药与生物工程中，但在传统体积受控（volume-imposed）条件下其流变行为（rheology）难以解读，缺乏统一的结构性描述。

Method: 将控制变量改为渗透压（osmotic pressure），采用新近研制的 Capillarytron 仪器，进行渗透压受控与体积分数受控两类测量，并以相对于堵塞点的距离为标度变量分析流变数据。

Result: 渗透压通过控制液滴变形（droplet deformation）设定压力依赖的堵塞体积分数；以距该堵塞点的距离表达时，两类测量数据坍缩为单一幂律发散（power-law divergence），与颗粒悬浮液（granular suspensions）类似，并由此得到参数无关的本构关系。

Conclusion: 结果表明从软球（soft spheres）到乳液、可能还包括泡沫（foams）的软非晶材料（soft amorphous materials）遵循与颗粒悬浮液相同的“硬规则”，软性通过压力依赖的堵塞点进入；该框架解释了 Herschel-Bulkley 流变学并赋予其参数微观意义，对包括生物组织（biological tissues）在内的软可变形系统的刚性转变（rigidity transitions）研究具有启示。

Abstract: The flow of dense emulsions underlies applications from food and pharmaceutical processing to bioengineering, yet their rheology remains difficult to interpret under conventional volume-imposed conditions. Here, we change the control variable to osmotic-pressure using a recently developed instrument -- the Capillarytron. This approach reveals a unified rheological structure where the osmotic pressure $Π$, by controlling droplet deformation, sets a pressure-dependent jamming volume fraction. When expressed in terms of the distance to this jamming point, all rheological data -- spanning both $Π$- and $φ$-imposed measurements -- collapse onto a single power-law divergence, akin to granular suspensions. The resulting constitutive relations provide a predictive, parameter-free description of emulsion rheology across Newtonian, yielding, and shear-thinning regimes. Together with recent results on soft spheres, our findings point to a unifying paradigm: Soft amorphous materials -- from soft spheres to emulsions and likely foams -- obey the same hard rules as granular suspensions, with softness entering through a pressure-dependent jamming point. This framework rationalizes Herschel-Bulkley rheology, assigns its parameters microscopic meaning, and opens perspectives on rigidity transitions in soft, deformable systems, including biological tissues.

</details>


### [14] [Frustration in system with intercalated and bilayer smectic structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34072)
*Calum J. Gibb,Claudio Flores Fernandez,Magdalena M. Majewska,John M. D. Storey,Corrie T. Imrie,Ewa Gorecka,Damian Pociecha*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 通过对非对称弯曲二聚体（bent dimers）同系物 CBO6.Om 的研究，借助共振X射线衍射（resonant X-ray diffraction）确认较长同系物形成 SmCTB-SH 与 SmCTB-DH 两种螺旋四层结构，后者叠加了一个周期随温度强烈变化的额外螺旋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二聚体介晶（dimeric mesogens）呈现向列相与近晶相等多样且堆积方式迥异的介相，其分子结构与介相结构之间的关系尚需澄清，尤其弯曲二聚体倾向形成扭曲结构所带来的额外复杂性值得深入表征。

Method: 以系列 Pd、Pt 催化交叉偶联反应（cross-coupling）合成的非对称弯曲二聚体同系物 CBO6.Om 为对象，采用共振X射线衍射（resonant X-ray diffraction）进行结构解析。

Result: 较短同系物形成插层结构（intercalated structure）的近晶相，较长同系物形成层内分子取向上下对称性破缺的双层近晶相，中间同系物因阻挫（frustration）出现向列相间隙；较长同系物被确认为 SmCTB-SH 螺旋四层结构，以及在其四层基本单元上叠加额外螺旋的 SmCTB-DH 相，该额外螺旋的周期随降温由 100 nm 变为 40 nm。

Conclusion: 弯曲二聚体介相的堆积与手性/螺旋调制具有高度的结构多样性，SmCTB-DH 相中额外螺旋的强温度依赖性表明层内取向调制可作为一个可调的结构自由度。

Abstract: The dimeric mesogens exhibit an unusual variety of mesophases - nematic and smectic, with distinctly different packing schemes. The relation between molecular and mesophase structure was studied for homologue series of non-symmetric bent dimers CBO6.Om, obtained by serial Pd- and Pt-catalyzed cross-coupling chemical reactions. The shorter homologues of the series form smectics with an intercalated structure, and longer homologues form bilayer smectics with broken up-down symmetry of molecular orientation within the layers, for intermediate homologues frustration leads to a nematic gap. The additional structure complexity arises from the tendency of bent dimers to form twisted structures, in which the azimuthal angle of tilted molecules forms both short- and long-pitch helices. Here we confirm, through resonant X-ray diffraction, that longer homologues form a helical four-layer structure of SmCTB-SH phase, along with a more complex one in which, in addition to the basic four-layer unit, an extra helix is superimposed, SmCTB-DH phase. This additional helix has a periodicity that is strongly temperature-dependent; upon cooling, it changes from 100 nm to 40 nm.

</details>


### [15] [Length-scales and fractal scaling in dairy gels: comparisons of the insights from ultra-small angle neutron scattering and rheology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34194)
*Koduvayur A. Ramya,Liliana de Campo,Markus Strobl,François Boué,Christopher J. Garvey*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究结合 USANS 与振荡流变学，考察酸化和酶诱导两种乳凝胶（dairy gel）中胶体颗粒聚集形成的分形结构，并用 Shih-Wu-Morbidelli 模型将分形絮体（floc）的局部弹性与宏观模量关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒凝胶（particulate gel）的质构性质由网络分形维数（fractal dimension, Df）与关联长度（correlation length, xi）所定义的自相似空间尺度决定，因此需要将微观结构与宏观力学性质建立定量联系。

Method: 以复原脱脂乳在给定酪蛋白胶束（casein micelle, CM）体积分数下通过酸化与凝乳酶（rennet）诱导成胶；用振荡流变学（oscillatory rheology）获取凝胶时间、力学性质成熟时间尺度与黏弹性；用超小角中子散射（ultra-small-angle neutron scattering, USANS）探测约 60 nm 至 15 微米的结构，并以受样品组成约束的模型拟合微分散射截面，再借助流变标度理论与 Shih-Wu-Morbidelli 模型进行关联。

Result: USANS 视角的结构平衡时间尺度短于流变学凝胶时间；低 q 区由 CM 聚集产生的分形标度可由幂律密度-密度关联函数描述；两种技术给出的分形维数数值相近，但对应尺寸不同。

Conclusion: 流变学可通过 Shih-Wu-Morbidelli 模型将分形絮体内部及絮体间的局部弹性与凝胶宏观模量关联；两种技术分形维数相近而特征尺寸不同，反映乳凝胶化过程中分形演化的复杂性，模型仍有改进空间。

Abstract: A key structural parameter that dictates the textural properties of a particulate gel is the fractal dimension (Df) of the network indicating self-similarity within the spatial range defined by the correlation length (xi). In this study dairy gels are formed from reconstituted skim milk at defined CM volume fractions via two different mechanisms: acidification, and enzymatic (rennet)-induction. Oscillatory rheology is performed to obtain the gelation times, the time-scale for the maturation of mechanical properties of the gel, and the viscoelastic properties of the gel. A direct probe of gel structure is ultra-small-angle neutron scattering (USANS) examining length-scales from approximately 60 nm up to 15 microns. Structural information is extracted from USANS of matured gels by comparing the measured differential scattering cross-section with that calculated from a model constrained by the sample composition. Time-scales for structural equilibration from USANS perspective were shorter than the rheological gelation time for both gels. Rheological scaling theories are utilized to relate the microstructure to their bulk mechanical properties. We show that the fractal scaling at low-q (scattering vectors), due to aggregation of CM, extracted from the USANS at the longest timescales is described by a power-law density-density correlation function, and that rheology relates - through Shih-Wu-Morbidelli models - the local elasticity both within and between the fractal flocs formed by these particles over different size ranges to the bulk moduli of the gels. There are similar values of fractal dimensions from both techniques but different sizes attributed to the complex fractal evolution during dairy gelation, which leaves space to refinements of the models.

</details>

A double glass transition is observed by calorimetry on heating for m = 5-7. The calorimetric glass transition temperature shows a composition-dependent correspondence with temperatures at which either the P$_H$ or $α$-relaxation time reaches 100 s, suggesting that different relaxation processes may contribute to vitrification.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 一维DIC与SIC充放电模拟在匹配电流与时长条件下展示了浓度再分布（concentration redistribution）、极化（polarization）与电极利用率（electrode utilization）；数值精度检验量化了对离散化的敏感性，不同初始库存量（initial inventories）用于限定比较条件。

Abstract: We present an isothermal, thermodynamically consistent model of a salt-in-polyelectrolyte battery with intercalation and deintercalation reactions of cations occurring at the electrode-electrolyte interfaces under galvanostatic conditions. For the electrolyte, we consider polyelectrolytes, which are polyanions, with neutralizing cations (i.e., counterions) such as lithium, along with a monovalent salt containing the same cations. Once the model is presented in its most general context, we make specific choices so that it captures well-known mechanisms. In particular, the governing diffusion equations recover Fick's law of diffusion, and the cation-transfer reaction rate is described by the Butler--Volmer equation. The DIC and SIC limiting cases provide a framework for comparing the corresponding batteries. ### [18] [A Genetic Algorithm Approach to Reconstructing Scalar-Tensor Lagrangians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34512)
*Mikel Artola,George Alestas,Dimitrios Efstratiou,Evangelos Achilleas Paraskevas*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文提出数据驱动的符号回归框架，直接从DESI BAO等观测重建标量-张量理论（scalar-tensor theory）的拉格朗日量，所得最优模型对ΛCDM的拟合改善（Δχ²=-16.6）优于CPL参数化（Δχ²=-10.6）。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESI BAO结合CMB与Ia型超新星数据暗示暗能量状态方程（equation of state）可能跨越幽灵分界（phantom divide）w=-1，而最小耦合标量场无法平滑实现该跨越，标量-张量理论则可自然容纳。

Method: 采用遗传算法（genetic algorithm）进化非最小耦合F(φ)与标量势U(φ)的符号表达式，并通过前向模型自洽计算膨胀历史、有效引力耦合与结构增长；候选模型以DESI DR2 BAO、Pantheon+（SH0ES校准）、压缩CMB距离先验及fσ8红移空间畸变数据的联合似然排序，同时限定无屏蔽分支并施加太阳系后牛顿参数γ、引力耦合时变与BBN约束。

Result: 64次独立运行中最佳重建相对ΛCDM的Δχ²=-16.6，优于CPL的-10.6；偏好耦合形式为F(φ)=c₁φ²+e^φ(1-c₂φ)；重建理论在z≳0.5呈幽灵行为，但在低红移接近w=-1。

Conclusion: 结果表明标量-张量框架能够以数据驱动方式拟合当前多探针观测，并在拟合优度上超越传统暗能量参数化，但其低红移行为仍与宇宙学常数一致。

Abstract: Recent DESI baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) data, combined with cosmic microwave background (CMB) and Type Ia supernovae observations, have been interpreted as evidence for dynamical dark energy whose equation of state crosses the phantom divide $w=-1$. Such a crossing cannot be realized smoothly by a minimally coupled scalar field $\varphi$, but can arise naturally in scalar-tensor theories. We present a data-driven framework that reconstructs the scalar-tensor Lagrangian directly from observations, using a genetic algorithm to evolve the symbolic expressions for the nonminimal coupling $F(\varphi)$ and the scalar potential $U(\varphi)$. Each candidate is evaluated through a forward model that self-consistently determines the expansion history, the effective gravitational coupling, and the growth of structure. Candidates are ranked by a joint likelihood of DESI DR2 BAO, Pantheon+ supernovae calibrated with SH0ES Cepheids, compressed CMB distance priors, and redshift-space distortion measurements of $fσ_8$. We restrict to the unscreened branch of the theory and impose Solar System constraints on the post-Newtonian parameter $γ$ and on the time variation of the gravitational coupling, together with a Big Bang nucleosynthesis bound on its evolution. Across 64 independent runs, the best reconstruction improves the fit relative to $Λ$CDM by $Δχ^2=-16.6$, compared with $Δχ^2=-10.6$ for the CPL dark-energy parametrization. The preferred coupling is well described by $F(\varphi)=c_1\varphi^2+e^{\varphi}(1-c_2\varphi)$. The reconstructed theory shows phantom behavior at $z\gtrsim0.5$, but it remains close to $w=-1$ at low redshift.

</details>

*Edward J. Daw*

Main category: eess.SP

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谐振器传递函数（transfer function）的根通常为虚数，与实数一阶滤波器的实现之间存在张力；论文旨在表明通过恰当解释该传递函数仍可获得实用的实数一阶滤波器。

Method: 采用FOREST的离散状态空间模型（discrete state space model）预测谐振器特性，并在数字信号处理平台（digital signal processing platform）的DSP切片上实现该滤波器。

Result: 摘要称FOREST为实数、一阶的数字谐振滤波器与正交状态发生器，其谐振特性可由离散状态空间模型预测，并已在DSP平台上完成实现；具体量化性能未在摘要中给出。

Conclusion: 该工作面向实时数字信号处理（real time digital signal processing）、电网监测与电机控制等工程领域、锁相环（phase locked loop）相关应用以及需要高品质因数谐振器（high-quality-factor resonator）的物理应用。

Abstract: We describe FOREST, a real, first order digital resonant filter and orthogonal state generator. We show that in spite of the imaginary nature of the roots of the transfer function of a resonator, appropriate interpretation of this transfer function nonetheless leads to a practical first order filter with real coefficients. We use a discrete state space model of FOREST to predict the resonator properties, and implement the filter in digital signal processing slices on a digital signal processing platform. Applications are in real time digital signal processing, engineering fields such as mains electricity monitoring and motor control, any application requiring a phase locked loop, and physics applications utilising high-quality-factor resonators.

</details>

### [20] [Generic non-convergence of low-temperature Ising Glauber dynamics on $\mathbb Z^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34193)
*Jonas Köppl,Yannic Steenbeck*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文在 Baire 纲（Baire category）意义下研究 $\mathbb Z^2$ 上零场最近邻 Ising 模型热浴 Glauber 动力学（heat-bath Glauber dynamics）的典型收敛性，证明在足够低的正温度下，同时以正相与负相为弱子列极限的初值律构成稠密 $G_\delta$ 集，从而依分布收敛的初值律是贫集（meagre set）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 Liggett 提出的开放问题（open problem）给出部分回答：考察平移不变初值律下动力学收敛的“典型性”，而非仅研究单个初值的收敛行为。

Method: 结合 Gheissari 与 Sly 的平衡态与混合估计（equilibrium and mixing estimates），构造密度趋于零的正自旋周期性格点（periodic grid of plus spins），并运用 Baire 纲定理（Baire's theorem）；论证适用于 Ising 模型的所有可逆单调 Glauber 动力学（reversible monotone Glauber dynamics）。

Result: 在足够低的正温度下，演化同时具有正相和负相作为弱子列极限（weak subsequential limits）的初值律构成平移不变律空间（弱拓扑）中的稠密 $G_\delta$ 子集；因此依分布收敛的初值律集合为贫集。特别地，即使对空间遍历（spatially ergodic）初值律，大的初始磁化强度也不强制收敛到正相。

Conclusion: 收敛在 Baire 纲意义下是非典型的：典型初值律不收敛于单一相，这为 Liggett 的开放问题提供了部分解答，并推广至 Ising 模型所有可逆单调 Glauber 动力学。

Abstract: We study how typical convergence is, in the topological sense of Baire category, for the heat-bath Glauber dynamics of the nearest-neighbour, zero-field Ising model on $\mathbb Z^2$ started from translation-invariant initial laws. At sufficiently low positive temperature, we show that the initial laws whose evolution has both the plus and the minus phase as weak subsequential limits form a dense $G_δ$ subset of the space of translation-invariant laws with the weak topology. Consequently, the initial laws for which the dynamics converges in distribution form a meagre set, which gives a partial answer to an open problem posed by Liggett. In particular, a large initial magnetisation does not force convergence to the plus phase, even for spatially ergodic initial laws. The proof combines equilibrium and mixing estimates of Gheissari and Sly with a periodic grid of plus spins of vanishing density and Baire's theorem, and it applies to all reversible monotone Glauber dynamics for the Ising model.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 由分布导出吸收体属性，与已有文献一致；所有参数间存在能量相关关联，簇射极大值与形状参数关联最强；条件能量分数分布与平移缩放Gamma分布、成对对数比与Fisher's-z分布相符。Copula采样在刻画簇射极大值与纵向形状参数的重尾（heavy tails）方面优于相关多元正态直接采样；生成模型在流形与分类器指标上接近数据-数据上限，最快生成时间约30 μs/粒子簇射。

Conclusion: 簇射形状变量可由对数正态分布及高斯Copula有效描述，所发展的条件化Copula生成方法能够高保真、快速地模拟完整电子簇射事件，为量能器模拟提供可行的生成式替代途径。

Abstract: Electron shower shapes were measured using standard shower profile models for events observed with a highly granular crystal electromagnetic calorimeter prototype, with data taken from a 2025 test beam at the CERN Proton Synchrotron T9 beamline. The shower maximum, longitudinal shape parameter, and lateral variance were found to be well approximated by log-normal distributions. From these distributions, properties of the absorber were measured to be in agreement with existing literature. Energy-dependent correlations were observed between all parameters and most strongly between the shower maximum and the shower shape parameter. The distributions of energy and event-by-event pairwise log ratios of energy fractions in individual active volumes of the calorimeter conditional on the shower shape were found to be consistent with shifted and scaled Gamma and Fisher's-z distributions, respectively. Generative methods were developed to simulate the logarithm of shower shape variables using Gaussian copulas. Copula sampling for shower shape parameters was found to produce superior modeling of heavy tails observed for the shower maximum and longitudinal shape parameter distributions when compared to direct sampling from a correlated multivariate normal distribution. A copula method was also devised to sample full shower events conditional on the shower shape parameters by exploiting the observation that the vector of energy fractions follows a logistic Log-Gamma distribution, the joint relation implied by the observed Fisher's-z form of the pairwise log-ratios. The generative model was evaluated using standard manifold and classifier metrics and was found to approach the data-versus-data ceiling. The fastest generation time was found to be around 30 μs per particle shower.

</details>

### [22] [Resolving the Conformal Mode Problem with Einstein-Cartan Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33278)
*Stephon Alexander,Raymond Isichei,João Magueijo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 当宇宙学常数 Λ>0 时，一阶 Einstein-Cartan 引力（Einstein-Cartan gravity）中不存在共形因子问题（conformal factor problem）：固定联络下对四元标架（tetrad）作共形重标度不产生共形动能项，作用量因而有下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量（Einstein-Hilbert action）中因共形因子导致的动能项无下界（ unbounded）问题，寻求在一阶形式框架下消除该病态。

Method: 采用自旋联络（spin connection）与四元标架离壳独立的 Einstein-Cartan 一阶表述，在联络固定时分析四元标架的共形重标度对作用量二次动能结构的影响，并考察作用量关于联络与共形模（conformal mode）的经典鞍点（classical saddles）。

Result: 固定联络下共形重标度不生成共形动能项，作用量有下界；原始 Einstein-Hilbert 作用量、去掉问题共形动能项的 Einstein-Hilbert 作用量，以及共形不变（conformally invariant）的 R²/Λ 引力，均作为该 Einstein-Cartan 作用量关于联络与共形模的经典鞍点出现。

Conclusion: 在 Λ>0 的一阶 Einstein-Cartan 引力中，共形因子问题被自然规避，且多种已知引力作用量可作为其经典极限/鞍点被统一刻画。

Abstract: We show that, when $Λ> 0$, the conformal factor problem is absent in first order Einstein-Cartan gravity in which the spin connection and tetrad are independent off-shell. At fixed connection, a conformal rescaling of the tetrad produces no conformal kinetic term so that the action is bounded from below. The original unbounded Einstein-Hilbert action, the Einstein-Hilbert action without the problematic conformal kinetic term and conformally invariant $R^{2}/Λ$ gravity arise as classical saddles of the Einstein-Cartan action with respect to the connection and conformal mode of the tetrad.

</details>


### [23] [Quasinormal modes of black holes in astrophysical environments: Rainbow in the dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33295)
*Kyriakos Destounis,Laura Pezzella*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文计算了嵌入相对论性 Hernquist 型暗物质晕（dark matter halo）中黑洞的轴模（axial）与极模（polar）引力准正规模（quasinormal modes），发现暗物质晕如同"棱镜"般色散引力频谱，破坏了对偶等谱性（parity-isospectrality）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞周围物质通过背景几何与物质自身响应两条途径影响铃宕（ringdown），需明确暗物质晕对引力波准正规模的修正机制及其是否保持对偶等谱性。

Method: 在相对论性 Hernquist 型暗物质晕背景下求解轴模与极模引力准正规模，追踪基模（fundamental mode）及对应真空 Schwarzschild 谱的前三个泛音（overtones）；极模部分需耦合度规扰动与物质扰动。

Result: 晕紧致度（compactness）较小时两扇区均遵循引力红移（gravitational redshift）规律；紧致度较大时，主要偏离可由背景精确的地平线失效因子（horizon lapse factor）解释，剩余偏离依赖扇区。轴模与极模频率出现劈裂，且由基模到第三泛音逐渐增大，大紧致度下劈裂增长一个量级，泛音与基模频率比也偏离统一的重标度关系。

Conclusion: 在所采用的暗物质剖面上，晕对铃宕起"棱镜"式色散作用，留下无法由统一频率重标度再现的宇称依赖印记，因此对偶等谱性被破坏。

Abstract: Matter around black holes affects the ringdown in two ways: through the background geometry and through the response of the matter itself. We compute axial and polar gravitational quasinormal modes of a black hole embedded in a relativistic Hernquist-type dark matter halo, following the fundamental mode and the first three overtones associated with the vacuum Schwarzschild spectrum. The polar sector couples metric and matter perturbations. At small halo compactness, both sectors follow the familiar gravitational redshift law. Most of the departure from the typical redshift at larger compactness is accounted for by the exact horizon lapse factor of the background. The remaining residual departure to this lapse is sector dependent. Axial and polar frequencies are split, with their splitting growing from the fundamental through the third overtone. At large halo compactness the split grows by an order of magnitude, while overtone-to-fundamental frequency ratios depart from a common rescaling. Within the adopted dark matter profile, the halo acts on the ringdown as a prism that disperses the gravitational spectrum, leaving a parity-dependent imprint that no common rescaling of the frequencies can reproduce. Parity-isospectrality is, thus, broken.

Abstract: Investigating black hole physics in semi-classical gravitational theory is an interesting and important direction. Our work serves as an essential stepping stone for studying black hole geometries under the full conformal anomaly.

Abstract: In this paper, we construct the charges associated with the residual local Lorentz transformations on a black hole horizon in the Lovelock class of theories of gravity. The horizons is assumed to be non- expanding horizons, \emph{i.e} a null hypersurface for which the expansion corresponding to the null generator vanish. Naturally, these hypersurfaces preserve only a subgroup of the full Lorentz group of the bulk, and the residual symmetries correspond to the little group which stabilizes the horizon null generator. We prove that there exist spherically symmetric, regular Cauchy data for the Einstein-scalar field system with $\mathbb S^3$ topology, for any scalar field mass $\mathfrak{m}\geq 0$, that lead to the formation of an exactly extremal Schwarzschild-de Sitter black hole in finite time, and that moreover the apparent horizon of the solution is exactly isometric to that of the subextremal Schwarzschild-de Sitter solution at earlier times. We also show that if the scalar field mass satisfies $\mathfrak{m}>\sqrtΛ/2$, an analogous result holds having the extremal Schwarzschild-de Sitter white hole or the Nariai solution in place of the black hole. In the black hole case, the exactly extremal Schwarzschild-de Sitter region is bounded to the future by two event horizons and can be seen as the analogue of the exterior region in the asymptotically flat case. We moreover establish that these two black hole horizons that bound the exactly extremal Schwarzschild-de Sitter region are the only event horizons present in the spacetime, something non-trivial given the existence of multi-black hole configurations when $Λ> 0$. Consequently, the solutions arising from these data can be thought to constitute, in particular, regular counterexamples to the third law of black hole thermodynamics with $Λ>0$ arising from the collapse of physically plausible matter which is moreover uncharged. </details>

Abstract: We present phenjax, a JAX implementation of the precessing time-domain IMRPhenomT family of models for the gravitational wave signal emitted in compact binary coalescence. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We provide a non-divergent resolution of the identity for coherent states of the Euclidean group $E(2)$, replacing the integral on the whole group with the Haar measure by an invariant integration with non local kernels constructed by Steinberg and Wolf in 1981, valid for solutions of Helmholtz equation, that appears in natural way as the eigenvalue equation of the Casimir operator of $E(2)$. </details>

Conclusion: 该结果验证了 Kojima 与 Tachikawa 的猜想，表明该傅里叶同构可作为研究约化群及其 Langlands 对偶之间计数对称性的统一框架。

The transform exchanges central twists with characters of the actual grades and preserves the invariant polynomial contributions of the full centralizers. In degree zero it yields central-coefficient reciprocity under the Poincaré pairing on $S^3/Γ$.
  In particular, $Γ$ admits equally many conjugacy classes of homomorphisms into $G$ and its Langlands dual, which verifies a conjecture of Kojima and Tachikawa.

</details>

*Ivan G. Avramidi,Forest Good*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文系统建立了自旋值微分形式（spinor valued differential forms）的代数与几何分析框架，包括Clifford代数、$\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R})$分解、自然一阶算子及Weitzenböck公式，并据此在四维情形下构造Seiberg–Witten单极方程（monopole equations）的两种推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将Seiberg–Witten理论从自旋子（spinor）与2-形式的经典设定推广到一般的自旋值$p$-形式，需要一个完整的代数与微分算子理论作为基础。

Method: 在紧致偶数维Riemann自旋（或$\mathrm{Spin}^\mathbb{C}$）流形上，研究扭化线丛$L$下的自旋值$p$-形式丛$\mathcal{S}_p$；发展其手性、分次与对偶对合、Fierz型恒等式，利用Clifford乘法与缩并生成的$\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R})$作用做不可约分解与正交基构造；引入扭化外微分、余微分、Dirac与Rarita–Schwinger算子等自然一阶算子，推导其共形变换律与Weitzenböck公式（以Weyl张量、Ricci张量等表达曲率项）。

Result: 得到扭化de Rham与Dirac Laplacian的Weitzenböck公式，并由此导出消灭定理（vanishing theorems）、热核不变量（heat trace invariants）与指标公式（index formulas）；在四维情形下构造非负泛函，其绝对极小给出基于Dirac算子与基于扭化de Rham Laplacian的两种右自旋值$p$-形式Seiberg–Witten单极方程推广，并讨论相应的可积性条件。

Conclusion: 自旋值微分形式的代数分解与Weitzenböck理论为推广Seiberg–Witten方程提供了统一框架，四维情形下所得的两类推广方程及其可积性条件构成进一步研究的出发点。

Abstract: We study spinor valued differential forms, that is, sections of the bundles $\mathcal{S}_p=\mathcal{S}\otimesΛ_p\otimes L$ of spinor valued $p$-forms twisted by a $U(1)$ line bundle $L$, on compact even-dimensional Riemannian spin (or $\mathrm{Spin}^\mathbb{C}$) manifolds. We develop the algebra of spinor valued forms: the chirality, grading and duality involutions, the bilinear tensors built from spinor valued forms and the Fierz-type identities they satisfy, and the action of the Lie algebra $\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R})$ generated by Clifford multiplication and contraction, which we use to decompose the space of spinor valued forms into irreducible components and to construct an adapted orthonormal basis. We introduce the twisted exterior derivative and coderivative, the Dirac and Rarita--Schwinger operators and, more generally, the natural first-order operators on spinor valued forms, as well as the twisted Hodge and signature operators, and derive their conformal transformation laws. We obtain the Weitzenböck formulas for the twisted de Rham and Dirac Laplacians, express the curvature terms through the Weyl tensor, the Ricci tensor, the curvature of $L$ and the bilinear tensors of the spinor valued forms, and derive vanishing theorems, heat trace invariants and index formulas. Finally, in four dimensions we use these Weitzenböck formulas to construct non-negative functionals whose absolute minima define two generalizations of the Seiberg--Witten monopole equations to right spinor valued $p$-forms, one based on the Dirac operator and one on the twisted de Rham Laplacian, and discuss the resulting integrability conditions.

</details>


### [40] [Cosmological rigidity in $3$ spacetime dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34800)
*Gregory J. Galloway,Eric Ling,Argam Ohanyan*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在3维宇宙时空（cosmological spacetimes）中证明刚性定理（rigidity theorems）：在零测地线完备（null geodesic completeness）与主能量条件（dominant energy condition）下，Bartnik分裂猜想（Bartnik Splitting Conjecture）框架中的空间拓扑要么为球面型，要么时空分裂为乘积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是考察Bartnik分裂猜想（Bartnik Splitting Conjecture）在宇宙时空情形下的刚性行为，即在附加零测地线完备与主能量条件等假设时，限制空间拓扑或迫使时空分裂为乘积结构。

Method: 方法基于边缘外陷获面（marginally outer trapped surfaces）理论、存在类时或零线（timelike or null line）时的已知刚性结果，以及作者前期在Penrose奇点定理（Penrose's singularity theorem）背景下得到的刚性结果，由此导出由全测地零超曲面（totally geodesic null hypersurfaces）构成的分层（foliation）的存在性；第二个结果利用度量的局部Kundt结构（local Kundt structure），为该零分层构造整体平行生成元（global parallel generator）。

Result: 得到两项主要结果：其一，在Bartnik分裂猜想设定下，附加零测地线完备与主能量条件后，空间拓扑要么为球面型，要么时空分裂为乘积；其二，对满足主能量条件并容许上述类型分层的宇宙且零测地线完备的3维时空，建立了分裂定理（splitting theorem）。

Conclusion: 两项结果相互衔接，共同完成论证：先由分层存在性将问题归约为特定几何结构，再通过分裂定理给出拓扑为球面或时空为乘积的二分结论。

Abstract: We establish rigidity theorems for cosmological spacetimes in $3$ spacetime dimensions. In the setting of the Bartnik Splitting Conjecture, under the additional assumptions of null geodesic completeness and the dominant energy condition, we show that the spatial topology is either spherical or the spacetime splits as a product. Our methods rely on the theory of marginally outer trapped surfaces, known rigidity results in the presence of a timelike or null line, and more recent rigidity results in the context of Penrose's singularity theorem due to the first two authors, from which the existence of a foliation by totally geodesic null hypersurfaces can be concluded. Our second main result closes the argument by establishing a splitting theorem for cosmological and null geodesically complete $3$-dimensional spacetimes which satisfy the dominant energy condition and which admit a foliation of the aforementioned type. In its proof, we make use of the local Kundt structure of the metric and construct a global parallel generator for the null foliation.

</details>

Conclusion: \system可改进并检验现有分析代码，例如优化背景概率密度函数（background probability density function）以减少叠加分析中的残差与虚假信号贡献，使高能中微子数据分析对更广泛研究群体更易获取。

Abstract: Identifying the sources of high-energy cosmic neutrinos is crucial for understanding cosmic particle accelerators and probing new physics. IceCube provides public data that allow researchers to search independently for neutrino sources, but these analyses require substantial technical expertise and careful scientific choices. We present \system, the first publicly available AI agent for high-energy neutrino source searches. Compared to public likelihood codes, \system is easier to install and use, and supports more analysis types. To start an analysis, users need only specify a source or catalog. The agent takes natural-language requests, runs likelihood analyses on public IceCube event data, and saves the settings and calculation records so users can repeat the analyses. We demonstrate \system through single-source and catalog stacking analyses, obtaining results consistent with IceCube results. These examples also show its ability to search the literature, analyze non-power-law spectra using user-provided model code, and develop new analysis functions, such as signal-injection simulations. \system is also a powerful tool for improving and testing existing analysis code, as we demonstrate by using it to refine the background probability density functions and reduce residuals and spurious signal contributions in stacking analyses. \system makes high-energy neutrino data analysis more accessible to the broader research community. Code and usage instructions: \href{https://github.com/beizhouphys/NuSrcAgent}{\faGithub\ {\tt github.com/beizhouphys/NuSrcAgent}}.

</details>

Abstract: The origin of the Milky Way's high-energy neutrinos is debated. Their flavor composition reflects how they are made and could help settle it, but remains to be measured. We show that IceCube and KM3NeT, which see the inner Galaxy in complementary ways from opposite hemispheres, could start resolving its flavor composition in about a decade. With upcoming telescopes, they would infer the $ν_e$ fraction at the sources to within 27%. A first comparison of Galactic and extragalactic flavor compositions could reveal strong magnetic fields in extragalactic sources or neutrino decay.

Method: 比较史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）与Joshi–Malafarina–Narayan（JMN-1）中心裸奇点经引力透镜（gravitational lensing）产生的中心图像，并计算光学薄球对称吸积（optically thin spherical accretion）至Sgr A*情形下的干涉可见度（interferometric visibility）。

Conclusion: 最宽的探测窗口需要分钟级相干积分（coherent integration），因而倾向于采用接近量子极限（near-quantum-limited）的空间干涉仪（space interferometer）进行观测验证。

Abstract: The images of the galactic centers, M87$^*$ and Sgr~A$^*$, obtained by the Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) provide hitherto unknown revelations on these supermassive objects. The key issue here is the presence or otherwise of an event horizon at these ultra-compact entities. We show here that distinguishing the central images generated by gravitational lensing of a Schwarzschild black hole and a Joshi--Malafarina--Narayan (JMN-1) central naked singularity provides important insights into the nature of these supermassive objects and would help test the cosmic censorship conjecture, central to black hole physics. For optically thin spherical accretion onto Sgr~A$^*$, their interferometric visibilities become separable, independently of the emission model, on three baseline ranges within 1.4--6.2 G$λ$ at errors of $\simeq36\,μ$Jy, and at $L_\star=2.64\,$G$λ$ at $\simeq71\,μ$Jy. This is about 28 times below the 2017 Event Horizon Telescope statistical uncertainty. We suggest that the widest detection window demands minutes-long coherent integrations, favoring a near-quantum-limited space interferometer.

### [44] [Is Gravitational-Wave Waveform Systematics a Bottleneck for Formation Channel Inferences using Effective-Spin and Mass-Ratio?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34731)
*Upasana Das,Suvodip Mukherjee*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文通过注入-恢复（injection-recovery）研究评估波形系统误差（waveform systematics）对有效自旋-质量比（χ_eff–q）平面结构的影响，结论是在设计灵敏度下总体人口性质推断不受波形系统误差瓶颈限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: χ_eff 与 q 在引力波参数估计中高度相关且难以区分，而二者常用于判别双黑洞形成通道；因此需检验波形系统误差是否会伪造成 χ_eff–q 相关性等天体物理信号。

Method: 以 NRSur7dq4 作为真实信号进行注入，分别用 SEOBNRv5PHM 与 IMRPhenomTPHM 做参数恢复，考察 χ_eff–q 平面中波形系统误差的影响分布。

Conclusion: 基于 χ_eff–q 相关性推断形成通道的结果可能对波形系统误差较稳健；GW190403_051519 与 GW240527_230910 位于易受影响的参数区域，比 GWTC-5 中其他事件更易受波形系统误差影响。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave measurements of the effective spin $χ_\text{eff}$ and mass ratio $q$ help distinguish binary black hole formation channels, motivating a search for structure in the $χ_\text{eff} - q$ plane. However, both these quantities are known to be strongly correlated in GW parameter estimation since both enter the leading-order spin-orbit term together, and thus, the detectors cannot easily tell them apart. In this work, we harness the susceptibility of such measurements to waveform systematics by using injection-recovery studies with NRSur7dq4 as the true signal and SEOBNRv5PHM and IMRPhenomTPHM for parameter inferences. Our study shows that, though there can be some parameter space in the $χ_\text{eff} - q$ plane, primarily in the unequal-mass regime, there can be some impacts from waveform systematics; overall, at a population level, the waveform systematics for the current detectors at their design sensitivity are not a bottleneck in recovering the underlying true population properties. We find that astrophysical properties such as $χ_\text{eff} - q$ correlations, which are signatures of different formation channels, are unlikely to be spuriously induced by waveform systematics, and inferences about formation channels based on such correlations are likely robust to waveform systematics at present, though some events that are in the parameter space of large impact can show some outlier behaviour. We find that some of the GW events such as GW190403$\_$051519 and GW240527$\_$230910 are in the vicinity of the $χ_\text{eff} - q$ parameter space, where large waveform systematics can arise, and these events are more susceptible to waveform systematics than others in GWTC-5. This study will play a vital role in precision astrophysical population inference from GW data in the future and in assessing the possibility of waveform systematics.

</details>
