TL;DR: LHCb 利用 2024 年升级探测器采集的 13.6 TeV 质子-质子碰撞数据（积分亮度 64 pb⁻¹），测量了即时产生的反Λ超子与 K⁰_S 介子的产额截面比随横动量与快度的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该截面比（cross-section ratio）可作为检验强子化模型与事件产生器（event generator）的敏感观测量，并为未来产生器参数调节提供实验输入。

Method: 基于质心能量 13.6 TeV 的质子-质子碰撞数据，在 0.15 < p_T < 2.5 GeV/c 与 2.0 < y < 4.5 范围内，将反Λ与 K⁰_S 的即时产额截面比表示为横动量（transverse momentum）与快度（rapidity）的函数。

Result: 测量结果与不同质心能量下的先前测量良好一致，并与现有事件产生器的预言进行了比较。

Conclusion: 该比值测量可用于约束和调节未来事件产生器中的强子化参数。

Abstract: The production cross-section ratio of promptly produced $\barΛ$ and $K_\rm{S}^0$ hadrons is measured in proton-proton collision data at a centre-of-mass energy of 13.6TeV recorded in 2024 with the upgraded LHCb detector and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $64\rm{pb}^{-1}$. The ratio is presented as a function of transverse momentum, $p_\rm{T}$, and rapidity, $y$, in the ranges $0.15 < p_\rm{T} < 2.5\rm{GeV}/c$ and $2.0 < y < 4.5$. The results are in good agreement with previous measurements at different centre-of-mass energies. They are compared to predictions from currently available event generators and can be used as input in future event generator tunings.

</details>


### [83] [Letter of Intent: AN Underground Belayed In-Shaft (ANUBIS) detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34882)
*ANUBIS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ANUBIS 提议利用 ATLAS 实验厅上方空腔作为衰变体积，以约 1600 m² 阻性板室（resistive plate chambers, RPC）探测长寿命粒子（long-lived particles, LLP），在高光度 LHC 下显著提升对奇异希格斯衰变、重中性轻子及类轴子粒子的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型扩展（如暗物质与重子生成模型）中自然出现长寿命粒子，其在电弱能标及以上产生、衰变长度为数十米时会逃逸通用 LHC 探测器，且前向与束流倾倒实验无法以足够速率产生这类粒子。

Method: 在 ATLAS 实验厅顶棚及通道竖井底部布设约 1600 m² 阻性板室（resistive plate chambers），构成间距约 1 m 的两层径迹探测层，并以 ATLAS 探测器上方充气空间作为衰变体积；利用完整 ATLAS 事例信息抑制本底，并与 ATLAS 交换触发。

Result: 在高光度 LHC 累积 3 ab⁻¹ 条件下，对奇异希格斯玻色子衰变为长寿命标量的灵敏度提升两至三个数量级，可探测三种味、混合参数低至约 2×10⁻⁸ 的重中性轻子（heavy neutral leptons），并扩展对类轴子粒子（axion-like particles）的探测范围；2024–2025 年运行的 proANUBIS 演示器已验证探测器技术。

Conclusion: 该意向书（Letter of Intent）给出探测器设计、触发方案、与 ATLAS 实验的集成方式，以及于 LHC 第四次长停机（Long Shutdown 4）期间完成安装的时间表。

Abstract: Long-lived particles (LLPs) arise naturally in many extensions of the Standard Model, notably in models with dark matter candidates or motivated by baryogenesis. LLPs produced at or above the electroweak scale with decay lengths of tens of metres escape the general-purpose LHC detectors and cannot be produced at sufficient rates in forward and beam-dump experiments. This Letter of Intent proposes the ANUBIS detector, which would instrument the ceiling of the ATLAS cavern and the bottom of its access shafts with about 1600~m$^2$ of resistive plate chambers in two tracking layers about 1~m apart, using the air-filled volume above the ATLAS detector as decay volume. The ANUBIS detector exploits the full ATLAS event information to suppress backgrounds, and exchanges triggers with the ATLAS experiment. With 3~ab$^{-1}$ at the High-Luminosity LHC, it would improve the sensitivity to exotic Higgs-boson decays into long-lived scalars by two to three orders of magnitude, probe heavy neutral leptons of all three flavours down to mixings of about $2\times10^{-8}$, and extend the reach for axion-like particles. The \textsl{pro}ANUBIS demonstrator, operated in the ATLAS cavern in 2024 and 2025, has validated the detector technology. The detector design, trigger concept, integration with the ATLAS experiment and a timeline towards installation in LHC Long Shutdown 4 are presented.

</details>

### [84] [Point Equivalence, Lie Symmetries, and Laurent Compatibility of the Time-Dependent Modified Dispersive Water-Wave System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33199)
*Manjit Singh*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文对含两个非零实解析时变系数的 (2+1) 维修正色散水波类（modified dispersive water-wave class）的局部点变换（local point transformation）进行完全分类，并给出系数作用的精确形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该波类含两个非零实解析时间依赖系数，其点对称与等价性结构此前未被完整刻画；作者希望厘清该类内部的全部局部点等价关系，并揭示系数不变量与可积性判据。

Method: 先通过时间变换将双系数类约化为单一系数 q；再用可逆微分替换（invertible differential substitution）将该类等同于已分类耦合系统的某个切片，从而得到一族点映射；随后在原场变量中直接计算，证明诱导的系数作用为 \widetilde q(F(T))=q(T)/k（F'>0，k≠0 为常数）穷尽了所有局部点等价；并用首个非零导数的阶数与相对符号对非恒定系数芽（germ）分类。

Result: 所有局部点等价均由该系数作用刻画；非恒定 pointed 系数芽按首个非零导数的阶与相对符号分类。恒定芽允许任意时间生成元；正则非恒定芽在模去零时间分量子代数后有一个独立时间生成元；临界非恒定芽经基时间无生成元。在 φ_xφ_y≠0 的可动解析超曲面上，双极点支与正则-V Laurent 支在共振 j=2 处均有正比于 q' 的障碍。

Conclusion: 两支形式相容当且仅当 q 为常数，而该条件恰也是与标准系统点等价的精确判据，从而把 Laurent 展开的可积性障碍与点等价不变量统一起来。

Abstract: We classify local point transformations between members of the \((2+1)\)-dimensional modified dispersive water-wave class with two nowhere-zero real-analytic time-dependent coefficients. A change of time reduces the class to a single coefficient \(q\). An invertible differential substitution identifies this class with a slice of a previously classified coupled system and supplies a family of point maps. A direct calculation in the original field variables proves that the induced coefficient action \(\widetilde q(F(T))=q(T)/k\), with \(F'>0\) and \(k\ne0\) constant, exhausts all local point identifications. The order and relative sign of the first nonzero derivative classify nonconstant pointed coefficient germs. Constant germs admit arbitrary time generators, regular nonconstant germs admit one independent time generator modulo the zero-time-component subalgebra, and critical nonconstant germs admit none through the base time. On movable analytic hypersurfaces with \(φ_xφ_y\ne0\), the double-pole and regular-\(V\) Laurent branches both have an obstruction at resonance \(j=2\) proportional to \(q'\). Both branches are formally compatible exactly for constant \(q\), which is also the precise criterion for point equivalence to the standard system.

</details>

### [85] [A Coupled Thermomechanical Model with Volterra Memory: Influence of the Kernel Class on Relaxation Asymptotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33185)
*Andrii Siasiev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究表明，在热力学耦合（thermomechanical coupling）保持不变的前提下，Volterra 记忆核（memory kernel）的类别单独即可决定耦合系统的长期弛豫法则：指数核给出指数弛豫，长尾幂律核给出代数弛豫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在确定 Volterra 记忆核（Volterra memory kernel）的类别与渐近行为如何影响耦合热力学模型（coupled thermomechanical model）中的弛豫过程，该模型通过温度相关材料属性与机械耗散描述内部力学变量与温度的相互作用。

Method: 方法结合 Volterra 积分表述（Volterra integral formulation）、记忆机制的谱表示（spectral representation）、遗传功率（hereditary power）的热力学分解、局部适定性（well-posedness）分析、平衡点附近线性化以及 Laplace 变换渐近论证（Laplace-transform asymptotic arguments）。

Result: 在构成函数与核的最小假设下得到解的局部存在唯一性；对热力学耗散型记忆（thermodynamically dissipative memory）与非负外部热源，绝对温度的正性得以保持。线性化动力学结果显示：指数核产生指数弛豫，长尾幂律核（power-law kernel）产生代数弛豫；同组无量纲参数下的数值比较印证了两种渐近机制。

Conclusion: 谱表示将弛豫模式中储存的能量与不可逆遗传耗散分离，并确立记忆项的热力学相容性（thermodynamic consistency）。结论指出，仅改变记忆核的类别便可改变耦合系统长期弛豫的定性规律。

Abstract: The aim of this study is to determine how the class and asymptotic behaviour of a Volterra memory kernel influence relaxation in a coupled thermomechanical model. The model describes the mutual interaction between an internal mechanical variable and temperature through temperature-dependent material properties and mechanical dissipation. The analysis combines a Volterra integral formulation, a spectral representation of the memory mechanism, thermodynamic decomposition of the hereditary power, local well-posedness analysis, linearization near equilibrium, and Laplace-transform asymptotic arguments. The spectral representation separates the energy stored in the relaxation modes from irreversible hereditary dissipation and establishes the thermodynamic consistency of the memory contribution. Under minimal assumptions on the constitutive functions and the kernel, local existence and uniqueness of solutions are obtained; for thermodynamically dissipative memory and a non-negative external heat source, positivity of the absolute temperature is preserved. The main result concerns the linearized dynamics near equilibrium: with the thermomechanical coupling kept unchanged, an exponential kernel produces exponential relaxation, whereas a power-law kernel with a long tail produces algebraic relaxation. A numerical comparison performed for the same set of dimensionless thermomechanical parameters illustrates these two asymptotic regimes. The results show that changing the class of the memory kernel alone can change the qualitative long-time relaxation law of the coupled system.

</details>


### [86] [Time-reflection nonlocal equations from $\mathcal{P}\mathcal{T}$-symmetric oscillator pairs: physical origin and variational formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33186)
*Ilmar Gahramanov,Yağızhan Şahin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文证明任意含时间反射的二阶微分方程可描述一对局部振子的 $\mathcal{P}\mathcal{T}$-对称解，并系统分析该振子对的拉格朗日结构、系数确定条件及例外点性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立二阶时间反射微分方程与 $\mathcal{P}\mathcal{T}$-对称（parity-time symmetry）振子对之间的普遍对应关系，并厘清此类系统的拉格朗日表述与参数可辨识性。

Method: 对线性情形，振子对由弹簧、平衡增益与损耗（balanced gain and loss）及陀螺耦合（gyroscopic coupling）构成；判定该振子对何时具有正则一阶拉格朗日量（regular first-order Lagrangian），以及方程的解何时确定其系数。以速度耦合不影响解的方程为主要案例，并借助正则变换（canonical transformation）构造 Bateman 型哈密顿量。

Result: 在对应振子对中，速度耦合仅作用于守恒的总动量（total momentum），而该动量对 $\mathcal{P}\mathcal{T}$-对称解为零；两个筛选该模型的条件各自使振子对处于例外点（exceptional point）。当弹簧耦合与速度耦合均非零时，振子对无正则一阶拉格朗日量，而方程需含二阶导数的拉格朗日量。

Conclusion: 论文给出该类 $\mathcal{P}\mathcal{T}$-对称振子对的统一描述，揭示例外点与拉格朗日量存在性的关联，并提出电子实现方案及通过正则变换消除速度耦合的 Bateman 型哈密顿量框架。

Abstract: We show that every second-order differential equation with time reflection describes the $\mathcal{P}\mathcal{T}$-symmetric solutions of a local pair of oscillators that is invariant under exchange combined with time reversal. For linear equations the pair is built from springs, balanced gain and loss, and a gyroscopic coupling. We determine when such a pair has a regular first-order Lagrangian, and when the solutions of the equation fix its coefficients. As the main example, we consider a recently studied equation whose solutions do not depend on its velocity coupling. In the corresponding pair this coupling acts only on the conserved total momentum, which vanishes for $\mathcal{P}\mathcal{T}$-symmetric solutions, and each of the two conditions that single out the model places the pair at an exceptional point. When the spring and velocity couplings are both nonzero, the pair has no regular first-order Lagrangian, while the equation has one with second derivatives. We also propose an electronic realization and construct a Bateman-type Hamiltonian, from which the velocity coupling is removed by a canonical transformation.

</details>


### [87] [A computer-assisted proof of one-cell snaking and lattice trapping for a nonlinear square lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33208)
*Jiangchao Qian*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对无限二维非线性方格子（nonlinear square lattice）中反平面裂纹（antiplane crack）远场作用下的晶格捕获（lattice trapping）现象给出计算机辅助证明（computer-assisted proof），构造出含两个非退化二次折返（nondegenerate quadratic folds）的平衡分支并给出严格正的捕获宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶格捕获是指裂纹在离散晶格中扩展需跨越正的能量势垒区间；既有结果多为数值观察，缺乏对无限晶格模型的严格数学证明。本文旨在为特定相互作用势与反平面裂纹远场提供严格验证。

Method: 构造连接平衡分支，结合重整化能量（renormalized energy）、Schur 补估计（Schur complement estimates）、解析无穷尾部界（analytic infinite-tail bounds）、区间算术（interval arithmetic）与精确整数正性证书（exact integer positivity certificates）完成证明，全部有限输入数据与验证程序随证书档案公开。

Result: 单胞段上恰有两个非退化二次折返，稳定性序列为稳定、Morse 指标一（Morse index one）、再稳定（能量空间中）；折返载荷满足 0.45897&lt;K_-&lt;0.45923 与 0.46219&lt;K_+&lt;0.46242，从而捕获宽度 K_+-K_-&gt;0.00296 严格为正。

Conclusion: 该证明严格确立了所述模型中的晶格捕获；唯一性论证给出精确平移闭合（translation closure）与跨晶格胞的光滑延拓，并配合可复核的证书档案，使结果具备可验证性。

Abstract: We establish a computer-assisted proof of lattice trapping in an infinite nonlinear square lattice with interaction potential $φ(t)=(1-e^{-3t^2})/6$ and an antiplane crack far field $K\sqrt{r}\sin(θ/2)$. We construct a connected branch of equilibria whose single-cell segment contains exactly two nondegenerate quadratic folds, with a stability sequence of stable, Morse index one, and stable in the full energy space. The fold loads satisfy $0.45897<K_-<0.45923$ and $0.46219<K_+<0.46242$, establishing a strictly positive lattice-trapping width $K_+-K_->0.00296$. A uniqueness argument establishes exact translation closure and smooth continuation across lattice cells. The proof combines renormalized energy, Schur complement estimates, analytic infinite-tail bounds, interval arithmetic, and exact integer positivity certificates. All finite input data, verification programs, and recorded certificate outputs are provided in the accompanying certificate archive.

</details>


### [88] [Bistable waves and propagation success on hexagonal lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33224)
*Jian Fang,Yifei Li,Yijun Lou,Jian Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在六边形格点上构建双稳态反应扩散方程（bistable reaction-diffusion equations）的理想化系统，研究行波解（traveling wave solutions）的存在性与波速，给出传播成功与否的锐利判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究受六边形结构介质上的传播现象启发，旨在刻画格点几何与双稳性（bistability）如何影响传播动力学。

Method: 在六边形格点（hexagonal lattice）上建立理想化的双稳态反应扩散方程组，并分析其行波解的存在性与波速性质。

Result: 建立了传播成功（非零波速）与失败（零波速）的锐利判据（sharp criterion），该判据由依赖角度的周期阈值函数（angle-dependent periodic threshold function）与非线性项中两参数之比共同决定；波速符号亦由角度与参数比控制。

Conclusion: 结果表明六边形结构与双稳性显著影响传播动力学。

Abstract: Inspired by propagation phenomena occurring on media with hexagonal structures, we propose an idealized system of bistable reaction-diffusion equations posed on a hexagonal lattice, and investigate the existence and speed of traveling wave solutions. We establish a sharp criterion for propagation success (nonzero wave speed) and failure (zero wave speed), where the criterion is determined by an angle-dependent periodic threshold function and the ratio of two parameters in the nonlinearity. Moreover, the sign of the wave speeds is governed by the angle and the parameter ratio. Our results imply that the hexagonal structure and bistability significantly affect the propagation dynamics.

</details>


### [89] [Equilibrium finiteness and Saari's conjecture for the planar circular restricted N-body problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33570)
*Qinglong Zhou*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对匀旋转平面牛顿中心（planar Newtonian centers）证明两项结果：二次加牛顿修正势（amended potential）的平衡点个数有限，且修正势恒定（即旋转系速率恒定）的无碰撞检验粒子运动必在旋转系中静止；对圆周相对平衡的主天体，这给出限制性 \(N\) 体问题中 Saari 猜想（Saari's conjecture）的修正势形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画匀旋转参考系下牛顿中心构型（Newtonian centers）的平衡点结构与检验粒子动力学，并寻求 Saari 猜想在限制性 \(N\) 体问题（restricted \(N\)-body problem）中的等价表述。

Method: 以平面牛顿中心在均匀旋转（uniform rotation）下的二次加牛顿修正势为对象，通过分析该势的临界点（equilibria）数目与修正势恒定的运动轨道性质进行论证。

Result: 证明两点：对任意至少两个质量为正且互异的中心构成的有限构型，修正势仅有有限个平衡点；任何无碰撞且修正势恒定（等价于旋转系速率恒定）的平面检验粒子运动，在旋转系中均为静止。

Conclusion: 当各中心为圆周相对平衡（circular relative equilibrium）中的主天体时，所得结果构成 Saari 猜想在限制性 \(N\) 体问题中的修正势形式证明。

Abstract: We establish two results for planar Newtonian centers in uniform rotation. First, the associated quadratic-plus-Newtonian amended potential has finitely many equilibria for every finite configuration of at least two distinct centers with positive masses. Second, every collision-free planar test-particle motion with constant amended potential, equivalently constant rotating-frame speed, is stationary in the rotating frame. When the centers are primary bodies in a circular relative equilibrium, this proves the amended-potential form of Saari's conjecture for the restricted \(N\)-body problem.

</details>


### [90] [On the level lines of bilayer potentials with a finite number of harmonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34033)
*A. Ya. Maltsev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究由两个具有相同旋转对称性的周期势叠加而成的“双层”势，在傅里叶展开取有限谐波近似下，分析准周期势在逾渗阈值附近的等值线行为，以刻画大尺度闭合等值线的形态，回应Novikov提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源于S.P. Novikov提出的数学问题，即理解准周期势（quasiperiodic potential）等值线（level lines）在逾渗阈值（percolation threshold）附近的结构，尤其关注大尺度闭合等值线的行为。

Method: 方法上考虑平面内两个周期势叠加构成的“双层”势，假设二者共享相同旋转对称性，并采用傅里叶展开（Fourier expansion）中有限谐波数的近似来简化势函数。

Result: 在上述近似下，通过分析准周期势等值线在逾渗阈值附近的性质，给出了该极限下大尺度闭合等值线行为的描述。

Conclusion: 所得结果亦可推广到具备良好解析性质（“good” analytical properties）的更广泛双层势类，表明方法具有一定的普适性。

Abstract: We consider ``bilayer'' potentials formed by the superposition of two periodic potentials in a plane. We assume that both potentials share the same rotational symmetry and employ an approximation involving a finite number of harmonics in their Fourier expansions. An analysis of the level lines of the resulting quasiperiodic potential (a problem posed by S.P. Novikov) near the percolation threshold makes it possible to describe the behavior of large-scale closed level lines in this limit. The results presented here can also be applied to broader classes of bilayer potentials that possess ``good'' analytical properties.

</details>


### [91] [Colored Jones polynomial of figure eight knot and non-perturbative topological recursion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34110)
*Alexander Hock,Sergey Shadrin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明八字结（figure eight knot）的着色琼斯多项式（colored Jones polynomial）的渐近展开（asymptotic expansion）与底层椭圆谱曲线（elliptic spectral curve）上的非微扰拓扑递归（non-perturbative topological recursion）相关联，验证了 Borot–Eynard 的提议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于 Borot 与 Eynard 提出的猜想：量子不变量（quantum invariants）的渐近展开应与谱曲线上的非微扰拓扑递归相联系。本文以八字结这一典型双曲纽结为对象检验该提议。

Method: 方法上，针对八字结的着色琼斯多项式进行渐近展开分析，并将其与定义在底层椭圆谱曲线上的非微扰拓扑递归（non-perturbative topological recursion）进行对照匹配。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schwinger-Dyson方程通常构成开放无穷维系统，难以直接求解；同时正则图计数（特别是9-与10-正则图）在现有方法下计算代价高，需寻找更高效的递推结构。

Method: 在含多项式作用量的零维量子场论（即带多项式插入的多元高斯积分）框架下，使用Gröbner消元（Gröbner elimination）将全部高阶关联函数（矩，moments）用固定基表示，从而得到“主”关联函数对耦合依赖的封闭有限维微分方程组；再将该闭包应用于标号正则图（labelled regular graphs）计数问题。

Result: 对n个顶点上的9-与10-正则图，得到有限深度、有限宽度的递推表述，可在O(n)次算术运算内计算图总数，较现有最优方法实现计算上的突破。

Conclusion: Schwinger-Dyson闭合使零维量子场论中的关联函数体系降为有限维，并为离散几何结构计数（如单纯形量子引力路径）提供高效算法工具。

Abstract: Schwinger-Dyson equations are a cornerstone of quantum field theory (QFT). Derived using integration by parts, they capture relations between correlators of different orders, forming an open, infinite-dimensional system. In zero-dimensional QFTs with polynomial actions, which is just a physical interpretation for multivariate Gaussian integrals with polynomial insertions, conventional Gröbner elimination can be used to express all higher-order correlators (moments) in terms of a fixed basis set. One consequence is a closed finite-dimensional system of differential equations for the coupling dependence of these `master' correlators.
  An important application of zero-dimensional QFTs is in counting discrete geometric structures (as in simplicial approaches to quantum gravity). For the problem of counting labelled regular graphs, applying this Schwinger-Dyson closure results in a computational breakthrough relative to the existing state-of-the-art: for 9- and 10-regular graphs on $n$ vertices, a finite-depth, finite-width recursive formulation is obtained, making it possible to compute the total graph counts in $O(n)$ arithmetic operations.

</details>

*Arkadiusz Jadczyk*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 得到测地线（geodesics）、平行移动（parallel transport）、平移变换（transvections）与曲率（curvature）的显式公式；正平方根$(JJ')^{1/2}$同时实现相应Hilbert空间之间的等距同构与测地平移变换。

Conclusion: 基本对称性空间具有自然的Banach对称空间结构，正平方根是其等距与测地平移的统一实现；文中同时更正了早先出版物中关于intertwining性质的证明。

Abstract: We study the space of fundamental symmetries of a real or complex Krein space in finite or infinite dimension. After recalling the equivalence of the associated Hilbert norms, we describe two global operator parametrizations and their relation. The space is realized as a Banach symmetric space under $m(J,J')=JJ'J$. Its canonical connection is the tangential projection of the flat derivative in the real Banach space of Krein-self-adjoint operators. This yields explicit formulas for geodesics, parallel transport, transvections, and curvature. The positive square roots $(JJ')^{1/2}$ implement both isometries between the associated Hilbert spaces and geodesic transvections. A proof of their intertwining property in an earlier publication is corrected.

</details>


### [94] [The transverse spin-gradient approximation in noncollinear spin density functional theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34301)
*Mihály A. Csirik*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为密度泛函理论中固定轴横向自旋梯度近似（transverse spin-gradient approximation, TSGA）的非共线变体建立了定量误差界，误差为能量量级 N 下的 O(N^{5/6})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非共线自旋密度泛函理论中，基于局域自旋梯度展开的近似缺乏严格的误差控制，需为固定轴 TSGA 变体提供定量误差界以确立其理论基础。

Method: 受 Eich 与 Gross 启发，以自旋螺旋波（spin-spiral wave, SSW）作为局部参考，构造恒定总密度与指定 SSW 磁化下的电子气热力学极限（thermodynamic limit），其单位体积能量 e_SSW 在局域波矢 ∇φ 处取值；φ 为磁化方位角时，∇φ 刻画 m 绕第三轴（third axis）的局域旋转。

Result: 在旋转框架下自旋极化密度与局域波矢随长度尺度 N^{1/3} 变化（N 为平均粒子数）的条件下，对量级为 N 的能量得到 O(N^{5/6}) 的误差；令相位为零即退化为任意磁化方向的非共线局域自旋密度近似（local spin density approximation, LSDA）。

Conclusion: 近似仅依赖总密度、磁化及其一阶导数，且所证误差界为其非共线推广提供了定量严格性；取相位为零的极限给出任意磁化方向下的非共线 LSDA。

Abstract: We prove a quantitative error bound for a fixed-axis variant of the transverse spin-gradient approximation (TSGA) in noncollinear spin density functional theory. Inspired by Eich and Gross, we use a spin-spiral wave (SSW) as the local reference for the grand-canonical Levy-Lieb energy with the Hartree term subtracted. We construct the thermodynamic limit of the electron gas with constant total density and a prescribed SSW magnetization. Its energy per unit volume $e_{\rm SSW}$ is evaluated at the local wavevector $\nabla\varphi$, where $\varphi$ is the spiral phase. When $\varphi$ is the azimuthal angle of the magnetization $\mathbf{m}=(m_1,m_2,m_3)$, the approximation reads \[
  E(ρ,\mathbf{m})\approx\int_{\mathbb{R}^3}e_{\rm SSW}\big(ρ(\mathbf{x}),\mathbf{m}(\mathbf{x}),\nabla\varphi(\mathbf{x})\big)\,d \mathbf{x}. \] Here $\nabla\varphi$ measures the local rotation of $\mathbf{m}$ about the third axis. Wherever $m_1^2+m_2^2>0$, it equals $(m_1\nabla m_2-m_2\nabla m_1)/(m_1^2+m_2^2)$. The approximation thus depends only on the total density $ρ$, the magnetization and its first derivatives. When the spin-polarized density in the rotating frame and the local wavevector vary on the length scale $N^{1/3}$, with $N$ the mean number of particles, we obtain an error $O(N^{5/6})$ for an energy of order $N$. Setting the phase to zero gives the noncollinear local spin density approximation (LSDA) for arbitrary magnetization directions.

</details>


### [95] [Backpropagation as Dirac-bracket dynamics of dissipative training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34479)
*Nicolaus Linneu Holanda,Ivan Eugenio Cunha*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文利用 Bateman-Galley 自由度加倍方法，将梯度下降、heavy-ball 与 Nesterov 加速梯度统一写成哈密顿系统（Hamiltonian system），并证明在此框架下反向传播（backpropagation）等价于狄拉克演化（Dirac evolution），网络各层激活被视为相空间坐标、层方程作为第二类约束（second-class constraints）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以阻尼粒子在损失势中的运动来理解基于梯度的参数化模型训练，并借助可赋予耗散系统作用量原理的自由度加倍方法，为梯度类优化器建立统一的哈密顿表述，从而重新解释反向传播的几何与力学含义。

Method: 采用 Bateman-Galley 自由度加倍（doubling of degrees of freedom），将梯度流（gradient flow）、heavy-ball 方法与 Nesterov 加速梯度方法表述为哈密顿系统；把前馈网络（feed-forward network）的激活作为相空间坐标、层方程作为约束，利用狄拉克括号（Dirac bracket）与哈密顿方程重建训练动力学；并通过数值实验验证，且在不使用反向传播的情况下积分约束的狄拉克流来训练网络。

Result: 约束对任意架构均为第二类；含狄拉克括号的哈密顿方程在哈密顿量仅含网络输出代价时可重现使用精确损失梯度的训练。激活与参数动量的狄拉克括号即其雅可比（Jacobian），约束矩阵之逆同时包含自动微分（automatic differentiation）的前向与反向模式，反向传播所传误差是层约束的拉格朗日乘子（Lagrange multiplier），即运动链在代价载荷下的约束力。这些结果在完整加倍相空间而非仅物理极限下成立；网络部分的约束代数与优化器无关，Nesterov 的前视（look-ahead）增加一层依赖动量的约束并耦合加倍系统的两份拷贝，梯度流可通过分阶段构造狄拉克括号得到。数值上验证至机器精度，并成功在不含反向计算的情况下训练网络。

Conclusion: 反向传播可被理解为受约束哈密顿系统中的狄拉克演化，其误差传播对应层约束的约束力；该表述对多种优化器统一成立，并在数值上以机器精度得到确认，同时实现了无需反向传播的、基于狄拉克流积分的网络训练。

Abstract: Gradient-based training of a parametric model is the motion of a damped particle in the potential set by the loss. Using the Bateman-Galley doubling of the degrees of freedom, which gives dissipative systems an action principle, we write gradient flow, the heavy-ball method and Nesterov's accelerated gradient method as Hamiltonian systems. We show that, within this Hamiltonian description, backpropagation is Dirac evolution. The activations of a feed-forward network are treated as coordinates of phase space, and the layer equations as constraints. These constraints are second class for every architecture, and Hamilton's equations with the resulting Dirac bracket reproduce training with the exact gradient of the loss, although the Hamiltonian contains only the cost of the network output. The Dirac bracket of an activation with the momenta of the parameters is its Jacobian, the inverse of the constraint matrix contains both the forward and the reverse modes of automatic differentiation, and the errors propagated by backpropagation are the Lagrange multipliers of the layer constraints: the constraint forces of a kinematic chain under the load of the cost. These results hold in the full doubled phase space, not only in the physical limit. The network's part of the constraint algebra is the same for every optimizer, Nesterov's look-ahead adds one layer of momentum-dependent constraints that couple the two copies of the doubled system, and gradient flow is obtained by constructing the Dirac bracket in stages. We confirm these statements numerically to machine precision and train a network by integrating the Dirac flow of its constraints, with no backward pass in the computation.

</details>


### [96] [Distribution of the ratio of the two smallest eigenphases of the Jacobi unitary ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34553)
*Shinsuke M. Nishigaki*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文计算了Jacobi酉系综（Jacobi unitary ensemble）两个最小特征值的联合分布及其比值分布，采用基于Tracy-Widom常微分方程组的Janossy密度（Janossy densities）框架，发现比值分布相对其Bessel核（Bessel-kernel）普适极限的首个有限N修正为O(N^{-4})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画Jacobi酉系综最小特征值比值的有限尺寸行为，并探讨特征相位比值（eigenphase ratios）能否揭示具有粒子-空穴（particle-hole）或手征对称性（chiral symmetry）的量子混沌系统中的细微有限尺寸效应。

Method: 构建基于Tracy-Widom常微分方程组的Janossy密度计算框架，用以导出两个最小特征值的联合分布及比值分布；并将该比值分布与Sp(N)的硬边极限（hard-edge limit）作比较，作为探索性的算术应用，对照二次Dirichlet L函数（quadratic Dirichlet L-functions）最低两个零点的比值分布。

Result: 所研究情形下，比值分布相对普适Bessel核极限的首个有限N修正为O(N^{-4})，反映了Jacobi核中低阶修正的非平凡抵消（nontrivial cancellation）。数值数据显示，二次Dirichlet L函数情形的N_{eff}^{-1}项消失，仅剩O(N_{eff}^{-2})修正。

Conclusion: 比值型统计量对Jacobi核的低阶修正不敏感，因而特征相位比值可作为探测量子混沌系统有限尺寸效应的细微探针；L函数零点数据与Sp(N)硬边极限的对照支持N_{eff}^{-1}项消失的判断。

Abstract: We compute the joint distribution of the two smallest eigenvalues of the Jacobi unitary ensemble and the distribution of their ratio. We use a framework for calculating Janossy densities based on the Tracy-Widom system of ordinary differential equations. For the cases studied here, the leading finite-N correction to the ratio distribution, relative to its universal Bessel-kernel limit, is O(N^{-4}). This behaviour reflects a nontrivial cancellation of lower-order corrections present in the Jacobi kernel. Eigenphase ratios may thus reveal subtle finite-size effects in quantum-chaotic systems with particle-hole or chiral symmetry. As an exploratory arithmetic application, we compare the ratio distribution for the two lowest zeros of quadratic Dirichlet L-functions with the hard-edge limit for Sp(N). The data indicate that the N_{eff}^{-1} term vanishes, leaving an O(N_{eff}^{-2}) correction.

</details>

### [98] [The Zero Mode Constraint in the Dirac-Bergmann Analysis of Kadomtsev-Petviashvili Type Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34628)
*Ali Pazarci*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文用Dirac-Bergmann算法由奇异拉格朗日量导出KP方程的哈密顿形式，并与Faddeev-Jackiw方法、已有量子化结果相互印证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是系统建立Kadomtsev-Petviashvili（KP）方程的哈密顿表述，并厘清其约束结构，尤其是周期性或衰减场情形下导数算符零模带来的额外约束。

Method: 采用Dirac-Bergmann算法处理含辅助场的奇异拉格朗日量，识别非正则次级约束；并以Faddeev-Jackiw方法交叉验证。随后考察第一空间坐标周期场与衰减场两种情形，并对KP型方程推广及辅助场作用量的光滑解分类。

Result: 在重现KP方程的分支上所有约束均为第二类（second class），Dirac括号给出已知的哈密顿量与Poisson算子；周期情形下导数算符零模产生关于势均值的额外次级约束，使反导数替换为零均值对应物，所得哈密顿量与Reiman–Semenov-Tyan-Shanskii结果在归一化约定下一致。

Conclusion: 该约束结构对一般KP型方程成立，衰减场情形下同一条件亦是保证快速衰减性质的必要条件；结果还与KP方程已有的量子化方案（其作用量由势拉格朗日量提供）相衔接。

Abstract: We derive the Hamiltonian formulation of the Kadomtsev-Petviashvili (KP) equation from a singular Lagrangian by means of the Dirac-Bergmann algorithm. The Lagrangian contains an auxiliary field, which leads to an irregular secondary constraint. On the branch that reproduces the KP equation, all constraints are second class, and the Dirac brackets give the known Hamiltonian and Poisson operator. We confirm these results by the Faddeev-Jackiw method. Then, we consider fields periodic in the first spatial coordinate. In this case, the zero modes of the derivative operator lead to an additional secondary constraint on the mean of the potential. This constraint replaces the inverse derivative by its zero mean counterpart, and the resulting Hamiltonian is in accordance with the one of Reiman and Semenov-Tyan-Shanskii, up to normalization conventions. For decaying fields, the same condition is necessary for rapidly decreasing potentials to remain rapidly decreasing. We show that this constraint appears for a general class of KP-type equations. In addition, we classify the smooth solutions of the auxiliary-field action. We also relate our results to an existing quantization of the KP equation, for which the potential Lagrangian provides an action.

</details>

*Tomáš Dohnal,Anton Lochmann*

Result: 证明该薛定谔算子不存在特征值（eigenvalues）；在两侧为周期势时，还证明不存在在平行方向准周期（quasi-periodic）、在横向局域化（localized）的界面态。

Abstract: We consider the Schrödinger operator in $\mathbb{R}^d$ with a flat interface separating two half-spaces in which the real potential varies only in the directions parallel to the interface. This problem generally possesses no shift invariance. Nevertheless, using the method of multipliers, we prove the absence of eigenvalues. In addition, in the case of periodic potentials on each side of the interface the same method shows the absence of interface states which are quasi-periodic in the directions parallel to the interface and localized in the transverse direction.

</details>


### [100] [Discotic columnar phase in a three-dimensional monomer-tetramer model of Heilmann and Lieb with side-to-side attraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34796)
*Qidong He*

TL;DR: 该文严格证明 Heilmann–Lieb 模型 III（立方格点上四聚体液晶模型）在 μ+2a>0 区域存在盘状柱状序（discotic columnar order），从而完全解决了原猜想，并给出恰为十二个极值周期 Gibbs 测度（extremal periodic Gibbs measures）的刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Heilmann–Lieb（1979）提出该四聚体格点模型并猜想：在化学势 μ 与侧向吸引强度 a 满足 μ+2a>0 时低温出现液晶序。此前该猜想未被严格解决，且零温图像仅强制分层结构、层间可独立平移，序的存在并非先验显然。

Method: 在立方格点（cubic lattice）上研究由四聚体（tetramers/plaquettes）构成、具硬核排斥（hard-core repulsion）与共面同向四聚体间侧向吸引 a>0 的模型，采用严格数学论证（Gibbs 测度框架）分析低温相结构，重点考察热涨落（thermal fluctuations）对零温简并的解除机制。

Result: 证明在整个 μ+2a>0 区域存在盘状柱状序：恰有十二个极值周期 Gibbs 测度，每个均破坏旋转对称性（三种四聚体取向之一占优）并在恰好两个方向上破坏平移对称性（该取向下四种四聚体宇称之一占优），四聚体由此组成排列于方格上的三维柱。

Conclusion: 主要新意在于揭示热涨落如何解除零温分层简并，选择出所有四聚体共享同一宇称（common parity）的十二个基态；该结果完整解决了 Heilmann–Lieb 的液晶序猜想。

Abstract: We study Model III of Heilmann--Lieb (1979), a lattice model for liquid crystals consisting of tetramers (plaquettes) on the cubic lattice at chemical potential $μ$, interacting via a hard-core repulsion and a lateral attraction of strength $a>0$ between coplanar, aligned tetramers. The model was conjectured to exhibit liquid-crystalline order at low temperature provided that $μ+2a>0$. We establish discotic columnar order throughout this regime, fully resolving the conjecture. Specifically, we prove the existence of exactly twelve extremal and periodic Gibbs measures, each breaking rotational symmetry, with one of three tetramer orientations dominant, and translational symmetry in exactly two directions, with one of four tetramer parities dominant within that orientation. Thus, the tetramers form one-dimensional columns arranged on a square lattice. This arrangement is not a priori evident from the zero-temperature picture, where energy only enforces a layered structure: each layer is fully packed and crystalline, but different layers are free to shift independently within their planes. The main novelty here lies in showing how thermal fluctuations lift this degeneracy in favor of the twelve ground states in which all tetramers share a common parity.

</details>


### [101] [Slow information spreading in quasi-periodic quantum spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34827)
*Yingte Sun*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究准周期外磁场下弱相互作用量子自旋链中信息传播的抑制问题，借助有限阶正规形构造在链内部建立局域化屏障，并给出链两端可观测量的对易子定量上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注弱相互作用量子多体系统中信息传播能否被有效抑制，特别是准周期外场是否会形成阻碍传播的局域化机制，从而为量子信息传输限制提供理论依据。

Method: 采用有限阶正规形构造（finite-order normal-form construction），在量子自旋链（quantum spin chain）内部构造一个局域化屏障（localized barrier），并据此分析对易子（commutator）的行为。

Result: 在所构造的屏障作用下，信息传播受到抑制；作者导出了位于链两端、支撑集分离的可观测量之间对易子的定量上界（quantitative bound）。

Conclusion: 准周期外磁场结合弱相互作用可在链内部形成阻止信息传播的局域化屏障，且该效应用对易子的显式界定量刻画，为信息传播受限提供可验证的数学表述。

Abstract: We investigate the suppression of information propagation in a weakly interacting quantum spin chain subject to a quasi-periodic external magnetic field. Using a finite-order normal-form construction, we establish a localized barrier in the interior of the chain that inhibits information propagation and derive a quantitative bound on the commutator of observables supported at opposite ends of the chain.

</details>


### [102] [Sharp constant in 2D critical fermionic Hardy inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34845)
*Marvin R. Schulz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明二维费米子多体 Hardy 不等式最优常数 κ_N^(2) 满足 (log N)κ_N^(2) → 4（N→∞），从而解决了 Frank–Hoffmann-Ostenhof–Laptev–Solovej 的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二维费米子多体 Hardy 不等式最优常数在粒子数 N 很大时的渐近行为，并验证文献中关于极限值为 4 的猜想。

Method: 将 Coulomb 核（Coulomb kernel）的二进 Fefferman–de la Llave 表示与对低、高粒子占据数（particle occupation）平方的分别估计相结合；低占据情形由迹不等式（trace inequality）控制，高占据情形通过 Neumann 特征值（Neumann eigenvalues）之和得到尖锐界。

Result: 证明 lim_{N→∞}(log N)κ_N^(2)=4，即给出了该渐近常数 4 的严格上、下界估计。

Conclusion: Frank–Hoffmann-Ostenhof–Laptev–Solovej 的猜想成立；该结论基于反称波函数（antisymmetric wave functions）上的二次型不等式框架。

Abstract: We study the large-$N$ behavior of the optimal constant $κ_N$ in the two-dimensional fermionic many-particle Hardy inequality \[ \sum_{i=1}^N -Δ_i \geq κ_N^{(2)}\sum_{1\leq i<j\leq N}\frac{1}{|x_i-x_j|^2}, \] understood as a quadratic-form inequality on antisymmetric wave functions. We prove that \[ \lim_{N\to\infty}(\log N)κ_N^{(2)}=4, \] thereby proving a conjecture of Frank, Hoffmann-Ostenhof, Laptev, and Solovej. The proof combines a dyadic Fefferman-de la Llave representation of the Coulomb kernel with separate estimates for squares of low and high particle occupation. A trace inequality controls the low-occupation regime, while sums of Neumann eigenvalues yield the sharp bound in the high-occupation regime.

</details>

### [103] [Continuous Universal Fake Factors for jet-to-tau background estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33221)
*Lei Guo,Antonio De Maria,Chengxin Liao,Zihang Liu,Wenyi Jin,Jiarong Yuan,Yuchen Cai,Da Xu,Xuai Zhuang,Yanjun Tu,Yang Liu*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: CUFF 方法将四个参考区的假因子（fake factor）加权组合，用于从被误判为强子 tau 的喷注中估计本底；在 ATLAS 公开数据混合样本中预测偏差为 +0.7% 至 +4.3%，且可在连续变化的 tau 鉴别阈值下无需重训练地应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是构建一种从被误识别为强子 tau 候选的喷注（jets misidentified as hadronic tau candidates）中估计本底的方法，并使其能适用于连续变化的 tau 鉴别阈值，而无需针对每个阈值重新训练。

Method: CUFF 对每个未通过 Loose 鉴别（fail-Loose）的目标 tau 候选，组合四个参考区的假因子，其权重依赖候选特征及扣除真实 tau 与电子/缪子误判贡献后的目标 fail-Loose 分布；方法在匹配到 20<p_T^jet≤60 GeV 喷注的 ATLAS 2015–2016 公开数据（Open Data）上检验。

Result: 在八个已知比例的参考样本混合中，Medium 鉴别选择下的预测总产额与扣除后数据的差异为 +0.7% 至 +4.3%；两个验证区（validation region）的差异为 −21.85% 和 −45.84%，统计不确定度（含训练涨落）相对数据参考为 1.58% 和 6.03%。CUFF 在分布形状描述上优于适配公开数据的 UFF 方法，且在九个鉴别阈值下产额差异平滑变化（−26.4% 至 −19.7%、−50.2% 至 −40.6%）。

Conclusion: 结果支持该加权组合方案，并表明 CUFF 可在连续可变的 tau 鉴别阈值下一致应用而无需重训练；验证区的较大偏差仍落在由模拟比较得到的宽经验不确定度范围内。

Abstract: We study Continuous Universal Fake Factors (CUFF), a method for estimating backgrounds from jets misidentified as hadronic tau candidates. CUFF combines four reference-region fake factors for each target tau candidate failing Loose identification. Their weights depend on candidate features and target fail-Loose distributions after subtracting simulated contributions from genuine taus and electrons or muons misidentified as tau candidates. We use ATLAS 2015-2016 Open Data with tau candidates matched to jets with $20<p_T^{\rm jet}\leq60$ GeV. In eight mixtures of reference samples with known proportions, predicted total yields for the Medium identification selection differ from data after subtraction by $+0.7\%$ to $+4.3\%$, supporting the combination. In two validation regions, the corresponding differences are $-21.85\%$ and $-45.84\%$, with statistical uncertainties, including training fluctuations, of $1.58\%$ and $6.03\%$ relative to the data references. These discrepancies lie within broad empirical uncertainty ranges from simulation comparisons between regions, widened by limited simulation statistics. For Loose identification, CUFF and the Universal Fake Factor (UFF) method adapted to public data give similar inclusive yields in one validation region, while CUFF agrees more closely with the data reference in the other. CUFF also describes the shapes of several kinematic distributions more accurately than adapted UFF, including that of an observable not used as a model input. Across nine identification thresholds, relative yield differences vary gradually from $-26.4\%$ to $-19.7\%$ and $-50.2\%$ to $-40.6\%$ in the two regions, respectively, demonstrating consistent application of CUFF at continuously variable tau-identification thresholds without retraining.

</details>


### [104] [CLEAR: A Contrastive-Masked Foundation Model for BESIII Event Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33223)
*Jingde Chen,Zijie Shang,Tong Liu,Yao Zhang,Beijiang Liu,Changzheng Yuan,Xiongfei Wang,Ke Li*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 该文提出面向BESIII物理分析的基础模型CLEAR（基于事件注意力的对比表示学习），融合掩码建模与对比学习，用于衰变道分类与异常检测，并分析表示空间几何性质与任务性能的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理实验中衰变道日益多样复杂，传统分析流程需要耗时耗力地逐道进行选择和优化，因此有必要发展能够学习跨道可迁移统一表示的基础模型。

Method: CLEAR结合掩码建模（masked modeling）与对比学习（contrastive learning）：掩码建模通过粒子间关联预测被掩码粒子特征训练编码器；对比学习提升表示空间的局部聚类性与线性可分性。

Result: 在排他末态识别中，匹配纯度下CLEAR的信号效率高于传统切割法（cut-based methods）；衰变道分类偏好对齐（alignment）与均匀性（uniformity）的平衡，而异常检测更受益于较高均匀性权重。

Conclusion: 对比学习塑造了表示空间的局部邻域几何，该几何与分类和异常检测性能相关；CLEAR框架有望扩展至其他高能物理实验。

Abstract: The increasing diversity and complexity of decay channels at high energy physics experiments pose a challenge to analysis workflows, which require time-consuming, labor-intensive channel-specific selections and optimization. This motivates the development of foundation models capable of learning a unified representation transferable across different channels. In this paper, we introduce CLEAR (Contrastive Learning for Event Attention-based Representation), a foundation model designed for physics analysis at BESIII that combines masked modeling and contrastive learning to support decay channel classification and anomaly detection. Masked modeling trains the encoder to predict masked particle features by learning inter-particle correlations, while contrastive learning improves the local clusterability and linear separability of the representation space for discrimination. By further examining the balance between alignment and uniformity, we investigate how contrastive learning shapes the local neighborhood geometry of the representation space and how this geometry relates to classification and anomaly detection performance. Decay channel classification favors a balance between alignment and uniformity, whereas anomaly detection benefits from a greater relative weight on uniformity. For exclusive final-state identification, CLEAR achieves higher signal efficiency than conventional cut-based methods at matched purity and provides a framework that can potentially be extended to other high-energy physics experiments.

</details>

# math.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Singular rational S-functions of second-order ODEs: structure, degree bounds and a Kovacic-type search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33518)
*L. G. S. Duarte,L. A. C. P. da Mota*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该文针对有理二阶常微分方程的首积分搜索，指出标准S-函数拟设（ansatz）会遗漏在分母N零点之外的极点，从而系统刻画奇异S-解的结构并给出可验证的搜索算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S-函数法（S-function method）把首积分（first integral）的寻找化归为Riccati型方程X(S)=S²+φ_z S−φ_y的有理解，但传统拟设S=P/N^k（N=den(φ)）会漏掉所有在N=0之外有极点的S；且对无超指数解（hyperexponential solutions）的线性方程作点变换（point transform）时，此类奇异解不可避免，故需刻画其结构。

Method: 先显式描述线性方程点变换下所有准正则（quasi-regular）S，并证明一般变换下不存在此类S；继而分析奇异解的极结构：N之外极点为单极点、留数为−1且非Darboux曲面，沿N的非特征因子主极系数为自由的Cauchy数据，唯一未知分母为无平方因子多项式D并满足线性方程；再用无穷远处的牛顿多边形（Newton-polygon）分析确定多项式部分至有界权重的修正项，并用线性微分系统的有理解确定D对x的依赖。

Result: 当余量为零且顶层Riccati方程无代数解时精确确定D的加权次数，否则（在修正项消失的条件下）由顶层域的留数给出界；据此实现两次的可验证搜索程序（certified search procedure），找到Airy与Bessel方程九个非Liouvillian拉回（pull-back）的奇异S；在某一情形下构成判定程序，例如证明y''=xy+1无有理S。

Conclusion: 该工作补全了S-函数法对奇异解的遗漏，给出奇异S-解的完整结构定理与可验证（部分为判定性）的算法，并通过Airy/Bessel拉回实例验证其有效性。

Abstract: For a rational second-order ODE $y''=phi(x,y,y')$, the $S$-function method reduces the search for a first integral to finding a rational solution $S$ of the Riccati-type equation $X(S)=S^2+phi_z S-phi_y$ along the total-derivative field $X$. The standard ansatz $S=P/N^k$, with $N=den(phi)$, misses every $S$ with poles outside $N=0$. Such singular solutions cannot be avoided: for point transforms of linear equations without hyperexponential solutions we describe every quasi-regular $S$ explicitly, and for a generic transformation none exists. We then determine the structure of singular solutions. Outside $N$ every pole is simple, with residue $-1$, and is not a Darboux surface; along non-characteristic factors of $N$ the leading polar coefficient is free Cauchy data; and the only unknown denominator is a squarefree polynomial $D$, which enters a linear equation. A Newton-polygon analysis at infinity gives the polynomial part up to a correction of bounded weight. The weighted degree of $D$ is determined exactly when the excess is zero and the top Riccati equation has no algebraic solution, and is bounded by residues of the top field otherwise, conditionally on the vanishing of that correction. The dependence of $D$ on $x$ is obtained, without a degree bound, from the rational solutions of a linear differential system. These results give a certified search procedure, implemented twice, which finds the singular $S$ of nine non-Liouvillian pull-backs of the Airy and Bessel equations. In one regime it is a decision procedure; for instance, it proves that $y''=xy+1$ has no rational $S$.

</details>


### [106] [Graph Parameterization for Invariant Manifolds Attached to Quasi-Periodic Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34437)
*Miquel Barcelona,Joan Gimeno,Marc Jorba-Cuscó*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高阶 Fourier-Taylor 图参数化方法（Fourier-Taylor graph-parameterization method），用于计算离散动力系统（尤其是 Poincaré 映射）在可约准周期不变环面（reducible quasi-periodic invariant tori）处的局部不变流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算附着于准周期不变环面的局部不变流形（local invariant manifolds）并将其低维表示映回原相空间，对分析离散动力系统邻近结构具有实际需求，需要兼顾稳定、不稳定与中心谱选择及数值验证。

Method: 在环面及其 Floquet 约化（Floquet reduction）已知的前提下，将流形表示为选定谱子空间上的图（graph over a spectral subspace），借助 jet 传输（jet transport）与环面上的上同调方程（cohomological equations）递推系数；中心流形情形下同时确定非线性约化动力学。针对实系统中复共轭 Floquet 乘子，基于 Hermitian 对称性与 Krawtchouk 变换（Krawtchouk transformations）导出等价的实值表述，并加入稳定方向逆动力学、多重打靶（multiple shooting）、坐标缩放与后验验证（a posteriori validation）测试。

Result: 准周期受迫摆（quasi-periodically forced pendulum）、地月系统振荡太阳帆（oscillating solar sail）及简化太阳系模型三个数值实验验证了构造及其计算性质；在太阳帆基准中，实值表述相比复数实现将计算时间缩短约 2.2 倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的高阶 Fourier-Taylor 图参数化框架可统一处理稳定、不稳定与中心不变流形，实值表述在保留复谱坐标上同调求解优势的同时显著提升效率。

Abstract: We develop a high-order Fourier-Taylor graph-parameterization method for computing local invariant manifolds attached to reducible quasi-periodic invariant tori of discrete dynamical systems, with particular emphasis on Poincaré maps. Assuming that the invariant torus and its Floquet reduction are known, we represent the manifold as a graph over a selected spectral subspace and compute its coefficients recursively through jet transport and cohomological equations on the torus. The formulation accommodates stable, unstable, and center spectral selections. For center manifolds, it simultaneously determines the nonlinear reduced dynamics, providing a low-dimensional representation from which nearby invariant objects can be lifted to the original phase space.
  For real systems with complex-conjugate Floquet multipliers, we derive an equivalent real formulation based on Hermitian symmetry and Krawtchouk transformations. This allows real-valued jet transport while retaining the advantages of complex spectral coordinates in the cohomological solver. The implementation also incorporates inverse dynamics for stable directions, multiple shooting, coordinate scaling, and a posteriori validation tests.
  Numerical experiments with a quasi-periodically forced pendulum, an oscillating solar sail in the Earth-Moon system, and a simplified solar system model illustrate the construction and its computational properties. In the solar-sail benchmark, the real formulation reduces computation time by approximately a factor of 2.2 compared with the complex implementation.

</details>


### [107] [Quantifying combined rate- and noise-induced transitions in a multiscale system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33449)
*Ryan Deeley,Peter Ashwin,Ulrike Feudel*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 以FitzHugh-Nagumo模型为例，研究速率相关的参数强迫与随机强迫共同诱发激发的机制，并用自适应多级分裂算法量化不同强迫情形下的跃迁概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多尺度动力系统中的激发表现为对静息态的持续但非永久的偏离；现有工作较少关注同时结合速率相关参数强迫与随机强迫时激发如何被诱发，以及其发生概率如何随噪声强度变化。

Method: 在无噪声情形下，借助时间紧化（compactification of time）构造自治系统框架，定义相对于亚稳激发态慢流形（slow manifolds）的拟阈值（quasithreshold），并利用奇异摄动理论（singular perturbation theory）分析canard轨迹如何塑造其几何结构；再以自适应多级分裂算法（adaptive multilevel splitting）数值估计弱随机强迫下样本路径穿越拟阈值的概率。

Result: 分别估计了（i）纯噪声诱发与（ii）速率-噪声共同诱发激发的概率，以及弱随机强迫阻止确定性速率诱发激发的概率；结果表明各跃迁情形的概率随噪声强度呈指数标度，符合样本路径大偏差原理（large deviation principles）。

Conclusion: 确定性子临界的参数强迫与随机强迫相结合，比单独随机强迫以指数更高概率诱发激发，其原因在于后拉吸引子（pullback attractor）与拟阈值相互趋近。

Abstract: Excitations in multiscale dynamical systems produce persistent but impermanent excursions away from a quiescent state. A novel mechanism for inducing excitations, through a combination of rate-dependent parametric and stochastic forcing, is discussed using the FitzHugh-Nagumo model as an example. In the absence of noise, a quasithreshold separating the state space between supercritical and subcritical behaviour is defined with respect to slow manifolds associated with the metastable excited state. This is formulated in an autonomous system framework, which can be obtained via a compactification of time. Techniques from singular perturbation theory elucidate how canard trajectories shape the geometric structure of the quasithreshold as the system's pullback attractor moves through it. The probability with which weakly stochastically driven sample paths travel through the quasithreshold and undertake (i) solely noise-induced and (ii) combined rate- and noise-induced excitations is estimated numerically for each of the corresponding forcing scenarios, using the adaptive multilevel splitting algorithm. The probability of weak stochastic forcing preventing a rate-induced excitation, which occurs deterministically, is likewise estimated. This quantifies the different rates at which the probability of each transition scenario scales exponentially with the noise strength, in accordance with sample path large deviation principles. It is revealed that a combination of (deterministically subcritical) parametric and stochastic forcing is exponentially more likely to induce excitations than stochastic forcing alone, which is attributed to the pullback attractor and quasithreshold converging towards one another.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Existence of single-resonance parametric bound states in the continuum: System of Schrödinger equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33784)
*Xiuchen Yu,Ya Yan Lu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文以两个一维薛定谔方程（one-dimensional Schrödinger equations）构成的简单系统为模型，演示单共振参数型连续域束缚态（single-resonance parametric BICs, srp-BICs）的形成，并建立其严格的存在性理论（rigorous existence theory）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续域束缚态（bound state in the continuum, BIC）是本征频率嵌入散射态辐射连续谱中的线性波系统本征模，在BIC频率处给定入射波的散射问题失去唯一性。对称保护型（symmetry-protected）、Friedrich-Wintgen型与Fabry-Perot型BIC已有存在性理论，而srp-BICs虽有重要性却缺乏严格的存在性论证。

Method: 采用由两个一维薛定谔方程（1D Schrödinger equations）组成的简单系统，通过调节实参数（real parameter）使单个共振态（single resonant state）演化为BIC，并据此建立严格的存在性理论证明。

Result: 在该双方程模型中成功演示了srp-BICs的形成，并给出了其存在性的严格理论证明。

Conclusion: 研究填补了srp-BICs严格存在性理论的空白，为这一重要BIC类别的理论分析提供了可验证的数学框架；摘要中未给出具体数值算例或实验验证。

Abstract: A bound state in the continuum (BIC) is an eigenmode of a linear wave system with its eigen-frequency embedded in the radiation continuum of scattering states. At the frequency of a BIC, scattering problems with given incident waves from infinity lose uniqueness. Due to their close connection with strong resonance effects, BICs have found many useful applications in classical and quantum wave systems. Existence theories of BICs have been established for some cases involving the so-called symmetry-protected BICs, Friedrich-Wintgen BICs, and Fabry-Perot BICs. The single-resonance parametric BICs (srp-BICs) are another important class of BICs. Such a BIC emerges from a single resonant state through tuning a real parameter. Using a simple system of two one-dimensional Schrödinger equations, we demonstrate the formation of srp-BICs and establish a rigorous existence theory.

</details>


### [109] [Transport Layer-Driven Optimization of Color and Efficiency in TADF OLEDs for Large-Area Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33933)
*Manish Kumar,Jawid Nikan,Luiz Pereira*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 通过调控电子/空穴传输层厚度，在简化结构的热活化延迟荧光（TADF）橙红光OLED中实现20.86%的最大外量子效率与32000 cd m^-2峰值亮度，并借助漂移-扩散与光学模拟揭示载流子平衡与复合区的移动机制，进而实现同一发光材料下绿至红的颜色调控及约20 cm^2大面积器件（η_EQE约17%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机发光二极管（OLED）在大面积照明应用中需同时兼顾结构简化和高效率，而多层器件中电荷传输层厚度对发光层内复合区的调控作用尚未被系统阐明，因而需要研究其对器件性能的影响。

Method: 以TXO-TPA为TADF发光体（10 wt%掺杂于CBP主体），实验与计算相结合，考察电子传输层与空穴传输层厚度变化对橙红光TADF OLED性能的影响，并采用漂移-扩散（drift-diffusion）与光学模拟联合分析载流子平衡及复合区位置。

Result: 器件获得20.86%的最大外量子效率（external quantum efficiency, η_EQE）与32,800 cd m^-2的峰值亮度；模拟表明传输层厚度变化可调节电荷载流子平衡并移动发光层内的复合区，在不更换发光材料的情况下实现绿至红的颜色调控；大面积器件（约20 cm^2）实现约17%的η_EQE。

Conclusion: 传输层工程（transport layer engineering）对实现高效、颜色可控且结构简化的OLED至关重要，且该策略具备面向通用照明应用的可扩展性与实用性。

Abstract: Balancing structural simplicity with high efficiency is crucial for practical applications of organic light-emitting diodes (OLEDs), especially in large-area lighting devices. Optimizing the charge-transport layers in multilayer OLEDs controls the recombination zone within the emissive layer, thereby enhancing the device's performance. Herein, we experimentally and computationally investigate how the thicknesses of the electron and hole transport layers affect the performance of orange-red thermally activated delayed fluorescence (TADF) OLEDs. Using a TADF emitter, 2-[4-(diphenylamino)phenyl]-10,10-dioxide-9H-thioxanthen-9-one (TXO-TPA), at 10 wt\% concentration in 4,4-Bis(N-carbazolyl)-1,1-biphenyl (CBP) host, we achieve a maximum external quantum efficiency ($η_{EQE}$) of 20.86\% and a peak luminance of 32,800 cd m$^{-2}$. Using a combination of drift-diffusion and optical simulations, we reveal how variations in hole- and electron-transport-layer thicknesses modulate the charge-carrier balance and shift the recombination zone within the emissive layer. This tuning not only optimizes exciton formation but also enables color tuning from green to red without altering the emissive material. Our findings highlight the critical role of transport layer engineering in achieving efficient, color-controlled OLEDs with simplified architectures. Furthermore, we successfully demonstrate a large-area OLED device (~20 cm$^{2}$), achieving an impressive $η_{EQE}$ of 17\%, underscoring the scalability and practicality of our approach for general lighting applications.

</details>


### [110] [Arbitrarily Weak Losses Destroy Fractal Bandstructure in Temporal Fibonacci Quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34552)
*Calvin M. Hooper*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 弱折射率对比的无损光子晶体（lossless photonic crystals）仅能阻挡有限频率，但按非周期斐波那契准晶（Fibonacci quasicrystal）排列时可阻挡除极稀少分形分布频率外的全部频率；类似分析预期时间上的弱准周期折射率变化可实现无限宽带增益。本文证明任何微小实验损耗（experimental losses）会立即破坏该效应，并为空间问题中拓扑相关的局域化长度（localisation lengths）给出光滑性与逼近定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱折射率对比体系通常只能获得有限频带的阻断或增益；已有分析表明斐波那契准晶排列与准周期时间调制（quasiperiodic temporal modulation）可能带来近乎无限宽带（infinitely broadband）的强效应，本文旨在检验这些理想化预言在实际损耗下的稳健性。

Method: 基于理论分析，考察弱准周期折射率变化（空间与时间两类情形）下的频率阻挡与增益行为，并引入微小实验损耗（experimental losses）进行扰动分析；同时建立针对局域化长度（localisation lengths）的光滑性（smoothness）与逼近（approximation）定理。

Result: 理想无损条件下，斐波那契准晶可阻挡除极稀少、呈分形分布（fractally-distributed）频率之外的全部频率；但任意小的实验损耗都会立即破坏这一效应。所发展的方法同时给出与空间问题中拓扑（topology）相关的局域化长度的光滑性与逼近定理。

Conclusion: 准周期结构所预言的无限宽带阻断与增益效应在存在微小损耗时不具稳健性（not robust），实际实现面临根本限制；关于局域化长度的光滑性与逼近定理则为空间拓扑相关分析提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Lossless photonic crystals with weak refractive index contrasts block limited frequencies. However, the same materials arranged in a non-periodic Fibonacci quasicrystal block all but infinitely rare fractally-distributed frequencies. Similar analysis shows that weak quasiperiodic refractive index variation in time should achieve infinitely broadband gain.
  We demonstrate that any small experimental losses immediately destroy this effect. Our methods also provide smoothness and approximation theorems for the localisation lengths central to topology in spatial problems.

</details>


<div id='physics.plasm-ph'></div>

# physics.plasm-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Uphill Diffusion in Yukawa Plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34459)
*Matt E. Caplan,Evan B. Bauer,Mohammed Bettioua*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究多组分流体混合物中无需外力即可出现的“上坡扩散（uphill diffusion）”，并将其用于检验作者此前建立的半解析扩散模型与广义Darken关系，在强耦合等离子体（strongly coupled plasmas）背景下与三组分分子动力学模拟比较，结果吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上坡扩散在化学与冶金领域已有认识，但据作者所知尚未在中子星与白矮星内部的强耦合等离子体中被研究；该现象提供了在非平衡条件下检验扩散模型的机会。

Method: 作者推导了上坡扩散的解析理论（analytic theory），并与三组分分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟结果进行对比；同时检验用于预测扩散系数的广义Darken关系（generalized Darken relation）。

Result: 解析理论与三组分分子动力学模拟结果高度一致（very good agreement）。

Conclusion: 上坡扩散为将扩散模型推广至任意多组分混合物提供了新的非平衡检验途径，验证了该半解析扩散模型在非平衡情形下的适用性。

Abstract: In a fluid mixture of many species an individual species can diffuse toward higher concentration without any external driving force because its flux is coupled to the gradients of other components. Such `uphill diffusion' is well known in the chemical and metallurgy literature but to our knowledge has not been studied for strongly coupled plasmas in neutron star and white dwarf interiors. We have previously developed a semi-analytic diffusion model that accurately predicts diffusion coefficients of individual species in a mixture in equilibrium. Uphill diffusion allows us to test this model out of equilibrium, and also test a generalized Darken relation for predicting diffusivities. We derive an analytic theory and compare to three-component molecular dynamics simulations and find very good agreement. This provides a novel out-of-equilibrium test of the generalization of our diffusion model to arbitrary multi-component mixtures.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Lower Bound on the Lightest Neutrino Mass with Resonant Neutrino Self-Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34213)
*Shao-Feng Ge,Joao Paulo Pinheiro,Shaoyang Qin,Ruitao Sun,Oleg Titov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出利用超高能天体物理中微子与低能宇宙中微子背景的共振 $s$-通道散射，对最轻中微子质量给出下限，并以 $\mathcal{O}(1)$ MeV 轻标量媒介子诱导的中微子自相互作用为具体实现，最后用 IceCube HESE 7.5 年数据进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中微子质量测量（如振荡实验）仅能确定质量平方差，最轻中微子的绝对质量仍未知，因此需要新的独立手段来约束其下限。

Method: 分析 $s$-通道共振的运动学（kinematics）与截面（cross section）特征，并从第一性原理推导含双再生项（double regeneration terms）的双输运方程（double transport equations），以描述中微子与反中微子的耦合演化；随后将模拟的事件能谱与 IceCube HESE 7.5 年观测数据对比。

Result: 模拟事件能谱与 IceCube HESE 7.5 年数据的比较给出了最轻中微子质量的明确下限（摘要未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: 共振 $s$-通道散射为探测最轻中微子质量提供了一种可行途径，中微子自相互作用情景下该方案可与现有天文中微子观测数据结合产生有效约束。

Abstract: We show the possibility of using the resonant $s$-channel scattering of ultra high energy astrophysical neutrinos with the low energy cosmic neutrino background to put a lower bound on the lightest neutrino mass. This can be realized and illustrated by neutrino self-interactions with a light scalar mediator of $\mathcal O(1)$ MeV mass. After analyzing the kinematics and cross section features of the $s$-channel resonance, we derive from first principles the double transport equations with double regeneration terms for the coupled evolution of neutrinos and anti-neutrinos. The simulated event spectrum is then compared with the HESE 7.5 year data observed at IceCube to give a clear lower bound on the lightest neutrino mass.

</details>


### [113] [Probing the CKM Origin of CP Violation with Paramagnetic EDMs and Rare Kaon Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34856)
*Giancarlo D'Ambrosio,Luca Di Luzio*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 顺磁电偶极矩（EDM）虽由长程双光子交换主导电子顶点，但双光子顶点保持CP守恒，须与核子耦合中CP破坏的企鹅（penguin）分量配对，其相对大小仅3×10⁻⁴，故电磁修正停留在百分之几水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型中顺磁EDM主要由K介子交换主导，但主导K_L→e⁺e⁻的长程双光子交换给出的电子顶点比短程顶点大约35倍，可能掩盖K介子交换这一起源，需检验其是否真正干扰EDM对CP破坏的探测。

Method: 通过分析双光子顶点的CP性质及其与核子弱耦合中CP破坏分量的配对关系，估算相对贡献与电磁修正幅度，并比较EDM与K_S→μ⁺μ⁻的CP破坏S波、K_L→π⁰νν̄所确定的CKM参数。

Result: 双光子顶点保持CP守恒，必须与核子耦合中CP破坏的企鹅分量配对，其相对大小为3×10⁻⁴，电磁修正处于百分之几量级；因此顺磁EDM实际测量的是Im(V_ts^* V_td)。

Conclusion: 顺磁EDM与K_S→μ⁺μ⁻的CP破坏S波、K_L→π⁰νν̄共同确定K介子CKM平面上同一虚坐标，无需B衰变输入；轻子特异新物理或绕过K介子交换的CP破坏会使这些测定分离。检验其共同CKM起源需要分子EDM搜寻、LHCb/NA62/KOTO的稀有K介子实验与格点（lattice）弱核子矩阵元计算的协同推进。

Abstract: In the Standard Model, paramagnetic electric dipole moments (EDMs) are dominated by kaon exchange, which pairs the CP-violating short-distance transition $s\to d\,e^+e^-$ with a CP-conserving weak kaon-nucleon coupling. Long-distance two-photon exchange, which dominates $K_L\to e^+e^-$, gives an electron vertex about thirty-five times larger than the short-distance one and could obscure this origin. We show that it does not: the two-photon vertex conserves CP and must be paired with the CP-violating penguin component of the nucleon coupling, whose relative size is $3\times10^{-4}$, so that electromagnetic corrections stay at the few-percent level. Paramagnetic EDMs therefore measure $\mathrm{Im}(V_{ts}^*V_{td})$ and, together with the CP-violating $S$ wave of $K_S\toμ^+μ^-$ and $K_L\toπ^0ν\barν$, determine the same imaginary coordinate of a kaon CKM plane with electrons, muons, and neutrinos, without $B$-decay input. Lepton-specific new physics, or CP violation that bypasses kaon exchange, would separate these determinations. Testing their common CKM origin calls for coordinated progress in molecular EDM searches, rare-kaon experiments at LHCb, NA62, and KOTO, and lattice determinations of weak nucleon matrix elements.

</details>


### [114] [The Look-Elsewhere Effect in Searches for New Physics: from Classical Methods to Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34888)
*Gaia Grosso,Sergio Rodriguez-Benitez,Louis Lyons*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了在对撞机新物理搜寻中出现的“别处效应（look-elsewhere effect, LEE）”：扫描大量参数位置会抬高最大超出的显著性，需将局部 p 值（local p-value）校正为全局 p 值（global p-value）。文章从统计基础出发，讨论连续扫描与多重离散检验的校正方法，并重点分析机器学习（machine learning, ML）方法带来的隐式后选择偏倚（post-selection bias）与选择性推断（selective inference）问题，最后给出开放问题与报告发现显著性的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当效应在参数空间中的位置未被预先指定时，扫描多个位置会夸大最大超出的显著性，因此必须进行全局校正；而“别处”的定义仍存歧义（范围可从单一分布到整个实验计划），且经典解析捷径无法直接推广到基于 ML 的搜寻，后者存在依赖模型分辨率与表达能力的隐式后选择偏倚，这些未决问题构成本综述的动机。

Method: 采用综述形式：以 Davies 的工作及 Gross 与 Vitells 在粒子物理中的实现为框架基础，梳理连续扫描与多重离散检验的 LEE 校正，并以简单数值示例加以说明；同时讨论 ML 与 LEE 的相互作用，并将其与更广泛的选择性推断文献相联系。

Result: 文章归纳了连续扫描和多重离散检验两类情形下的 LEE 校正方法及数值示例，指出经典解析捷径在 ML 检验统计量下不再适用，并将 ML 引发的隐式后选择偏倚与选择性推断文献建立关联。

Conclusion: 提出仍待解决的开放问题，包括复杂模型经验校准（empirical calibration）的计算成本、面向 ML 检验统计量的解析捷径开发，并就经典与基于 ML 的粒子物理搜寻中如何报告发现显著性给出建议。

Abstract: The look-elsewhere effect (LEE) arises when the location of an effect in parameter space is not specified in advance: scanning many locations inflates the significance of the largest excess, and the local $p$-value must be corrected into a global one. A well-established framework, rooted in the work of Davies and operationalised for particle physics by Gross and Vitells, provides this correction for scans over continuous parameters. Some aspects, however, remain unsettled. The definition of "elsewhere" is ambiguous and ranges from a single distribution to an entire experimental programme. Moreover, the analytical shortcuts of the classical framework do not carry over to searches based on machine learning (ML), which are affected by an implicit post-selection bias that depends on the model's resolution and expressiveness. This review covers the LEE in searches for new physics at colliders, from its statistical foundations to these modern challenges. We discuss the corrections for continuous scans and for multiple discrete tests, illustrated with simple numerical examples, and the interaction between ML and the LEE, which we connect to the broader literature on selective inference. We highlight open problems, including the computational cost of empirical calibration for complex models and the development of analytical shortcuts for ML test statistics, and offer recommendations for reporting discovery significance in both classical and ML-based searches in particle physics.

</details>


### [115] [Divergence-type hydrodynamics of radial oscillations in viscous neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33537)
*J. Peralta-Ramos*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在牛顿极限下构建黏性中子星模型，并将线性流体力学与二阶（二次型）耗散流体理论进行对比，以考察非线性效应对径向扰动演化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性流体力学在中子星高体黏性条件下可能不足以描述长期存在的非流体力学模式（non-hydrodynamic modes），故需评估非线性效应的作用。

Method: 采用简化的牛顿极限黏性中子星模型，耦合仅含体黏性（bulk viscosity）的二次型散度型耗散流体理论（quadratic dissipative fluid theory of divergence type），计算径向扰动演化并与线性流体力学结果比较。

Result: 当体黏性 ζ_B ≳ 5×10^31 g/cm/s 时，线性理论出现增长振荡，而二次理论仅在更高 ζ_B 下才出现；二次理论表现出更强的振荡阻尼、更长的非流体力学模式衰减时间以及更“硬”的径向扰动演化（形变更小）。

Conclusion: 非线性效应对刻画高体黏性下长寿命非流体力学模式的动力学可能具有重要性。

Abstract: Based on a simplified model of a viscous neutron star in the Newtonian limit coupled to a quadratic dissipative fluid theory of divergence type with bulk viscosity only, we explore the impact of nonlinear effects on the evolution of radial perturbations, and compare it with the results of linear hydrodynamics. For high values of the bulk viscosity $ζ_B \gtrsim 5\times 10^{31}$ g/cm/s, the linear theory develops growing oscillations, which do appear in the quadratic theory but at much higher values of $ζ_B$. The quadratic theory shows stronger damping of oscillations, longer decay rates of non-hydrodynamic modes and also a ``stiffer'' evolution of radial perturbations that results in less deformation. These results suggest that nonlinear effects may be relevant to capture the dynamics of the long-lived non-hydrodynamic modes present at high $ζ_B$.

</details>


### [116] [Ultralight Dark Matter Spectrum with Gravitational Focusing and Doppler Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34464)
*Shao-Feng Ge,Yong Tang,Yue-Hui Yao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究太阳引力聚焦（gravitational focusing）与探测器运动引起的多普勒效应（Doppler effect）如何改变超轻暗物质（ultralight dark matter）实验信号谱，并评估其对暗物质参数推断的影响，指出信号建模需纳入局部环境效应以避免参数估计偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻暗物质与标准模型（Standard Model）粒子的耦合可产生实验信号，其谱形由暗物质的相空间分布（phase-space distribution）决定，编码了产生机制与局部宇宙历史信息。然而局部环境效应会改变观测谱，若不在信号建模中纳入，将导致有偏的参数估计。

Method: 作者针对太阳引力聚焦与探测器运动导致的多普勒效应两类局部效应展开研究：解析估计其对信号显著性（signal significance）的影响，并对模拟数据进行贝叶斯分析（Bayesian analysis）以展示其效应。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值结果，但指出上述两类效应会改变信号谱并影响对潜在暗物质参数的推断，贝叶斯分析用于量化其影响。

Conclusion: 局部环境效应（太阳引力聚焦与多普勒效应）应在超轻暗物质信号建模中予以考虑，否则参数估计可能出现偏差。

Abstract: Ultralight dark matter can produce signals in experiments through its couplings with the Standard Model particles. The resulting signal spectrum is determined by the phase-space distribution of dark matter, which encodes information about its production mechanism and the history of the local universe. However, the local environmental effects can modify the observed spectrum and should be included in signal modeling to avoid biased parameter estimates. In this work, we investigate how the gravitational focusing by Sun and the Doppler effect due to the detector's motion modify the signal spectrum and affect inferring the underlying dark matter parameters. We analytically estimate the signal significance and perform Bayesian analysis on simulated data to demonstrate their impact.

</details>


### [117] [Coherent quantum-circuit simulation of Mueller dipole branching at small $x$: multiplicity fluctuations and DIS phenomenology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33979)
*Wei Kou,Xurong Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文为小 $x$ 下 Mueller 偶极分支（Mueller dipole branching）构建了一个相干量子线路（coherent quantum circuit），并在有限态受调控版本中再现截断时钟马尔可夫（truncated-clock Markov）结果，同时通过连续横向坐标模拟与 H1 数据比较考察多重数涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小 $x$ 偶极分支是分裂率依赖横向几何（transverse geometry）的随机过程，需在量子线路框架下给出显式相干编码，并厘清其与连续级联涨落及深度非弹性散射（DIS）唯象学的关系。

Method: 采用泊松时钟均匀化（Poisson-clock uniformization）控制更新次数，并用正交事件历史寄存器（orthogonal event-history registers）保留分裂路径（含导致相同末态构型的路径）；线路由局域分裂率直接制备跃迁振幅（transition amplitudes），而非加载预先计算的演化分布。另以连续横向坐标独立模拟 Mueller 级联。

Result: 态矢量多重数概率（statevector multiplicity probabilities）在数值精度上复现截断时钟马尔可夫结果，并与完整连续时间马尔可夫链基准（continuous-time Markov-chain benchmark）在约 $7\times10^{-4}$ 内吻合。最大快度（rapidity）处归一化四阶阶乘矩为 1.81，等均值 Yule 过程为 2.84；并给出多重数熵（multiplicity entropies）与偶极尺寸扇区（dipole-size sectors）间关联。固定负二项式（negative-binomial）偶极—带电粒子响应可复现 H1 带电粒子多重数熵随 $x$ 减小的上升趋势，但未能完全描述测量到的多重数分布。

Conclusion: 结果给出了有限态偶极分支的显式相干编码，并澄清了其与连续级联涨落及 DIS 唯象学之间的关系。

Abstract: Mueller dipole branching at small $x$ is a stochastic process in which splitting rates depend on transverse geometry. We construct a coherent quantum circuit for a regulated finite-state version of this process. Poisson-clock uniformization controls the number of updates, while orthogonal event-history registers retain the splitting paths, including those leading to the same final configuration. The circuit prepares transition amplitudes from local splitting rates rather than loading a precomputed evolved distribution. Its statevector multiplicity probabilities reproduce the truncated-clock Markov result to numerical precision and agree with the full continuous-time Markov-chain benchmark within approximately $7\times10^{-4}$. To examine fluctuations beyond the small circuit, we independently simulate the Mueller cascade with continuous transverse coordinates. At the largest rapidity studied, its normalized fourth factorial moment is $1.81$, compared with $2.84$ for an equal-mean Yule process. We also determine multiplicity entropies and correlations between dipole-size sectors. Applying a fixed negative-binomial dipole-to-charged-particle response reproduces the rising trend of the H1 charged-particle multiplicity entropy toward smaller $x$, but does not fully describe the measured multiplicity distributions. These results demonstrate an explicit coherent encoding of finite-state dipole branching and clarify its relation to continuous-cascade fluctuations and DIS phenomenology.

</details>


### [118] [Inverted Pendulum in the Sky: Floquet Stabilization of Cosmological Axions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34439)
*Tokiro Numasawa,Masahito Yamazaki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文探讨能否借助快速振荡耦合（类比卡皮查倒立摆）动态稳定轴子势山顶（hilltop）并降低微调，运用 Floquet 理论给出稳定窗口。结论是所研究的简单实现一般无法消除山顶微调，但可延迟振荡起始并增强遗迹丰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诸多宇宙学情景要求标量场位于势能顶部附近，这通常需要微调的初始条件（fine-tuned initial condition）。作者试图检验快速振荡耦合能否像卡皮查（Kapitza）倒立摆那样动态稳定轴子势（axion potential）山顶，并评估其对微调的削减程度。

Method: 采用 Floquet 理论确定山顶的稳定窗口（stability window），并分析多种实现：振荡耦合、自旋或振荡的第二标量场，以及作为陀螺类比的、由压扁三维球面（squashed three-sphere）上 sigma 模型实现的倒立旋转陀螺。另按有效频率的红移方式对稳定机制进行分类。

Result: 在膨胀宇宙中，稳定相持续的 e 折叠数（e-folds）与尺度层级成对数关系，且与宇宙学背景无关。分类分析表明：在辐射或物质主导期，这些实现无法既吸引场又借哈勃摩擦（Hubble friction）使其冻结；在暴胀期，吸引受哈勃摩擦与能量预算限制。

Conclusion: 所研究的简单实现一般不能消除山顶微调，但可延迟振荡起始，并在辐射主导期以 n_φ/n_{φ,std}∝(m̃/H_c)^{3/2} 增强遗迹丰度（relic abundance）。文中讨论了对轴子暗物质（axion dark matter）、暴胀（inflation）、精质（quintessence）与曲率子（curvaton）的含义。

Abstract: Many cosmological scenarios require a scalar field to sit near the top of its potential, which usually requires a fine-tuned initial condition. We ask whether the hilltop of an axion potential can be dynamically stabilized by a rapidly oscillating coupling, as for the inverted pendulum of Kapitza, and to what extent this can reduce the fine-tuning. Using Floquet theory, we determine the stability window of the hilltop and analyze several realizations: an oscillating coupling, a spinning or oscillating second field, and a gyroscopic analogue, an inverted spinning top realized by a sigma model on a squashed three-sphere. In an expanding Universe the stabilized phase lasts for a number of $e$-folds logarithmic in the hierarchy of scales, independently of the cosmological background. Classifying the stabilizers by how their effective frequency redshifts, we show that in these realizations the hilltop cannot both attract the field and leave it frozen by the Hubble friction during radiation or matter domination, and that during inflation the attraction is limited by the Hubble friction and the energy budget. Thus the simple realizations studied here do not generically remove the hilltop fine-tuning. They can instead delay the onset of the oscillation and enhance the relic abundance as $n_φ/n_{φ,{\rm std}}\propto(\tilde m/H_c)^{3/2}$ in radiation domination. We discuss the implications for axion dark matter, inflation, quintessence and curvatons.

</details>


### [119] [KEBBABS: Kilometric Enhanced Balloon-Baseline for Axion Birefringence Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34927)
*Tommaso Feri,Silvia Gasparotto*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出利用气球间自由空间光链路中激光偏振旋转搜寻超轻轴子暗物质（ultralight axion dark matter）的宽带方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共振光腔（resonant optical cavity）依赖多次反射实现长基线，本文以气球间长物理基线作为替代途径，拓展轴子-光子耦合（axion--photon coupling）的探测质量窗口。

Method: 两枚在30--40 km高度飞行的平流层气球（stratospheric balloons）构成自由空间光链路：一枚发射线偏振光束，另一枚反射回首个气球上的偏振计（polarimeter），通过测量偏振旋转搜寻轴子。

Result: 目标质量范围 $4\times10^{-15}\,\mathrm{eV}\lesssim m_a\lesssim10^{-11}\,\mathrm{eV}$；下限约对应1 Hz频率，更低频段吊舱绕视线滚动会伪造偏振信号。在 $m_a=10^{-14}\,\mathrm{eV}$，Pathfinder可达 $g_{a\gamma}\simeq2.8\times10^{-11}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$（约低于CAST限两倍），Feasible today略低于天体物理界限，Futuristic达 $g_{a\gamma}\simeq6.4\times10^{-15}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$（约低两个数量级）。

Conclusion: 该方案可在宽带质量范围内探测超轻轴子暗物质，且各阶段构型（Pathfinder、Feasible today、Futuristic）分别对应逐步提升的耦合灵敏度。

Abstract: We propose a broadband search for ultralight axion dark matter through the rotation of laser polarization induced by the axion--photon coupling. Two stratospheric balloons flying at altitudes of 30--40 km form a free-space optical link. One balloon sends a linearly polarized beam to the other, which reflects it back to a polarimeter on the first balloon. The long physical baseline provides an alternative to the repeated reflections used in resonant optical cavities. We target axion masses in the range $4\times10^{-15}\,\mathrm{eV}\lesssim m_a\lesssim10^{-11}\,\mathrm{eV}$. The lower edge corresponds to a frequency of about $1\,\mathrm{Hz}$, below which gondola roll about the line of sight can mimic a polarization signal. At $m_a=10^{-14}\,\mathrm{eV}$, a low-cost Pathfinder using flight-demonstrated technology could probe couplings as small as $g_{aγ}\simeq2.8\times10^{-11}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$, about a factor of two below the CAST bound. At the same mass, the Feasible today configuration could probe couplings just below the astrophysical bounds considered here, while the Futuristic configuration could reach $g_{aγ}\simeq6.4\times10^{-15}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$, roughly two orders of magnitude below those bounds.

</details>

### [120] [Quantum critical collapse to an extremal black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33264)
*Hugo Lange,Harvey S. Reall*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究量子引力（quantum gravity）对带电尘埃薄壳坍缩临界解的影响，证明近临界区哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）与具负势的刘维尔理论（Liouville theory）正则等价，量子化后预言存在量子反弹（quantum bounce），使经典临界面被"加厚"为临界层（critical layer）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究指出部分极端黑洞（extremal black holes）是位于引力坍缩与弥散之间的"临界"解；本文旨在考察量子引力效应对带电尘埃薄壳（thin shell of charged dust）坍缩临界解的影响。

Method: 采用相对于外部Killing时间生成薄壳运动方程的哈密顿量（在壳上等于ADM能量），证明其在近临界区与具负势的刘维尔理论（Liouville theory with negative potential）正则等价，随后对该模型进行量子化。

Result: 量子化后模型预言：当薄壳能量超出极端性的部分与"击穿尺度"（breakdown scale）相当时，坍缩薄壳有非平庸的量子力学反弹（quantum mechanical bounce）概率，且该概率在极端性处趋近于1；黑洞能量概率密度函数在击穿尺度以下的压低方式与Turiaci与Wu近期结果一致。

Conclusion: 该模型预言经典理论中的临界面（critical surface）在量子层面被加厚为"临界层"（critical layer），即临界性由面结构变为具有有限厚度的层结构。

Abstract: It has been emphasized recently that some extremal black holes are "critical" solutions, lying at the threshold between gravitational collapse and dispersion. We study the effect of quantum gravity on critical solutions for the collapse of a thin shell of charged dust. We consider the Hamiltonian that generates the shell equation of motion with respect to the exterior Killing time and coincides with the ADM energy on-shell. Near criticality, this Hamiltonian is shown to be canonically equivalent to that of a Liouville theory with negative potential. After quantization, this model predicts that a collapsing shell has a non-trivial probability of undergoing a quantum mechanical bounce when its energy above extremality is comparable to the "breakdown scale". The bounce probability approaches unity at extremality. Hence this model predicts that the critical surface in the classical theory is thickened quantum mechanically into a "critical layer". The probability density function for the black hole energy is suppressed below the breakdown scale in the same way as found recently by Turiaci and Wu.

</details>


### [121] [The black hole IR triangle for scalar QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33271)
*Fabiano Feleppa,Nava Gaddam,Nico Groenenboom*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在拉伸史瓦西视界（stretched Schwarzschild horizon）上树级（tree level）构造了标量量子电动力学（scalar QED）的黑洞红外三角（infrared triangle）。核心发现是：拉伸视界上场的两个径向分支（radial branches）是独立的边界数据，导致软因子（soft factor）退化为每条腿上的一个符号，并由此导出与零无穷远（null infinity）情形显著不同的记忆效应与守恒结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞红外三角（软定理、记忆效应、渐近对称性三者的等价关系）已在零无穷远上得到确立，但视界处的对应结构尚待澄清。本文旨在检验该三角在拉伸史瓦西视界上的标量QED情形，并刻画视界几何不同于零无穷远的独特性质。

Method: 在近视界麦克斯韦场（near-horizon Maxwell field）的协变相空间（covariant phase space）框架下，将软电荷（soft charge）识别为角项（corner term）；由近视界费曼规则（near-horizon Feynman rules）推导软光子定理（soft photon theorem），在费曼规则无法控制的发射情形下改用领头阶场方程；并以Ward恒等式（Ward identity）联系残余规范变换（residual gauge transformation）与大规范变换（large gauge transformation）。同时讨论红外发散（infrared divergence）、外态 dressing 以及Hartle–Hawking态中的记忆行为。

Result: 记忆（memory）是规范不变辐射场沿生成元的零模，与大规范模式共轭，并由高斯定律通过穿过视界的电荷通量确定。穿过拉伸视界的Noether通量因带电物质双向穿越而不守恒；去除物质部分后即得软电荷。硬电荷（hard charge）为径向通量，两分支并存时按径向运动方向加权，软因子为每条腿一个符号。两分支端点数据对应两个边界可观测量：时间积分的横向磁场与电场。散射辐射仅贡献于前者并抵消电荷库仑记忆的变化，使软电荷守恒；穿越视界的电荷则进入两者。此外，记忆在Hartle–Hawking态中受霍金辐射驱动做随机游走（random walk）。

Conclusion: 拉伸视界的红外三角与零无穷远情形的关键差异在于两个径向分支均为独立边界数据，这重塑了软因子与硬电荷的结构。软电荷守恒由辐射场对横向磁场可观测量与库仑记忆变化的相互抵消保证，而穿越视界的电荷破坏Noether通量守恒。视界记忆在Hartle–Hawking态下表现为霍金辐射驱动的随机游走。

Abstract: We construct the black hole infrared triangle of scalar QED on a stretched Schwarzschild horizon at tree level. In the covariant phase space of the near-horizon Maxwell field, the soft charge is a corner term. The memory is the zero mode of the gauge-invariant radiative field along the generators. It is conjugate to the boundary gauge mode that labels the degenerate horizon vacua, and Gauss' law fixes it by the flux of charge through the horizon. The Noether flux of the residual gauge transformation through the stretched horizon is not conserved by the scattering, because charged matter crosses the stretched horizon in both directions. The flux with its matter part removed is the soft charge, which generates the large gauge transformation. Its Ward identity is equivalent to a soft photon theorem in every radiative partial wave, which we derive from the near-horizon Feynman rules and, where these do not control the emission, from the field equations at leading order. The main difference from null infinity is that on a stretched horizon both radial branches of the field are independent boundary data. The hard charge is a radial flux. With both branches present it weights each leg by the direction of its radial motion, and the soft factor is a sign per leg. The endpoint data on the two branches give two boundary observables, the time-integrated transverse magnetic and electric fields. The field radiated by a scattering process contributes only to the first, where it cancels the change in the Coulomb memory of the charges, so that the soft charge is conserved. A charge that crosses the horizon enters both. We also discuss the infrared divergence, the dressing of the external states, and the fate of the memory in the Hartle--Hawking state, where it performs a random walk driven by the Hawking radiation.

</details>


### [122] [The OPE of lines beyond CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33272)
*Jan Albert,Matthew Forslund,Alexandre Homrich*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文提出一种描述共形不动点相关变形理论中平行线缺陷融合的算符乘积展开（operator product expansion, OPE）框架，并以二维 Ising 模型磁性杂质间短距势的 DMRG 结果作为非微扰检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平行线缺陷的融合极限缺乏系统描述手段，现有局域算符 OPE 无法直接推广到缺陷情形；动机在于建立可逐阶计算的缺陷融合理论框架。

Method: 提出用 Wilsonian 有效作用量（Wilsonian effective action）描述融合极限，其系数可在紫外（UV）附近通过共形微扰论（conformal perturbation theory）计算，从而逐阶固定含缺陷关联函数的融合极限，仅留下与融合缺陷上凝聚（condensate）关联函数相关的非微扰系数。

Result: 在二维 Ising 模型两个磁性杂质间的短距势上，OPE 框架与 DMRG 提取的势及凝聚量高度吻合，凝聚量另用可积性（integrability）精确求解；另以若干可解模型展示框架的不同特征。

Conclusion: 该框架将量子场论（QFT）中熟悉的局域算符 OPE 推广至平行线缺陷融合，兼顾微扰可计算性与非微扰检验，并具备向更一般缺陷与模型扩展的潜力。

Abstract: We discuss the fusion of parallel line defects in theories defined by relevant deformations of a conformal fixed point. We propose an operator product expansion to describe this limit in terms of a Wilsonian effective action whose coefficients are computable in conformal perturbation theory around the UV. This allows one to fix, order by order, the fusion limit of correlation functions involving the defects, up to non-perturbative coefficients associated to correlation functions (condensates) on the fused defect. It thus generalizes the familiar OPE of local operators in QFT. As a non-perturbative test of our proposal, we consider the short-distance potential between two magnetic impurities in the $2d$ Ising model. We find excellent agreement between the OPE framework and DMRG extractions of the potential and condensates (the latter also computed exactly with integrability). In addition, we consider a number of solvable models to illustrate different features of the framework.

</details>


### [123] [The String Theory Photinoverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33273)
*Andreas P. Braun,Thibaut Coudarchet,Arthur Hebecker,Joerg Jaeckel,Jonathan Steiner*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在IIB型弦紧化中，RR光子通过Wilson线与光微子质量项产生显著动能混合，使大量暗光微子与bino混合并耦合到标准模型，可解释暗物质并为当前伽马射线观测所检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IIB型弦紧化（Type-IIB string compactification）通常产生大量无质量、无轻电荷态的"超隐蔽"（superhidden）RR光子，但高能标超对称（supersymmetry）要求存在对应的"暗光微子"（dark photino）；问题在于这些暗扇区通常不可观测，需要寻找使其与可见扇区发生联系的机制。
