Method: 识别使动能混合（kinetic mixing）由Wilson线诱导的几何结构，并论证其期望值一般非零；结合光微子的超对称破缺质量项（SUSY breaking mass terms），分析暗光微子与bino的混合及与标准模型（Standard Model）的耦合强度。

Result: 比可见扇区超对称破缺标度更轻的多数暗光微子与bino显著混合，从而足够强地耦合到标准模型；体模量（volume modulus）与暴胀子（inflaton）衰变一般会产生暗光微子的宇宙学丰度，可解释暗物质（dark matter）。

Conclusion: "自然的Photoniverse暗物质参数空间"（natural Photinoverse dark matter parameter space）已可被当前实验（尤其是伽马射线观测）触及，因而具有近期内的发现潜力。

Abstract: Type-IIB string compactifications typically give rise to a significant number of RR-photons. These are generically massless and do not possess any light charged states, making them `superhidden'. However, since supersymmetry is present at least at some high scale, corresponding `dark photinos' are also predicted. The kinetic mixing of the photons together with the SUSY breaking mass terms of the photinos makes the latter observable. We identify geometries in which the mixing is induced by Wilson lines and show that their expectation values are generically non-zero. As a result, most of the dark photinos, which are lighter than the visible sector SUSY breaking scale, mix significantly with the bino and hence couple to the Standard Model sufficiently strongly to leave potentially observable traces. In particular, volume modulus and inflaton decays generically induce a cosmological abundance of dark photinos that can explain dark matter. We find that the `natural Photinoverse dark matter parameter space' is already accessible to current experiments, notably gamma ray observations, which therefore have discovery potential in the near future.

</details>


### [124] [Gauge groups of type 0 orientifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33275)
*Vittorio Larotonda,Salvatore Raucci*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用微扰态与膜态（brane states）的约束确定正交型与辛型0型orientifold规范群的全局形式，并修正文献中流传的若干0型真空振幅（vacuum amplitudes）笔误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 0型orientifold的规范群全局形式此前未被明确确定，且文献中0型真空振幅存在影响相关推导的印刷错误。

Method: 基于微扰态与膜态的约束，对正交型与辛型两种0型orientifold的规范群全局形式进行判定，同时订正真空振幅中的错误。

Result: 在0A型中，正交orientifold给出关于D9-膜数目的宇称条件（parity condition），辛orientifold则出现关于快子D0-膜衰变（tachyonic D0-brane decay）的疑难。

Conclusion: 研究确定了0型orientifold规范群的全局形式，纠正了文献中的振幅笔误，并提出0A型中需进一步解释的宇称条件与快子D0-膜衰变疑难。

Abstract: We determine the global form of the gauge groups of the orthogonal and symplectic type 0 orientifolds, using the constraints from perturbative and brane states. In the process, we correct some misprints in type 0 vacuum amplitudes that have propagated in the literature. For type 0A, our analysis reveals a parity condition on the numbers of D9-branes in the orthogonal orientifold and a puzzle concerning the decay of a tachyonic D0-brane in the symplectic orientifold.

</details>


### [125] [Unitarization from Spin Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33277)
*Francesco Bertucci,James Bonifacio,Andrew Schrader*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在一般闭合六维自旋流形上对10维超杨-米尔斯理论做谱分解降维，保留无穷多Kaluza-Klein（KK）模，并通过树级2→2散射振幅的高能行为与微扰幺正性导出涉及内部流形Laplace与Dirac算子本征值的求和规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于将自发破缺规范理论中已知的幺正性求和规则（unitarity sum rules）落实到高维理论紧致化的具体谱数据上，检验KK塔在恢复幺正性中的作用。

Method: 方法采用对高维规范场与gaugino（规范微子）的谱分解（spectral decomposition）实现维数约化（dimensional reduction），保留KK模的无穷塔；随后计算含外线有质量旋量与矢量的树级2→2振幅，并施加高能行为下的微扰幺正性约束。

Result: 结果为若干求和规则，其中将内部六维流形上Laplace算子（Laplace operator）与Dirac算子的本征值同KK模立方耦合常数（cubic coupling constants）的乘积联系起来；对平坦环面（flat tori）与圆球（round spheres）显式验证了最简单求和规则的满足情况。

Conclusion: 结论是已知的自发破缺规范理论幺正性求和规则可在一般紧致化背景下获得具体实现，其形式由内部流形的谱性质与KK耦合共同决定。

Abstract: We derive the dimensional reduction of 10D super Yang-Mills theory on a general closed 6D spin manifold using spectral decompositions for the higher-dimensional gauge field and gaugino, keeping the infinite towers of Kaluza-Klein (KK) modes in the lower-dimensional theory. We then calculate tree-level $2 \rightarrow 2$ scattering amplitudes with external massive spinors and vectors in this lower-dimensional theory. By studying the high-energy behavior of these amplitudes and imposing perturbative unitarity, we derive several sum rules that involve the eigenvalues of Laplace and Dirac operators on the internal 6-manifold, together with products of cubic coupling constants of the KK modes. These give concrete realizations of the known unitarity sum rules for spontaneously broken gauge theories. We illustrate explicitly how the simplest sum rule is satisfied for flat tori and round spheres.

</details>


### [126] [Neural Network Field Theory and the AdS/CFT Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33281)
*Dmitry S. Ageev,Yulia A. Ageeva,Anna S. Bernakevich*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在神经网络场论（neural network field theory, NN-FT）框架下实现了欧氏反德西特空间（Euclidean anti-de Sitter space）上的大质量标量场论及其全息边界可观测量。所构造的Fourier-Bessel余弦神经元在有限网络宽度下精确复现正规化的大质量AdS传播子，并在无穷宽极限下恢复高斯场论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于将神经网络场论扩展至弯曲时空与全息（holography）情境，构建可在神经网络框架内研究相互作用标量全息对应（scalar holography）的工具，并厘清神经网络架构在编码体动力学（bulk dynamics）与边界可观测量中的作用。

Method: 方法包括：构造协方差精确匹配正规化大质量AdS传播子的Fourier-Bessel余弦神经元；通过两种互补途径引入四次相互作用（quartic interaction）——变形神经参数测度以施加指定相互作用，以及由辅助二值神经元（auxiliary binary neurons）的统计生成局域四次相互作用；并将BDHM prescription直接应用于神经系综。

Result: 结果上，作者恢复了标量体到边界传播子（bulk-to-boundary propagator）、共形边界两点函数（conformal boundary two-point function）以及领头连通四点接触Witten图（leading connected four-point contact Witten diagram）；并表明不同神经架构可复现相同领头边界关联函数，却对应不等价的体理论。

Conclusion: 结论是这些结果建立了在NN-FT框架内研究相互作用标量全息对应的框架，并阐明了神经架构在编码体动力学与边界可观测量中的角色。

Abstract: We develop a neural realization of massive scalar field theory on Euclidean anti--de Sitter space and its holographic boundary observables within neural network field theory (NN--FT). We construct Fourier--Bessel cosine neurons whose covariance reproduces the regulated massive AdS propagator exactly at finite network width, with the Gaussian field theory recovered in the infinite-width limit.
  We introduce quartic interactions through two complementary approaches. The first implements a prescribed interaction by deforming the neural parameter measure, while the second generates a local quartic interaction from the statistics of auxiliary binary neurons. Applying the BDHM prescription directly to the neural ensemble, we recover the scalar bulk-to-boundary propagator, the conformal boundary two-point function, and the leading connected four-point contact Witten diagram.
  We further show that distinct neural architectures can reproduce the same leading boundary correlators while defining inequivalent bulk theories. These results establish a framework for studying interacting scalar holography within NN--FT and clarify the role of neural architecture in encoding bulk dynamics and boundary observables.

</details>


### [127] [Energy plateau cascades from complex-time local quenches in thermal two-dimensional CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33283)
*Dmitry S. Ageev,Vladimir A. Bykov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究热态二维共形场论（2D CFT）中带复时间演化（complex-time evolution）的局域算符淬火（local operator quench），将其视为系统与环境相互作用条件下条件动力学的有效描述，并发现多共形族竞争可产生能量平台（energy plateaux）级联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真空淬火已有较多研究，但热初态允许多个共形族（conformal families）共同参与演化，其竞争行为尚不清楚；同时复时间演化可刻画系统与环境耦合下的条件动力学，具有独立研究价值。

Method: 在柱面（cylinder）与环面（torus）上运用共形Ward恒等式（conformal Ward identities）确定局域能量密度，并在自由场论、Ising模型与Wess–Zumino–Witten模型中给出具体演示；进一步建立冷极限谱描述（cold-limit spectral description）。

Result: 多共形族竞争产生一系列近似均匀的能量平台，后续平台逐步逼近不同主态（primary states）的能量；平台高度由主导主通道（primary channel）决定，平台间跃迁受共形维数与算符矩阵元支配。对于固定紧致幺正CFT，仅有限多个主通道能贡献领头平台序列；在大中心荷（central charge）极限下，轻算符平台与热AdS中的少粒子扇区相关联，而重淬火情形下该图像存在局限。

Conclusion: 热态局域算符淬火的复时间演化可呈现由主通道竞争驱动的平台级联，冷极限谱描述与有限主通道结论为定性刻画提供框架；大中心荷下的AdS少粒子图像适用于轻淬火，但对重淬火失效。

Abstract: In this work, we study local operator quenches in thermal two-dimensional conformal field theories with complex-time evolution, which can be viewed as an effective description of conditional dynamics in a system interacting with an environment. In contrast to quenches of the vacuum, the thermal initial state allows several conformal families to participate in the evolution. We show that their competition can produce cascades of approximately homogeneous energy plateaux, with successive plateaux approaching the energies of different primary states. Using conformal Ward identities on the cylinder and torus, we determine the local energy density and illustrate this behavior in free-field theories, the Ising model and Wess--Zumino--Witten models. We develop a cold-limit spectral description in which the dominant primary channel determines the height of each plateau, while conformal dimensions and operator matrix elements govern the transitions between them. For a fixed compact unitary CFT, we show that only finitely many primary channels can contribute to the leading plateau sequence. Finally, we discuss the large-central-charge regime, relating light-operator plateaux to few-particle sectors in thermal AdS and examining the limitations of this picture for heavy quenches.

</details>


### [128] [Bounds on Love Numbers and Quasinormal Modes from the Surface Impedance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33285)
*Daniel McLoughlin,Mikhail P. Solon*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 基于稳定天体内部的表面阻抗（surface impedance）约束，推导了潮汐洛夫数（Love numbers）与准正规模（quasinormal modes）的上下界：无耗散（lossless）体给出精确至广义相对论（general relativity）的洛夫数界，并排除复频率平面中的特定区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面阻抗由天体内部唯一决定并编码其全部潮汐响应，因此可借阻抗性质对潮汐响应量施加普遍约束。此前的界对牛顿星（Newtonian stars）精确，但对致密星失效，原因在于其将任意快潮汐的响应取为零或非负，而在广义相对论中该响应为负。

Method: 从稳定天体的两种性质出发——无耗散（不耗散能量）与被动（passive，回馈能量不超过吸收能量）——推导阻抗约束，并据此界定动态洛夫数与准正规模的取值范围；结论在广义相对论下、任意重整化方案（renormalization scheme）与任意能标下均精确成立。

Result: 对无耗散体，每个动态洛夫数均有上下界；其准正规模被排除于由质量与半径设定的复频率平面区域外，且每已知一个洛夫数即扩大该排除区；反之，已知较低阶洛夫数时准正规模可给出每个洛夫数的下界。对视界尺度（horizon scale）的被动体（如黑洞），静态洛夫数位于一个窗口内，该窗口随表面趋近视界而收缩至黑洞的零值。

Conclusion: 表面阻抗为统一刻画潮汐响应提供了框架：稳定性（无耗散或被动）本身即蕴含洛夫数与准正规模的严格界，并将黑洞的零静态洛夫数作为视界极限纳入同一图景。

Abstract: The surface impedance of a body is fixed by its interior alone and encodes its entire tidal response. We derive bounds on Love numbers and quasinormal modes from constraints on the impedance that follow from either of two properties of a stable body: it is lossless, meaning that it dissipates no energy, or it is passive, meaning that it never gives back more energy than it has received. For a lossless body, every dynamical Love number is bounded from above and below, exactly in general relativity, in any renormalization scheme and at any scale. Earlier bounds are exact for Newtonian stars but fail for compact stars, because they take the response to arbitrarily fast tides to be zero or non-negative, whereas in general relativity it is negative. The quasinormal modes of a lossless body are also excluded from a region of the complex frequency plane set by the mass and radius. Each known Love number enlarges the excluded region. Conversely, the quasinormal modes bound each Love number from below, given the lower ones. For a passive body of horizon scale, such as a black hole, the static Love number lies in a window that closes onto the vanishing black-hole value as the body's surface approaches the horizon.

</details>


### [129] [Chaotic singularities inside vacuum black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33287)
*Robie A. Hennigar,Ayan K. Patra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文构造了渐近局部AdS$_5$的稳态黑洞，其齐次内部实现五维真空引力的宇宙学台球（cosmological billiard），并给出判断内部动力学混沌性的代数条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BKL猜想（Belinski–Khalatnikov–Lifshitz conjecture）预言一般时空奇点附近存在混沌动力学，但此前尚无真空黑洞被证明具有该行为，故本文旨在寻找真空黑洞中混沌动力学（chaotic dynamics）的实现。

Method: 构造具有正则、三轴压扁视界（triaxially squashed horizons）及因果外部的稳态渐近局部AdS$_5$黑洞；在其齐次内部中实现五维真空引力的宇宙学台球（cosmological billiard）；并在视界数据上找出使内部台球混沌的简单代数条件。

Result: 所构造的黑洞其齐次内部可复现五维真空引力的宇宙学台球行为；存在一个视界数据需满足的必要代数条件，且具有正则视界的解可局部覆盖台球轨迹的开集（open set of billiard trajectories）。

Conclusion: 结果表明真空黑洞内部亦可实现BKL型混沌台球动力学，为BKL猜想的黑洞实现提供了具体构造与代数判据。

Abstract: According to the conjecture of Belinski, Khalatnikov and Lifshitz, general relativity gives rise to chaotic dynamics in the vicinity of generic spacelike singularities. However, no vacuum black hole has been shown to exhibit this behaviour. Here we construct stationary, asymptotically locally AdS$_5$ black holes with regular, triaxially squashed horizons and causal exteriors, whose homogeneous interiors realize the cosmological billiard of generic five-dimensional vacuum gravity. We identify a simple algebraic condition on the horizon data that is necessary for the interior billiard to be chaotic, and show that solutions with regular horizons locally access an open set of billiard trajectories.

</details>


### [130] [RMT${}_2$: Random Spectral Correlations for CFT${}_2$ and AdS${}_3$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33291)
*Jan Boruch,Gabriele Di Ubaldo,Felix M. Haehl,Eric Perlmutter,Moshe Rozali*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出 RMT₂ 框架，将随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory）与模不变性（modular invariance）统一，通过引入自旋（spin）把随机矩阵关联函数提升为 SL(2,ℤ) 庞加莱和（Poincaré sums），并给出与 AdS₃ 引力中离壳虫洞振幅（off-shell wormhole amplitudes）对比的具体候选表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有构造需在随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory）与模不变性（modular invariance）之间建立一致统一，且需纳入自旋自由度，以在 AdS₃ 引力中与多环面边界的虫洞振幅（wormhole amplitudes）进行全息对照。

Method: 提出系统性方案，在原有构造基础上加入自旋（spin），将随机矩阵关联函数提升为 Mellein 变换（Mellin transforms）的 SL(2,ℤ) 庞加莱和（Poincaré sums），从而在每个自旋主扇区（spinning primary sector）近极值处强制随机矩阵关联；所得关联函数用爱森斯坦级数（Eisenstein series）与马斯峰形式（Maass cusp forms）的正交基展开。

Result: 以 Airy 模型（Airy model）为例，在幺正与正交对称类（unitary and orthogonal symmetry classes）中完成了任意亏格（genus）两点关联函数的提升，展示模不变的拓扑展开（topological expansion）；重求和（resummation）表明模不变谱形状因子（spectral form factor）仍保持 ramp-plateau 结构。此外显式计算了普适的 RMT₂ 三点关联函数（three-point correlator），对应假定的三边界虫洞（three-boundary wormhole）。

Conclusion: RMT₂ 构成随机矩阵理论与模不变性的一致统一框架，为 AdS₃ 引力中任意数目环面边界（torus boundaries）的离壳虫洞振幅（off-shell wormhole amplitudes）提供具体基准与显式候选表达式。

Abstract: We present the full framework of RMT${}_2$, a consistent unification of random matrix theory and modular invariance. The systematic prescription, which augments the existing construction by incorporating spin, lifts random matrix correlators to $\text{SL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ Poincaré sums of their Mellin transforms, in a manner which enforces random matrix correlations near extremality in every spinning primary sector. The resulting RMT${}_2$ correlators are further expressible in terms of an orthogonal basis of Eisenstein series and Maass cusp forms. We demonstrate the construction by lifting the two-point correlators at arbitrary genus of the Airy model, in both unitary and orthogonal symmetry classes, exhibiting the modular-invariant topological expansion of RMT${}_2$; a resummation of the genus expansion shows that the modular-invariant spectral form factor preserves the ramp-plateau structure. Holographically, RMT${}_2$ provides a concrete benchmark and explicit candidate expressions for comparison with off-shell wormhole amplitudes in AdS${}_3$ gravity, with any number of torus boundaries. We explicitly compute the universal RMT${}_2$ three-point correlator, corresponding to the putative three-boundary wormhole.

</details>


### [131] [Machine Learning for Calabi--Yau Geometry and Heterotic Compactification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33311)
*Vishnu Jejjala,Damian Mayorga Pena*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该综述（review）总结了利用机器学习（machine learning）方法预测卡拉比-丘流形（Calabi-Yau manifolds）拓扑性质、搜索新卡拉比-丘四维流形（Calabi-Yau fourfolds）以及数值逼近里奇平坦度规（Ricci-flat metrics）的研究进展，并讨论其在异弦紧致化（heterotic compactifications）等场景中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦紧致化（string compactification）的定量预言依赖难以解析计算的几何数据（geometric data），因此需要发展机器学习与数值方法来高效获取这些数据。

Method: 综述形式（review survey），涵盖机器学习预测拓扑性质、卡拉比-丘四维流形搜索、里奇平坦度规的数值近似，以及异弦紧致化中丛度规（bundle metrics）与调和物质形式（harmonic matter forms）在正则归一化汤川耦合（canonically normalized Yukawa couplings）与轴子-物质相互作用（axion-matter interactions）计算中的作用。

Result: 梳理了相关方法在通量紧致化（flux compactifications）、翘曲与修正背景（warped and corrected backgrounds）以及超越卡拉比-丘的几何结构（geometric structures beyond the Calabi-Yau setting）中的应用，并整理了可用数据集与软件（datasets and software）。

Conclusion: 综述强调与解析方法的联系、几何约束（geometric constraints）、数值验证（numerical validation）以及严格误差控制（rigorous error control）方面的近期进展，指出机器学习与数值方法在弦紧致化几何计算中的价值与前沿方向。

Abstract: Quantitative predictions from string compactification depend on geometric data that are difficult to compute analytically. This review surveys machine learning approaches to predicting topological properties of Calabi-Yau manifolds, discusses the search for new Calabi-Yau fourfolds, as well as numerical methods for approximating Ricci-flat metrics. In the context of heterotic compactifications, we explain how bundle metrics and harmonic matter forms enter calculations of canonically normalized Yukawa couplings and axion-matter interactions. We also outline applications to flux compactifications, warped and corrected backgrounds, and geometric structures beyond the Calabi-Yau setting. Finally, we review available datasets and software, highlight connections with analytic methods, and discuss geometric constraints, numerical validation, and recent progress in rigorous error control.

</details>


### [132] [On the Structure Constants of ${\cal N}=2$ Liouville Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33343)
*Jingxin Tu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文给出 \(\mathcal{N}=2\) 类空 Liouville 理论（spacelike Liouville theory）的结构常数（structure constants），借助与 \(SL(2,\mathbb{R})/U(1)\) 超陪集理论的费米型 FZZ 对偶（fermionic FZZ duality）建立代数对应，并在超空间中导出三点结构常数，最后数值验证交叉对称性（crossing symmetry）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: \(\mathcal{N}=2\) Liouville 理论在 \(b\leftrightarrow b^{-1}\) 下缺乏自对偶性（self-duality），因而不适用于 \(\mathcal{N}=0\) 与 \(\mathcal{N}=1\) 情形中的标准 bootstrap 方法，需要新的求解途径。

Method: 受费米型 FZZ 对偶启发，建立该理论与 \(SL(2,\mathbb{R})/U(1)\) 超陪集理论之间的代数对应，包括零向量（null vectors）的匹配，并在超空间（superspace）框架下推导三点结构常数。

Result: 中性扇区（neutral sector）结构常数对应于 \(SL(2,\mathbb{R})\) WZW 模型中保持谱流（spectral-flow-preserving）的结构常数，两个带电扇区（charged sectors）对应于破坏谱流（spectral-flow-violating）的结构常数；并确定了中性与带电扇区之间的相对归一化（relative normalization）。

Conclusion: 所给出的相对归一化经交叉对称性（crossing symmetry）的数值检验，结果支持结构常数的自洽性。

Abstract: We show the structure constants of $\mathcal{N}=2$ spacelike Liouville theory. The absence of self-duality under $b\leftrightarrow b^{-1}$, where $b$ is the Liouville coupling, prevents a straightforward application of the standard bootstrap methods used in $\mathcal{N}=0$ and $\mathcal{N}=1$ Liouville theory. Motivated by the fermionic FZZ duality with the $SL(2,\mathbb{R})/U(1)$ supercoset theory, we establish an algebraic correspondence between the two theories, including a matching of their null vectors. We then derive the three-point structure constants in superspace. The neutral sector structure constants are associated with the spectral-flow-preserving structure constant of the $SL(2,\mathbb{R})$ WZW model, while the two charged sectors correspond to its spectral-flow-violating structure constants. Finally, we give the correct relative normalization between the neutral and charged sectors and test crossing symmetry numerically.

</details>


### [133] [Identifying Renormalization Group Interfaces of Massless WZW Flows $\widehat{\mathfrak{su}}(2)_k \to \widehat{\mathfrak{su}}(2)_1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33354)
*Yuma Furuta,Yuya Kusuki,Toshiki Onagi,Yin Tang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在拓扑修饰（topological dressing）意义下，确定了猜想中的无质量 $\widehat{\mathfrak{su}}(2)_k \to \widehat{\mathfrak{su}}(2)_1$ Wess-Zumino-Witten 流（flows）在任意奇数能级 $k\geq 3$ 下的重整化群界面（renormalization-group interface）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重整化群界面（renormalization-group interface）刻画了流（flow）如何联系短距离与长距离的算符（operators），而此前对猜想的 $\widehat{\mathfrak{su}}(2)_k \to \widehat{\mathfrak{su}}(2)_1$ WZW 流尚缺乏对其界面的确定。

Method: 利用整体 $SU(2)$ 对称性要求界面保持由两侧流（currents）构成的 $k+1$ 能级对角流代数（diagonal current algebra），再由 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 中心对称性（center symmetry）挑选标记界面的表示（representation）。

Result: 该确定预言了流向同一红外不动点（infrared fixed point）时低能界面激发（low-lying interface excitations）的不同模式；自旋链（spin-chain）计算在 $k=3$ 与 $k=5$ 时重现了这些模式。

Conclusion: 自旋链结果与理论预言一致，为两个猜想中的流提供了独立证据（independent evidence）。

Abstract: A renormalization-group interface encodes how a flow relates operators at short and long distances. We identify this interface up to topological dressing for the conjectured massless $\widehat{\mathfrak{su}}(2)_k \to \widehat{\mathfrak{su}}(2)_1$ Wess-Zumino-Witten flows at every odd level $k\geq 3$. Global $SU(2)$ symmetry requires the interface to preserve a diagonal current algebra at level $k+1$, built from the currents on its two sides. The $\mathbb{Z}_2$ center symmetry then selects which representation labels the interface. This identification predicts distinct patterns of low-lying interface excitations for flows to the same infrared fixed point. Spin-chain calculations reproduce these patterns for $k=3$ and $5$, providing independent evidence for both conjectured flows.

</details>


### [134] [An Argument for the Weak Gravity Conjecture from Constrained Wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33415)
*Gabriele Di Ubaldo,Luca V. Iliesiu,Dawid Maskalaniec*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文通过构造新一类约束瞬子（constrained instantons）计算多边界虫洞振幅，证明引力路径积分（gravitational path integral, GPI）的正半定性（positive semidefiniteness）要求等效地蕴含弱引力猜想（weak gravity conjecture, WGC），并将其推广至磁弱引力猜想与多个 U(1) 规范场的凸包条件（convex hull condition）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPI 计算开宇宙与闭宇宙态之间的内积，而良定义的希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）要求这些内积满足正半定性；但该正性在低能有效理论中并不显式可见，因此需要研究正性对具体理论施加的约束。

Method: 在 D≥4 维渐近平直时空（asymptotically flat space）中考察与带电物质耦合的爱因斯坦–麦克斯韦理论（Einstein–Maxwell theory），构造一类新的约束瞬子以计算多边界虫洞振幅（multi-boundary wormhole amplitudes），并分析这些振幅的正性条件。

Result: 在温和假设下，若不存在满足 WGC 的带电粒子，则虫洞振幅违反 GPI 的正性；若存在满足 WGC 的粒子，虫洞会产生一种不稳定性（instability），从而使正性得以恢复。相同论证表明，正性的恢复还要求磁 WGC 成立，且多 U(1) 规范场情形需满足凸包条件。

Conclusion: GPI 的正半定性可作为推导弱引力猜想及其磁对偶与多规范场推广的一条独立途径，表明引力路径积分的一致性条件对低能有效理论具有实质性的限制作用。

Abstract: The gravitational path integral (GPI) computes inner products between states of open and closed universes in any low-energy effective theory. A well-defined Hilbert space requires these inner products to be positive semidefinite, but this positivity is not manifest in the effective theory. In this paper, we study the constraints that positivity imposes on Einstein--Maxwell theory coupled to charged matter in asymptotically flat space in $D\geq 4$ dimensions. We start by constructing a new class of constrained instantons that compute multi-boundary wormhole amplitudes. We show that, under mild assumptions, if no charged particle satisfies the weak gravity conjecture (WGC), these amplitudes violate positivity of the GPI. If instead some particle satisfies the WGC, the wormholes develop an instability that allows positivity to be restored. The same argument shows that positivity can only be restored if the magnetic WGC holds and the convex hull condition applies to theories with several $\mathrm{U}(1)$ gauge fields.

</details>


### [135] [Probe branes from equivariant volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33445)
*Kiril Hristov,Pedro Vicente Marto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个在任意 BPS 背景中处理 M2-膜以及在 type IIB 五形式扇区中处理 D3-膜几何处方，将探针作用量表示为相对等变配对（relative equivariant pairings），并在无需显式背景度规的情况下通过等变体积（equivariant volumes）求值，同时为非紧致探针提供正则化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典欧氏探针作用量（classical Euclidean probe actions）在全息（holography）中控制非微扰权重与延展可观测量的计算，因而需要一套能统一处理各类 BPS 背景中膜探针的几何框架。

Method: 给出适用于任意 BPS 背景中 M2-膜及 type IIB 五形式扇区中 D3-膜的几何处方，兼容内空间与时空方向上的紧致及非紧致超对称世界体（supersymmetric worldvolumes），将作用量表达为相对等变配对，并在给定延拓与边界假设下用等变体积求值。

Result: 在 AdS₄×SE₇ 背景（对偶于 ABJM、ADHM 及带味箭图理论）中复现并改进了世界面型瞬子指数（worldsheet-type instanton exponents）与有限 Wilson 圈作用量；在 AdS₇×S⁴ 中恢复了 M2 巨引力子（giant graviton）与面缺陷作用量；在 SE₅ 紧致化（对偶于 N=4 超杨–米尔斯与 Klebanov–Witten 理论）中通过 D3 探针给出涉及巨引力子、黑洞探针与面缺陷的检验。

Conclusion: 该等变配对框架可在不依赖显式背景度规的前提下统一计算 M2 与 D3 探针作用量，并在多个全息对偶背景中再现及精细化已知结果，表明其具有较广的适用性。

Abstract: Classical Euclidean probe actions control non-perturbative weights and extended observables in holography. We present a geometric prescription for M2-branes in arbitrary BPS backgrounds and D3-branes in the five-form sector of type IIB. The framework accommodates compact and non-compact supersymmetric worldvolumes in both internal and spacetime directions, expressing their actions as relative equivariant pairings. Under stated extension and boundary assumptions, equivariant volumes evaluate these pairings without explicit background metrics and provide a regularization for non-compact probes. Examples in $\mathrm{AdS}_4\times SE_7$ backgrounds dual to ABJM, ADHM and flavored quiver theories reproduce and refine worldsheet-type instanton exponents and finite Wilson-loop actions. We also recover M2 giant-graviton and surface-defect actions in $\mathrm{AdS}_7\times S^4$. D3 probes in $SE_5$ compactifications dual to $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang--Mills and Klebanov--Witten theory provide tests involving giant gravitons, black hole probes and surface defects.

</details>


### [136] [Pfaffian solutions to the Ernst equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27420)
*Arsen R. Melikyan,Aleksandr Pinzul*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文给出 Ernst 方程（Ernst equation）的 Yamazaki-Hori 解的 Pfaffian 表示（Pfaffian representation），并据此证明了将 Ernst 方程与二维 Toda 模型（2D Toda model）相关联的 Nakamura 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于建立 Ernst 方程的 Yamazaki-Hori 解与可积系统之间的明确联系，并验证 arXiv:2611.19558 中提出的 Nakamura 猜想。

Method: 方法上，构造 Yamazaki-Hori 解的 Pfaffian 表示，利用该代数结构建立与二维 Toda 模型的对应关系。

Result: 主要结果为得到 Yamazaki-Hori 解的 Pfaffian 表示，并由此完成对 Nakamura 猜想的证明。

Conclusion: 结论表明，Ernst 方程与二维 Toda 模型之间的猜想性联系成立，Pfaffian 表示为该对应提供了关键工具。

Abstract: We obtain a Pfaffian representation of the Yamazaki-Hori solutions to the Ernst equation. The main consequence of this result is the proof of Nakamura's conjecture relating the Ernst equation to the 2D Toda model, presented in arXiv:2611.19558.

</details>


### [137] [All known supersymmetric $\mathrm{AdS}_4 \times S^7$ uplifts from maximal supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33564)
*Krzysztof Pilch,Oscar Varela*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文给出了 D=4、N=8 的 SO(8) 规范超引力（gauged supergravity）七手征多重态截断（seven-chiral truncation）到十一维的提升（uplift），并借此统一描述已知超对称 AdS$_4$ 真空的十一维内禀几何，具体分析了 U(1)$^2$ 与 SO(3) 对称性两类真空的 Kaluza–Klein 型几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该理论所有已知超对称 AdS$_4$ 真空均位于包含七个手征多重态（chiral multiplets）的 N=1 截断中，但缺乏统一的十一维几何描述，故有必要完成该七手征扇区的提升。

Method: 推导七手征扇区的十一维提升（uplift），得到该类全部已知超对称 AdS$_4$ 解的统一的十一维几何描述；随后专门处理具有 U(1)$^2$ 与 SO(3) 对称性的 N=1 真空，构造其新的 D=11 提升并刻画内禀几何（internal geometry）。

Result: 两类真空的内禀几何均可由 Kaluza–Klein 型描述给出：U(1)$^2$ 解基于 S$^7$ 在 $\mathbb{CP}^3$ 上的整体 Hopf 纤维化（global Hopf fibration），其中一个 U(1) 作用于纤维、另一个生成变形 $\mathbb{CP}^3$ 底空间的唯一连续等距（isometry）；SO(3) 解则呈局部非阿贝尔 Kaluza–Klein 形式（local non-abelian Kaluza–Klein），SO(3) 作用于纤维，而四维底空间无残余连续对称性。

Conclusion: 七手征扇区的提升为 SO(8) 规范超引力中已知超对称 AdS$_4$ 真空提供了统一的十一维几何框架，并揭示 U(1)$^2$ 与 SO(3) 真空分别对应整体与局部非阿贝尔的 Kaluza–Klein 型几何。

Abstract: The solutions of $D=4$ $\mathcal N=8$ SO(8)-gauged supergravity uplift consistently to eleven dimensions. All known supersymmetric AdS$_4$ vacua of this theory lie in an $\mathcal N=1$ truncation containing seven chiral multiplets. In this paper, we work out the uplift of this seven-chiral sector, providing a unified eleven-dimensional geometric description of all known supersymmetric AdS$_4$ solutions in this class. We then specialise to the $\mathcal N=1$ vacua with U(1)$^2$ and SO(3) symmetry, obtain their new $D=11$ uplifts and bring out their internal geometries. Both of them admit a Kaluza--Klein-type description. For the U(1)$^2$ solution, this uses the global Hopf fibration of $S^7$ over $\mathbb{CP}^3$. One U(1) acts on the fibre, while the other generates the only continuous isometry of the deformed $\mathbb{CP}^3$ base. For the SO(3) solution, the geometry takes a local non-abelian Kaluza--Klein form, with SO(3) acting on the fibres and no residual continuous symmetry on the four-dimensional base.

</details>


### [138] [A proof of Nakamura's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19558)
*Arsen R. Melikyan,Aleksandr Pinzul*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文借助前期工作（arXiv:2610.27420）中获得的普法夫式解（Pfaffian solutions），证明了中村（Nakamura）关于二维 Toda 模型（2D Toda model）与恩斯特方程（Ernst equation）解之间关系的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中村（Nakamura）曾提出关于二维 Toda 模型（2D Toda model）与恩斯特方程（Ernst equation）解之间关系的猜想，该猜想此前尚缺乏证明，本文旨在予以严格论证。

Method: 证明以作者前期工作（arXiv:2610.27420）中所得到的普法夫式解（Pfaffian solutions）为主要工具，据此建立两个方程解之间的对应关系。

Result: 中村（Nakamura）的猜想得到证明，即二维 Toda 模型（2D Toda model）与恩斯特方程（Ernst equation）的解之间所断言的关系成立。

Conclusion: 普法夫式解（Pfaffian solutions）为联系二维 Toda 模型（2D Toda model）与恩斯特方程（Ernst equation）提供了有效途径，所证结论确认了中村猜想的正确性。

Abstract: We prove Nakamura's conjecture on the relation between the 2D Toda model and solutions of the Ernst equation, using the Pfaffian solutions obtained in our previous work arXiv:2610.27420.

</details>


### [139] [Holographic probes and Krylov complexity on asymptotically Lifshitz rotating black branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33599)
*Moises Bravo-Gaete,Mariano Chernicoff,Benjamin Mellado,Julio Oliva,Maria Guadalupe Olmos Andrade*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在五维渐近Lifshitz旋转黑洞背景下，计算全息静态夸克-反夸克势与Krylov复杂度增长率，揭示旋转与非相对论标度对探针观测量（probe observables）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该背景由爱因斯坦引力（Einstein gravity）耦合伸缩子（dilaton）、两个轴子标量（axionic scalars）、Maxwell双重态及两个矢量双重态（经宇称奇Chern-Simons型组合引入）构成，为研究非相对论全息（non-relativistic holography）中旋转对探针的印记提供了具体自下而上（bottom-up）模型。

Method: 采用两类全息探针：其一按标准方案，将边界静态夸克-反夸克势（static quark-antiquark potential）对应为端点锚定于边界的弦构型（string configuration）的在壳作用量（on-shell action）；其二依全息方案，将Krylov复杂度增长率对应为落入红外（IR）的大质量粒子探针的动量。

Result: 屏蔽长度（screening length）在固定温度提高角动量、以及固定角动量提高温度时均减小，分别归因于离心效应与热涨落增强导致的关联削弱；在给定时刻，复杂度随动力学指数（dynamical exponent）z增大而增长更快。

Conclusion: 旋转与Lifshitz标度在非相对论全息框架下对弦与粒子探针的观测量产生可辨识的印记，验证该几何作为研究此类效应的具体平台的适用性。

Abstract: We study two types of holographic probes on a five-dimensional, asymptotically Lifshitz, rotating black hole. This geometry solves the field equations of Einstein gravity coupled to a dilaton, two axionic scalars, and a Maxwell doublet, supplemented by two vector doublets that enter the action through a parity-odd, Chern-Simons-like combination, as recently found in \cite{Bravo-Gaete:2026bdq}. This spacetime provides a concrete bottom-up arena to explore the imprints of rotation on probes in non-relativistic holography. First, we compute the holographic static quark-antiquark potential following the standard prescription that relates this boundary observable to the on shell-action of a string configuration whose endpoints are anchored at the boundary. We find that the screening length decreases both when the angular momentum is increased at fixed temperature and when the temperature is increased at fixed angular momentum. The former reflects centrifugal effects on the $q\bar{q}$ pair, whereas the latter stems from enhanced thermal fluctuations, which weaken correlations at a given separation. Second, we compute the growth rate of the Krylov complexity, using the holographic prescription that relates this observable to the momentum of a massive particle probe falling into the IR. In particular, we find that, at a given instant, the complexity grows faster as the dynamical exponent $z$ is increased.

</details>


### [140] [The Dirac equation in arbitrary coordinate systems and curved spacetimes - An orthonormal frames approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33620)
*Andrew DeBenedictis,Saša Ilijić*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 这是一篇教学性文章，系统讲解任意坐标系下的狄拉克方程（Dirac equation），强调旋量场（spinor field）需要引入联络（connection），并给出计算狄拉克算子（Dirac operator）的实用步骤，涵盖弯曲时空与引力最小耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非笛卡尔坐标系及弯曲时空（curved spacetime）中处理旋量场时，必须引入联络，而入门教材通常只讨论平直时空恒定伽马矩阵（gamma matrices）情形，读者缺乏可直接照做的计算指南，故本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用简洁、务实的教学路线而非冗长推导，允许任意坐标系和位置相关的正交归一伽马矩阵，说明引力最小耦合（minimal coupling）的直接推广，并以球对称、柱对称及克尔时空（Kerr spacetime）为例演示计算。

Result: 给出了在一般设定下计算狄拉克算子的具体步骤，构建了球对称与柱对称（含或不含引力）以及克尔时空中的狄拉克方程，并简要评述了不同四元标架（tetrad）与正交归一伽马矩阵的优缺点。

Conclusion: 文章强调正交归一标架（orthonormal frame）的使用，因其对应多数场论读者熟悉的坐标系及实际测量所在的物理参考系；目标是使具备入门水平基础的读者能迅速具备独立计算能力。

Abstract: In this pedagogical article we discuss the Dirac equation in arbitrary coordinate systems. We explain the need for connections when dealing with spinor fields and present the practical steps needed to compute the Dirac operator in a general setting, allowing for arbitrary coordinate systems and position-dependent orthonormal gamma matrices, both of which can simplify computations in certain scenarios. The extension to include minimally coupled gravity is straightforward. Examples are provided, including constructing the Dirac equation in spherical and cylindrical symmetry (with or without gravity) and the Kerr spacetime. We also briefly comment on the advantages and disadvantages of various tetrads and orthonormal gamma matrices. This manuscript should be accessible to readers who have studied the Dirac equation at an introductory level (e.g., in Cartesian coordinates in flat spacetime with constant gamma matrices). The emphasis is on the use of orthonormal frames, since these correspond to the coordinate systems most familiar to readers when dealing with most field theories, and represent the physical frames where measurements are performed. The aim is to get the reader quickly to the point of being able to do calculations, so the presentation favors a concise, pragmatic approach over extended derivations.

</details>


### [141] [Late-time OTOCs in Toda conformal field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33744)
*Giwan Kim,Masahito Yamazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究二维 A_{N-1} Toda 共形场论中半退化主场的晚期乱序关联函数（OTOC），修正了 Liouville 情形此前的常数极限结论，并揭示 N=3 时的纯编织相位及 N≥4 时的交叉对称性破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对 Liouville 理论（N=2）OTOC 晚期行为的报道为常数极限，且高阶 Toda 理论中半退化主场的晚期 OTOC 结构尚不清楚，需要澄清连续谱边缘与离散极点对晚期衰减的贡献。

Method: 计算二维 A_{N-1} Toda 共形场论（conformal field theory）中半退化主场（semi-degenerate primary fields）的晚期 OTOC；对 N=3 采用横向围道挤压（transverse contour pinch）将谱积分约化为一维，并对边缘块作数值检验。

Result: N=2 时连续谱边缘主导给出 t^{-3/2} 衰减，修正此前的常数极限；N=3 时约化展开若完备，则连续相中受调控的边缘表现为离散态，OTOC 趋于纯编织相位（braiding phase），且边缘块在电荷中性的 Weyl 标架下为自由场关联函数，与交叉对称性（crossing symmetry）一致；两种情形下主导离散留数均给出常数晚期极限。N≥4 时同一维约化违反正对角展开最低中间权重的交叉界。

Conclusion: N=2、3 的相图不能无附加贡献地推广到更高秩；高阶情形需在约化连续边缘之下或正对角展开之外引入额外贡献。

Abstract: We study late-time out-of-time-order correlators (OTOCs) of semi-degenerate primary fields in two-dimensional $A_{N-1}$ Toda conformal field theory. In Liouville theory ($N=2$), we find that the OTOC decays as $t^{-3/2}$ when the continuum edge dominates, correcting a previously reported constant limit. For $N=3$, a transverse contour pinch reduces the spectral integral to one dimension. Assuming that the reduced expansion is complete, in the continuous phase the regulated edge behaves as a discrete state, and the OTOC approaches a pure braiding phase. As we check numerically, the edge block is a free-field correlator in a charge-neutral Weyl frame; this explains the pure phase and is consistent with crossing symmetry. For both $N=2$ and $N=3$, a dominant discrete residue gives a constant late-time limit. For $N\geq4$, the same one-dimensional reduction violates a crossing bound on the lowest intermediate weight of a positive diagonal expansion. Hence the $N=2,3$ phase diagram does not extend to higher rank without additional contributions below the reduced continuum edge or outside a positive diagonal expansion.

</details>


### [142] [Defect Correlators from Supergravity: Finding the Missing Contact Terms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34082)
*Qi Chen,Xinan Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文解决了含缺陷时利用超引力（supergravity）计算全息关联函数（holographic correlators）的不一致问题：先前直接超引力计算得到的缺陷两点函数与自举（bootstrap）结果相差一组接触项（contact contributions）。作者指出该差异源于体区非线性 Kaluza-Klein 场重定义（field redefinitions）与混合体–缺陷顶点（mixed bulk–defect vertices）中的离壳项（off-shell terms）两种效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含缺陷情形下，直接超引力计算得到的缺陷两点函数与自举结果之间存在一组接触项差异，构成一个待解释的疑难（puzzle），本文旨在厘清该差异的来源。

Method: 通过分析两种机制来解释差异：一是体区非线性 Kaluza-Klein 场重定义在一致地应用于局域源（localized sources）时诱导出额外的二次耦合（quadratic couplings）；二是混合体–缺陷顶点中的离壳项可使内部缺陷传播子（internal defect propagators）收缩。

Result: 在 4 维 $\mathcal{N}=4$ 超 Yang-Mills 理论（SYM）的两个具体例子中验证了这两种机制能够解决该疑难，即分别考虑存在 $\frac{1}{2}$-BPS Wilson 线（Wilson line）与 $\mathbb{RP}^4$ 上的两点关联函数。

Conclusion: 非线性 Kaluza-Klein 场重定义与混合体–缺陷顶点离壳项这两类效应共同解释了此前超引力计算与自举结果之间的接触项差异，从而化解了含缺陷全息关联函数计算中的疑难。

Abstract: We resolve a puzzle in the computation of holographic correlators using supergravity when defects are present. Previous direct supergravity calculations of defect two-point functions were found to differ from bootstrap results by a set of contact contributions. We show that the discrepancy arises from two effects: nonlinear Kaluza-Klein field redefinitions in the bulk, which induce additional quadratic couplings when applied consistently to localized sources, and off-shell terms in mixed bulk--defect vertices, which can collapse internal defect propagators. We demonstrate how these two mechanisms resolve the puzzle in two concrete examples in 4d $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM, where we consider two-point correlators in the presence of a $\frac{1}{2}$-BPS Wilson line and on $\mathbb{RP}^4$.

</details>


### [143] [Higher derivatives and ghosts through three centuries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34097)
*Andrei Smilga*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该综述梳理了含高阶导数（higher derivatives, HD）拉格朗日量的动力学系统研究史，指出“高阶导数必然破坏幺正性（unitarity）”是旧有误解，并讨论多类“良性鬼（benign ghosts）”系统；同时指出4维高阶导数引力及6维超对称高阶导数规范理论中鬼的性质尚未定论或并非良性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清20世纪下半叶广泛存在的误解——认为高阶导数必然导致幺正性破坏，并系统整理支持“良性鬼”存在的研究脉络。

Method: 以历史回顾与实例综述为主：从1744年Euler的工作、Ostrogradsky（1850）与Pais–Uhlenbeck（1950）出发，评述若干（量子）力学系统与2维场论例子，并讨论4维高阶导数引力与超对称高阶导数理论。

Result: 存在多类高阶导数但幺正性不被破坏的良性鬼系统；4维高阶导数引力因缺乏非微扰哈密顿量而无法定论，但经典层面可论证鬼是良性的（到达奇点需无限长时间，宇宙监督猜想（cosmic censorship conjecture）意味着奇点被视界包围）；共形超引力（conformal supergravity）中鬼可能良性，而6维超对称高阶导数规范理论虽无量纲耦合、可重整化却非幺正。

Conclusion: 高阶导数与幺正性破坏之间不存在必然联系；鬼是否良性需逐例判断，量子引力层面仍悬而未决，而6维超对称高阶导数规范理论提供了可重整化但非幺正的反例。

Abstract: We outline the history of the research of dynamic systems including higher derivatives in the Lagrangian, The story starts in 1744 when Euler published a book where the Euler--Lagrange differential equations for a HD system were first written. The next important landmarks were the papers of Ostrogradsky (1850) and of Pais and Uhlenbeck (1950). Many articles written in the second half of the last century were influenced by a misconception that was widespread at the time --- it was believed that higher derivatives necessarily bring about a violation of unitarity.
  We understand today that this is not the case and there are many systems with benign ghosts where HD are present but unitarity is not violated. In this review, we present and discuss several examples of such (quantum) mechanical systems and 2D field theories. Then we will briefly discuss higher-derivative gravity theories in 4 dimensions. In that case, the question whether the ghosts there are benign or not has a special status due to the absence of a nonperturbative Hamiltonian and does not have a definite answer yet. However, if one restricts oneself to classical theory, one can argue that the ghosts {\it are} benign (benign classical ghostly system is a system that needs an infinite time to reach a singularity, and it seems to the case both in ordinary and in HD gravity: the cosmic censorship conjecture implies that the singularities are always surrounded by horizons, and it takes an infinite time on the clocks of a distant observer to reach a horizon).
  Finally, I discuss supersymmetric HD theories: conformal supergravity, where the ghosts are also probably benign, and then an interesting supersymmetric HD gauge theory in 6 dimensions. It involves a dimensionless coupling constant, is renormalizable and rather beautiful. Unfortunately, it is not unitary --- ghosts are not benign there.

</details>


### [144] [Reconstructing massive quivers on a defect circle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34129)
*Ting-Kai Chen,Jin Chen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文利用缺陷圆上的截断共形空间方法（TCSA），研究最小模型 $\mathcal M_{k+1,k+2}$ 的大质量 $\phi_{2,1}$-形变中的扭结（kink）与粒子谱，并借助非可逆对称性（non-invertible symmetries）对应的箭图（quiver diagram）重构 $k=3,4,5$ 的谱结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量 $\phi_{2,1}$-形变保持一组非可逆对称性，其对真空与扭结、粒子的多重态组织具有约束作用；但如何在有限体积下从谱数据直接检验并重构这些箭图结构，尚需系统方法。

Method: 在缺陷圆上采用有限体积的截断哈密顿量方法，作用于缺陷希尔伯特空间 $\mathcal H_{X_i}$（$X_i\in\mathcal C_k$）；先建立缺陷圆上分数动量态与标记真空对的物理扭结/粒子态之间的关系，再利用局域场形状因子（form factors）独立测量两基之间的平方振幅，并结合缺陷 TCSA 谱。

Result: 在不预先假设箭图的前提下，重构了 $k=3,4,5$ 的箭图；对 $k=4$ 重构出有序相（ordered phase）与无序相（disordered phase）的不同箭图；对 $k=5$ 识别出轻多重态（light multiplet）与重多重态（heavy multiplet）的箭图。

Conclusion: 缺陷 TCSA 结合形状因子测量可自洽地重构由非可逆对称性自发破缺所组织的扭结与粒子箭图，为 $\mathcal C_k\simeq{\rm SO}(3)_k$ 融合范畴（fusion category）所刻画的谱结构提供定量验证。

Abstract: We study the kink and particle spectra for the massive $φ_{2,1}$-deformations of minimal models $\mathcal M_{k+1,k+2}$ via truncated conformal space approach (TCSA) on a defect circle. The massive $φ_{2,1}$-deformation preserves a set of non-invertible symmetries, furnishing a fusion category $\mathcal C_k\simeq {\rm SO}(3)_k$. These non-invertible symmetries are spontaneously broken, and thus label the vacua and organize the kinks and particles into their multiplets in terms of quiver diagrams. We employ a finite-volume truncated Hamiltonian approach on defect Hilbert spaces, $\mathcal H_{X_i}$ for $X_i\in\mathcal C_k$, to study these quivers in the IR theories. The symmetry defect can then close an individual kink on a circle. We first establish the relation between states of definite fractional momentum on the defect circle and physical kink and particle states labelled by their vacuum pairs. Using form factors of local fields, we independently measure the squared amplitudes between the states of the two bases in defect TCSA. Combining these measurements with defect TCSA spectra, we reconstruct the quiver diagrams for $k=3,4,5$ without assuming them in advance. In particular, for $k=4$ we reconstruct the distinct quivers of the ordered and disordered phases. For $k=5$, we identify the quivers of both the light and heavy multiplets.

</details>


### [145] [Robin--Hadamard states for unstable field in half-Minkowski spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34369)
*Bruno S. Felipe,João P. M. Pitelli,Claudio Dappiaggi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维半闵可夫斯基时空（half-Minkowski spacetime）中带吸引 Robin 边界条件的无质量实标量场量子化，指出经典谱完备性所需的不稳定模（unstable modes）同样是恢复完整量子场正则对易关系（CCR）的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典谱完备性要求存在不稳定模，但这类模在量子化中的地位及其对量子场一致性的作用尚不明确，因此需澄清其在恢复 CCR 与构造量子态中的必要性。

Method: 通过指定不稳定扇区（unstable sector）的 Cauchy 协方差（Cauchy covariances），构造一族保持总能量守恒的正准自由态（positive quasifree states），并推导所选稳定真空扇区两点函数（two-point function）的闭式位置空间表示。

Result: 该态族中正性与平稳性（stationarity）不相容，故所得态必然破坏背景的时间平移对称性；两点函数显示出直接与反射奇异性（direct and reflected singularities），并含依赖 Robin 参数的对数项。

Conclusion: 由于剩余不稳定贡献是光滑的，所构造态族中所有态共享相同奇异性结构，与 Robin–Hadamard 框架一致。

Abstract: We investigate the quantization of a real massless scalar field in four-dimensional half-Minkowski spacetime subject to an attractive Robin boundary condition. We show that the unstable modes, required for classical spectral completeness, are also essential to recover the canonical commutation relations (CCR) of the complete quantum field. By specifying the Cauchy covariances of the unstable sector, we construct a family of positive quasifree states compatible with the conservation of total energy. Within this family, positivity is incompatible with stationarity, so that the resulting states necessarily break the time-translation symmetry of the background. We also obtain a closed position-space representation of the two-point function for the chosen stable vacuum sector, including the contribution fixed by the CCR in the unstable sector. The resulting expression exhibits the direct and reflected singularities, including Robin-dependent logarithmic terms. Since the remaining unstable contribution is smooth, all states in the constructed family share the same singularity structure, consistent with the Robin--Hadamard framework.

</details>


### [146] [Quark-Loop Corrections and Auxiliary Field-Strength Mixing in First-Order QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34421)
*Hasnat Mohammad Nayeem*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在一阶形式（first-order formulation）的 Yang-Mills 理论中，胶子场强作为独立辅助场 F 存在；本文确定夸克如何修正这一结构，表明夸克发散由辅助场与场强的混合（mixing）完全吸收，重夸克积分掉后该混合以有限阈值系数存活，并在低能有效理论中将 F 与场强区分开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶 Yang-Mills 表述中唯一的胶子自相互作用是与动量无关的三点顶点，但夸克仅耦合规范场，其在单圈层次的作用如何改变该结构（尤其是纯 Yang-Mills 中不存在的 f(A)·f(A) 型发散）尚需澄清。研究动机在于厘清夸克扇区重整化是否可由已知的辅助场-场强混合完全描述。

Method: 采用一般协变规范下的背景场方法（background-field method），计算夸克圈对一阶拉氏量的发散，并分析辅助场 F 与场强 f(A) 的混合（F_0 = F + z f(A)）以及胶子场与 F 的重整化（rescaling）。随后在重夸克质量 M 较大时将其积分掉，考察有限阈值系数的匹配与解耦（decoupling）行为。

Result: 夸克扇区的重整化完全由纯 Yang-Mills 已知的混合 F_0 = F + z f(A)（夸克系数 z = -α_s T_F N_f/(6π \barε)）加上胶子场重整化、而 F 不重整化来实现，重整化后的辅助场最终跑向胶子场强。积分掉重夸克后同一混合以有限阈值系数 c = (α_s T_F/6π) ln(M²/μ²) 保留，其标度依赖恰为跑动（running）中的重夸克份额，不变耦合组合重现 α_s 解耦。阈值以下保留领头幂次修正时，F = f(A) + (gα_s T_F/15πM²)(D_α J_β - D_β J_α)，J 为轻夸克色流。

Conclusion: 该恒等式经与完整理论比对得到验证，并显示其将长程胶子交换（由 F 携带）与短程重夸克效应（由四夸克接触项（four-quark contact term）携带）分离。结果表明一阶形式下辅助场并非恒等于场强，其与场强的偏离编码了重夸克的有效贡献。

Abstract: In the first-order formulation of Yang-Mills theory the gluon field strength is an independent auxiliary field $F$, and the only gluon self-interaction is a momentum-independent cubic vertex. We determine how quarks, which couple only to the gauge field, act on this structure. Their divergences are multiples of $f(A)\cdot f(A)$, a term absent from the first-order Lagrangian that no rescaling can produce. Working with the background-field method in a general covariant gauge, we find that the quark sector is renormalized entirely by the mixing of the auxiliary field with the field strength known from pure Yang-Mills theory, $F_0=F+z\,f(A)$, with quark coefficient $z=-α_sT_FN_f/(6π\barε)$, together with a rescaling of the gluon field and no rescaling of $F$, so that the renormalized auxiliary field runs into the gluon field strength. When a heavy quark of mass $M$ is integrated out, the same mixing survives as a finite threshold coefficient $c=(α_sT_F/6π)\ln(M^2/μ^2)$, whose scale dependence is exactly the heavy-quark share of the running, and the invariant combination of the couplings reproduces the decoupling of $α_s$. Below the threshold, when the leading power correction is kept as a gauge-sector operator, the auxiliary field is no longer the field strength: $F=f(A)+(gα_sT_F/15πM^2)(D_αJ_β-D_βJ_α)$, with $J$ the light-quark colour current. We verify this identity against the full theory and show that it separates the long-distance gluon exchange, carried by $F$, from the short-distance heavy-quark effect, carried by a four-quark contact term.

</details>


### [147] [Black hole entropy from angular holonomies of Sine-Gordon soliton lattices in AdS$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34444)
*Marcela Cárdenas,Francisco Correa,Nicolás Salazar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文为 AdS$_3$ 引力构造实现正弦-戈尔登（Sine-Gordon）动力学的边界条件，并将周期孤子晶格（soliton lattice）的角向和乐（angular holonomy）与精确声学色散（acoustic dispersion）联系起来；在单手征绕数情形下得到趋近极值的 BTZ 黑洞，其极值熵修正系数反比于晶格声速，与一维量子流体的低温熵行为一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为 AdS$_3$ 引力寻找能实现 Sine-Gordon 动力学的边界条件，并建立孤子晶格声学性质与黑洞热力学（尤其极值附近熵修正）之间的对应关系。

Method: 方法上，针对固定绕数的周期孤子晶格，通过将 Bloch 动量（Bloch momentum）延拓穿过与晶格刚性平移相关的零频带边（band edge），把角向和乐与精确声学色散关联；并构造仅单一手征扇区携带绕数的边界条件族，使渐近区延伸至 BTZ 黑洞。

Result: 结果表明：在合适嵌入角下稳定真空具有正则 BTZ 几何；当绕数扇区的谱参数（spectral parameter）趋于相应带边时，黑洞趋近极值（extremality）。极值熵的首阶修正对以实验室时间定义的霍金温度（Hawking temperature）呈线性，其系数与晶格声速成反比。

Conclusion: 结论是孤子晶格声速的倒数依赖同样出现在一维量子流体的低温熵中，提示引力边界构造与一维量子流体热力学之间存在对应关系。

Abstract: We construct boundary conditions for AdS$_3$ gravity that realize Sine-Gordon dynamics. For a periodic soliton lattice at fixed winding, we relate the angular holonomy to the exact acoustic dispersion by continuing the Bloch momentum through the zero-frequency band edge associated with rigid translation of the lattice. For suitable embedding angles, the stable vacuum has a regular BTZ geometry. When only one chiral sector carries winding, we construct a family whose asymptotic region extends to a BTZ black hole. As the spectral parameter of the winding sector tends to the corresponding band edge, the black hole approaches extremality. The leading correction to the extremal entropy is linear in the Hawking temperature defined with respect to laboratory time, with a coefficient inversely proportional to the sound velocity of the lattice. The same inverse-velocity dependence appears in the low-temperature entropy of one-dimensional quantum fluids.

</details>


### [148] [Superconformal Index Identities for Dual Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34487)
*Min-xin Huang,Gao-fu Ren,Xin Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究三类四维对偶规范理论（dual four-dimensional gauge theories）的超共形指数（superconformal index）恒等式：证明 SO(2n+1) 与 USp(2n) 的 N=4 超杨-米尔斯理论（super-Yang–Mills）在任意阶（rank）下带味 Schur 指数（flavored Schur index）的 S-对偶（S-duality）等式；证明 Kutasov–Schwimmer 对偶的指数恒等式；并检验 Cho–Song 关于 Argyres–Douglas 理论的四族对偶猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三类对偶关系（S-对偶、Kutasov–Schwimmer 对偶、Cho–Song 猜想的四维 Argyres–Douglas 对偶）此前缺乏在指数层面的一般性严格验证；本文旨在以超共形指数恒等式为工具，在任意阶或一般参数下检验这些对偶预言。

Method: 对 N=4 理论借助 Rains 的对角迹变换（diagonal trace transformation）证明带味 Schur 指数等式；对 Kutasov–Schwimmer 对偶在独立椭圆模数（elliptic nomes）p、q 下、取紫外 R 荷分配 R(Q)=0，证明 SU(k+1) 与 SU(k-1)（两个味）之间的指数恒等式，并在 p=q^k 轨道上将其约化为普通超对称 QCD（supersymmetric QCD）的恒等式；对 Argyres–Douglas 对偶则逐族计算广义库仑指数（generalized Coulomb index）。

Result: 在 N=4 情形，于任意阶证明了 S-对偶预言的带味 Schur 指数相等；Kutasov–Schwimmer 对偶的指数恒等式在独立 p、q 下成立，并在 p=q^k 且特化表达式一般正则（generically regular）时约化为超对称 QCD 的恒等式；Argyres–Douglas 情形下，阶一 H_1 理论的两个完整带味指数相等，且对每一族在任意阶下两种描述的广义库仑指数均给出与 Buican–Jiang 猜想一致的乘积公式（product formula）。

Conclusion: 超共形指数恒等式为多类四维对偶（含 S-对偶、Kutasov–Schwimmer 对偶及 Argyres–Douglas 相关对偶）提供了严格且可推广的检验，并验证了 Buican–Jiang 的乘积公式猜想。

Abstract: We study superconformal index identities for three classes of dual four-dimensional gauge theories. For $\mathcal N=4$ super-Yang--Mills theory with gauge groups $SO(2n+1)$ and $USp(2n)$, we prove at every rank the equality of flavored Schur indices predicted by $S$-duality, using Rains's diagonal trace transformation. For Kutasov--Schwimmer duality with adjoint superpotential $\textrm{Tr} X^{k+1}$, we prove the index identity between $SU(k+1)$ and $SU(k-1)$ with two flavors at independent elliptic nomes $p,q$, in the ultraviolet assignment $R(Q)=0$. On the locus $p=q^k$, we reduce the identity to that of ordinary supersymmetric QCD whenever the specialized expressions are generically regular. Finally, we test the dualities conjectured by Cho and Song between quiver and single-group descriptions of four families of Argyres--Douglas theories. For the rank-one $H_1$ theory we prove equality of the two full flavored indices, and for each family at arbitrary rank we evaluate the generalized Coulomb index in both descriptions, obtaining the product formula conjectured by Buican and Jiang.

</details>


### [149] [Power-law tunneling in the Razavy hyperbolic double well: Heun connection formulas and their resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34588)
*Jie Ren,Xuanzhuang Wu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究对称 Razavy 双曲双阱中非微扰能级劈裂，将双合流 Heun 连接问题与精确 WKB、准精确可解性相联系，给出弱耦合下能级劈裂的完整解析结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称 Razavy 双曲双阱的能级劈裂涉及非微扰效应与特殊函数连接问题，其弱耦合行为及与精确 WKB、准精确可解性的关系尚未被系统刻画，尤其屏障作用的对数增长所导致的非整数幂依赖需要解析说明。

Method: 在弱耦合极限下，两阱对数式分离并趋于两个 Morse 系统；利用反射将双端谱条件分解为独立的偶、奇量子化条件，并对非整数衰减指数的 Morse 能级采用非规则块（irregular-block）连接公式。经典周期以完全椭圆积分（complete elliptic integrals）表示，并推导首个量子周期修正；在整数指数情形通过收敛到两个共振 Floquet 模的约化处理。

Result: 对每个可归一化 Morse 能级，得到领头劈裂及其完整伽马函数（gamma function）系数、两项解析涨落修正与领头对数三程（three-traversal）贡献；耦合的非整数幂源于屏障作用的对数增长。整数指数情形下，奇异固定阶展开被分解为两个局域谱分支，并重现已知的有限自旋隧穿（finite-spin tunneling）能隙。

Conclusion: 该工作将双合流 Heun 连接问题、精确 WKB 与准精确可解性统一于同一框架，给出弱耦合非微扰劈裂的解析表达式，并澄清整数指数下的奇异展开与有限自旋隧穿能隙的对应关系。

Abstract: We study nonperturbative level splitting in the symmetric Razavy hyperbolic double well, relating its double-confluent Heun connection problem to exact WKB and quasi-exact solvability. In the weak-coupling limit the wells separate logarithmically and approach two Morse systems. Reflection factorizes the two-ended spectral condition into separate even and odd quantization conditions. For each normalizable Morse level with noninteger decay exponent, the irregular-block connection formula yields the leading splitting with its full gamma-function coefficient, two analytic fluctuation corrections, and the leading logarithmic three-traversal contribution. The noninteger power of the coupling follows from the logarithmic growth of the barrier action. We express the classical periods in terms of complete elliptic integrals and derive the first quantum-period correction. At integer exponent, a convergent reduction onto the two resonant Floquet modes resolves the singular fixed-order expansion into two local spectral branches and reproduces the known finite-spin tunneling gap.

</details>


### [150] [The all-loop spectrum of GGK strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34619)
*Marius de Leeuw,Andrea Fontanella,Cameron Gibson,Juan Miguel Nieto García*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究AdS$_5\times$S$^5$中弦论非相对论极限（GGK理论）的非微扰谱，证明其玻色谱在光锥规范下由AdS$_2$中的自由标量场描述，并给出静态规范下含费米子的推广及量子能量修正为零的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在验证de Leeuw、Fontanella与Nieto-García（arXiv:2403.09563）关于GGK理论谱的猜想，并厘清其非微扰能谱结构。

Method: 采用光锥规范（light-cone gauge）分析多个真空上的玻色谱；另在静态规范（static gauge）下对任意经典弦真空并包含费米子（fermions）进行推广；计算经典能量的量子修正，采用正则化Hurwitz级数（regularised Hurwitz series）处理。

Result: 在所有圈阶上，玻色谱由AdS$_2$中的自由无质量与有质量标量场（free massive and massless scalar fields）描述，证实前述猜想；静态规范下得到相同结果；量子修正在采用正则化Hurwitz级数后消失。

Conclusion: 建立在任意GGK弦上的量子能谱仅由经典真空能量（classical vacuum energy）构成，即无额外量子激发贡献。

Abstract: We study the non-perturbative spectrum of the non-relativistic limit of string theory in AdS$_5\times$S$^5$, also known as GGK theory. In light-cone gauge, we show that the bosonic spectrum on a number of vacua is described at all-loops by free massive and massless scalar fields in AdS$_2$, confirming a conjecture by de Leeuw, Fontanella and Nieto-García, arXiv:2403.09563. In static gauge, we obtain the same result for any classical string vacuum, and including fermions. We compute the quantum correction to the classical energy, and show that it vanishes once we adopt the regularised Hurwitz series. We conclude that the quantum energy spectrum built upon any GGK string consists solely of its classical vacuum energy.

</details>


### [151] [Interfaces in Long Range CFT$_1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34705)
*Bastien Girault,Miguel F. Paulos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 AdS$_2$ 中单个自由标量场理论框架下研究界面（interface），通过边界部分区域添加相关算符构造 Neumann/Dirichlet 界面与长程相互作用界面，计算标度维度与 $g$-因子比，并建立四点界面关联函数的自举（bootstrap）问题以给出紧致束缚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 AdS$_2$ 中由边界局域相关算符诱导的界面，旨在刻画界面共形数据（标度维度、$g$-因子比）并探索将自举方法推广至界面关联函数的可行性，尤其是连接广义自由场与长程相互作用理论（如长程 Ising 模型）的界面情形。

Method: 从 AdS$_2$ 单个自由标量出发，先解析计算 Neumann/Dirichlet 界面的标度维度与 $g$-因子比，并给出任意界面关联函数的高效数值计算方案；随后在微扰论（perturbation theory）框架下研究广义自由场与长程相互作用理论之间的界面；最后为四点界面关联函数建立自举问题。

Result: 得到 Neumann/Dirichlet 界面的标度维度与 $g$-因子比，并在微扰论中确定长程相互作用界面（含长程 Ising）的多个物理量；利用四点界面关联函数的自举得到 $g$-因子比作为界面标度维度函数的紧致上下界（tight bounds）。

Conclusion: 界面关联函数可高效数值计算，且界面四点函数自举为约束界面共形数据提供了有效途径，可在缺少精确解时给出 $g$-因子比的严格界限。该框架将自由场界面、长程相互作用界面与自举方法统一起来。

Abstract: We consider interfaces in the theory of a single free scalar in AdS$_2$ defined by adding relevant operators along portions of the boundary. We begin with Neumann/Dirichlet interfaces, computing the associated scaling dimension and $g$-factor ratio; we also explain how arbitrary interface correlators can be computed numerically efficiently. Then we move on to interfaces between generalized free field and long-range interacting theories such as long range Ising, determining many quantities in perturbation theory. Finally we setup a bootstrap problem for four-point interface correlator. We use this to find tight bounds on the $g$-factor ratio as a function of the interface scaling dimension.

</details>


### [152] [Entanglement and HBAR entropy in a Lorentzian non-commutative black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34706)
*A. A. Araújo Filho,N. Heidari,P. H. M. Barros,V. B. Bezerra,A. R. Queiroz,A. F. Santos,Ali Övgün*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究洛伦兹非对易涂抹（Lorentzian non-commutative smearing）对黑洞外部纠缠与视界增亮加速辐射（horizon brightened acceleration radiation, HBAR）的修正。结果表明，涂抹参数Θ在表面引力、发射概率与熵产等量中引入√Θ与Θ阶修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨非对易几何中的洛伦兹涂抹效应对黑洞外部量子纠缠结构与HBAR热力学响应的具体影响，明确修正出现的阶次。

Method: 对无质量标量场，对内部伙伴模式求迹得到外部量子信道（quantum channel），计算对数负度（logarithmic negativity）、互信息（mutual information）与贝尔纠缠保真度（Bell entanglement fidelity）；并分析自由下落原子的发射/吸收细致平衡及随机原子注入下的模式热化与熵产。

Result: 固定质量与Killing频率时，增大涂抹会降低表面引力、提高可及关联并压低主导原子发射概率；视界位移始于√Θ阶，无穷远处热修正始于Θ阶。固定局部频率时红移在固定面半径处引入√Θ项，固定固有距离时该项抵消并恢复近视界的共同Rindler响应（Rindler response）。自由下落原子在主导近似下满足Hawking温度下的细致平衡；随机注入驱使模式以精确弛豫律热化且熵产非负。近平衡时得Ṡ_HBAR≃Ȧ_p/4（辐射能取自视界热量），若归于固定涂抹长度ℓ=4√(Θ/π)下的ADM质量损失则为Ṡ_HBAR≃Ȧ_p/[4(1−ℓ/r₊)]。

Conclusion: 外部源的質量依赖以√Θ阶进入单位面积损失的熵，表明非对易涂抹效应在黑洞纠缠与HBAR熵平衡关系中留下可辨识的阶次印记。

Abstract: We investigate how Lorentzian non-commutative smearing affects entanglement and horizon brightened acceleration radiation (HBAR) outside a black hole. For a massless scalar field, tracing over the interior partner gives the exterior quantum channel, its logarithmic negativity, mutual information, and Bell entanglement fidelity. At fixed mass and Killing frequency, increasing the smearing lowers the surface gravity, increases the accessible correlations, and suppresses the leading atomic emission probability. The horizon displacement begins at order $\sqrtΘ$, while the thermal correction at infinity begins at order $Θ$. At fixed local frequency, the redshift introduces a term of order $\sqrtΘ$ at fixed areal radius. Holding the proper distance fixed, it cancels this term and recovers the common Rindler response close to the horizon. For a freely falling atom, emission and absorption obey detailed balance at the Hawking temperature in the leading horizon approximation. Random atomic injection drives the selected mode from the vacuum to a thermal state with an exact relaxation law and nonnegative entropy production. Near equilibrium, we obtain $\dot S_{\rm HBAR}\simeq\dot A_p/4$ when the radiated energy is drawn from horizon heat, where $\dot A_p$ is the positive radiative area loss. When the radiation is assigned to ADM mass loss at fixed smearing length $\ell=4\sqrt{Θ/π}$, the result is $\dot S_{\rm HBAR}\simeq\dot A_p/[4(1-\ell/r_+)]$. The mass dependence of the exterior source thus enters the entropy per unit area lost at order $\sqrtΘ$.

</details>


### [153] [Rényi multi-entropy from Riemann surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34715)
*Kenya Tasuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在二维紧致玻色共形场论（compact-boson conformal field theory, CFT）中计算了真空三分 Rényi 多重熵（Rényi multi-entropy），并给出了与格点数值结果的一致性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重熵（multi-entropy）作为多体纠缠（multipartite entanglement）的度量，其在共形场论中的解析计算尚不充分，尤其缺乏对非相邻区间构型及整数 Rényi 指标的显式结果。

Method: 对任意整数 Rényi 指标 n≥2，在不交区间（disjoint intervals）的复制黎曼面（replica Riemann surface）上计算配分函数，将其化为 theta 函数之比给出的扭曲四点函数（twist four-point function）；另在 Fermat 曲线（Fermat curve）上独立计算配分函数以获得相邻三点系数。

Result: 在融合极限（fusion limit）下黎曼面退化为两条 Fermat 曲线，四点函数的留数（residue）复现三点系数的平方，符合算符乘积展开（operator product expansion, OPE）的要求；真实多重熵趋近其严格相邻值的两倍。CFT 预言与临界 XXZ、Ising 自旋链及谐振子链（harmonic chain）的格点计算一致；此外还给出一个幺正共形场论（unitary CFT）实例，其标准复制构造给出复数 Rényi 多重熵。

Conclusion: 结果表明多重熵的复制构造在二维共形场论中可精确求解并与格点结果吻合，同时揭示标准复制方法在特定幺正理论中可能产生非实数的多重熵，提示该构造存在适用性限制。

Abstract: We compute vacuum tripartite Rényi multi-entropy in a two-dimensional compact-boson conformal field theory (CFT). For every integer Rényi index $n\geq2$, we evaluate the partition function on the replica Riemann surface for disjoint intervals, obtaining the twist four-point function in terms of a ratio of theta functions. An independent calculation of the partition function on a Fermat curve yields the adjacent three-point coefficient. In the fusion limit, the Riemann surface for disjoint intervals degenerates into two Fermat curves, and the residue of the four-point function reproduces the squared three-point coefficient, as required by the operator product expansion. The genuine multi-entropy therefore approaches twice its strictly adjacent value. The CFT predictions agree with lattice calculations for the critical XXZ and Ising spin chains and a harmonic chain. We also exhibit a unitary CFT in which the canonical replica construction yields complex Rényi multi-entropy.

</details>


### [154] [Logarithmic scalar soft factorization from matching conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34791)
*Matías Briceño,Hernán A. González,Oriana Labrin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明无质量标量与有质量费米子通过Yukawa相互作用耦合时，其对数软因子化可由渐近场方程与空间无穷远处的匹配条件推出，并构造出满足对跖映射的守恒面积分，从而导出重现单圈标量发射对数软系数的Ward恒等式（含经典与量子两部分）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QED与引力中的对数软定理可理解为渐近对称性对应的守恒律，但无质量标量情形缺乏规范对称性来组织这一关系，故需寻找替代的推导框架。

Method: 从首个次领头对数衰减项构造守恒面积分，利用过去与未来零无穷远之间的对跖映射（antipodal map）及空间无穷远匹配条件；由粒子轨迹导出经典尾项，由渐近Dirac流得到硬荷（hard charge），并区分标量场特解（经典贡献）与齐次分量（量子贡献）。

Result: 所得Ward恒等式重现了单圈标量发射的对数软系数（sphere上的零模除外），并同时涵盖其经典部分与量子部分。

Conclusion: 即使没有规范对称性，无质量标量的对数软因子化仍可由渐近场方程与匹配条件组织为守恒律形式，表明该类软定理具有更普适的渐近结构基础。

Abstract: Logarithmic soft theorems in QED and gravity can be understood as conservation laws associated with asymptotic symmetries. In contrast, for a massless scalar, no gauge symmetry is available to organize this relation. Despite that, we show that the logarithmic soft factorization for a massless scalar coupled to massive fermions through a Yukawa interaction follows from the asymptotic field equations and matching conditions at spatial infinity. From the first subleading logarithmic fall-off, we construct a conserved surface integral whose boundary values satisfy the antipodal map between past and future null infinity. At future null infinity, the field equations relate this quantity to the $1/u$ tail of the scalar radiation, while at past null infinity, for scattering states without incoming scalar radiation, it is carried by a non-radiative logarithm of the incoming particles. We derive the classical tail from the particle trajectories and obtain the hard charge from the asymptotic Dirac current. The particular solution of the leading scalar field gives the classical contribution, while its homogeneous component gives the quantum contribution. The resulting Ward identity reproduces the logarithmic soft coefficient of the one-loop scalar emission (up to a zero mode on the sphere), including its classical and quantum parts.

</details>


### [155] [A Pole-Cut Formula for Holographic Extremal Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34824)
*Jyotirmoy Bhattacharya,Amiya Mishra,Aditya Sharma,Sourav Singha*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明在极端Reissner–Nordström黑膜（extremal Reissner–Nordström black brane）背景下，中性无质量标量的全息谱函数（holographic spectral function）可写成由准正规模式（quasi-normal modes, QNMs）构成的无穷乘积与一个分支割（branch cut）因子的干净分解形式，并给出其数值验证与时间域解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端黑洞背景下谱函数的极点结构与割贡献之间的关系尚缺乏统一的解析表述，尤其分支割数据（branch-cut data）在QNM表述中的地位及其与近视界AdS_2区域的联系需要澄清。

Method: 将热态全息关联函数的QNM乘积公式取零温极限，得到“极点-割公式（pole-cut formula）”；分支割贡献来源于QNMs在复频率平面负虚轴上的并合（coalescence）。并借助边界关联函数算子乘积展开（OPE）的一致性条件导出极端求和规则（sum-rules），进行数值检验。

Result: 谱数据由边界源函数的零点（即QNMs）与负虚轴上跨越分支割的相位不连续（phase discontinuity）共同构成；公式分解为QNM极点无穷乘积与割不连续因子的乘积。数值上验证了公式及OPE约束，且全息一致性要求QNM的渐近行为与相位不连续函数的渐近形式相关联。时间域中极点因子与割因子各自产生复时间奇点（complex-time singularities），其位置由边界与黑膜奇点间的光学距离（optical distance）决定。

Conclusion: 极点-割因子化（pole-cut factorization）结构被认为可推广到更一般的极端黑膜涨落，为极端全息谱函数提供统一的解析框架。

Abstract: We show that the holographic spectral function of a neutral massless scalar on the extremal Reissner-Nordstrom black brane in $AdS_5$ admits an elegant formula in terms of the quasi-normal modes (QNMs) and branch-cut data. In the extremal case, the spectral data comprises the zeros of the boundary source function, i.e. the QNMs, as well as its phase discontinuity across the branch cut on the negative imaginary axis of the complex frequency plane. This formula neatly factorizes into an infinite product over the QNM poles and a factor encoding the branch cut discontinuity. We derive this pole-cut formula as the zero-temperature limit of the QNM product formula for holographic thermal correlators, with the branch-cut contribution arising from the coalescence of the QNMs on the negative imaginary axis. We also clarify the role of the near-horizon $AdS_2$ region in this context. Consistency with the OPE of the boundary correlator provides extremal sum-rules involving both the QNMs as well as the phase discontinuity function. We also find that this holographic consistency forces the asymptotic form of the QNMs to be related to the asymptotics of the phase discontinuity function. We verify our formula and these OPE constraints numerically. We also demonstrate that, in the time domain, the pole and cut factors each produce complex-time singularities, whose location is determined by the optical distance between the boundary and the black-brane singularity. We expect the pole-cut factorization structure of spectral functions to hold for more general fluctuations of extremal black branes.

</details>


### [156] [Character expansion of the Itzykson-Zuber pair correlator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34829)
*Alexei Morozov,Hasib Sifat*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视 Harish-Chandra-Itzykson-Zuber（HCIZ）测度下的关联函数问题，目标是以特征展开（character expansion）形式重现双点关联子（pair correlator）的已知答案，即带有特定系数的 Schur 多项式之和，并借助 Cauchy 恒等式将无穷级数折叠为指数形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双点关联子的经典结果虽早已已知，但其特征展开中的系数结构尚未以投影算子形式清晰刻画；且相较于 Hermitian 积分，酉积分（unitary integrals）的特征展开方法发展更为困难，亟需推进以支持更一般的推广。

Method: 采用特征展开方法，将双点关联子写成 Schur 多项式（Schur polynomials）的求和，利用 Cauchy 恒等式（Cauchy identity）将无穷和折叠为指数形式；核心是将展开系数表示为由 Cherednik 算子（Cherednik operators）构造的投影算子（projectors）。

Result: 成功复现了双点关联子的特征展开答案，并将系数以 Cherednik 算子投影算子的形式显式表达；无穷求和经 Cauchy 恒等式化为指数形式。

Conclusion: 该工作发展了酉积分（相较 Hermitian 情形更困难）的特征展开方法，为后续推广至含非平凡测度（non-trivial measures）的任意关联子提供了可能。

Abstract: We revisit a long-standing problem of correlation functions with the Harish-Chandra-Itzykson-Zuber measure. The immediate goal is to reproduce the long-known answer for pair correlator in the form of character expansion - a sum over Schur polynomials with carefully adjusted coefficients. The infinite sum can be folded up into exponentials with the help of the Cauchy identity. What matters are the coefficients, which we express in terms of projectors, made from Cherednik operators. Development of character expansion methods for unitary integrals, which are much more difficult than the Hermitian ones, gives a hope for further generalizations - up to arbitrary correlators with non-trivial measures.

</details>


### [157] [Time-weighted energy correlators are infrared unsafe in gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34886)
*Siqing Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 能量关联函数因按能量加权而红外安全，但按到达时间加权会使探测器双线性算符（light-ray operator ∫du u T_{uu}）在规范理论中红外不安全；两环计算显示分离方向上出现与时间窗口对数成正比的发散，该对数源于能量相当、分别进入两个探测器的软部分子对，系数为角度的闭式函数，并在 N=4 SYM 中可由双胶子 Regge 项复现与电荷关联函数对数的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量关联函数（energy correlators）在规范理论中因每个探测器按部分子能量加权而保持红外安全（infrared safe），但若将权重改为到达时间，则红外安全性可能被破坏。作者据此研究时间加权能量探测器的关联行为，以刻画其红外结构与潜在的对数发散。

Method: 在弱耦合共形规范理论（conformal gauge theory）中，考虑标量算符（scalar operator）产生的两粒子态，计算两个分离方向上的时间加权能量探测器的关联函数（即光射线算符 light-ray operator ∫du u T_{uu}）到两环阶。

Result: 该关联函数在分离方向上出现正比于时间窗口对数的两环发散；对数并非来自单个软胶子，而来自能量可比、分别进入两个探测器的软部分子对（soft parton pair），其系数对任意物质内容是角度的闭式函数。在 N=4 超杨-米尔斯理论（super Yang-Mills）中，该系数可由已知四点函数的双胶子 Regge 项复现，并导出与电荷关联函数（charge correlator）中对数的简单关系。

Conclusion: 时间加权的能量关联函数在规范理论中红外不安全，其两环对数发散具有明确的软部分子对物理解释并可由角度闭式表达；在平面极限下，领头对数重求和为时间窗口的幂律，指数由 BFKL 截距（BFKL intercept）决定。

Abstract: Energy correlators are infrared safe because each detector weights a parton by its energy, but a weight in the arrival time makes them infrared unsafe in gauge theories. For the state created by a scalar operator that makes two partons at lowest order in a weakly coupled conformal gauge theory, we show that the correlator of two time-weighted energy detectors (the light-ray operators $\int\!du\,u\,T_{uu}$) at separated directions diverges at two loops like the logarithm of the time window. The logarithm does not come from a single soft gluon, but from a soft parton pair with comparable energies, one parton in each detector; its coefficient is a closed-form function of the angle for any matter content. In $N=4$ super Yang-Mills theory, we recover this coefficient from the two-gluon Regge term of the known four-point function and derive a simple relation to the logarithm in the charge correlator. In the planar theory the leading logarithms resum to a power of the window, set by the BFKL intercept.

</details>


### [158] [Massive Triangle in Cosmological Correlator beyond Conformal Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34892)
*Taushif Ahmed,Syed Mehedi Hasan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文给出了与暴胀子成对耦合的大质量标量场在一圈原初双谱（primordial bispectrum）中、任意外部动量下两项领头质量修正的闭合解析表达式，以均匀权重（uniform weight）三与四的多重多对数（multiple polylogarithms）表示，并经独立全圈计算（含盲测）验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共形耦合（conformal coupling）之外，任意动量下的一圈原初双谱质量修正长期缺乏闭合解析表达式，构成该计算的主要空白。

Method: 采用辅助能量修饰（auxiliary-energy dressings）使时间积分保持有理形式，从而以多重多对数（multiple polylogarithms）表达任意外部动量下的两项领头质量修正；并通过独立的全圈评估（full-loop evaluations），包括盲预测（blinded predictions），进行验证。

Result: 获得两项领头质量修正的闭合形式，其权重分别为三与四且权重均匀；尽管中间几何结构涉及椭圆（elliptic）情形，结果仍表现出意外的简洁性。同时确定了次领头的轻场奇异性（light-field singularity），并恢复了重质量有效理论（heavy-mass effective theory）。

Conclusion: 任意动量下的一圈原初双谱质量修正可写为闭合解析形式，并经独立全圈计算交叉验证；相关结果也为轻场奇异性与重质量有效理论极限提供了新的刻画。

Abstract: A massive scalar coupled in pairs to the inflaton generates a one-loop primordial bispectrum whose mass corrections at generic momenta have lacked closed analytic expressions beyond conformal coupling. We obtain two leading mass corrections at arbitrary external momenta in terms of multiple polylogarithms of uniform weights three and four. Auxiliary-energy dressings preserve rational time integrals, revealing unexpected simplicity despite elliptic intermediate geometry. Independent full-loop evaluations, including blinded predictions, validate the closed forms. We also determine a subleading light-field singularity and recover the heavy-mass effective theory.

</details>


### [159] [Vacuum correlation measures as CFT partition functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34922)
*Hewei Frederic Jia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文证明任意d维共形场论（CFT）中两个不相交球的整数阶真空夹心Rényi互信息（SRMI）可映射为特定拓扑流形上的配分函数，并据此给出n=2与n>2的不同行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立真空夹心Rényi互信息（SRMI）与CFT配分函数之间的精确对应，从而借助配分函数的结构性质刻画SRMI并给出von Neumann互信息的界。

Method: 将两不相交球的整数阶SRMI映射为拓扑为#^{n-1}(S^1 × S^{d-1})的流形上的CFT配分函数，其模数（moduli）由球构型与单球模流（single-ball modular flow）确定；n=2时约化为S^{d-1}上的热配分函数。

Result: n=2时SRMI等于S^{d-1}上的热配分函数，并给出von Neumann互信息的上界；n>2时由于配分函数的热点极限（hot-spot limit），SRMI在有限间隔处发散。显式CFT计算与密度矩阵计算结果吻合良好。

Conclusion: SRMI的配分函数映射为理解纠缠度量的拓扑与几何结构提供了统一框架，并揭示了高阶Rényi互信息在有限间隔处的发散特性。

Abstract: We show that in any $d$-dimensional CFT, the vacuum sandwiched Rényi mutual information (SRMI) of integer order $n$ between two disjoint balls maps to a CFT partition function on a manifold of topology $\#^{n-1}(S^1 \times S^{d-1})$, with moduli fixed by the ball configuration and single-ball modular flow. At $n=2$, it is given by the thermal partition function on $S^{d-1}$, upper-bounding the von Neumann mutual information. For $n>2$, SRMI diverges at finite separation due to a hot-spot limit of the partition function. Explicit CFT evaluations agree well with density matrix computations.

</details>


### [160] [An algebraic quantization of gravity from the null cone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34924)
*Venkatesa Chandrasekaran*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种四维洛伦兹时空上真空广义相对论（general relativity）的背景无关量子化（background independent quantization）方案，通过在零锥（null cone）上求解约束得到有限牛顿常数下的约化相空间（reduced phase space），并构造非微扰希尔伯特空间表示与收敛的洛伦兹路径积分（Lorentzian path integral），进而导出黑洞熵与半经典爱因斯坦方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真空广义相对论的背景无关量子化长期缺乏令人满意的非微扰框架，尤其在洛伦兹签名下如何定义收敛的路径积分与有限维希尔伯特空间尚不明确，本文旨在从第一性原理出发构建此类量子化并检验其物理推论。

Method: 从一阶引力（first order gravity）出发，在大块子区域的相交零锥上求解引力约束，获得有限 G_N 的约化相空间；将正则泊松括号（Poisson brackets）提升为对易关系，利用约化作用量的形变定义可观测量代数，并以正则对的高斯波函数（Gaussian wavefunctions）构造非微扰希尔伯特空间表示，通过零锥上态之间的振幅复合得到中间态求和绝对收敛的洛伦兹路径积分。

Result: 该代数生成 type I 因子；非微扰计算零锥上引力子（graviton）对产生与湮灭振幅得到有限闭式表达式，而按 G_N 展开则出现对数发散；利用微分同胚不变性（diffeomorphism invariance）证明给定最大尺寸的宏观因果钻石（causal diamond）具有有限维希尔伯特空间；由洛伦兹迹得到边缘陷获面（marginally trapped surface）的 Bekenstein–Hawking 熵，并计算大史瓦西黑洞的对数修正，在一圈（one loop）水平重现 Sen 用欧几里得方法得到的系数；最后导出一圈半经典爱因斯坦方程作为低能极限。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一个自洽的背景无关、非微扰的洛伦兹量子引力框架，其预言涵盖引力子振幅、因果钻石的有限维希尔伯特空间、黑洞熵及其对数修正，以及半经典爱因斯坦方程的低能极限，为量子引力与黑洞热力学的统一描述提供了新途径。

Abstract: We propose a background independent quantization of vacuum general relativity on four dimensional Lorentzian spacetimes. Starting from first order gravity, we solve the gravitational constraints on intersecting null cones of a bulk subregion and obtain the reduced phase space at finite $G_N$. We promote its canonical Poisson brackets to commutation relations and use deformations of the reduced action to define an algebra of observables. Equipped with this, we use Gaussian wavefunctions of canonical pairs to construct a nonperturbative Hilbert space representation, for which this algebra generates a type I factor. By composing amplitudes between states prepared on null cones, we then obtain a Lorentzian path integral in which each sum over intermediate states converges absolutely. As a check, we compute the amplitude for graviton pair creation and annihilation on a null cone nonperturbatively in $G_N$ and find a finite closed form expression, even though expanding first in $G_N$ yields a logarithmic divergence. We next use diffeomorphism invariance of the path integral to show that a macroscopic causal diamond with a given maximum size has a finite dimensional Hilbert space. From the Lorentzian trace we obtain the Bekenstein Hawking entropy for marginally trapped surfaces and calculate its logarithmic correction for large Schwarzschild black holes, recovering at one loop the coefficient Sen obtained through Euclidean methods. We end by deriving the semiclassical Einstein equations to one loop as the low energy limit of this theory.

</details>

### [161] [The regular polygon maximizes the first positive Steklov eigenvalue among simple polygons at fixed area](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33253)
*Senping Luo,Bingbing Xie*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文证明：对任意 $N\ge5$ 及边数不超过 $N$ 的简单平面多边形（simple planar polygon）$P$，有 $\sqrt{|P|}\,\sigma_1(P)\le\sqrt{|R_N|}\,\sigma_1(R_N)$，其中 $\sigma_1$ 为第一正 Steklov 特征值（first positive Steklov eigenvalue），$R_N$ 为正 $N$ 边形，等号仅在 $P$ 与 $R_N$ 相似时成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 Oudet 提出的固定面积开问题（fixed-area open problem）在 $N\ge5$ 的全部情形，即确定在给定面积与边数约束下使第一正 Steklov 特征值最大化的多边形形状。

Method: 依据摘要，论证采用对面积归一化量 $\sqrt{|P|}\,\sigma_1(P)$ 的极值比较，将一般简单多边形与正 $N$ 边形（regular $N$-gon）作上界对照，并刻画等号成立的相似性条件。

Result: 对 $N\ge5$ 与边数至多 $N$ 的所有简单平面多边形，$\sqrt{|P|}\,\sigma_1(P)$ 被正 $N$ 边形的相应值控制，且等号当且仅当 $P$ 与 $R_N$ 相似（similar）。

Conclusion: 正 $N$ 边形在 $N\ge5$ 时是固定面积下第一正 Steklov 特征值的最优多边形，从而完整回答了 Oudet 的固定面积开问题在该范围内的情形。

Abstract: For $N\ge5$ and every simple planar polygon $P$ with at most $N$ sides, $\sqrt{|P|}\,σ_1(P)\le\sqrt{|R_N|}\,σ_1(R_N)$, where $σ_1$ is the first positive Steklov eigenvalue and $R_N$ is a regular $N$-gon. Equality holds precisely for polygons similar to $R_N$. This answers Oudet's fixed-area open problem \cite{AIM} for all $N\ge5$.

</details>


<div id='hep-lat'></div>

### [162] [Unfreezing topology using out-of-equilibrium replica exchanges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34367)
*C. Bonanno,A. Bulgarelli,E. Cellini,R. Dionisio,A. Nada,D. Panfalone,D. Vadacchino,L. Verzichelli*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该文将边界条件并行退火（PTBC）中的瞬时构型交换推广为非平衡短程协议，并借助 Crooks 与 Jarzynski 涨落关系从理论上预测交换率与最优协议长度，在二维 CP^{N-1} 模型（N=21）中验证使固定统计精度下的计算成本降低约 40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑冻结（topological freezing）会严重降低格点模拟的采样效率，PTBC 通过在周期与开边界之间插值的副本间交换构型来缓解该问题，但标准 PTBC 的瞬时交换方式仍有改进空间。

Method: 将瞬时交换替换为短时非平衡协议：两个副本的缺陷耦合（defect couplings）逐步交换，同时更新缺陷附近场变量；基于 Crooks 和 Jarzynski 涨落关系推导交换率仅依赖非平衡轨迹上的平均功，并从理论上给出功随副本数、协议长度和缺陷尺寸的标度，从而预测交换率与最优协议长度。

Result: 在二维 CP^{N-1} 模型（N=21）中，理论预测的最优设置与实测一致；对所有测试的格点间距，固定统计精度下的总计算成本相较标准 PTBC 降低约 40% 以上，固定接受率下所需副本数减半。

Conclusion: 非平衡协议推广后的 PTBC 在保持统计精度的同时显著降低计算成本并减少副本需求，涨落关系为交换率与协议优化提供了可靠的理论指导。

Abstract: Parallel Tempering on Boundary Conditions (PTBC) mitigates topological freezing by exchanging configurations between replicas that interpolate between periodic and open boundaries on a small defect. We generalize PTBC by replacing the instantaneous swap with a short out-of-equilibrium protocol, in which the defect couplings of two replicas are exchanged gradually while the fields around the defect are updated. We show that the swap rate depends only on the average work spent along the out-of-equilibrium trajectory by virtue of Crooks' and Jarzynski's fluctuation relations. From the theoretical derivation of the scaling of the work as a function of the number of replicas, protocol length, and defect size, we predict the swap rate and the optimal protocol length. These predictions are tested in the $2d$ $\mathrm{CP}^{N-1}$ model with $N=21$. The optimal setup agrees with the measured one, the total computational cost at fixed statistical precision decreases by $\gtrsim40\%$ with respect to standard PTBC for all explored lattice spacings, and the required number of replicas at fixed acceptance is halved.

</details>
