### [163] [Floquet Decomposition: Floquet Multipliers and Time-Periodic Modes from Period-Sampled Snapshot Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34901)
*John Mathew Quinlan*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文将Schmid关于“按振荡周期采样可识别Floquet矩阵”的观察发展为Floquet分解（FD）与集合Floquet分解（EFD）两种方法，并给出其精确性条件、与Hankel-DMD的关系及适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态模式分解（dynamic mode decomposition, DMD）以单一常数线性算子拟合快照序列，但在参数激励流动或固定平面观察旋转结构等周期性变化情形下该算子不存在，稳定性须由Floquet乘子（Floquet multipliers）判定。

Method: FD对固定采样率数据按每周期一次进行子采样后施加DMD；EFD将每周期的快照堆叠为一列，得到一组乘子与相位分辨模态。二者使用与Hankel（延迟嵌入）DMD相同的数据矩阵，仅列间位移不同。

Result: 对线性时间周期（linear time-periodic）系统二者均精确：FD要求单时刻测量可确定状态，EFD要求一周期内测量可确定状态，故EFD可处理部分测量。每周期p个样本时，Hankel-DMD将每个Floquet模态表示为带边带的载波，即其乘子的p次方根梳状结构，而周期位移将其坍缩为乘子本身。

Conclusion: 方法经已知乘子验证；在非线性或含噪数据等超出假设的情形下，估计模值存在偏差，不能解释为衰减率。旋流稳定燃烧器上速度、标量与声压测量示例说明了周期、秩与数据缩放的选择。

Abstract: Dynamic mode decomposition (DMD) fits one constant linear operator to a sequence of snapshots. When the dynamics, or the view of them, vary periodically, as in a parametrically excited flow or a rotating structure seen in a fixed plane, no such operator exists, and stability is decided by the Floquet multipliers, the eigenvalues of the map over one period. In the paper that introduced DMD, Schmid observed that snapshots sampled at the period of an oscillatory flow would identify the Floquet matrix. We develop that observation into a method and analyze it. The Floquet decomposition (FD) applies DMD to fixed-rate data subsampled once per period; the ensemble Floquet decomposition (EFD) stacks the snapshots of each period into one column, giving one set of multipliers and phase-resolved modes. For linear time-periodic systems both are exact: FD when the measurement at one instant determines the state, EFD when the measurements over one period do---so EFD succeeds with partial measurements. EFD and Hankel (delay-embedded) DMD use the same data matrix with different shifts between columns. With $p$ samples per period, Hankel-DMD returns each Floquet mode as a carrier with sidebands, a comb of $p$-th roots of its multiplier, which the period shift collapses onto the multiplier. The methods are verified against known multipliers; outside their hypotheses, on nonlinear or noisy data, the estimated moduli are biased and are not decay rates. Measurements of velocity, scalars, and acoustic pressure in a swirl-stabilized combustor illustrate the choice of period, rank, and data scaling.

</details>


### [164] [Anomalous scaling of coalescence in a Hele-Shaw cell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33348)
*Luke Neville,Rodolfo Brandão,Marco A. Fontelos,Jens Eggers*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文研究狭窄平板间（Hele-Shaw 单元）两液滴的合并过程，结合数值模拟与匹配渐近分析（matched asymptotic analysis），发现早期聚并动力学具自相似性（self-similarity），液滴间接触区域尺寸按 $t^{3/13}$ 增长，且其间夹带一个复杂的小气泡结构，预测与模拟及最新实验数据一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注 Hele-Shaw 单元中受表面张力（surface tension）驱动的两液滴合并问题，尤其是初始重连（reconnection）之后的早期阶段，该阶段此前缺乏清晰的理论刻画。

Method: 求解二维 Hele-Shaw 方程，将数值模拟与理论分析相结合，并采用匹配渐近分析（matched asymptotic analysis）推导早期聚并的自相似标度律。

Result: 早期聚并呈现自相似动力学，接触区域尺寸按 $t^{3/13}$ 的幂律增长；分析揭示液滴靠拢时其间隙中夹带一个复杂的小气泡几何结构。理论预测与模拟完全吻合，并与最新实验数据一致。

Conclusion: Hele-Shaw 单元中液滴合并的早期动力学可由自相似理论与 $t^{3/13}$ 标度律统一描述，夹带气泡是其几何本质组成部分，结果为相关实验提供了定量理论支持。

Abstract: We investigate the merging of two drops between two narrowly spaced plates, a so-called Hele-Shaw cell. For this, we solve the Hele-Shaw equations in two dimensions, driven by surface tension, focusing on early times after initial reconnection. Combining numerical simulations with theory, we show that the coalescence dynamics are self-similar at early times, with the size of the contact region between the drops growing as $t^{3/13}$. We find this power law using a matched asymptotic analysis, which reveals a complex geometry consisting of a small bubble that gets entrained between the drops as they come together. These predictions match perfectly to our simulations, and agree with the most recent experimental data.

</details>


### [165] [Energy cascade spectrum in wave turbulence of a two-dimensional quantum fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33944)
*Ying Zhu,Jason Laurie,Giorgio Krstulovic,Sergey Nazarenko*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文针对二维 Gross-Pitaevskii 模型中直接能量级联的标度预测与热平衡态重合（即退化）问题，导出了带对数修正的波湍流谱，并给出指数 1/3 与谱前因子估计，模拟结果支持该谱形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维 Bose 气体的朴素量纲分析所给出的直接能量级联幂律预测与热平衡谱重合，不携带能量通量，无法描述真实的非平衡级联，因此需要解决这一退化（degeneracy）问题。

Method: 对波动力学碰撞积分（wave-kinetic collision integral）作系统渐近展开，确定对数修正的指数与截断尺度；通过对驱动尺度处边缘红外发散（marginal infrared divergence）的正则化，估计谱前因子；并用 Gross-Pitaevskii 方程与波动力学方程的数值模拟进行验证。

Result: 对数修正指数确定为 1/3，修正的上截断由小尺度耗散尺度设定；所得谱支撑朝小尺度的正的、常值的能量通量，模拟结果与预测谱形一致。在有限谱区间内，修正导致表观幂律比量纲预测更陡。

Conclusion: 该工作为幂律预测与热力学平衡退化的情形提供了构造波湍流级联谱的系统框架，并为二维 Bose 气体实验中观测到的陡谱提供了可能解释。

Abstract: In the two-dimensional (2D) Gross-Pitaevskii model for a Bose gas, the naive dimensional non-equilibrium wave-turbulence power-law prediction for the direct energy cascade coincides with thermal equilibrium and therefore carries no energy flux. We resolve this degeneracy by deriving a logarithmically corrected spectrum that supports a positive, constant energy flux toward small scales. A systematic asymptotic expansion of the wave-kinetic collision integral determines the logarithmic exponent to be 1/3, while the small-scale dissipation scale sets the upper cutoff in the logarithmic correction. Regularization of a marginal infrared divergence at the forcing scale yields an estimate of the spectral prefactor for asymptotically well-separated forcing and dissipation scales. Simulations of the Gross-Pitaevskii and wave-kinetic equations support the predicted spectral shape. Over finite spectral ranges, the correction produces apparent power laws steeper than the dimensional prediction, offering a possible explanation for the steep spectra observed in 2D Bose gas experiments. Our approach provides a systematic framework for constructing wave turbulent cascade spectra when the power-law prediction is degenerate with thermodynamic equilibrium.

</details>
