### [166] [Regular Additive Scalar Invariants of ${\mathcal N}=1$ Hyperbolic Spin Supertriangles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23209)
*Ilia Gaiur,Robert Penner*

Main category: math.GT

TL;DR: 该文对非退化双曲 ${\mathcal N}=1$ 自旋超三角形（spin supertriangles）上、在测地细分下可加的规则循环标量不变量（regular cyclic scalar invariants）进行分类，证明其空间超维数为 $3|1$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在双曲超几何（hyperbolic supergeometry）框架下刻画超三角形的可加标量不变量结构，并借助共同首项规范（common-first gauge）给出的完备模作为分类工具。

Method: 固定自旋提升（spin lift）后，采用此前工作引入并在此进一步发展的共同首项规范，得到此类超三角形的完备 $3|4$ 模（modulus）；在此基础上分析可加标量不变量空间的分次结构。

Result: 可加标量不变量空间超维数为 $3|1$：零次为普通双曲面积（hyperbolic area），二次有两个偶生成元，三次有一个奇生成元；一次与四次部分为零。一个二次生成元是体面积的费米型一阶变分（fermionic first variation），等价于从普通体角亏到超角亏（super angle defect）的幂零修正；三次生成元在自旋提升反向下变号。

Conclusion: 由于只有三次生成元在自旋提升反向下变号、其余生成元不变，普通超三角形仅携带三个偶不变量；该结果给出可加标量不变量的完整分类。

Abstract: We classify regular cyclic scalar invariants of non-degenerate hyperbolic ${\mathcal N}=1$ spin supertriangles which are additive under geodesic subdivision. After fixing the spin lift, the common-first gauge (introduced in earlier work by the second-named author and further developed here) gives a complete $3|4$ modulus of such supertriangles.. The space of additive scalar invariants is proved to have superdimension $3|1$: ordinary hyperbolic area in degree zero, two even generators in degree two, and one odd generator in degree three; the degree-one and degree-four sectors vanish. One degree-two generator is the fermionic first variation of body area, equivalently the nilpotent correction from ordinary body angle defect to super angle defect. The degree-three generator changes sign under reversal of the spin lift, while the other generators are fixed, so ordinary supertriangles carry only three even invariants.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Exact Lyapunov spectrum and localization length of the multichannel disordered Schrödinger equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34455)
*Aurélien Grabsch*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文精确计算了有限宽度导线中多通道薛定谔方程（multichannel Schrödinger equation）在幺正态对称类（unitary symmetry class）高斯白噪声势下、任意能量 E 与无序强度 T 对应的 Lyapunov 指数集合，并揭示了局域化长度与态密度之间的直接关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 DMPK 方法在大能量区预测 Lyapunov 指数等间距分布，但其在任意能量下的有效性尚待检验，本文旨在给出任意 E、T 下的精确结果。

Method: 将问题归结为修正 Riccati 矩阵（modified Riccati matrix）的矩阵随机微分方程（matrix stochastic differential equation）进行分析，从而获得 Lyapunov 指数的精确解。

Result: 大能量区复现 Lyapunov 指数等间距的 DMPK 结论；但在任意能量下该性质失效，Lyapunov 谱更为丰富；并发现局域化长度（最小 Lyapunov 指数之倒数）与态密度（density of states）存在直接联系。

Conclusion: 多通道无序导线的 Lyapunov 谱在一般能量下并不遵循 DMPK 的等间距图像，局域化长度与态密度之间存在此前未被揭示的直接关系。

Abstract: We consider the multichannel Schrödinger equation, describing the evolution of $N$ transverse modes in a finite width wire, in the presence of a Gaussian white noise potential in the unitary symmetry class. For arbitrary energy $E$ and disorder strength $T$, we compute exactly the set of Lyapunov exponents $\{ γ_n(E,T) \}_{1 \leq n \leq N}$ which define the decay rates of the wave functions. In the large energy regime, we recover that the Lyapunov exponents are equally spaced, as predicted by the so-called DMPK approach. However we show that this property breaks down at arbitrary energy, giving rise to a richer Lyapunov spectrum. We also uncover a direct relation between the localization length, given by the inverse of the smallest Lyapunov exponent, and the density of states. Our derivation relies on the analysis of matrix stochastic differential equation for a modified Riccati matrix.

</details>


### [168] [From Spin Glass Theory to Optimization in High Dimensions and Modern Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34713)
*Riccardo Zecchina,Gianmarco Perrupato*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本章综述 Giorgio Parisi 关于自旋玻璃（spin glasses）与副本对称破缺（replica symmetry breaking, RSB）的工作如何影响组合优化（combinatorial optimization）与人工神经网络（artificial neural networks）研究，并以解空间几何、计算难度与学习的关系为主线串联相关概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在致敬 Parisi 的工作，并梳理自旋玻璃理论与 RSB 思想在组合优化和神经网络领域的传播与影响，厘清约束优化、推断与自旋玻璃理论概念之间的关联。

Method: 采用综述性回顾方式：先以匹配问题（matching）和旅行商问题（traveling salesman problem）引入副本方法（replica method）与空腔方法（cavity method），再讨论随机可满足性（random satisfiability）及调查传播（survey propagation）的发展，最后考察其在神经网络中的应用。

Result: 梳理出解空间几何、计算硬度（computational hardness）与学习之间的关联，并特别强调高局部熵的稀有区域（rare regions of high local entropy）及其在算法可达性（algorithmic accessibility）中的作用；以过参数化模型（overparameterized models）和非对称循环网络（asymmetric recurrent networks）为例说明上述联系。

Conclusion: 自旋玻璃理论中的 RSB 等概念可将约束优化与推断联系起来，为理解组合优化与神经网络中的计算现象提供统一视角。

Abstract: This chapter pays tribute to Giorgio Parisi by reviewing the influence of his work on spin glasses and replica symmetry breaking (RSB) on combinatorial optimization and artificial neural networks. We first introduce replica and cavity methods through the matching and traveling salesman problems, before discussing random satisfiability and the development of survey propagation. We then examine applications to neural networks, emphasizing the connections between the geometry of solution spaces, computational hardness, and learning. Particular attention is given to rare regions of high local entropy and their role in algorithmic accessibility. Examples involving overparameterized models and asymmetric recurrent networks illustrate how these ideas connect constrained optimization and inference with concepts developed in spin-glass theory.

</details>


### [169] [Magnons in solids with correlated atomic and nanoscale disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34926)
*Alex Moorhouse,Joseph Barker,Michele Simoncelli*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 论文提出一套理论与计算框架，用于在量子精度下模拟原子缺陷与无序对磁振子（magnon）激发及宏观物性（如自旋刚度）的影响，并在含缺陷亚铁磁体中定量预测空位分布对有效磁振子能带模糊化的调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实磁性材料中的原子缺陷与无序同时影响微观磁振子激发与宏观物理性质，但二者关系的理论阐释长期受阻：既难以量子精度地模拟原子/纳米尺度的非均匀性与关联，也难以刻画其对决定自旋刚度等可观测量的磁振子谱函数（magnon spectral function）的作用。

Method: 框架分三步：其一，提出耦合自旋环境优化（Coupled Optimization of SpIN Environment, COSINE）算法，将含缺陷或无序固体的原子结构与磁构型弛豫至局部能量极小；其二，高效获取计入局域组分、结构与磁矩非均匀性的交换相互作用（exchange interactions）；其三，针对Bogoliubov玻色子发展准酉展开（paraunitary unfolding）方法，可预测任意磁序（含倾角及磁矩完全、部分或无补偿）体系的磁振子谱函数。

Result: 在代表性缺陷亚铁磁体（ferrimagnet）上验证了方法的普适性，并与先进的“自旋信息机器学习势”（spin-informed machine-learning potentials）结合，定量预测了空位缺陷（vacancy defects）空间分布的非均匀性如何控制有效磁振子能带的模糊化（blurring）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了从原子尺度缺陷与无序出发、以量子精度联系微观磁激发与宏观磁性的统一计算途径，并可借助机器学习势推广至更大尺度与更复杂磁序体系的定量预测。

Abstract: Atomic defects and disorder affect both the microscopic magnon excitations and the macroscopic physical properties of real-world magnetic materials. The rationalization of this relation has hitherto remained elusive, because of the intertwined challenges of simulating with quantum accuracy atomic- and nanoscale inhomogeneities and correlations, and their effect on the magnon spectral function determining observables such as spin stiffness. Here, we introduce a theoretical and computational framework to address these challenges. First, we develop the Coupled Optimization of SpIN Environment (COSINE) algorithm for efficiently relaxing the atomic structure and magnetic configuration of a solid with defects or disorder to a local energy minimum. Then, we discuss how to efficiently obtain exchange interactions accounting for local compositional, structural, and magnetic-moment inhomogeneities. Finally, we develop a paraunitary unfolding approach for Bogoliubov bosons, which allows us to predict the magnon spectral function in materials with any magnetic order, including systems with canting and full, partial, or no magnetic-moment compensation. We showcase our approach in a representative defective ferrimagnet for generality, and then combine it with state-of-the-art spin-informed machine-learning potentials to quantitatively predict how nonuniformities in the spatial distribution of vacancy defects control blurring of effective magnon bandstructures.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Quantum limit cycles from phase coupling: A framework for NISQ thermodynamic devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15905)
*Nana Siddhartha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出量子化 Kuramoto 模型与 Van der Pol 振子并与量子比特耦合，证明在典型量子热力学环境中仍存在稳健极限环，据此提出可实现量子同步的玩具模型器件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子退相干（quantum decoherence）使得在量子层面运行的微型化器件与协议无法脱离用户对量子域的显式干预而自主工作，且经典驱动信号（classical driving signal）不足以描述完全构建于量子域中的真实量子器件；因此需要一种在小型化下仍不牺牲量子相干性（quantum coherence）的机制，而同步（synchronisation）被认为优于热化（thermalisation）。

Method: 对 Kuramoto 模型与 Van der Pol 振子进行量子化（quantise），并将其与一个或多个量子比特（qubit）耦合，以考察典型量子热力学（quantum thermodynamical）设定下的动力学行为；理论框架为作者所称的谱力学（Spectral Mechanics），其定位为对常规量子力学基础的重新审视。

Result: 即使在典型量子热力学环境中仍可观察到稳健的极限环（limit cycle）；并观察到纠缠态复苏（Entanglement State Revivals）、Zeno 锁定（Zeno locking）与热流干涉（thermal current interference）等新效应。

Conclusion: 提出可利用量子比特（或任意量子比特集合）与非线性相位振子（nonlinear phase oscillator）之间同步的玩具模型器件（toy model devices）；谱力学框架可扩展至实验环境中全新形式的相互作用与模型。

Abstract: Quantum decoherence does not permit the operation of (new) miniaturised devices or protocols that can otherwise run unassisted in classical settings, without explicit user agent intervention in the quantum realm. Classical driving signals are inadequate to model a truly quantum device built entirely in the quantum realm. Synchronisation endures miniaturisation without sacrificing quantum coherence, unlike thermalisation. We quantise the Kuramoto model and the Van der Pol oscillator and couple it with qubit(s) to show a robust limit cycle even in typical quantum thermodynamical settings. Novel effects such as Entanglement State Revivals, Zeno locking and thermal current interference are observed. We propose toy model devices that can be implemented harnessing the Synchronisation of a qubit (or an arbitrary collection of qubits) with a nonlinear phase oscillator. The framework theory used here, Spectral Mechanics, is a fresh look at the foundations of conventional quantum mechanics and is extensible to completely new forms of interactions and models in experimental settings.

</details>


### [171] [Sharp one-shot bounds and optimal exponents of universal randomness extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33270)
*Bartosz Regula,Hao-Chung Cheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将量子隐私放大（quantum privacy amplification）误差分析关联到带二次约束的假设检验（hypothesis testing）变体，结合测度平滑熵（measured smooth entropy）方法，精确刻画了多区间下最优误差标度，并确定了2-universal哈希函数下量子隐私放大的确切强逆指数（strong converse exponent）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子隐私放大在基于迹距离（trace distance）的标准操作安全判据下的误差最优标度尚未被精确确定，尤其针对通用类哈希函数（universal classes of hash functions）缺乏紧致的单次（one-shot）界与渐近误差指数刻画。

Method: 把随机性提取（randomness extraction）对量子边信息（quantum side information）的问题转化为带二次约束的假设检验任务变体，并借助近期提出的测度平滑熵方法；技术上引入与非交换Khintchine不等式（non-commutative Khintchine inequalities）相关的算子空间理论（operator space theory）方法。

Result: 给出单次逆界（one-shot converse bound），证明leftover hash lemma对所有强4-universal哈希函数在常数因子内紧致；由此得到所有速率下通用随机性提取的渐近i.i.d.误差指数，与[Dupuis, arXiv:2105.05342]的界相符并改进[Hayashi, arXiv:1010.1358]的经典结果；在强逆区间给出新的单次可达性与逆界，二者在常数因子内匹配。

Conclusion: 研究确立了2-universal哈希函数下量子隐私放大的确切强逆指数，补充了[Shen, Gao, Cheng, arXiv:2202.10263]的逆界，并揭示量子隐私放大误差分析与算子空间理论方法之间的技术联系。

Abstract: We introduce techniques for the study of randomness extraction against quantum side information (privacy amplification) by relating this problem to variants of hypothesis testing tasks with quadratic constraints. Building also on the recently introduced measured smooth entropy approach of [Regula and Tomamichel, arXiv:2603.04493], we precisely determine the optimal scaling of the error of quantum privacy amplification in several regimes. Our results apply to the standard operational security criterion based on trace distance and focus in particular on universal classes of hash functions. On the technical side, our investigation reveals connections with operator space theory methods used in non-commutative Khintchine inequalities. As one application, we give a one-shot converse bound that shows the leftover hash lemma of [arXiv:2603.04493] to be tight up to a constant factor for all strongly 4-universal hash functions. This allows us to evaluate the asymptotic i.i.d. error exponent of universal randomness extraction for all rates, matching the bound of [Dupuis, arXiv:2105.05342] and improving also earlier classical asymptotic results of [Hayashi, arXiv:1010.1358]. We furthermore give new one-shot achievability and converse bounds on the error in the strong converse regime, which we show to match up to a constant factor. This establishes the exact strong converse exponent of quantum privacy amplification under 2-universal hash functions, complementing the converse bound of [Shen, Gao, and Cheng, arXiv:2202.10263].

</details>


### [172] [Structure theorem for quantum limit cycle attractors: A geometric path-integral approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33776)
*Nana Siddhartha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文证明：任意可分离量子系统（separable quantum system，dim ℋ ≤ ℵ₀）经对称双向相位界面（symmetric bidirectional phase interface）与自维持相位振子环境耦合时，在 U(1) 协变（U(1) covariance）、瞬时 Lindblad 有界正性（instantaneous Lindblad bounded positivity）与可拖曳周期吸引子（entrainable periodic attractor）三条假设下，约化生成元（reduced generator）被强制具有"态依赖相干反冲（coherent recoil）+ 单量子反馈（single-quantum feedback）"的普适算子结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是确定"量子系统—自维持相位振子环境"这类双向耦合在一般假设下是否会唯一地约束约化动力学形式，并厘清耗散性饱和（dissipative saturation）与相干反馈（coherent feedback）何时必需、何时可替代。

Method: 采用结构定理（Structure Theorem）式的公理化推导：以 U(1) 协变、瞬时 Lindblad 有界正性与可拖曳周期吸引子为条件推出约化生成元的普适形式；再借助正性给出的维数二分（dimensional dichotomy）与迹范数收缩（trace-norm contraction）论证有限维禁戒；最后将反馈泛函在耦合的一阶展开，并对生成元的自治周期轨存在性给出逆耦合定理（Inverse Coupling Theorem），以基本群 π₁(ℳ) 为判据。

Result: 得到三项主要结论：(1) 约化生成元被强制为相干反冲加单量子反馈的普适结构；(2) 有限维中纯耗散增益饱和（purely dissipative gain saturation）被迹范数收缩禁戒，故量子比特与 qudit 必须依赖相干反馈，而无界阶梯代数（unbounded ladder algebra）允许纯耗散饱和；(3) 一阶展开恢复出一个规范多项式主方程（canonical polynomial master equation），在各维数下完全正且保迹（completely positive and trace-preserving），并与普通线性 Lindblad 通道可加叠加。

Conclusion: 逆耦合定理表明自治周期轨仅在相互作用流形满足 π₁(ℳ)=ℤ 时可维持，从而将正弦型 U(1) 协变相位耦合识别为一般机制；所得框架在单量子比特、双体两量子比特系统与玻色 Fock 空间上给出显式实现。

Abstract: We show a Structure Theorem for an arbitrary separable quantum system (\(\dim\mathcal{H}\leq\aleph_0\)) that is coupled, through a symmetric bidirectional phase interface, to a self-sustaining phase-oscillator environment. Under the assumptions of \(U(1)\) covariance, instantaneous Lindblad bounded positivity, and the existence of an entrainable periodic attractor, the reduced generator is forced into a universal operator structure consisting of a state-dependent coherent recoil together with single-quantum feedback. Positivity further imposes a sharp dimensional dichotomy: purely dissipative gain saturation is forbidden in finite dimensions by trace-norm contraction, so coherent feedback is mandatory for qubits and qudits, whereas unbounded ladder algebras permit purely dissipative saturation. Expanding the general feedback functionals to leading order in the coupling recovers a canonical polynomial master equation that remains completely positive and trace-preserving in every dimension and combines additively with ordinary linear Lindblad channels. An accompanying Inverse Coupling Theorem shows that an autonomous periodic orbit can be sustained only when the interaction manifold has non-trivial fundamental group \(π_1(\mathcal{M})=\mathbb{Z}\), thereby isolating the sinusoidal \(U(1)\)-covariant phase coupling as the generic mechanism. The resulting implementation is realized explicitly for a single qubit, a bipartite two-qubit system, and a bosonic Fock space.

</details>


### [173] [The integrable Lie-Trotter-Suzuki decomposition for nonlinear dynamics: Efficient Hamiltonian simulation of the Stepanoff flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34768)
*Ilon Joseph,Ivan Novikau,Michael R. Montgomery,Joanna Slawinska,Dimitrios Giannakis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出可积李-特罗特-铃木分解（integrable Lie-Trotter-Suzuki decomposition, I-LTS）这一酉数值时间积分策略，并将其应用于环面上的非可积 Stepanoff 流的量子模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非线性动力系统时间演化算子难以高效数值积分与模拟的问题，寻求一种能保持酉性、且可借助张量网络（tensor network）与量子块编码（quantum block-encoding）高效实现的分解构造。

Method: 先将生成元写成可积部分之和，再用所需时间步阶数的 LTS 分解近似完整演化算子，使其各项具备显式张量积分解；进而为 Stepanoff 流构造酉量子算法，给出二维系统的量子线路编码与所需算子的高效块编码，并以交替的量子傅里叶变换（quantum Fourier transform, QFT）与量子信号处理（quantum signal processing, QSP）线路实现哈密顿量模拟（Hamiltonian simulation）。

Result: 所构造线路在容错量子计算机（fault-tolerant quantum computer）数值仿真器上运行，结果与多种经典计算方法一致。

Conclusion: 方法具有效率，但 Trotter 化（Trotterization）带来根本性复杂度约束：精确近似需足够小的时间步长，而求解传输算子（transfer operator）须面对平流算子（advection operator）随网格规模的线性增长。

Abstract: We propose an efficient unitary numerical time integration strategy for nonlinear dynamical systems: the integrable Lie-Trotter-Suzuki decomposition (I-LTS). First, express the generator as a sum of integrable parts. Second, approximate the full evolution operator using a Lie-Trotter-Suzuki (LTS) decomposition of the desired approximation order in time step. The I-LTS construction ensures that the full evolution operator can be represented as a product of terms that each have an efficient explicit tensor product factorization that can be handled efficiently with tensor network and/or quantum block-encoding methods. Next, we apply this method to an important non-integrable point example of a dynamical system: the Stepanoff flow on the torus. First, we derive an efficient unitary quantum algorithm for simulating the Stepanoff flow that can be thought of as a unitary classical version of a quantum map. Then, we provide a detailed description of the encoding of the two-dimensional system into an efficient quantum circuit, including an efficient block-encoding for all required operators. An efficient Hamiltonian simulation algorithm is constructed using a sequence of alternating quantum Fourier transforms (QFT) and Quantum Signal Processing (QSP) circuits. Finally, the circuit is simulated using a numerical emulator of fault-tolerant quantum computers, and the results are found to be consistent with several alternative classical computational methods. While efficient, the need for Trotterization introduces fundamental complexity in requiring small enough time step for an accurate approximation and solving for the transfer operator requires one to confront the linear scaling of the advection operator with grid size.

</details>


### [174] [Gauge symmetry permits extremely low-depth random unitary circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33312)
*Yunfei Wang,Yuqing Li,Junqi Wang,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Junyu Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究格点规范对称性（gauge symmetry）是否会同连续全局对称性一样，限制对数深度（logarithmic-depth）的对称幺正 k-设计（unitary k-design）与伪随机幺正（pseudorandom unitaries, PRUs）。作者为 U(1) 格点规范理论（lattice gauge theories）引入随机化尺度 c 与扩散尺度（diffusive scale），指出局域屏蔽（screening）是否完备决定了设计保证的强度，并据此提出弱引力猜想（weak gravity conjecture）的量子信息类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续全局对称性（continuous global symmetries）通过长程关联（long-range correlations）阻碍对数深度对称 k-设计与伪随机幺正；本文追问规范对称性（gauge symmetries）是否施加类似限制，并探讨其与黑洞蒸发及弱引力猜想的可能联系。

Method: 针对 U(1) 格点规范理论，定义随机化尺度 c（物质屏蔽任意外边界通量失配（boundary-flux mismatch）所需的最小片块尺寸）与刻画边界通量关联的扩散尺度，并分析由亚扩散支撑（subdiffusive support）的规范对称幺正粘合而成的电路（glued circuits）与规范对称 Haar 随机幺正的区分性；同时构造带电黑洞蒸发（charged black-hole evaporation）的玩具模型。

Result: 支撑度亚扩散的粘合电路可被高效区分于规范对称 Haar 随机幺正；而当重叠超过扩散尺度乘以对数因子时，局域受控设计可粘合为全局近似设计。屏蔽不完备将设计保证限制在常数阶；完备局域屏蔽（c=1）的系统可具局域张量积描述，容许超多项式设计阶数及对数深度设计与 PRUs。玩具模型中，屏蔽不足使无中断完全放电在 c 上呈指数不可能。

Conclusion: 作者提出弱引力猜想的量子信息类比，要求可用带电谱（charged spectrum）在超极值性（superextremality）之外还支持完备局域屏蔽。并猜想该条件可使规范对称系统与无约束系统同等快速地扰乱（scramble），通过构造对数深度近似强设计与强 PRUs 予以支持。

Abstract: Continuous global symmetries can obstruct logarithmic-depth symmetric unitary $k$-designs and pseudorandom unitaries (PRUs) through long-range correlations. We ask whether gauge symmetries impose analogous limitations. For $\mathrm{U}(1)$ lattice gauge theories, we identify a randomization scale $c$, the minimum patch size needed for matter to screen every allowed boundary-flux mismatch, and a diffusive scale governing boundary-flux correlations. Glued circuits built from gauge-symmetric unitaries with subdiffusive support are efficiently distinguishable from gauge-symmetric Haar-random unitaries, whereas local controlled designs can be glued into global approximate designs when overlaps exceed the diffusive scale multiplied by a logarithmic factor. Our analysis suggests incomplete screening limits design guarantees to constant order, whereas strictly locally random systems with complete local screening ($c=1$) admit a local tensor-product description, permitting superpolynomial design orders and logarithmic-depth designs and PRUs. We construct a toy model of charged black-hole evaporation in which insufficient screening makes uninterrupted complete discharge exponentially unlikely in $c$, whereas strict local randomization removes this screening obstruction. This motivates a quantum-information analogue of the weak gravity conjecture, requiring the available charged spectrum to support complete local screening in addition to superextremality. We conjecture that this condition allows gauge-symmetric systems to scramble as rapidly as unconstrained ones and support this by constructing logarithmic-depth approximate strong designs and strong PRUs.

</details>


### [175] [Chiral Domain-Wall Modes in Topological Adaptive Quantum Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33328)
*Asadullah Bhuiyan,Haining Pan,Chao-Ming Jian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作研究由幺正演化与测量共同驱动的量子动力学中的拓扑畴壁模式：在2+1维电荷守恒自由费米子自适应线路中，拓扑性质不同的两相之间的1+1维时空畴壁会自发承载手性动力学拓扑畴壁模式（DTDMs），其长时行为与1+1维手性自由费米子共形场论基态一致，并在纯化动力学上表现为代数式慢纯化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量与幺正演化驱动的非平衡物相中，拓扑（topology）所起作用的理解远不如纠缠（entanglement）与对称性（symmetry）充分；受保护的动力学拓扑畴壁模式（dynamical topological domain-wall modes, DTDMs）此前仅在1+1维费米子动力学的0+1维畴壁情形下被较好理解，更高维畴壁情形尚属空白。

Method: 构建2+1维电荷守恒自由费米子动力学中的拓扑自适应线路（topological adaptive circuits），通过测量与前馈校正（feedforward corrections）将每个畴壁两侧分别驱动至陈绝缘体（Chern insulator）与平庸绝缘体，从而形成1+1维拓扑畴壁；线路本身并不以特定畴壁态为目标态。

Result: 每条畴壁均承载1+1维手性DTDMs：在每条量子轨迹的长时极限下，纠缠与电荷涨落呈对数标度（logarithmic scaling）、密度关联呈代数衰减，与1+1维手性自由费米子共形场论（conformal field theory）基态一致，区别于纯1+1维电荷守恒自由费米子动力学在有限测量率下仅有面积律纠缠（area-law entanglement）。此外，DTDMs表现出缓慢纯化（slow purification）：从最大混态出发时其熵代数衰减并伴随消失的 Lyapunov gap，而各畴区在 O(1) 时间尺度上即完成纯化。

Conclusion: 作者将自适应线路模型视为一种量子冷却算法（quantum cooling algorithm），为DTDMs的长时行为提供物理图像；结果表明自适应动力学的拓扑相可在反复波函数坍缩与 Born 规则随机性下保护时空畴壁上的手性模式。

Abstract: Quantum dynamical systems driven by both unitary evolution and measurements open a new landscape of nonequilibrium phases of matter. The role of topology in enriching this landscape remains less understood than that of entanglement and symmetry. A particularly interesting question concerns protected dynamical modes localized on spacetime domain walls between topologically distinct dynamical phases. These dynamical topological domain-wall modes (DTDMs) are well understood only for $0+1$D walls in $1+1$D fermionic dynamics. In this work, we study $1+1$D topological domain walls in $2+1$D charge-conserving free-fermion dynamics, modeled by topological adaptive circuits that steer the two sides of each wall toward a Chern insulator and a trivial insulator through measurements and feedforward corrections. Although the circuits target no particular wall state, each wall hosts $1+1$D chiral DTDMs. At long times in each quantum trajectory, they exhibit logarithmic scaling of entanglement and charge variance and algebraically decaying density correlations, surprisingly consistent with the ground state of a $1+1$D chiral free-fermion conformal field theory. This contrasts with purely $1+1$D charge-conserving free-fermion dynamics, which exhibits only area-law entanglement at finite measurement rates. We also show that the chiral DTDMs exhibit slow purification dynamics. When the system starts from a maximally mixed state, the DTDMs' entropy decays algebraically, accompanied by a vanishing Lyapunov gap, whereas each domain purifies on an $\mathcal{O}(1)$ timescale. Finally, we propose a physical picture for the long-time behavior of the DTDMs by treating the adaptive circuit model as a quantum cooling algorithm. Our results show that topological phases of adaptive dynamics can protect chiral modes on spacetime domain walls against repeated wavefunction collapse and Born-rule randomness.

</details>


### [176] [Nitrogen-Vacancy Nuclear Magnetic Resonance Spectroscopy of $^{15}$N Spins in Potassium Nitrite Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33360)
*Sehrish Iqbal,Jitender Kumar,Suvechhya Lamichhane,Rupak Timalsina,Sy-Hwang Liou,Abdelghani Laraoui*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用金刚石浅层氮空位中心构建微流控量子传感平台，在室温低磁场下实现飞升体积内KNO₃溶液中¹⁵N核磁共振检测，并观测到远超统计极化的磁场方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氮是农业肥料的核心元素，但其纳米尺度界面动力学研究不足；¹⁴N因四极展宽难以检测，¹⁵N虽核自旋信号清晰，却因天然丰度低、旋磁比小而难以在纳米尺度探测。

Method: 采用基于金刚石中浅层氮空位（nitrogen vacancy, NV）中心的微流控量子传感（quantum sensing）平台，在室温与低磁场条件下对KNO₃溶液进行¹⁵N核磁共振（NMR）检测，传感体积为飞升（femtoliter）量级。

Result: 测得高达约442 nT²的磁场方差，约为统计极化（statistical polarization）预期值的43倍；¹⁵N自旋关联在约500 μs内衰减，主要受NV自旋晶格弛豫时间（spin lattice relaxation time）限制，而非KNO₃分子的分子扩散限制。

Conclusion: 衰减特征与金刚石表面形成KNO₃吸附层的推断一致，并获得表面表征结果支持，表明纳米尺度界面吸附层显著影响¹⁵N自旋信号。

Abstract: Nitrogen is essential in agricultural fertilizers, yet its nanoscale interfacial dynamics remain largely unexplored. Although quadrupolar broadening complicates $^{14}$N nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) detection, $^{15}$N (I=1/2) provides well-defined nuclear-spin signatures but remains challenging to detect at the nanoscale due to its low natural abundance and small gyromagnetic ratio. Here, we use a microfluidic quantum sensing platform based on shallow nitrogen vacancy (NV) centers in diamond to detect $^{15}$N NMR from KNO$_{3}$ solution at room temperature and in a low magnetic field regime. Within femtoliter-scale sensing volumes, we measure a magnetic-field variance of up to ~442 nT$^{2}$, ~43 times greater than the expected statistical polarization. The $^{15}$N spin correlation decays within ~500 $μ\mathrm{s}$, mainly limited by the NV spin lattice relaxation time and not by the molecular diffusion of the KNO$_{3}$ molecules. This is consistent with the formation of a KNO$_{3}$ adsorption layer on the diamond surface, supported by surface characterization.

</details>


### [177] [Optimal Controlled Quantum Instrument for Monitored Many-Body Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33472)
*Jimin Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将贝叶斯规则推广至受监测多体动力学，利用受测量后态的非线性函数对量子轨迹加权，构造无需后选择的受控量子仪器以最优速率产生偏置轨迹，并给出张量网络表示与数值示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受监测量子动力学中稀有轨迹的后选择（post-selection）需要指数多次重复实验，代价过高，因此需要一种无需后选择即可高效产生偏置轨迹的方法。

Method: 把贝叶斯规则（Bayes' rule）扩展至受监测多体动力学，以测量后态的非线性函数偏置量子轨迹；对在适当有限划分的各集合上取常数的函数，在系统与附加寄存器（register）上构造量子仪器（quantum instrument），其自然对应系统—寄存器张量的左正则形式（left canonical form）张量网络表示。

Result: 该受控量子仪器以最优速率、无需后选择地产生偏置轨迹；两个示例分别为受场方向自旋分量偏置的进动量子比特，以及受粒子数偏置的带增益与损耗的自由费米子，后者偏置轨迹发生相变；并在一个假设下论证按 von Neumann 熵偏置受测 Majorana 链可导致纠缠转变。

Conclusion: 偏置轨迹可通过受控量子仪器与张量网络形式高效实现，避免指数级后选择开销，并可将偏置引入的相变与纠缠转变纳入同一框架。

Abstract: Post-selecting rare trajectories of monitored quantum dynamics requires exponentially many repeated experiments. We extend Bayes' rule to monitored many-body dynamics and bias the quantum trajectories by nonlinear functions of the post-measurement state. For functions that are constant on the sets of a suitable finite partition of the post-measurement states, we construct a quantum instrument on the system and an additional register that produces the biased trajectories at the optimal rates without post-selection. This optimal controlled quantum instrument has a natural tensor-network representation as the left canonical form of the system--register tensors. We illustrate the construction with two examples: a precessing qubit, biased by its spin component along the field, and free fermions with gain and loss, biased by the particle number, where the biased trajectories undergo a phase transition. We also argue, under one assumption, that biasing a measured Majorana chain by its von Neumann entropy leads to entanglement transitions.

</details>


### [178] [Infrared-absorption magnetometry in a diamond nanocavity: demonstration and limits at the nanoscale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33481)
*Nicholas J. Sorensen,Elham Zohari,Chris Coutts,Sean McNaney,Matteo Feder,Hadi Zadeh-Haghighi,Sigurd Flågan,Vinaya K. Kavatamane,Christoph Simon,Paul E. Barclay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作首次演示基于自旋高密度金刚石纳米腔（spin-dense diamond nanocavities）的单态吸收磁测量（singlet-absorption magnetometry），利用1042 nm附近NV色心单态跃迁的腔模探测实现自旋相关腔透射与腔增强磁场传感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金刚石量子传感器兼具高空间分辨率与高灵敏度，但传统基于光致发光（photoluminescence）的自旋读出难以高效集成；氮-空位（nitrogen-vacancy, NV）色心的红外吸收是替代方案，然而紧凑型吸收传感器的灵敏度受限于探测场与NV系综间较短的光学相互作用长度。

Method: 采用自旋高密度金刚石纳米腔，探测1042 nm附近NV单态跃迁（singlet transition）的腔模，观测自旋相关的腔透射并开展腔增强磁场传感；同时建立并实验验证可准确预测自旋相关腔响应、描述最优工作条件、识别不同光学检测磁共振（optically detected magnetic resonance）模式实际局限的模型。

Result: 观测到自旋相关的腔透射，并成功实现腔增强的磁场传感；所建模型经实验验证，可准确预测自旋相关腔响应、给出最优工作条件并揭示不同读出模式的实用限制。

Conclusion: 腔增强单态吸收可作为集成金刚石量子传感器的可扩展读出机制（scalable readout mechanism），并为下一代纳米光子金刚石量子磁力计（nanophotonic diamond quantum magnetometers）的设计提供实用框架。

Abstract: Diamond quantum sensors promise both high spatial resolution and high sensitivity; however, conventional photoluminescence-based spin readout can be difficult to integrate efficiently. Infrared absorption by nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centres in diamond provides a promising alternative, although the sensitivity of compact absorption-based sensors is limited by the short optical interaction length between the probe field and the NV ensemble. To overcome this limitation, we demonstrate, for the first time, singlet-absorption magnetometry using spin-dense diamond nanocavities. By probing cavity modes near the 1042 nm NV singlet transition, we observe spin-dependent cavity transmission and perform cavity-enhanced magnetic field sensing. We further develop and experimentally validate a model that accurately predicts the spin-dependent cavity response, describes the optimal operating conditions, and identifies the practical limitations of different optically detected magnetic resonance modalities. Together, these results establish cavity-enhanced singlet absorption as a scalable readout mechanism for integrated diamond quantum sensors and provide a practical framework for engineering next-generation nanophotonic diamond quantum magnetometers.

</details>


### [179] [A scalable dense silicon qubit architecture with logical operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33590)
*Masahiro Tadokoro,Tetsuo Kodera*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种面向大规模容错量子计算（FTQC）的可扩展致密硅自旋量子比特（silicon spin qubit）架构，由量子比特存储器、数据比特通道与交叉结组成，结合旋转表面码（rotated surface code）与自旋穿梭（spin shuttling）实现纠错与逻辑操作，并估算其在100 mm×100 mm芯片上可集成逾12万个逻辑量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FTQC需要大量物理量子比特以低错误率编码逻辑量子比特；硅自旋量子比特具备尺寸小、与半导体工艺兼容及高保真度操作等优势，但尚缺乏同时支持量子纠错、比特传输、布线与逻辑门操作的可扩展架构。

Method: 提出由量子比特存储器（以旋转表面码编码逻辑比特并支持单比特逻辑操作）、数据比特通道（通过高速一维自旋穿梭实现逻辑比特间连通）与交叉结（提供路由并实现横向逻辑CNOT门）构成的架构；基于实验已实现的硅自旋量子比特参数评估逻辑错误率随码距的变化，并依据CMOS兼容的多路复用控制方案分析布线约束。

Result: 估计每个综合周期逻辑错误率低于10^-10至少需要码距21；在优化布线效率条件下，100 mm×100 mm芯片可集成超过120,000个逻辑量子比特。

Conclusion: 该架构为基于硅自旋量子比特技术实现大规模容错量子计算提供了切实可行的路线图（roadmap）。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) requires a large number of physical qubits to encode logical qubits with sufficiently low error rates. Silicon spin qubits are a promising platform because of their small footprint, compatibility with semiconductor manufacturing technologies, and demonstrated high-fidelity operations. However, realizing a scalable architecture that supports quantum error correction, qubit transport, wiring, and logical gate operations remains a major challenge. Here, we propose a scalable dense silicon spin qubit architecture designed to support large-scale FTQC. The architecture consists of qubit memories, data qubit lanes, and cross junctions. Qubit memories store logical qubits encoded using the rotated surface code and support logical single-qubit operations. Data qubit lanes enable high-speed one-dimensional spin shuttling for connectivity between logical qubits, while cross junctions provide routing functionality and implement transversal logical CNOT gates. Using experimentally demonstrated silicon spin qubit parameters, we evaluate logical error rates as a function of code distance and estimate that a code distance of at least 21 is required to achieve logical error rates below $10^{-10}$ per syndrome cycle. We further analyze wiring constraints based on CMOS-compatible multiplexed control schemes and show that, with optimized wiring efficiency, more than 120,000 logical qubits could be integrated on a 100 mm $\times$ 100 mm chip. These results provide a practical roadmap toward large-scale FTQC based on silicon spin qubit technology.

</details>


### [180] [Unified preparation of exact and asymptotic quantum many-body scars from Dicke states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33987)
*Yuki Asari,Masaya Kunimi,Tetsuro Nikuni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种统一的量子算法（quantum algorithm），可在自旋-1（spin-1）的 XY 模型、畴壁守恒（domain-wall-conserving）模型与 Onsager 疤痕模型中制备精确（exact）与渐近（asymptotic）的量子多体疤痕态（quantum many-body scar, QMBS）。借助模型相关的等距映射（isometry）将疤痕态联系到 Dicke 态与自旋-1/2 铁磁 Heisenberg 链的无能隙磁振子激发（magnon excitation），并以块编码（block encoding）实现渐近态制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体疤痕态是非遍历动力学（nonergodic dynamics）研究的关键对象，但其在量子模拟器上的系统制备缺乏统一、可扩展的方案；该工作旨在提供一种与模型相关但结构统一的态制备算法。

Method: 对精确 QMBS 态，直接对 Dicke 态施加逆等距映射（inverse isometry）得到目标态；对渐近 QMBS 态，先用块编码从 Dicke 态制备磁振子激发至指定保真度（fidelity），再通过逆等距映射映回物理自旋链。

Result: 在固定目标保真度下，块编码的成功概率存在与系统尺寸及疤痕指标（scar index）无关的正下界，因此常数轮振幅放大（amplitude amplification）即可满足需求，说明算法开销不随规模发散。

Conclusion: 该统一算法可在可编程量子模拟器（programmable quantum simulators）上系统研究非遍历动力学，为多模型的 QMBS 态制备提供可行路径。

Abstract: We propose a unified quantum algorithm for preparing exact and asymptotic quantum many-body scar (QMBS) states in the spin-1 {\it XY}, domain-wall-conserving, and Onsager scar models. Model-dependent isometries map the exact scar states to Dicke states and the target asymptotic QMBS states to gapless magnon excitations of the spin-1/2 ferromagnetic Heisenberg chain. Exact QMBS states are prepared by applying the inverse isometries to Dicke states. For asymptotic QMBS states, block encoding first prepares the magnon excitations from Dicke states to a prescribed fidelity, after which the inverse isometries map the prepared states back to the physical chains. At fixed target fidelity, the block-encoding success probability has a positive lower bound independent of system size and scar index, implying that a constant number of amplitude amplification rounds suffices. Our algorithm enables systematic studies of nonergodic dynamics on programmable quantum simulators.

</details>


### [181] [Discrete truncated Wigner approximation of the quantum phase transition in the transverse-field Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34004)
*Tuan-Vu Truong,Hoang-Long Nguyen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作系统检验离散截断Wigner近似（DTWA）能否刻画量子相变动力学，结论是DTWA在维度与连通度越高时越可靠：一维链上失效（表现为经典热化），二维/三维可指示相变位置但不复现Kibble-Zurek标度，全连接情形则与精确解一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTWA是大规模自旋动力学的常用方法，但其跨越量子相变（quantum phase transition）的动力学能力此前缺乏系统检验，本文旨在明确其适用范围与失效边界。

Method: 以横场Ising模型（transverse-field Ising model, TFIM）为对象，在一至三维及全连接（all-to-all）连通的格点上，分别采用慢速线性扫描（slow linear ramp）与快速扫描（fast ramp），并与量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo, QMC）结果对比。

Result: DTWA无法刻画一维链动力学，行为与经典热化（classical thermalization）一致，慢扫描下经典自旋弛豫到有限有效温度，而一维链在任何有限温度下均不能有序；正方与立方格点上DTWA能建立序，且序的出现接近QMC给出的量子临界点，但关联不遵循Kibble-Zurek指数增长；全连接耦合下DTWA在整个扫描区间与序参量精确增长一致。

Conclusion: 每个自旋的相互作用伙伴越多，DTWA越可靠；超过一维时，DTWA可指示量子相变的位置，但无法捕捉其临界标度（critical scaling）。

Abstract: The discrete truncated Wigner approximation (DTWA) has been a workhorse for large-scale spin dynamics, but whether it captures dynamics across a quantum phase transition has not been tested systematically. In this work, we use the transverse-field Ising model (TFIM) to study the quantum phase transition with DTWA from one to three dimensions and with all-to-all connectivity, using slow linear ramps and fast ramps. We find that DTWA cannot capture the dynamics on the one-dimensional chain, and that its behavior is consistent with classical thermalization, in which its classical spins relax toward a thermal state at a finite effective temperature that does not vanish for slow ramps, while a one-dimensional chain cannot order at any finite temperature. For square and cubic lattices, in contrast to the chain, DTWA builds order, and the order sets in close to the quantum critical point found by quantum Monte Carlo (QMC). However, its correlations do not grow with the Kibble-Zurek exponents, so DTWA captures the onset of order but not the universal dynamics of the transition. For all-to-all coupling, DTWA follows the exact growth of the order parameter along the entire ramp. Our results suggest that DTWA becomes more reliable as each spin interacts with more partners, and that beyond one dimension it can indicate where a quantum phase transition lies but cannot capture its critical scaling.

</details>


### [182] [Quantum dynamics of a charged particle on rotating curved surfaces and space curves in electromagnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34448)
*Long Du,Yong-Long Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个规范一致的框架，用于描述电磁场中限制在旋转弯曲流形上的带电粒子，将旋转效应转化为有效规范势，并将非惯性量子动力学在薄层极限下归结为有效规范势与几何势两个要素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于处理旋转弯曲流形上带电粒子量子动力学的规范一致性问题，特别是维度约化（dimensional reduction）中规范场与几何之间的纠缠（gauge–geometry entanglement）障碍。

Method: 从协变Klein–Gordon方程（covariant Klein–Gordon equation）出发，作非相对论约化，将旋转效应表述为保持U(1)对称性的有效规范势（effective gauge potential），几何以da Costa势（da Costa potential）单独进入；通过广义横向Poincaré规范（generalized transverse Poincaré gauge）、旋转最小化Bishop标架（rotation-minimizing Bishop frame）和横向基态宇称（parity of the transverse ground state）三种机制消除规范–几何纠缠。

Result: 在环面上，轴向场产生符号可调的Sagnac–Aharonov–Bohm联合残差且不破坏极向–方位角可分性（poloidal–azimuthal separability），面内场仅通过结构的面外延展耦合；轴向场可调控曲率诱导局域化（curvature-induced localization）；波纹量子环将周期几何与磁通调制转化为人工超晶格（artificial superlattice），其面内场免疫性源于平面性与宇称而非场强小。

Conclusion: 任意形状曲面与曲线上非惯性量子动力学在薄层区域（thin-layer regime）内可约化为两个要素：有效规范势与几何势。

Abstract: We develop a gauge-consistent framework for a charged particle confined to a rotating curved manifold in an electromagnetic field, starting from the covariant Klein--Gordon equation. The non-relativistic reduction casts the rotational effects as effective gauge potentials while preserving the $U(1)$ symmetry, so that rotation and the external field enter alike, the geometry entering separately as the da Costa potential. The obstruction to dimensional reduction is gauge--geometry entanglement: transverse magnetic fields on a two-dimensional (2D) surface and structural torsion on a one-dimensional (1D) curve couple the tangential dynamics to the transverse degrees of freedom. We remove this entanglement by three mechanisms---a generalized transverse Poincaré gauge, the rotation-minimizing Bishop frame, and the parity of the transverse ground state, the last of which annihilates the residual first-order magnetic coupling for a single inversion-symmetric well. On a torus, an axial field generates a joint Sagnac--Aharonov--Bohm residual of tunable sign without spoiling the poloidal--azimuthal separability, whereas an in-plane field couples only through the out-of-plane extent of the structure: it breaks the axial symmetry there but leaves a planar curve unaffected to first order. The same torus shows the axial field tuning the curvature-induced localization, and a corrugated quantum ring converts periodic geometric and flux modulations into an artificial superlattice whose in-plane-field immunity follows from planarity and parity, not from the smallness of the field. Non-inertial quantum dynamics on surfaces and curves of arbitrary shape is thus reduced, within the thin-layer regime, to two ingredients: an effective gauge potential and a geometric potential.

</details>


### [183] [Slow mixing of Gibbs sampling for the quantum Curie-Weiss model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34682)
*Xiaoyang Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出，即便哈密顿量演化使局部算符在稠密量子磁体上全局扩散，量子Curie-Weiss模型中热化的瓶颈依然存在：在固定低温与弱横场下，Chen-Kastoryano-Gilyen Gibbs采样器的混合时间存在指数级下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有热化与混合时间分析框架多依赖空间局域性（spatial locality），而哈密顿量演化可将局部算符扩散至全空间，因此需要考察这种全局支持（global support）是否消除了热化瓶颈。

Method: 针对量子Curie-Weiss模型，在固定低温和弱横场条件下分析Chen-Kastoryano-Gilyen Gibbs采样器（Gibbs sampler）的混合时间，并提出推广经典电导（conductance）概念的动力学量子瓶颈理论（dynamical quantum bottleneck theory），辅以有效Fokker-Planck方程（effective Fokker-Planck equation）给出互补解释。

Result: 该Gibbs采样器的混合时间（mixing time）具有指数级大的下界，表明算符的全局扩散并未消除热化瓶颈。

Conclusion: 全局算符支持不足以保障快速热化；作者提出的动力学量子瓶颈理论为不依赖空间局域性的分析提供了框架，并以有效Fokker-Planck方程对慢混合给出补充刻画。

Abstract: Hamiltonian evolution can spread local operators across a dense quantum magnet. We show that this global support does not remove the bottleneck to thermalization in the quantum Curie-Weiss model: at fixed low temperature and weak transverse field, the Chen-Kastoryano-Gilyen Gibbs sampler [arXiv: 2311.09207] has an exponentially large mixing-time lower bound. A dynamical quantum bottleneck theory that generalizes the classical notion of conductance has been proposed alongside existing frameworks that rely on spatial locality. We also give a complementary interpretation of slow mixing using an effective Fokker-Planck equation.

</details>


### [184] [Transition Path Sampling for Rare Events in Non-Markovian Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34883)
*Ritesh Pant,Kai Müller,Tarun Gera,Doran I. G. B. Raccah*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文为非马尔可夫量子态扩散（non-Markovian quantum state diffusion）建立过渡路径采样（transition path sampling, TPS）框架，从而将稀有跃迁概率与速率的精确计算扩展到强耦合、非马尔可夫的开放量子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有跃迁（rare transitions）支配许多开放量子系统（open quantum systems）的动力学，但其概率与速率难以计算；已有的量子过渡路径采样仅局限于马尔可夫（Markovian）、微扰（perturbative）的描述。

Method: 作者为非马尔可夫量子态扩散（对高斯浴（Gaussian baths）精确）构造过渡路径采样（TPS）方法，并将其应用于非平衡开放 Dicke 模型（unbalanced open Dicke model）中从常态（normal）到超辐射态（superradiant）的隧穿过程。

Result: 在该模型中，方法可确定低至 \(10^{-8}\) 的跃迁概率，以及超出暴力计算（brute force）能力的跃迁速率。

Conclusion: 该工作将过渡路径采样推广至非马尔可夫、强耦合的开放量子系统，为稀有跃迁概率与速率的精确高效计算提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Rare transitions govern the dynamics of many open quantum systems, but their probabilities and rates are difficult to calculate. Transition path sampling (TPS) of quantum systems has so far been limited to Markovian, perturbative descriptions. Here, we formulate TPS for non-Markovian quantum state diffusion, which is exact for Gaussian baths, extending it to non-Markovian and strongly coupled open quantum systems. For normal-to-superradiant tunneling in the unbalanced open Dicke model, we determine probabilities down to $10^{-8}$ and rates beyond the reach of brute force.

</details>


### [185] [The thermodynamic budget of objectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34909)
*Wonyong Chung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文将“事件成为事实”定义为存在足够多独立记录使任何操作都无法让所有观察者重新对其无知，并在一个最小广播模型（minimal broadcast model）中精确计算记录创建、保存与销毁的热力学代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记录（records）是物理的，其创建、保存与销毁均有热力学代价；作者希望以热力学而非仅信息论的方式刻画客观性（objectivity）与事实（fact）的形成条件。

Method: 构建最小广播模型：驱动中心量子比特（driven central qubit）向 N 个初始处于热态的寄存器（registers）写入记录，并配有功源与温度 T 的热浴；在冗余度（redundancy）R 与信息亏缺（information deficit）δ 下精确计算各操作代价，并分析基失配角 θ 与临界角 θ_c。

Result: 创建一条事件记录需功 W ≥ R k_B T ln2 (1-δ) H(ε)，在 Landauer 系数下随冗余度线性增长；基失配超过 θ_c = 2arcsin√(H₂⁻¹(δ)) 时任意功都无法实现客观性，低于 θ_c 时最优功在指针基（pointer basis）中有闭式解。记录冗余是可提取的自由能，抹除被访问的记录除首份拷贝外带来净功收益；第二定律约束关联的销毁：抹除对手控制扇区中的事实至少耗散所抹除的互信息（mutual information），而整合（consolidation）不耗散。再热化介质中的记录为非平衡稳态（nonequilibrium steady states），维持记录的最小功率为在两态驱动界（two-state driving bound）于记录质量边界处的取值。

Conclusion: 客观性与事实的形成受热力学代价与信息论条件的双重约束：冗余度决定创建功的线性标度，失配角阈值与记录可及数量决定客观性可达性，且记录的冗余携带可利用自由能而关联销毁受第二定律限制。

Abstract: An event becomes a fact when enough independent records of it exist such that no operation is possible that leaves all observers ignorant of it again. Records are physical, and creating, keeping, and destroying them have thermodynamic costs. We compute these costs exactly in a minimal broadcast model with a driven central qubit writing records into $N$ initially thermal registers, with work sources and baths at temperature $T$. The basic arguments for the operations are outlined here, with full treatment in the text. Creation of one event record at redundancy $R$ and information deficit $δ$ requires work $W \ge R\, k_\mathrm{B}T \ln 2\, (1-δ) H(\varepsilon)$, linear in redundancy at the Landauer coefficient. Recording in a basis misaligned by $θ$ with the einselecting interaction fails at a critical angle, $θ_c = 2\arcsin\sqrt{H_2^{-1}(δ)}$, beyond which objectivity is unreachable at any work, and below $θ_c$ the optimal work is a closed form in the pointer basis that minimizes the work of objectivity. Reducing objectivity is impossible at any work with access to fewer than $R - R_{\min} + 1$ records, while work protects nothing if given access. Inter-record redundancy is extractable free energy and erasing the accessed records yields a net gain of work beyond the first copy. The second law constrains the destruction of the correlation: erasing a fact from a sector an adversary controls dissipates at least the mutual information erased, but a consolidation dissipates nothing. Records in re-thermalizing media are nonequilibrium steady states, and the minimal power to maintain a record at $(R,δ)$ is the known two-state driving bound evaluated at the record-quality boundary.

</details>


### [186] [Bound Entanglement with Negative Partial Transpose Exists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34921)
*Islam Faisal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该摘要指出 $\mathbb{C}^8\otimes\mathbb{C}^8$ 上的特定 Werner 态（Werner state）具有负部分转置（negative partial transpose），属于束缚纠缠（bound entangled）态，并由此推出可提纯纠缠（distillable entanglement）既不具可加性（additivity）也不具凸性（convexity）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确交代研究动机，仅以构造反例的方式针对可提纯纠缠是否可加、是否凸这两个量论纠缠理论中的基本性质给出否定性结论。

Method: 考察 $\mathbb{C}^8\otimes\mathbb{C}^8$ 上参数固定的 Werner 态 $\rho=(23I-3\,\mathrm{SWAP})/1448$，判定其部分转置为负，并分析任意副本数下 LOCC 协议及可分操作（separable operations）的纠缠提纯能力。

Result: 该态部分转置为负且为束缚纠缠态：任意多份拷贝均无法通过 LOCC 提纯出纠缠；即使允许可分操作，两量子比特输出与 Bell 态的保真度（fidelity）上界为 $(1+\sqrt{2})/4\approx0.604$。

Conclusion: 由上述性质可得，可提纯纠缠既不可加也不凸，从而否定了该量在纠缠理论中两项被广泛讨论的结构性假设。

Abstract: We show that the Werner state $ρ= (23 I - 3\,\mathrm{SWAP})/1448$ on $\mathbb{C}^8 \otimes \mathbb{C}^8$ has a negative partial transpose and is bound entangled: no LOCC protocol can distill entanglement from any number of copies of $ρ$, and even with separable operations the fidelity of a two-qubit output with a Bell state never exceeds $(1+\sqrt{2})/4 \approx 0.604$. It follows that distillable entanglement is neither additive nor convex.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Pairing from anti-Hund's coupling under infinite Hubbard repulsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33286)
*Donggyu Kim,Ya-Hui Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在Hubbard排斥U占主导的强关联体系中，光学声子介导的反洪德耦合（anti-Hund's coupling）J_A可在三角晶格四味t-J_A模型中产生稳健的键间库珀对配对，最大配对能隙出现在J_A∼|t|，为超越BCS理论的强耦合超导提供证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）虽是超导的自然来源，但在强关联材料中声子介导的相互作用通常远弱于在位Hubbard排斥（Hubbard repulsion）U；当U占主导时，声子机制能否以及如何仍产生配对，是一个基本未解问题。

Method: 研究含反洪德耦合（anti-Hund's coupling）J_A的最小模型：由光学声子介导、倾向于形成局域轨道间自旋单态的在位自旋交换。在U=+∞极限下，三角晶格（triangular lattice）上双层轨道Hubbard模型在n=2绝缘态掺杂后约化为四味t-J_A模型，并采用密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group, DMRG）进行计算。

Result: 在1≤J_A/|t|≤10范围内均发现稳健配对，最大配对能隙位于J_A∼|t|处；库珀对（Cooper pairs）位于键上，并非源于J_A提供的直接吸引，因为无穷大U将在位配对排除出希尔伯特空间。当J_A≫|t|时，正常态为第二费米液体（second Fermi liquid），其小费米面违反微扰Luttinger定理；以t/J_A的强耦合展开给出该非常规金属中超导的可控有效理论。

Conclusion: 结果有力表明存在由动能、声子介导的反洪德耦合与强Hubbard U三者相互作用驱动的、超越BCS理论的强耦合超导体；文中还讨论了其在魔角扭转双层石墨烯（magic-angle twisted bilayer graphene）中的可能应用。

Abstract: Electron-phonon coupling is a natural source of superconductivity, but in strongly correlated materials the phonon-mediated interaction is often much weaker than the on-site Hubbard repulsion $U$. Whether, and how, it can still produce pairing when $U$ dominates is a basic open question. We address this question by studying a minimal model with an anti-Hund's coupling $J_A$: an on-site spin exchange between two orbitals or two valleys, mediated by optical phonons, that favors a local inter-orbital spin singlet. In the limit $U=+\infty$, the two-orbital Hubbard model on the triangular lattice, doped away from the insulator at $n=2$ electrons per site, reduces to a four-flavor $t$-$J_A$ model. Density-matrix renormalization group calculations find robust pairing throughout $1\le J_A/|t|\le10$, with the largest pairing gap at $J_A\sim|t|$. The Cooper pairs reside on bonds and do not come from a direct attraction provided by $J_A$, since infinite $U$ excludes on-site pairs from the Hilbert space. For $J_A\gg|t|$, the normal state is a second Fermi liquid whose small Fermi surface violates the perturbative Luttinger theorem, and a strong-coupling expansion in $t/J_A$ provides a controlled effective theory of superconductivity from this unconventional metal. Our results provide strong evidence for a strongly coupled superconductor beyond BCS theory, driven by the interplay of the kinetic energy, the phonon-mediated anti-Hund's coupling, and the strong Hubbard $U$. Finally, we discuss possible applications to magic-angle twisted bilayer graphene.

</details>


### [188] [Spin-Wave Excitations in $\mathrm{La_3Ni_2O_7}$: A Dynamical Mean-Field Theory and Bethe-Salpeter Equation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33298)
*Zhihui Luo,Zhong-Yi Xie,Cui-Qun Chen,Wéi Wú,Dao-Xin Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文采用动态平均场理论（DMFT）结合Bethe-Salpeter方程（BSE），基于双层双轨道Kanamori-Hubbard模型研究La₃Ni₂O₇中的集体自旋激发，计算得到的阻尼自旋波谱与RIXS和INS实验数据吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在弥合La₃Ni₂O₇中自旋激发的局域描述与巡游描述之间的鸿沟，理解其集体自旋激发的微观机制。

Method: 基于密度泛函理论导出的双层双轨道Kanamori-Hubbard模型，进行DMFT计算并结合BSE求解晶格横向自旋磁化率，同时分析轨道分辨裸磁化率与不可约顶点。

Result: DMFT计算揭示了双自旋条纹基态；BSE计算得到带宽约0.1 eV、波矢为(π/2, π/2)的阻尼自旋波谱，与共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）和非弹性中子散射（INS）数据吻合。轨道分辨裸磁化率解释了自旋波线型的动量依赖，不可约顶点在d_{z²}轨道显示显著频率依赖，并考察了磁化率谱随Hund耦合和电子填充的演化。

Conclusion: 该研究实现了La₃Ni₂O₇中自旋激发的局域与巡游图像的统一描述。

Abstract: We perform a dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) + Bethe-Salpeter equation (BSE) study of collective spin excitations in $\mathrm{La_3Ni_2O_7}$, using a bilayer two-orbital Kanamori-Hubbard model derived from density functional theory. Our DMFT calculations reveal a double spin-stripe ground state. On this basis, we apply the BSE to compute the lattice transverse spin susceptibility, which exhibits a damped spin-wave spectrum with a bandwidth of $\sim$0.1 eV and a wave vector of ($π$/2, $π$/2), in good agreement with resonant inelastic x-ray scattering and inelastic neutron scattering data. We inspect the orbital-resolved bare susceptibility that provides insight into the momentum dependence of the spin-wave line shape. Further analysis of the irreducible vertex reveals notable frequency dependence in the $d_{z^2}$ orbital. Finally, we investigate the evolution of the spectrum with varying Hund's coupling and electron fillings. Our study bridges the localized and itinerant descriptions of spin excitations in $\mathrm{La_3Ni_2O_7}$.

</details>


### [189] [Exact high-order spectral moments and moment-based reconstruction of polaron spectra at finite temperature for arbitrary linear electron-phonon coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33431)
*Jeet Shannigrahi,Mona Berciu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出一族精确且高效的高阶频率矩递推关系，用于计算单带电子-声子哈密顿量（electron-phonon Hamiltonian）的单电子附加谱函数（spectral function），并据此实现极化子谱（polaron spectrum）的低成本近似重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中可获得的谱函数频率矩（frequency moments）阶数有限，缺乏对数值方法与实验谱的严格基准，且对未探索或数值上难以处理的模型缺少廉价而准确的近似手段。

Method: 在零载流子密度（zero carrier density）极限下，针对与谐声子线性耦合的通用单带电子-声子哈密顿量，推导精确且高效的频率矩递推关系（recurrence relations）；该形式适用于零温与有限温度，并可推广到任意维度与晶格几何。以 Holstein 与 Peierls 模型分别耦合 Einstein 声子与色散声子（dispersive phonons）为例给出闭式矩表达式，并将高阶矩用于弱至中等耦合、低温下极化子谱的近似重建。

Result: 以适中的计算代价获得了远高于文献已有水平的闭式高阶矩；重建的谱函数在弱耦合极限与原子极限下精确，并满足极化子能量与权重（polaron energy and weight）的严格界，同时表明自洽玻恩近似（self-consistent Born approximation）在整个耦合-绝热性平面内违反这些界。

Conclusion: 所提方法为数值方法与实验谱提供严格基准，并给出一种廉价而准确的近似途径，尤其适用于尚未探索或数值上难以处理的模型，可作为高成本精确方法应用之前的先行手段。

Abstract: We present a family of exact and efficient recurrence relations for the frequency moments of the single-electron addition spectral function of a generic single-band electron-phonon Hamiltonian with linear coupling to harmonic phonons. Working in the limit of zero carrier density, the formulation applies at zero and finite temperature and generalizes to arbitrary dimensions and lattice geometry. Using the Holstein and Peierls models coupled to Einstein and dispersive phonons as examples, we obtain closed-form expressions for moments of far higher order than those available in the literature, at modest computational cost. Beyond providing stringent benchmarks for numerical methods and experimental spectra, the high-order moments serve as input to approximate reconstructions of polaron spectra at weak-to-intermediate coupling and low temperature. The resulting spectral functions are exact in the weak-coupling and atomic limits, and respect rigorous bounds on the polaron energy and weight that we show the self-consistent Born approximation violates throughout the coupling-adiabaticity plane. The approach therefore offers an inexpensive yet accurate approximation, especially for unexplored or numerically intractable models, before computationally demanding exact methods are applied.

</details>


### [190] [Controlling truncation errors in loop tensor network renormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33807)
*Rikuto Fushio,Kokin Cho,Yuta Yamamoto,Akira Otsuki,Masahito Yamazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在有限键维 $χ$ 下，LoopTNR 对六顶角模型 $c=1$ 临界线的研究显示，截断注入的二聚化种子决定有隙相的关联长度 $ξ$，并可通过 sine-Gordon 质量-耦合关系无自由参数地预测 $ξ$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限键维 $χ$ 的张量网络重整化（tensor network renormalization, TNR）在处理临界模型时最终会打开能隙，其相关截断误差的来源与控制方式尚需系统澄清。

Method: 针对具有 $O(2)$ 对称性的 LoopTNR，在六顶角模型（six-vertex model）$c=1$ 临界线上以 Luttinger 参数 $K$ 参数化，分析截断注入的二聚化种子、其与 $χ$ 的关系，并借助 sine-Gordon 质量-耦合关系与 $π$ 扭曲符号读数进行校准；同时对粗粒化张量施加重复修正。

Result: 同一运行早期弱耦合窗口读出的种子可在数个百分点内（$K\\le1.5$）及约 10%（$K=1.75$）预测 $ξ=10^3$–$10^{16}$；因种子随 $χ$ 强烈涨落，$ξ$ 对 $χ$ 非单调。顶点权重交替移动种子，校准预测的反项在预先设定战役的 17 个补偿组中 16 组延迟了崩溃；重复修正使 $K=1$ 与 $1.5$ 保持临界至第 160 步，并暴露残余的边缘截断误差。

Conclusion: 研究给出了在有限键维 TNR 中控制相关截断误差、从而延长临界窗口的系统化流程。

Abstract: At finite bond dimension $χ$, tensor network renormalization (TNR) of a critical model eventually becomes gapped. For LoopTNR with $O(2)$ symmetry on the $c=1$ critical line of the six-vertex model, parametrized by the Luttinger parameter $K$, we show that the correlation length $ξ$ of the gapped phase is set by the seed of the dimerization injected by the truncation, and depends on $χ$ only through it. Read from a weak-coupling window early in the same run, the seed predicts $ξ=10^3$-$10^{16}$ via the sine-Gordon mass-coupling relation with no free parameter, to within a few percent for $K\le1.5$ and about 10% at $K=1.75$; since the seed fluctuates strongly with $χ$, $ξ$ is not monotonic in $χ$. An alternation of the vertex weights shifts the seed; with its sign read from a $π$ twist, one short calibration predicts a counterterm that delays the breakdown in 16 of the 17 compensated groups of a campaign fixed in advance. A correction of the coarse-grained tensors, repeated during the flow, keeps the runs at $K=1$ and $1.5$ critical up to step 160, the last step computed, and exposes the marginal truncation error that remains. Our findings illustrate a systematic procedure for controlling the relevant truncation error, and thereby extending the critical window, in tensor network renormalization at finite bond dimension.

</details>


### [191] [Spin-vibronic order in Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33814)
*Zhishuo Huang,Takumi Gengo,Teruki Matsuzaki,Liviu F. Chibotaru,Naoya Iwahara*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用第一性原理导出的自旋-振动耦合哈密顿量（spin-vibronic Hamiltonian），以单一弹性耦合标度因子复现了Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$的隐藏四极序（quadrupolar order）与倾斜磁序（canted magnetic order），并与RIXS谱所确定的振动子态（vibronic states）自洽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $5d$离子中的强自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）产生的多极序（multipolar orders）难以被常规探针探测；作者此前提出的振动子协同有序解释基于简化模型，需更严格的定量检验。

Method: 构建从第一性原理（first principles）推导的自旋-振动耦合哈密顿量，仅引入一个弹性耦合标度因子（scale factor），并沿用RIXS所确立的自旋-轨道-晶格纠缠振动子态进行计算。

Result: 将该因子调节至使四极转变温度为34.7 K（实验值33 K）后，理论预测磁转变温度为19.3 K（实验值18 K），并复现两转变之间的反铁畸变（antiferro distortion）与铁性伸长（ferro elongation）。

Conclusion: 结果支持Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$中的自旋-振动子有序（spin-vibronic order），并表明其是$5d^1$双钙钛矿（double perovskites）隐藏多极相的起源。

Abstract: Strong spin-orbit coupling in $5d$ ions produces multipolar orders that conventional probes barely detect. In cubic $5d^1$ double perovskites, the spin-orbit quartet couples to Jahn-Teller vibrations, and resonant inelastic x-ray scattering (RIXS) has established the resulting spin-orbit-lattice entangled (vibronic) states [Phys. Rev. Lett. \textbf{133}, 036501 (2024)]. We proposed that these vibronic states order cooperatively and thereby explain the hidden quadrupole and canted magnetic phases of Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$ [Phys. Rev. B \textbf{107}, L220404 (2023)], but that proposal rested on a simplified model. Here, we show that a spin-vibronic Hamiltonian derived from first principles, with a single scale factor for the elastic coupling, reproduces the ordered phases of Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$ with the same vibronic states that describe its RIXS spectra. With this factor bringing the quadrupolar transition to 34.7 K, near the measured 33 K, the theory predicts the magnetic transition at 19.3 K, against the measured 18 K, and reproduces the antiferro distortion and the ferro elongation between the transitions. These results support spin-vibronic order in Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$ and point to it as the origin of the hidden multipolar phases of $5d^1$ double perovskites.

</details>


### [192] [Nontrivial criticality and weakly first-order transitions in the frustrated spin-1 chain with single-ion anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34145)
*Qiang Luo,Shijie Hu,Jize Zhao,Sebastian Eggert,Xiaoqun Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合大规模密度矩阵重整化群与玻色化方法，本文绘制了含次近邻耦合与单离子各向异性的自旋-1链相图，揭示了弱一级相变、退禁闭量子临界点及多种涌现有序相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阻挫自旋链（frustrated spin chains）存在超越朗道范式（Landau paradigm）的奇异相，但弱一级相变与非传统临界性仍难以刻画，需系统研究阻挫与各向异性如何生成涌现有序（emergent order）与非朗道临界性。

Method: 将大规模密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group, DMRG）与玻色化（bosonization）方法相结合，并采用能级谱学（level spectroscopy）配合 Richardson 外推（Richardson extrapolation）定位连续相变。

Result: 识别出两类拓扑相变：Haldane 相与 NNN-Haldane 相之间的弱一级相变（能隙极小），以及 Haldane 相到大-$D$ 相的连续相变。负单离子各向异性下，Néel 相与 NNN-Néel 相之间出现自发二聚化相，其进入 Néel 相的转变为退禁闭量子临界点（deconfined quantum critical point），属八顶点普适类（eight-vertex universality class），中心电荷 $c=1$ 且临界指数沿相边界连续变化；正单离子各向异性下识别出普通手性相与窄的 Haldane-手性相两种矢量自旋手性序，其间为弱一级相变，表现为弦序参量的软化跳变。

Conclusion: 阻挫与各向异性可生成涌现有序与非朗道临界性，该体系为研究退禁闭相变与拓扑相变提供了平台。

Abstract: Frustrated spin chains host exotic phases beyond the Landau paradigm, yet weak first-order transitions and unconventional criticalities remain elusive. Combining large-scale density-matrix renormalization group with bosonization methods, we map the phase diagram of a spin-1 chain with next-nearest-neighbor (NNN) coupling and single-ion anisotropy (SIA). We pinpoint two topological phase transitions: a weakly first-order transition between the Haldane and NNN-Haldane phases with a tiny gap and a continuous transition from the Haldane phase to the large-$D$ phase located via level spectroscopy with Richardson extrapolation. For negative SIA, a spontaneous dimerized phase emerges between the Néel and NNN-Néel phases. Its transition into the Néel phase is a deconfined quantum critical point falling into the eight-vertex universality class, with central charge $c = 1$ and critical exponents varying continuously along the phase boundary. For positive SIA, we identify two vector-spin-chirality orders---an ordinary chiral phase and a narrow Haldane-chiral phase---with a weakly first-order transition between them, evidenced by a soft jump in the string order parameter. Our results reveal that frustration and anisotropy generate emergent orders and non-Landau criticalities, providing a platform for deconfined and topological phase transitions.

</details>


### [193] [Quantum-centric assessment of saturated ferromagnetism beyond the flat-band limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34204)
*Kosuke Nogaki,Tomonori Shirakawa,Seiji Yunoki,Ryotaro Arita*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作利用量子选择组态相互作用（quantum-selected configuration interaction）与基于采样的量子对角化（sample-based quantum diagonalization），在 IBM Heron 处理器上研究色散能带在四分之一填充下的饱和铁磁性（saturated ferromagnetism），给出 U–W 平面上的稳定性相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带 Hubbard 模型中任意 U>0 即可实现饱和铁磁性已被证明，但能带具有色散时所需相互作用随带宽 W 增长；需要在色散情形、四分之一填充下定量确定饱和铁磁性的稳定区域与临界相互作用。

Method: 采用量子选择组态相互作用与基于采样的量子对角化，在 IBM Quantum Heron 处理器上计算，重点关注所获态的总自旋平方的量子化取值；研究对象为 64 量子比特上 16 个单胞的锯齿链（sawtooth chain）及 48 量子比特上 4×3 单胞的装饰方格子（decorated square lattice）。

Result: 锯齿链的全组态空间达 1.1×10^14 个行列式，超过最大规模的全组态相互作用（full configuration interaction）计算之一；仅占全空间 3×10^-5 的子空间即可给出超过 97% 权重处于最大自旋扇区的态。4×3 装饰方格子中，占比不足 1% 的子空间所计算态在 U≃2W 处由低自旋态转为近完全极化态，并给出临界相互作用 1.9W 的严格下界。

Conclusion: 结果在 U–W 平面上给出装饰方格子上饱和铁磁性的稳定性相图，表明极小子空间量子计算即可捕捉强关联自旋极化行为，并为 4×3 格子临界相互作用提供严格下界。

Abstract: Saturated ferromagnetism has been proven for every on-site repulsion $U > 0$ in Hubbard models with a flat lowest band, and the interaction required for it grows with the band width $W$ when the band becomes dispersive. We study the dispersive case at quarter filling by quantum-selected configuration interaction and sample-based quantum diagonalization with IBM Quantum's Heron processors, focusing on the quantized square of the total spin of the computed states. We treat a sawtooth chain of 16 unit cells on 64 qubits, whose full configuration space of $1.1\times 10^{14}$ determinants exceeds that of one of the largest full configuration interaction calculations, and obtain the stability diagram of saturated ferromagnetism on the decorated square lattice in the plane of $U$ and $W$. For the chain, a subspace of only $3\times 10^{-5}$ of the full configuration space gives a state with more than $97\%$ of its weight in the sector of maximal spin. For the decorated square lattice of $4\times 3$ unit cells on 48 qubits, the computed states in subspaces of less than $1\%$ of the full configuration space switch from low-spin states to nearly fully polarized states at $U \simeq 2W$. The calculations also give a rigorous lower bound of $1.9W$ on the critical interaction of the $4\times 3$ lattice.

</details>


### [194] [Hidden f-wave TIVC and Quantum Phase Transitions in Metallic States of Twisted Bilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34225)
*Arindam Pramanik,Unmesh Ghorai,Rajdeep Sensarma*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究魔角转角双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene, TBG）在填充因子 ν=-2 至 ν=-3 区间、随门极距离（gate distance）调控库仑相互作用（Coulomb interaction）时的基态相图。研究发现近 ν=-2 的 Kramers 谷间相干（Kramers intervalley coherent, KIVC）相内部隐藏着 f 波、时间反演对称的谷间相干（TIVC）序，并揭示随载流子密度降低依次出现谷极化相与量子反常霍尔（quantum anomalous Hall）相的多次相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画魔角转角双层石墨烯基态相图随载流子密度与库仑相互作用的演化，并检验近期实验在超净样品中报道的 TIVC 相特征的理论依据。

Method: 方法为在魔角下以载流子密度（填充因子 ν=-2 到 ν=-3）与库仑相互作用强度（通过改变门极距离调节）为参数，计算系统的基态相图，并分析各相的序参量与费米轮廓（Fermi contours）重构。

Result: 结果表明，近 ν=-2 已确立的 KIVC 相中包含隐藏的 f 波、时间反演对称的谷间相干（TIVC）序，其强度在 IVC 相金属区域内随远离 ν=-2 而逐渐增强。随密度降低，系统由 IVC 相经类二阶相变（second-order-like phase transition）进入谷极化相（valley polarized phase），进一步调控密度则通过亚晶格极化（sublattice polarization）的逐步发展进入量子反常霍尔相。上述相变均伴随费米轮廓的显著重构。

Conclusion: 结论指出，魔角转角双层石墨烯在 ν=-2 至 ν=-3 区间呈现由密度驱动的多级相变序列，并在 IVC 金属区内存在渐强的隐藏 TIVC 序，这与近期超净样品中的实验迹象相符，凸显了谷间相干序内部结构的丰富性。

Abstract: We study the ground state phase diagram of twisted bilayer graphene at magic angle as function of carrier density and the Coulomb interaction. The carrier density is varied between the fillings $ν=-2$ and $ν=-3$ and the Coulomb interaction is tuned by changing the gate distance. Our results reveal that the well established Kramers intervalley coherent (KIVC) phase near $ν=-2$, contains a hidden $f$-wave time-reversal symmetric intervalley coherent (TIVC) order, whose strength gradually increases within the metallic regime of the intervalley coherent (IVC) phase away from $ν=-2$. This finding is interesting in the light of a recent experimental work [26], where the signatures of the TIVC phase has been reported in ultra-clean samples. We further show that the system goes through multiple phase transitions as the carrier density is tuned. Near the $ν=-2$, the IVC phase is the ground state, which gives way to valley polarized phase through a second-order-like phase transition as the carrier density is lowered. Further tuning of density pushes the system into the quantum anomalous Hall phase through gradual development of the sublattice polarization. These phase transitions are accompanied by major reconstructions of the Fermi contours.

</details>


### [195] [Fractional Chern Insulator in a Finite-Range One-Band Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34255)
*Tong Liu,Hoi Chun Po*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文证明分数量子陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulators, FCIs）可在无单粒子量子几何（single-particle quantum geometry）的单带模型中由相互作用驱动产生，并给出方晶格上仅含元胞内（plaquette）项的单带纯相互作用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流实现FCI的思路是构造模拟朗道能级（Landau levels）的能带，其作为强关联分数量子霍尔态的单粒子拓扑背景；作者质疑单粒子量子几何是否为FCI的必要条件。

Method: 在超越朗道能级范式的FCI模型中系统性地"平凡化"单粒子量子几何，从而构造出方晶格（square lattice）上的单带、纯相互作用模型，所有项局限于单个元胞（plaquette）；采用精确对角化（exact diagonalization）与密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization-group）进行计算。

Result: 在1/3填充下发现三态低能流形（three-state low-energy manifold）、霍尔电导（Hall conductance）为$-2e^2/(3h)$，且存在无平移对称破缺（translation-symmetry breaking）的分数量子电荷泵浦（fractional charge pumping）。

Conclusion: 该行为源于排斥性两粒子扇区（repulsive two-particle sector）中的非平凡拓扑，为分数量子霍尔序（fractional quantum Hall order）的强关联起源提供了微观视角。

Abstract: A common route for realizing fractional Chern insulators (FCIs) is to engineer bands that mimic the physics of Landau levels, which serve as the single-particle topological background for the emergence of strongly correlated fractional quantum Hall states. Here we show that FCIs can also arise in a one-band model devoid of any single-particle quantum geometry. Through a systematic trivialization of the single-particle quantum geometry in FCI models that go beyond the Landau-level paradigm, we construct a single-band, interaction-only model on the square lattice, with all terms confined to individual plaquettes. At one-third filling, exact diagonalization and density-matrix renormalization-group calculations reveal a three-state low-energy manifold, a Hall conductance of $-2e^2/(3h)$, and fractional charge pumping without translation-symmetry breaking. We trace this behavior to nontrivial topology in the repulsive two-particle sector, offering a microscopic perspective on the strongly correlated origin of fractional quantum Hall order.

</details>


### [196] [Spin-Orbit Coupling in Symmetry-Forbidden Reactions: A Selection Rule Tested on the Chalcogen Trimers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34375)
*Shadan Ghassemi Tabrizi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出并检验一条基于自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的轨道对称性禁忌规则：不同对称性的占据与空轨道沿反应路径交换位置时反应被禁阻，而在自旋轨道耦合下轨道变为旋量（spinor），仅当两轨道相差一个旋转的对称性时才能混合。以氧族三聚体 O3→Po3 的开环反应为检验对象，结果表明该规则所指的算符分量主导势垒降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轨道对称性守恒规则（orbital symmetry rules）在无自旋轨道耦合时判定某些反应禁阻，但引入自旋轨道耦合后轨道变为旋量，原有禁阻判据需要修正；本文旨在给出并验证一条针对该情形的新对称性规则。

Method: 在 C2v 势能面上研究氧族三聚体 O3 至 Po3 的开环反应，其中一条面内轨道与一条面外轨道发生交叉；采用 NEVPT2 与自旋轨道组态相互作用（spin-orbit configuration interaction），并令自旋轨道算符各分量分别作用以检验规则。

Result: 在 Se3 与 Te3 中，规则所指的分量单独作用即分别使势垒降低 2.2 与 5.1 kcal/mol（完整算符为 2.2 与 5.2），另两个分量仅降低 0.2 与 1.3 kcal/mol；Po3 中则为二阶贡献。三重态在禁阻鞍点下方 6.5 至 9.0 kcal/mol 处与基态交叉；自旋轨道耦合使该交叉在 O3 与 S3 中可通过，自 Se3 起两态并合为单一势能面，其鞍点分别降低 2.6、5.5 与 13.1 kcal/mol。

Conclusion: 自旋轨道耦合可缓解轨道对称性禁阻，并随原子序数增大在 Se3 起使基态与三重态并合为单一反应面；分子整体旋转遵循同一规则但耦合强度弱 6 至 2.8×10^3 倍，这一结果对环状臭氧（cyclic ozone）寿命的预测具有意义。

Abstract: A reaction is forbidden by orbital symmetry when an occupied and an empty orbital of different symmetry change places along its path. With spin-orbit coupling orbitals become spinors, and those of two such orbitals can mix only if the orbitals differ by the symmetry of a rotation; the operator component of that symmetry couples ground state and diradical triplet. We test this rule on the ring opening of the chalcogen trimers O3 to Po3, where an in-plane and an out-of-plane orbital cross ($C_{2v}$ surfaces; NEVPT2, spin-orbit configuration interaction), by letting the operator components act separately. In Se3 and Te3 the component named by the rule alone lowers the barrier by 2.2 and 5.1 kcal/mol (complete operator: 2.2 and 5.2), the other two by 0.2 and 1.3 kcal/mol; in Po3 they contribute in second order. The triplet crosses the ground state 6.5 to 9.0 kcal/mol below the forbidden saddle. Spin-orbit coupling makes this crossing passable in O3 and S3 and, from Se3 on, merges the two states into one surface whose saddle lies 2.6, 5.5, and 13.1 kcal/mol lower. Molecular rotation obeys the same rule but is 6 to $2.8\times 10^{3}$ times weaker, which bears on the lifetime predicted for cyclic ozone.

</details>


### [197] [Signatures of multiorbital density waves in the electronic Raman response of bilayer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34471)
*Lauro B. Braz,Ge He,Luis G. G. V. Dias da Silva,Ilya M. Eremin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作基于含 d_{3z^2-r^2} 与 d_{x^2-y^2} 轨道的双层两轨道模型，指出低压双层镍酸盐的自旋密度波（spin-density-wave, SDW）态在不同对称性通道中产生轨道选择性拉曼信号：B_{2g} 峰位于 (M_{z^2}+M_{x^2})/2，B_{1g} 主峰位于 (M_{z^2}^2+M_{x^2}^2)^{1/2}，从而统一解释 La_3Ni_2O_7 的多项拉曼特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层镍酸盐的拉曼实验已观测到 SDW 态中的显著谱学特征，但其微观起源存在争议，解释从双磁振子（two-magnon）激发到跨越 SDW 能隙的电子跃迁不等，亟需厘清其电子结构根源。

Method: 构建正交结构下包含 d_{3z^2-r^2} 与 d_{x^2-y^2} 轨道的两轨道双层模型（two-orbital bilayer model），计算多轨道“无自旋”双条纹 SDW 态（spin-spinless double stripe SDW state）的电子拉曼响应（electronic Raman response），并按对称性通道分析。

Result: B_{2g} 响应在 (M_{z^2}+M_{x^2})/2 能量尺度出现峰值，对应弱杂化能带中的 SDW 能隙打开；B_{1g} 通道的主导峰则出现在多轨道尺度 (M_{z^2}^2+M_{x^2}^2)^{1/2}，反映相关电子态的强杂化特征。

Conclusion: 不同能量尺度的峰位可统一解释 La_3Ni_2O_7 中多项拉曼特征，并提供分离各轨道对 SDW 序参量贡献的途径；与超导态不同，SDW 态的拉曼峰位一般不能等同于单一 SDW 能隙，对称分辨电子拉曼散射（symmetry-resolved electronic Raman scattering）可作为多轨道密度波序的灵敏探针。

Abstract: Recent Raman scattering experiments on bilayer nickelates have revealed pronounced spectral features in the spin-density-wave state, whose microscopic origin remains controversial, with interpretations ranging from two-magnon excitations to electronic transitions across SDW-induced gaps. Here, we demonstrate that the multiorbital electronic structure of low-pressure bilayer nickelates naturally generates distinct orbital-selective Raman signatures in different symmetry channels. Using a two-orbital bilayer model incorporating the $d_{3z^2-r^2}$ and $d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbitals in the orthorhombic structure, we analyze the electronic Raman response of the multiorbital spin-spinless double stripe SDW state and show that the $B_{2g}$ response exhibits a peak at the energy scale $(M_{z^2}+M_{x^2})/2$, associated with SDW gap openings in weakly hybridized bands. In contrast, the dominant peak in the $B_{1g}$ channel emerges at the multiorbital energy scale $(M_{z^2}^2+M_{x^2}^2)^{1/2}$, reflecting the strongly hybridized character of the relevant electronic states. These distinct energy scales provide a unified interpretation of several Raman signatures observed in La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ and offer a means of disentangling the orbital contributions to the SDW order parameters. Our results highlight that in contrast to the superconducting state, Raman peak positions in the SDW state cannot generally be identified with a single SDW gap and establish symmetry-resolved electronic Raman scattering as a sensitive probe of multiorbital density-wave order.

</details>


### [198] [Emergent Chern metal in strongly correlated moiré bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34780)
*Kaushal Kumar Kesharpu,Evgenii A. Kochetov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了强关联莫尔过渡金属硫族化物双层中自旋-电荷分离态与层极化螺旋自旋织构耦合的拓扑性质，发现非共面自旋子背景下空穴能带的显著不对称翘曲会导致拓扑陈金属相（C=±1），并给出可观的非量子化反常霍尔响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对莫尔体系拓扑相的认识多基于弱关联或共面自旋背景，层极化螺旋自旋织构与局域自旋-电荷分离态耦合下的拓扑行为尚不明确，需澄清强关联下是否仍存在真正的拓扑绝缘体。

Method: 构建描述无自旋空穴（holon）在非共面自旋子（spinon）背景下运动的有效低能哈密顿量（effective low-energy Hamiltonian），分析能带翘曲（band warping），并在参数空间中计算陈数（Chern number）与态密度（density of states）。

Result: 不对称能带翘曲在大部分参数空间中闭合全局间接带隙（indirect band gap），使真正的拓扑绝缘体（topological insulator）仅限于微调临界轨迹；但局域直接带隙（direct gap）保持有限，稳定了宽广的陈金属（Chern metal，C=±1）区域，并伴随可观的非量子化反常霍尔响应与有限纵向电导。

Conclusion: 该工作提出一种纯电子的强耦合机制（strong-coupling mechanism），用于解释莫尔体系中耗散型反常霍尔态（dissipative anomalous Hall state）的起源，并将拓扑绝缘体的存在条件限定为临界参数轨迹。

Abstract: We investigate the topological properties of a strongly correlated moiré transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) bilayer (such as twisted $\mathrm{MoTe}_2$ or $\mathrm{WSe}_2$) hosting a spin-charge separated state coupled to a layer-polarized helical spin texture. Analyzing an effective low-energy Hamiltonian for mobile spinless holons moving through this non-coplanar spinon background, we reveal pronounced asymmetric band warping. This warping closes the global indirect band gap across most of the parameter space, restricting true topological insulators to fine-tuned critical trajectories. However, a finite local direct gap persists, stabilizing broad regions of a topological Chern metal ($C = \pm 1$). Computing the Chern number and density of states, we demonstrate that this phase naturally yields a sizable, non-quantized anomalous Hall response accompanied by finite longitudinal conduction, providing a purely electronic strong-coupling mechanism for dissipative anomalous Hall states in moiré systems.

</details>


### [199] [Spin Loop Currents and Bulk-Edge Structure in Correlated Topological States of WSe$_2$/MoTe$_2$ Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34880)
*Po-Jui Chen,Kevin P. Lucht,Andreas Gleis,Andrew J. Millis,J. H. Pixley,Daniele Guerci*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在 parton 平均场框架下理论研究 AB 堆叠 WSe₂/MoTe₂ 双层，发现 MoTe₂ 层载流子间的非局域相互作用可在 ν=2 时驱动拓扑近藤半金属到拓扑近藤绝缘体的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期实验启发，理论探究 AB 堆叠 WSe₂/MoTe₂ 双层中价涨落、局域与扩展哈伯德相互作用（尤其 MoTe₂ 层的非局域相互作用）的效应。

Method: 理论包含价涨落、局域及扩展（长程）哈伯德相互作用，并在 parton 平均场（parton mean-field）框架下处理。

Result: MoTe₂ 载流子间的非局域相互作用可在填充率 ν=2 时驱动拓扑近藤半金属（TKSM）与拓扑近藤绝缘体（TKI）之间的转变，该转变归因于 Ising 自旋轨道耦合与非局域电子关联共同产生的自旋环流（spin-loop current）。

Conclusion: 进一步研究表明自旋环流影响边缘输运，两点电导在特定偏压下取量子化值。

Abstract: Motivated by recent experiments, we theoretically investigate AB-stacked WSe$_2$/MoTe$_2$ bilayers. Our theory incorporates valence fluctuations, local and extended (longer-range) Hubbard interactions, including non-local interactions between carriers on the MoTe$_2$ layer. Within the parton mean-field framework, we show that the non-local interaction between MoTe$_2$ carriers can drive a transition between a topological Kondo semimetal(TKSM) and a topological Kondo insulator(TKI) at the filling fraction $ν=2$. We attribute this transition to an emergent spin-loop current arising from the interplay between Ising spin-orbit coupling and non-local electron correlations. In addition, we investigate how the emergent spin-loop current affects edge transport and show that the two-point conductance takes a quantized value at certain bias voltages.

</details>


### [200] [A microscopic continuum model for moiré transition metal dichalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34937)
*Julian Ingham,Harshitra Mahalingam,Raquel Queiroz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文给出扭角过渡金属硫族化物（twisted transition metal dichalcogenides, tTMDs）的一个微观推导的连续模型（continuum model），其参数与扭角无关，可准确描述多个扭角下的能带结构；模型同时保留单层价带与导带，并揭示其对层间耦合、量子几何与轨道磁矩的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: tTMDs 是研究分数陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulators）与超导等强关联相的重要平台，但缺乏一个参数不随扭角变化、且能跨多个扭角精确描述能带的微观连续模型，故需建立此类模型并阐明其物理来源。

Method: 构建连续模型：保留单层价带（valence band）与导带（conduction band），以计入通过 AB 堆叠区域（AB-stacked regions）的隧穿路径（tunnelling paths）；再下折叠（downfolding）至价带，使这些路径对层间耦合（interlayer coupling）产生可随扭角调节的显著贡献。

Result: 该模型可视为具有魔角（magic angle）的手性模型（chiral model）的微扰，从而解释莫尔能带（moiré bands）近乎理想的量子几何（quantum geometry）；模型给出与单轨道理论显著不同的轨道磁矩（orbital magnetic moment），预言反常的朗道能级斜率（Landau level slope），并产生层间电荷重新分布，改变莫尔铁电势（moiré ferroelectric potential）。

Conclusion: 保留价带与导带的双带处理是获得扭角无关参数描述的关键，并可统一解释莫尔能带的量子几何、轨道磁性及层间电荷分布等物理性质。

Abstract: Twisted transition metal dichalcogenides (tTMDs) have emerged as a fascinating platform for many-body phases --- displaying fractional Chern insulators and superconductivity as a function of doping. Here, we give a microscopic derivation of a continuum model which accurately describes their bandstructure across a range of twist angles with angle-independent parameters. This angle-independent description rests on retaining both the monolayer valence and conduction bands, which introduce tunnelling paths through AB-stacked regions; upon downfolding to the valence band, these paths supply a substantial twist-tunable contribution to the interlayer coupling. We show that the model is a perturbation of a chiral model with a magic angle, explaining the near-ideal quantum geometry of the moiré bands. The model further produces a strongly different orbital magnetic moment to the single-orbital theory --- predicting an anomalous Landau level slope --- and a redistribution of charge between the layers, modifying the moiré ferroelectric potential.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

### [201] [Almost sure lower bound for the Hausdorff dimension of the spectrum of critical AMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33930)
*Yanhui Qu,Pengyu Wang*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该文证明存在绝对常数（absolute constant）$d>0$，使得对几乎所有频率（almost all frequencies）$\alpha\in[0,1]\setminus\mathbb{Q}$，临界几乎马蒂厄算子（critical almost Mathieu operator）$H_{1,\alpha,\theta}$ 的谱（spectrum）的豪斯多夫维数（Hausdorff dimension）有下界 $d$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确说明研究动机，仅指出所关注的对象为临界几乎马蒂厄算子谱的豪斯多夫维数在几乎所有频率下的下界估计问题。

Method: 摘要仅陈述结论形式，即构造或证明一个与 $\alpha$、$\theta$ 无关的绝对常数 $d$，未给出具体技术手段或证明路线。

Result: 得到一致的正下界：对几乎所有的无理频率 $\alpha$，$\dim_H \sigma(H_{1,\alpha,\theta})\ge d$，其中 $d>0$ 为绝对常数。

Conclusion: 临界几乎马蒂厄算子的谱在几乎所有频率下具有一致正维数，即谱集不可能在豪斯多夫维数意义下任意稀薄；该结论以绝对常数给出与参数无关的统一刻画。

Abstract: We show that there exists an absolute constant $d>0$ such that for almost all frequencies $α\in[0,1]\setminus\Q$, the Hausdorff dimension of the spectrum of the critical almost Mathieu operator $H_{1,α,θ}$ is bounded from below by $d$.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>
