### [202] [Can the chemical potential and the many-body correlated phases of a two-dimensional dipolar fluid be approximated analytically? A Quantum Monte-Carlo study of a universal behavior of dipolar excitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33289)
*Yotam Mazuz-Harpaz,Michal Zimmerman,Snir Gazit,Ronen Rapaport*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 利用量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo, QMC）模拟二维排斥偶极玻色子（dipolar bosons），发现粒子周围的显著耗尽区（depletion region）导致短程物理被抑制，从而产生由偶极相互作用主导的普适性（universality），并据此建立经QMC定量验证的统一解析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体物理的核心挑战在于将微观相互作用与实验可测量的宏观物态联系起来，尤其当关联（correlations）跨越多个长度尺度时；本文旨在为已在多种实验平台实现的二维偶极玻色流体提供统一、可验证的描述框架。

Method: 采用量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）方法模拟二维排斥偶极玻色子，以间接（层间）激子（indirect (interlayer) excitons）为模型体系，将先前研究导出的解析相互作用能与精确QMC结果在三种关联区域（经典气体、量子气体、高密度液体）进行比较，并构建化学势（chemical potential）的三分量分解及密度标定曲线。

Result: 粒子周围的相关性诱导出显著的耗尽区，抑制短程物理并产生由偶极相互作用而非材料细节主导的涌现普适性（emergent universality）；解析相互作用能在三种关联区域与QMC结果一致，而常用的无关联平板电容器（plate capacitor）公式严重高估数值；化学势可由理想玻色气体动能项、区域依赖的偶极相互作用项与高密度量子限制项三部分准确描述。

Conclusion: 针对从测得能移（energy shifts）提取激子密度的关键实验难题，给出了经QMC基准标定的砷化镓（GaAs）与过渡金属硫族化合物（TMD）异质结构标定曲线，可同时确定流体密度与集体态；该普适框架可推广至超冷偶极原子（ultracold dipolar atoms）与极性分子等其它二维体系。

Abstract: A central challenge in quantum many-body physics is to connect microscopic interactions to experimentally accessible macroscopic states of matter, where correlations extend across multiple length scales. Using quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) simulations of two-dimensional repulsive dipolar bosons, recently realized across various experimental platforms, we show that correlations within the dipolar fluid lead to an unexpected simplification. Specifically, we find that these correlations induce a pronounced depletion region around each particle that suppresses short-range physics and yields an emergent universality, where the fluid is governed by dipolar interactions rather than material-specific details. Exploiting this universality, we establish a unified, quantitatively validated analytical framework for the dipolar fluid, taking indirect (interlayer) excitons as a model system. We show that analytical interaction energies derived in an earlier study agree with exact QMC results across three correlation regimes: a classical gas, a quantum gas, and a high-density liquid. In contrast, the widely used uncorrelated plate capacitor formula severely overestimates the numerical values. Building on these expressions, we show that the chemical potential is accurately captured by a three-component decomposition: an ideal Bose-gas kinetic term, a regime-dependent dipolar interaction term, and a high-density quantum confinement term. Finally, we address a key experimental challenge: extracting exciton densities from measured energy shifts. Our QMC-benchmarked calibration curves for typical gallium arsenide (GaAs) and transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) heterostructures offer a reliable tool for experimentalists to determine both the fluid's density and collective state. This universal framework extends to other two-dimensional systems, including ultracold dipolar atoms and polar molecules.

</details>


### [203] [Bosonic Magic Resources at Quantum Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33306)
*Paolo Stornati,Piotr Sierant,Emanuele Tirrito*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文利用量子相对熵（quantum relative entropy, QRE）量化一维扩展Bose-Hubbard模型基态的非高斯性（non-Gaussianity），并以其随系统尺寸的标度行为识别涉及隐藏序（hidden order）与常规序的量子相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非高斯性是连续变量玻色量子信息处理的关键资源，也是通用量子计算的必要成分；但该资源与强关联多体临界性之间的联系尚待建立，因此需要可行的量化手段来刻画基态非高斯性及其标度行为。

Method: 采用仅由单体关联（one-body correlations）决定的量子相对熵作为非高斯性度量，计算一维扩展Bose-Hubbard模型基态中QRE随系统尺寸的标度行为，并将其与Luttinger液体（Luttinger-liquid）关联、Ising临界性（Ising criticality）等已知理论预言对照。

Result: 在弱相互作用超流相中，QRE标度可与Luttinger液体关联相联系，其广延项（extensive contribution）与次领头项（subleading contribution）呈现不同的临界特征；在Mott-Haldane转变处，扣除广延背景后次领头项出现显著峰值，标志隐藏弦序（string order）的开启；在Haldane-密度波转变处，广延项则表现出与Ising临界性一致的标度。

Conclusion: 结果确立了非高斯性可作为多体临界性的探针，从而将玻色量子资源与强关联物质（strongly correlated matter）研究相联系。

Abstract: Non-Gaussianity is a resource for bosonic quantum information processing, providing a necessary ingredient for universal quantum computation in continuous-variable systems. Here we quantify non-Gaussianity in the ground state of the one-dimensional extended Bose-Hubbard model using quantum relative entropy (QRE), a measure determined solely by one-body correlations. The scaling of the QRE with system size reveals quantum phase transitions involving hidden and conventional order. We connect QRE scaling in the weakly interacting superfluid phase to Luttinger-liquid correlations and identify distinct critical signatures in its extensive and subleading contributions. At the Mott-Haldane transition, subtracting the extensive background reveals a pronounced peak in the subleading contribution, providing a clear signature of the onset of hidden string order. At the Haldane-density-wave transition, the extensive contribution to the QRE instead exhibits scaling consistent with Ising criticality. These results establish non-Gaussianity as a probe of many-body criticality, linking bosonic quantum resources to strongly correlated matter.

</details>


### [204] [Designing quantum dynamics in squeezed potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34309)
*Rhombik Roy,Ofir E. Alon*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该论文研究二维通道中具有两个局域挤压（squeezes）的相互作用玻色子，揭示几何诱导的有效势垒可调控基态密度分布、产生多体碎裂（many-body fragmentation），并主导自囚禁（self-trapping）、振荡周期、角动量产生与多体坍缩等非平衡输运行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何被视为控制量子输运（quantum transport）的多功能平台，但局域几何收缩如何同时影响相互作用玻色子的基态性质与非平衡动力学尚待系统阐明，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建二维通道模型，在其中设置两个局域挤压，沿纵向（longitudinal direction）产生有效势垒；在此基础上分析相互作用玻色子的基态密度分布与失平衡动力学，并考察两挤压从相反横向方向引入时的影响。

Result: 几何诱导势垒改变基态密度分布并诱发多体碎裂；挤压通道可调控自囚禁程度与振荡周期；多体层面出现动力学碎裂（dynamical fragmentation），导致密度振荡坍缩（collapse）；反向横向挤压可在输运过程中产生振荡角动量（angular momentum），并与不同碎裂模式对应的多体坍缩行为相关联。

Conclusion: 工程化挤压束缚（engineered squeezed confinement）可作为设计基态性质与调控量子输运的有力工具，涵盖角动量产生与丰富的多体输运动力学。

Abstract: Geometry offers a versatile platform to control quantum transport. We investigate the ground-state properties and out-of-equilibrium dynamics of interacting bosons in a two-dimensional channel with two localized squeezes. The squeezes generate effective barriers along the longitudinal direction. These geometry-induced barriers modify the ground-state density distribution and lead to the emergence of many-body fragmentation. The squeezed channel also provides control over the transport dynamics, including the degree of self-trapping and the oscillation period. At the many-body level, the transport develops dynamical fragmentation, leading to the collapse of the density oscillations. We further demonstrate that introducing the two squeezes from opposite transverse directions generates oscillatory angular momentum during transport. The modified geometry also exhibits distinct many-body collapse behaviors associated with different patterns of fragmentation. Together, these results establish engineered squeezed confinement as a powerful tool for designing the ground-state properties and controlling the quantum transport, including angular-momentum generation and rich many-body transport dynamics.

</details>


### [205] [Moiré-induced self-trapping in a Bose-Einstein Condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34908)
*Keshav Badri,Kevin P. Lucht,Justin H. Wilson,Jedediah H. Pixley*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作将莫尔调制（moiré modulation）思想推广到蜂窝晶格上相互作用玻色子的准周期跃迁模型，揭示非相互作用下的魔角（magic-angle）转变与相互作用诱导的自囚禁（self-trapping）及扩散波包动力学，并提出一种求解无平移对称性下 Gross-Pitaevskii 方程的高效数值方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料中的莫尔调制可压平电子能带并增强相互作用效应，从而产生多种涌现电子相；作者希望检验这种机制能否推广到准周期（quasiperiodic）跃迁的相互作用玻色子体系。

Method: 构建蜂窝晶格（honeycomb lattice）上相互作用玻色子的准周期跃迁模型；在无相互作用情形分析魔角转变处的速度归零、零能态密度（density of states）突变与多重分形（multifractal）低能态；在含相互作用情形通过调节自囚禁与扩散波包动力学相关尺度研究其与准周期性的相互作用；数值上采用分步时间演化（split-step time evolution）与截断 Chebyshev 展开相结合的方法求解 Gross-Pitaevskii 方程。

Result: 无相互作用时速度在魔角转变处消失，同时出现零能态密度陡增和多重分形低能态；在魔角附近，显著更弱的相互作用即可导致自囚禁与扩散波包动力学，作者将其归因于莫尔诱导的微带宽度（minibandwidth）趋于零。

Conclusion: 莫尔调制效应可延伸至准周期相互作用玻色子体系，魔角附近微带宽度收缩放大了相互作用的作用；所提数值方法为无平移对称性下的 Gross-Pitaevskii 方程提供通用高效求解途径，结果可在超冷原子（ultracold atom）实验的动量空间晶格（momentum space lattices）或光镊阵列（tweezer arrays）中检验。

Abstract: The effect of moiré modulations on two-dimensional materials flattens their electronic energy bands, which enhances the effects of interactions and has been seen to give rise to several emergent electronic phases. We demonstrate this behavior with a moiré modulation extends to a quasiperiodic hopping model of interacting bosons on the honeycomb lattice. Without interactions, the velocity vanishes at a magic-angle transition, where a sharp onset of the zero-energy density of states and multifractal low-energy states appear. Including interactions, we study its interplay with quasiperiodicity by tuning the scale associated with self-trapping and diffusive wave packet dynamics. Near the magic-angle, we find that substantially weaker interactions lead to self-trapping and diffusive wavepacket dynamics, which we attribute to the vanishing moiré-induced minibandwidth. A new computational approach is presented that combines split-step time evolution with a truncated Chebyshev expansion as an efficient and general purpose approach to solving the Gross-Pitaevskii equation in the absence of translational symmetry. We discuss how our results can be explored in ultracold atom experiments using momentum space lattices or tweezer arrays.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [206] [Geometry Sets the Neutral MLIP-DFT Gap in Non-Covalent Binding, and Charged Systems Fail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33830)
*Rui Ding,Zixin Ding,Rodrigo Pires Ferreira,Diantong Li,Junhong Chen,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 预训练通用机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）在中性化学的非共价结合筛选上可与显式DFT相当，但在带电体系上系统性失效。作者提出免训练的推理阶段支架RAPIDS，实现与DFT相当的精度、更低算力及可控的灾难性失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域内普遍预期预训练通用MLIP可替代显式量子化学（quantum chemistry）用于分子相互作用筛选；作者检验该预期在中性化学与带电化学两类非共价结合（non-covalent binding）上是否成立。

Method: 在9个保真度分支（fidelity arms）、18个基准（benchmarks）的5,567个探针-靶标二聚体上，对未做任务微调的FAIRChem UMA OMol头进行审计；所用RAPIDS（Rapid Atomistic Probe-target Interaction Discovery Scaffold）为免训练（training-free）的推理期支架，并辅以RAPIDS-Select闭式规则与湿实验基准WetSenseBench评估。

Result: 中性化学上RAPIDS平均绝对误差（mean absolute error, MAE）为1.37 kcal/mol，匹配或优于所有显式DFT单点保真度（1.42-1.50 kcal/mol），残余差距源于几何结构（geometry）优化不足；带电体系MAE升至8.5-22.9 kcal/mol，单点DFT重打分（rescoring）无效，几何重优化（geometry reoptimization）可修复离子氢键与离子-分子二聚体，且失效跨三个MLIP骨干（backbones）一致存在。残差失效具有结构性：带符号偏差按化学族聚类，保真度是否发生灾难性失败（catastrophic failure）比误差幅度更可跨族迁移。

Conclusion: RAPIDS-Select在同等精度下较强静态DFT基线节省约37%算力，并在新计算带电基准上将灾难率从68%降至5.7%；在WetSenseBench上，无DFT后续步骤的RAPIDS在高于约1 kcal/mol精度下限的七项研究中达到82.9%的成对层级一致率。

Abstract: Pretrained universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are widely expected to substitute for explicit quantum chemistry when screening molecular interactions. On non-covalent binding we find that this expectation holds for neutral chemistry and fails for charged chemistry. We audit the FAIRChem UMA OMol head, without task-specific fine-tuning, on 5,567 probe-target dimers from 18 benchmarks across nine fidelity arms, using RAPIDS (Rapid Atomistic Probe-target Interaction Discovery Scaffold), a training-free inference-time scaffold. First, on neutral chemistry RAPIDS reaches a mean absolute error (MAE) of 1.37 kcal/mol, matching or beating every explicit DFT single-point fidelity (1.42-1.50 kcal/mol), so the remaining MLIP-DFT gap is geometric rather than a learned-surface error. Second, charged systems form a qualitatively different regime: MAE jumps to 8.5-22.9 kcal/mol across three charged benchmarks; DFT single-point rescoring does not restore accuracy, geometry reoptimization restores it for ionic hydrogen bonds and ion-molecule dimers, and the failure persists across all three MLIP backbones. Third, the residual failures are structured: signed biases cluster by chemistry family, and whether a fidelity fails catastrophically transfers across families more reliably than error magnitude, enabling cost-aware fidelity selection. RAPIDS-Select, a closed-form rule on cheap precomputed features, uses about 37% less compute than a strong static DFT baseline at matched accuracy, and on a newly computed charged benchmark cuts the catastrophe rate from 68% to 5.7%. On WetSenseBench, a wet-lab-grounded host-guest selectivity benchmark from eight sensing studies, RAPIDS without DFT follow-up recovers 82.9% pair-level agreement on the seven studies above its ~1 kcal/mol precision floor.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [Theory of Anomalous Diffusion and Superdiffusion in Integrable Spin Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33274)
*Jitendra Kethepalli,Andrew Urilyon,Jacopo De Nardis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文通过多组分粒子气体（multispecies particle gas）框架，解释可积系统（integrable systems）中两点关联函数的普适动力学标度（扩散或KPZ）与电流全计数统计（full counting statistics, FCS）可以相互分离的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可积系统即使其电流FCS偏离通常与扩散或KPZ普适类相关的形式，两点关联仍可表现出扩散或KPZ标度，这一分离现象缺乏统一解释，是本文研究动机。

Method: 构造保持可积自旋链（integrable spin chains）散射结构的多组分粒子气体，区分各向异性参数Δ>1（大粒子被可动粒子浴平流输运）与各向同性点Δ=1（所有粒子均可动、大粒子电荷转移离散）两种情形，分别推导FCS形式，并借助涨落流体力学（fluctuating hydrodynamics）分析两点关联。

Result: Δ>1时FCS呈高斯混合（Gaussian-mixture）形式，晚期趋于M-Wright分布，并因可动组分出现有限时间展宽修正；两点关联由大示踪粒子的布朗运动给出高斯扩散。Δ=1时FCS由自洽泊松分布（Poisson distribution）描述，整体近高斯、尾部依赖模型且非高斯；两点关联中出现KPZ Prähofer–Spohn标度函数，其来源为最大粒子与连续慢粒子谱的有限耦合及长程速度涨落。

Conclusion: 普适的动力学两点关联可与依赖模型细节的电流统计共存；多组分粒子气体框架为二者的分离提供了统一描述。

Abstract: Integrable systems can exhibit diffusive or Kardar--Parisi--Zhang (KPZ) scaling in two-point correlations even when their current full counting statistics (FCS) differ from those usually associated with these universality classes. We explain this separation using a multispecies particle gas that preserves the scattering structure of integrable spin chains. For anisotropy $Δ>1$, large particles are advected by a mobile bath. The resulting FCS has a Gaussian-mixture form and approaches the M-Wright distribution at late times, with finite-time rounding due to mobile species. At the isotropic point $Δ=1$, all particles are mobile, and the charge transfer by large particles is discrete. A self-consistent Poisson distribution describes the FCS, with a nearly Gaussian bulk and model-dependent, non-Gaussian tails. By contrast, two-point correlations follow from the statistics of a large tracer particle. For $Δ>1$, it undergoes Brownian motion, yielding Gaussian spreading. At $Δ=1$, we provide evidence from fluctuating hydrodynamics for the emergence of the KPZ Prähofer--Spohn scaling function in dynamical correlations and trace it to the finite coupling of the largest particles to a continuum of slower ones, with long-ranged velocity fluctuations. Our framework shows how universal dynamical two-point correlations coexist with distinct model-dependent current statistics.

</details>


### [208] [Extrinsic thermodynamic geometry, symmetry, and the cost of constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33443)
*Jordan R. Sawchuk,David A. Sivak*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文在摩擦张量（friction-tensor）形式下为离散随机系统发展外在热力学几何（extrinsic thermodynamic geometry），证明零外曲率的子流形可由跃迁网络的对称性预先识别，其最小功协议即使解除约束也保持平稳，并给出判定全局最优的凸性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限时间驱动随机系统会产生额外功（excess work），其大小取决于控制器可访问的参数与分布空间路径；受约束控制器是否被迫走次优路径，在摘要中未被现有框架回答，需借助外在热力学几何来刻画。

Method: 在慢驱动（slow driving）情形下将平衡分布空间视为黎曼流形（Riemannian manifold），把控制器约束视为子流形；为离散系统发展外在热力学几何，从跃迁网络（transition network）的对称性识别零外曲率子流形，并用横向环境曲率与热力学比较定理（thermodynamic comparison theorem）检验局部极小性。

Result: 对称子流形上的最小功协议在解除约束后仍为额外功的驻点（且该平稳性对非慢驱动亦精确成立）；慢驱动下横向曲率可分解为各对称破缺模式的独立贡献，并量化破缺对称的热力学激励；凸性条件下对称最优即全局最优。

Conclusion: 结果证明某五参数协议与四自旋伊辛系统（four-spin Ising system）的十五参数全控制达到同等最优性，并解释了在局域场控制下部分对称破缺为何最优；曲率由此获得“对称破缺热力学激励”的操作性含义。

Abstract: Driving a stochastic system in finite time costs excess work; exactly how much depends on which system parameters, and hence which paths in distribution space, a controller can access. In the friction-tensor formalism for slow driving, the space of equilibrium distributions is a Riemannian manifold and constraints on a controller define submanifolds. Whether a constrained controller is forced onto suboptimal paths is a question of the extrinsic thermodynamic geometry of the submanifold, which we develop for discrete systems. We show that submanifolds with zero extrinsic curvature can be identified from symmetries of the transition network, before any optimization. A minimum-work protocol on such a submanifold is therefore stationary in the excess work even when the constraints are lifted. The stationarity of symmetric optima holds exactly, beyond slow driving. In the slow-driving regime, we show that the ambient curvature transverse to a symmetric submanifold determines whether nearby symmetry-broken protocols have lower excess work. This curvature, which separates into independent contributions from each pattern of symmetry breaking, thus acquires an operational meaning as a measure of the thermodynamic incentive to break a given symmetry. A thermodynamic comparison theorem provides an inexpensive test of local minimality that weighs the extremal curvatures along the symmetric protocol against its excess work. There is no need for such tests under certain convexity conditions on the transition rates and control parameters, which guarantee that a symmetric optimum is a global optimum. These results certify the global optimality of a five-parameter protocol previously observed to match 15-parameter full control of a four-spin Ising system, and explain why partial symmetry breaking is optimal for the same system under local-field control.

</details>


### [209] [The Phases of Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33566)
*Gerard McCaul*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文提出“层级动力学相理论（dynamical phase theory of hierarchy）”：深度决定由规模为 N 的群体能否在不发生过度纠正的情况下纠正其所处环境。分析给出层级深度的有界可行窗口，并将结果与组织经济学中的对数界及 Transformer 注意力机制建立联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究已知大群体可通过统计聚合检测微小偏差（孔多塞陪审团定理，Condorcet jury theorem），但未说明这种响应反馈回被判定环境后是否稳定。论文旨在从动力学稳定性出发解释层级深度的必要性与限度。

Method: 构建动力学相理论：将群体响应随 N 以 √N 增长的锐化效应反馈至环境，在“自适应范式（adaptive normal forms）”模型类中解析求得临界环路增益处的翻转分岔（flip bifurcation）。进一步通过层级分层与信号链上行传输分析，并将每一层视为实空间重整化（real-space renormalisation）步骤以分类聚合规则。

Result: 层级分层可将不稳定性削弱，所需深度随 N 对数增长，从而由动力学稳定性条件直接导出组织经济学中已知的对数界。深度本身亦有代价：超过有限深度后纠正无法在截止时间前抵达顶层，而其下到达成本随深度指数增长。因此层级过平会振荡、过深则无法及时调控，形成介于不稳定平坦相与冻结惯性相之间的有界深度窗口。

Conclusion: 结果表明层级深度存在由动力学相边界界定的可行区间，且该框架可推广至以层为重整化步骤的聚合规则分类，并与 Transformer 注意力（attention）中残差更新（residual update）作为 token 图上的扩散、深度充当尺度角色相对应。相边界在有限采样下可观测，论文据此提出可用于刻画真实自适应系统相态的测量方案。

Abstract: We develop a dynamical phase theory of hierarchy, in which depth decides whether a population of size $N$ that revises its own environment can correct it without overcorrecting. The statistics that let a large majority detect a small bias - the basis of the Condorcet jury theorem - also make its response grow as $\sqrt{N}$. Fed back into the environment it is judging, this sharpening destabilises the matched state through a flip bifurcation at a critical loop gain, which we obtain analytically for a class of models we term adaptive normal forms. We show that this instability can be attenuated by hierarchical layering, requiring a depth scaling logarithmically with $N$. This recovers known logarithmic bounds in organisational economics directly from dynamic stability conditions. Hierarchical depth carries its own costs however, and by analysing the signal chain transmission up the hierarchy we show that beyond a finite depth no correction reaches the top before its deadline, and that below it the cost of arrival grows exponentially with depth. A hierarchy that is too flat therefore oscillates and one that is too deep cannot be steered in time, leaving a bounded window of depth between an unstable flat phase and a frozen inertial phase. We further show that these results can be generalised by treating each layer as a real-space renormalisation step, classifying aggregation rules by how they transmit bias. This establishes a connection between these dynamic results to attention in Transformers, where a residual update is diffusion on the token graph and depth plays the role of scale. Finally we show these phase boundaries are observable in finite sampling, and propose measurements which may characterise the proposed phases in real adaptive systems.

</details>


### [210] [Universal Work Extraction from Individual Sequences by Finite--State Information Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33984)
*Neri Merhav*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究由自主棘轮（autonomous ratchets）级联构成的信息引擎（information engines），证明了可提取功的逆界（converse bound）可用磁带的经验块熵（empirical block entropies）刻画，并给出普适可达方案，同时建立引擎与通用编码（universal coding）之间的双向对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是刻画处理给定确定性序列 x 的信息引擎所能提取的最大功，以及擦除该序列所需的最小功，并把这一热力学问题与通用编码理论联系起来。

Method: 将引擎定义为与热浴、功库和携带序列 x 的磁带相互作用的自主棘轮级联：每级固定能级、满足细致平衡（detailed-balance）动力学、处理 m 个单元的块且内部状态有限。通过证明引擎与通用编码（universal coding）之间的双向对应——能级定义编码、编码长度对应提取功——把编码的逆界（converse）移植到引擎上。

Result: 可提取功的逆界由 x 的经验块熵给出，且被能级不依赖 x 的引擎普适达到；每符号最大功为 kT[ln|X|−H̄(x)]，其中 H̄(x) 为渐近归一化对齐块熵，等于其块 Lempel–Ziv 复杂度（block Lempel–Ziv complexity）；擦除 x 每符号最小功为 kT H̄(x)。此外，无记忆通用引擎（memoryless universal engines）的最优冗余（redundancy）由 Shtarkov 和给出。

Conclusion: 信息引擎的可提取功与擦除功由序列的块熵决定，并与通用编码的编码长度及冗余界完全对应，因此通用编码的逆界结论可直接适用于此类引擎。

Abstract: We consider information engines that interact with a heat bath at temperature $T$, a work reservoir, and a tape carrying an individual (deterministic) sequence $x$ over a finite alphabet $\X$. An engine is a cascade of autonomous ratchets with fixed energy levels and detailed--balance dynamics, each processing segments of $m$ tape cells and having finitely many internal states. We prove a converse bound on the extractable work in terms of the empirical block entropies of $x$,and show that it is achievable \emph{universally}, by engines whose energy levels do not depend on $x$. The maximal work per symbol is $kT[\ln|\X|-\bar H(x)]$, where $k$ is Boltzmann's constant and $\bar H(x)$ is the asymptotic normalized aligned block entropy of $x$, which equals its block Lempel--Ziv complexity, and the minimal work needed to erase $x$ is $kT\bar H(x)$ per symbol. Underlying these results is a two--way correspondence with universal coding: energy levels define a code, and a suitably operated cascade extracts $kT$ times $\ln|\X|$ per symbol minus the code length; conversely, a fluctuation identity shows that every engine fixed in advance defines a code. Hence, converses of universal coding apply verbatim to engines; e.g., the optimal redundancy of memoryless universal engines is given by the Shtarkov sum.

</details>


### [211] [Exploring Physical Mechanisms Underlying the Emergence of Collective Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33988)
*Yitong Cai,Ruizhe Yan,Dongrun Jian,Jie Su,Jin Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究比较Vicsek模型中角向（内禀）噪声与矢量（外禀）噪声诱导的集体有序涌现，发现前者呈连续式转变、后者呈突变式转变，并用非平衡景观-流理论揭示其机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vicsek模型（Vicsek model, VM）是研究活性物质（active matter）集体有序的典型系统，但密度诱导涌现在内禀角向噪声与外禀矢量噪声之间是否存在差异、以及转变由何种机制支配，仍缺乏系统研究。

Method: 结合非平衡景观-流理论（nonequilibrium landscape-flux theory）与粗粒化映射方法（coarse-grained mapping method），刻画有序涌现过程中的景观演化、非平衡流、平均流（mean flux）与熵产生率（entropy production rate）。

Result: 内禀噪声下景观由单势阱连续演化为双势阱再回到单势阱；外禀噪声下双势阱景观在临界转变处突然出现。逆时针非平衡流维持全局圆周运动并驱动景观重塑；平均流与熵产生率在涌现阈值附近陡变，并在共存区内出现显著峰值。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了外禀噪声下独特的突变式涌现机制，为定位相边界、区分转变类型提供实用手段，并可推广至其他非平衡集体系统。

Abstract: Emergence of collective order from disordered constituents is a hallmark of active matter, and the Vicsek model (VM) serves as a paradigmatic system for its study. However, whether density-induced emergence in the VM differs between angular (intrinsic) and vectorial (extrinsic) noise, and what mechanisms govern the transitions, remain largely unexplored. Remarkably, we uncover that collective order emerges from a disordered state in a continuous-like manner under intrinsic noise but abruptly under extrinsic noise. Combining nonequilibrium landscape-flux theory with a coarse-grained mapping method, we show that the landscape evolves continuously from a single basin to two and back to one under intrinsic noise, but the two-basin landscape appears abruptly under extrinsic noise across the critical transition to flocking. The counterclockwise nonequilibrium flux sustains global circular motion, providing the driving force for reshaping the landscape. The mean flux and entropy production rate change steeply near the emergence threshold and exhibit pronounced peaks within the coexistence region, identifying them as the dynamical and thermodynamic origins of emergence. Our findings reveal a distinct abrupt emergence under extrinsic noise and offer deeper mechanistic insight, practical applications (e.g., locating phase boundaries and distinguishing transition types), and extensibility to other nonequilibrium collective systems.

</details>


### [212] [Rotation curves of dwarf galaxies and dark matter as a condensate of $q$-deformed bosons: reconstruction and fits of the deformation profile](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33999)
*Mahnaz Maleki,Asef Kheirandish,Morteza Nattagh Najafi,Hosein Mohammadzadeh*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究暗物质自相互作用玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose-Einstein condensate, BEC）模型中引入 $q$-形变玻色子后的矮星系晕结构，指出均匀形变不改变标准 BEC 密度轮廓，而唯象地引入空间变化的形变可改善旋转曲线拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质主导的矮星系（dwarf galaxies）是检验自相互作用 BEC 暗物质模型的敏感场所；标准 BEC 晕在 Thomas-Fermi 极限下的旋转曲线形状由两个尺度参数完全固定，缺乏灵活性，故需考察形变玻色子能否提供额外自由度。

Method: 考虑 $q$-形变玻色子（$q$-deformed bosons）凝聚体，在 Thomas-Fermi 极限下推导形变对晕结构的影响，并以高斯函数（含两个附加参数）唯象描述形变参数在内区的压低，对矮星系旋转曲线（rotation curves）进行拟合，用贝叶斯信息准则（Bayesian information criterion, BIC）与标准 BEC、Navarro-Frenk-White 及 Burkert 轮廓比较。

Result: 空间均匀的形变参数不改变标准 BEC 密度轮廓；各星系中 $χ$ 在 $kr\lesssim1$ 内区相对外晕被压低，外晕近似由标准凝聚体描述而核心需更弱形变（$q$ 更接近 1），但压低深度与宽度均非普适。拟合 rms 残差为 $0.6$–$2.6$ km/s，优于标准 BEC 的 $1.6$–$8.5$ km/s；BIC 显示形变凝聚体在四个星系中优于标准 BEC、NFW 与 Burkert，两个星系中与最优者不可区分，两个星系中不被偏好。

Conclusion: 形变 BEC 模型可改善部分矮星系旋转曲线拟合，但结论受限于数据无误差棒、忽略重子（baryons）以及位置相关形变的唯象性质，尚不能视为确定性证据。

Abstract: Dark-matter-dominated dwarf galaxies are a sensitive testing ground for models in which dark matter is a self-interacting Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC). In the Thomas-Fermi limit the standard BEC halo has the density profile $ρ\propto\sin(kr)/(kr)$, so that the shape of its rotation curve is fixed up to two scale parameters. Here we consider a condensate of $q$-deformed bosons. We show that, in the Thomas-Fermi limit, the deformation enters the structure of the halo only through the chemical potential of the condensate; consequently, a spatially uniform deformation parameter leaves the standard BEC profile unchanged. In every galaxy $χ$ is depressed in the inner region, $kr\lesssim1$, relative to the outer halo, where it is approximately constant in most cases: the standard condensate describes the outer halo, while the core requires a weaker deformation, i.e., $q$ closer to unity. The profiles share this qualitative shape, but neither the depth nor the width of the depression is universal. Modelling the depression by a Gaussian, with two additional parameters, we fit the rotation curves with rms residuals of $0.6$--$2.6$~km\,s$^{-1}$, against $1.6$--$8.5$~km\,s$^{-1}$ for the standard condensate. According to the Bayesian information criterion, the deformed condensate is preferred over the standard condensate, the Navarro-Frenk-White profile, and the Burkert profile in four galaxies, is indistinguishable from the best of them in two, and is disfavoured in two. We discuss the limitations of these results, in particular the absence of error bars in the data, the neglect of baryons, and the phenomenological status of a position-dependent deformation.

</details>


### [213] [Patterns and correlations in time-reversal symmetry breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34244)
*Ido Patish,Mathias Casiulis,Dov Levine*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出以局域熵产生率（local entropy production rate）作为时空场来描述拓展的均匀非平衡系统，揭示其空间关联的存在及其在非平衡相变附近发散的行为，并给出涉及控制参数与观测时间的标度律。工作确立了局域熵产生作为识别时间反演对称性破缺（TRS breaking）尺度与来源的可观测量的地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间反演对称性破缺（time-reversal symmetry breaking, TRS）是非平衡统计物理（non-equilibrium statistical physics）的核心标志，但在平移不变系统中通常仅被当作全局量处理，缺乏对破缺在空间上分布与尺度的刻画。作者因此希望引入局域化的时空场描述，以识别TRS破缺的尺度与来源。

Method: 将局域熵产生率（local entropy production rate）作为时空场（spatio-temporal field），用于描述拓展的均匀非平衡系统（extended homogeneous non-equilibrium systems），并分析该场的空间关联及其与非平衡相变、控制参数和轨迹观测时间的关系。

Result: 局域熵产生率场普遍呈现空间关联（spatial correlations），其特征长度在趋近非平衡相变时增大；相应的关联长度（correlation length）遵循此前未知的标度律（scaling laws），其中同时涉及控制参数与轨迹的观测时间（observation time）。

Conclusion: 该工作为将局域熵产生用作强有力可观测量的路径奠定基础，可用于识别非平衡系统中TRS破缺的尺度与来源。

Abstract: Time-reversal symmetry (TRS) breaking is the hallmark of non-equilibrium statistical physics. This notwithstanding, in translationally invariant systems, it is almost always treated only as a global quantity. We propose to use the local entropy production rate as a spatio-temporal field to describe extended homogeneous non-equilibrium systems. We find that this field generically displays spatial correlations, with a typical length that grows as we approach some non-equilibrium phase transitions. We also show that the correlation length associated with the local entropy production rate follows heretofore unknown scaling laws involving control parameters as well as the observation time used for trajectories. Our work paves the way to the use of the local entropy production as a powerful observable to identify the scale and source of TRS breaking in non-equilibrium systems.

</details>


### [214] [Diffusion Effects on a Stochastic Amplifier Driven by the Square of a Gaussian Field: Discontinuous Transition to Intermittency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34532)
*Philippe Mounaix*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究扩散（diffusion）对由高斯场平方驱动的随机放大（stochastic amplification）中间歇性转变（transition to intermittency）的影响，并证明扩散诱导的阻尼可将连续相变改为不连续相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究以空间均匀性序参量（order parameter）刻画间歇性转变，但扩散在这一随机放大框架中的作用尚未明确；本文旨在厘清扩散如何改变该转变的性质。

Method: 构建将扩散—放大过程嵌入随机点吸收体（point absorbers）格点的模型，使费曼—卡茨公式（Feynman-Kac formula）仅限作用于范围不超过有限截断（cut-off）的布朗路径（Brownian paths）；在高斯驱动场处于一类广泛物理相关情形下进行解析分析。

Result: 扩散诱导的放大阻尼足够强，从而改变转变性质：无扩散时为连续转变（continuous transition），存在扩散时转为不连续转变（discontinuous transition）。

Conclusion: 扩散并非仅定量削弱放大，而可定性改变间歇性转变的普适类别，表明在随机放大模型中忽略扩散可能误判相变性质。

Abstract: We study the effects of diffusion on the transition to intermittency in stochastic amplification driven by the square of a Gaussian field. The transition is characterized by an order parameter quantifying the spatial uniformity of the amplification. We consider a model in which the diffusion-amplification process is embedded in a random lattice of point absorbers in such a way that the Feynman-Kac formula is restricted to Brownian paths with a range not exceeding a finite cut-off. In this setting, with the Gaussian driving field in a wide class of physical interest, we show that diffusion-induced damping of the amplification is sufficiently strong to change the nature of the transition: from continuous in the absence of diffusion, to discontinuous in the presence of diffusion.

</details>


### [215] [Measuring the Roughness of Quasi-Circular Interfaces at Finite Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34556)
*Ralph Bulanadi,Asraful Haque,Sumner B. Harris,Rama Vasudevan,Yongtao Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究利用傅里叶合成生成自仿射界面（self-affine interfaces）并映射到圆形轮廓与像素网格，基准测试了界面粗糙度指数（roughness exponent）与粗糙度幅值（roughness amplitude）的恢复精度，揭示有限采样、图像分辨率与圆形映射对粗糙度测量的系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 界面粗糙度（interface roughness）可反映材料的钉扎景观（pinning landscape）与集体界面行为，但有限采样与图像分辨率会使标度（scaling）性质估计产生偏差；尤其对不同尺寸圆畴（circular domains）进行比较时，可分辨边界点数量及角度间距与物理间距的关系随界面半径变化，使问题更为复杂。

Method: 采用傅里叶合成（Fourier synthesis）生成合成自仿射界面，映射到圆形轮廓并栅格化到像素网格；比较位移关联（displacement correlation, B(r)）与结构因子（structure factor, S(q)）两类估计方法在不同界面长度下的表现；并评估一种以斜率最小化与界面中位位置（medial interface position）加权折中的重建流程，以应对圆形界面角度与径向间距不均的问题。

Result: 谱估计（spectral estimates）在原始界面上最为有效，但在圆形映射后该优势不再保持；适度平滑（smoothing）可改善参数恢复，而较强平滑会使标度指数与粗糙度幅值同时产生偏差。上述结果在合成数据集与实验铁电（ferroelectric）体系中均得到验证。

Conclusion: 研究识别了圆形界面粗糙度测量的固有限制，并为有限分辨率实验下圆畴演化（evolution of circular domains）的后续研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Interface roughness characterizes the boundary fluctuations across different length scales, which can reveal information about a material''s pinning landscape and collective interfacial behaviour. Finite sampling and image resolution can bias the measured scaling properties, however. These effects can particularly complicate comparisons between circular domains of different sizes, where the number of resolved boundary points, and the relationship between angular and physical separations vary with the interface radius. Here, we benchmark the recovery of the roughness exponent and amplitude using synthetic self-affine interfaces generated by Fourier synthesis, mapped onto circular contours, and rasterized onto a pixel grid. We first compare displacement-correlation (B(r)) and structure-factor (S(q)) estimates for different interface lengths. To address non-uniform angular and radial spacing in a circular interface, we assess a reconstruction procedure that weights slope minimization against medial interface position. We find that while spectral estimates were most effective in the original interfaces, this advantage did not persist after circular mapping. Moderate smoothing was found to improve parameter recovery, but stronger smoothing can bias both the scaling exponent and roughness amplitude. These results, verified on both synthetic datasets, and an experimental ferroelectric system, identify limitations of circular-interface roughness measurements and provide a basis for further investigations into the evolution of circular domains under finite resolution experiments.

</details>


### [216] [Data-enhanced self-learning projective quantum Monte Carlo with Rydberg-atom measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34636)
*Vichayuth Imchitr,Luca Brodoloni,Sebastiano Pilati,Estelle Maeva Inack*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作在自学习投影量子蒙特卡罗（projective quantum Monte Carlo, PQMC）的引导波函数中引入可编程量子模拟器的投影测量数据，并在二维里德堡原子阵列（Rydberg-atom array）上验证其可减少有限粒子数效应、加速收敛并降低所需行走子数与迭代次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自学习 PQMC 的引导波函数（guiding wavefunction）训练通常从零开始，存在有限粒子数效应（finite-population effects）与收敛缓慢问题；作者探索能否用有限且不完美的实验测量为训练提供有效初始化。

Method: 将可编程量子模拟器的投影测量结果嵌入基态投影的引导波函数中，采用受限玻尔兹曼机（restricted Boltzmann machine, RBM）与循环神经网络（recurrent neural network, RNN）两类引导波函数，在二维里德堡原子阵列上进行实验预训练（experimental pretraining），并结合虚时投影（imaginary-time projection）与后续无监督学习（unsupervised learning）精化波函数。

Result: 实验预训练降低了有限粒子数效应并加速自学习收敛，以更少行走子（walkers）与更少自学习迭代即可获得无偏的基态性质估计；RBM 的改善尤为显著，更准确的 RNN 引导波函数获益较小但仍可观测；不同系统尺寸的模拟提示预训练的相对优势可能随系统尺寸增大。棋盘序参量（checkerboard order parameter）亦有改善，尽管实验测量偏离平衡数值结果。此外，未使用实验预训练的自学习 PQMC 在有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）下给出的无序—棋盘相变临界点与临界指数（critical exponents）与既有数值研究一致。

Conclusion: 有限且不完美的实验测量可为自学习 PQMC 提供有效初始化；虚时投影结合后续无监督学习能够逐步将引导波函数精化至基态。结果表明实验数据与自学习数值方法可形成互补。

Abstract: We introduce a data-enhanced self-learning projective quantum Monte Carlo (PQMC) method that incorporates projective measurements from programmable quantum simulators into the guiding wavefunction of a ground-state projection. We demonstrate the approach for a two-dimensional Rydberg-atom array using restricted Boltzmann machine (RBM) and recurrent neural network (RNN) guiding wavefunctions. Experimental pretraining reduces finite-population effects and accelerates self-learning convergence, allowing unbiased estimation of ground-state properties to be obtained with fewer walkers and fewer self-learning iterations. The improvement is particularly pronounced for the RBM, while the more accurate RNN guiding wavefunction exhibits a smaller, though still observable, benefit from pretraining. Simulations across different system sizes suggest that the relative advantage of experimental pretraining may increase with system size. Improvements are also observed for the checkerboard order parameter, despite deviations of the experimental measurements from the equilibrium numerical results. Finally, finite-size scaling with self-learning PQMC without experimental pretraining yields the critical point and critical exponents of the disordered-to-checkerboard transition in agreement with previous numerical studies. Our results show that limited and imperfect experimental measurements can provide an effective initialization for self-learning PQMC, while imaginary-time projection combined with subsequent unsupervised learning progressively refines the guiding wavefunction toward the ground state.

</details>


### [217] [Monotonic probes of asymmetry and robustness of the quantum Mpemba effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34853)
*Riccardo Travaglino,Michele Mazzoni,Pasquale Calabrese*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文基于夹层 Rényi 散度（sandwiched Rényi divergence）构建满足单调性（monotonicity）的纠缠不对称性（entanglement asymmetry）度量族，证明其与传统 Rényi 不对称性在 Rényi 指数反转 α↔1/α 下存在精确对应关系，并揭示量子 Mpemba 效应（quantum Mpemba effect）在该单调量全族中的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠不对称性已成为探测对称性恢复（symmetry restoration）与量子 Mpemba 效应的有力工具，但以 Rényi 熵差定义的传统 Rényi 推广在资源理论（resource theory）意义下并非单调量，其物理合法性与信息论基础存在缺陷。

Method: 采用双参数复制方法（two-parameter replica approach），从带电矩（charged moments）计算基于夹层 Rényi 散度的不对称性量，并在纯态、均匀量子淬火（quantum quench）标度极限及 Haar 随机态下分析其与传统 Rényi 不对称性的 α↔1/α 对应关系。

Result: 该 α↔1/α 关系对纯态严格成立，在均匀淬火（含相互作用可积系统）与 Haar 随机态的领头阶标度极限下亦成立；倾斜铁磁态（tilted ferromagnetic state）淬火中量子 Mpemba 效应在整族夹层 Rényi 不对称性单调量中均出现；空间非均匀初态淬火中传统不对称性与真正单调量表现出定性差异，且可解玩具模型显示 Mpemba 交叉点可能依赖 Rényi 指数。

Conclusion: 研究建立了传统 Rényi 不对称性与量子资源理论之间的联系，解释了其在广泛非平衡协议（nonequilibrium protocols）中有效的原因，同时指出真正的单调量可提供额外信息。

Abstract: Entanglement asymmetry has emerged as a powerful probe of symmetry restoration and the quantum Mpemba effect. However, its conventional Rényi generalizations, defined as differences of Rényi entropies, are not monotones in the resource-theoretic sense. Here, we investigate an alternative family of asymmetry quantifiers based on sandwiched Rényi divergences, which satisfy monotonicity under symmetry-preserving quantum channels. Developing a two-parameter replica approach, we show how these quantities can be computed from charged moments and establish a remarkable correspondence with conventional Rényi asymmetries under the inversion of the Rényi index, $α\leftrightarrow 1/α$. This relation, exact for pure states, holds at leading order in the relevant scaling limits for homogeneous quantum quenches, including interacting integrable systems, and for Haar-random states. Applying these results to quenches from tilted ferromagnetic states, we demonstrate that the quantum Mpemba effect is remarkably robust, occurring throughout the entire family of sandwiched Rényi asymmetry monotones. We identify dynamical regimes in quenches from spatially inhomogeneous initial states where conventional Rényi asymmetries and genuine monotones exhibit qualitatively different behaviours. We also investigate a solvable toy model illustrating the possible dependence of Mpemba crossings on the Rényi index. Our findings establish a connection between conventional Rényi asymmetries and quantum resource theory, explaining their effectiveness in broad classes of nonequilibrium protocols while highlighting the additional information provided by genuine asymmetry monotones.

</details>

### [218] [Modeling the LISA response in the time-frequency domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33215)
*Tomasz Kinowski,Christopher J. Moore*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出一种直接在时频域（time-frequency domain）建模LISA响应的方案，基于Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer（WDM）变换，并用JAX实现向量化以支持批量信号的高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时频方法（time-frequency methods）是LISA数据分析中颇具前景的路径，但需要一套在时频域内精确且高效的LISA响应模型作为支撑组件。

Method: 通过Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer（WDM）变换在时频域直接构建LISA响应模型，并采用JAX进行向量化实现，使其可对成批信号（batches of signals）高效求值。

Result: 与现有LISA单臂响应（one-arm response）实现相比，白噪声失配（white-noise mismatches）约为$10^{-9}$量级；向量化实现可高效处理批量信号。

Conclusion: 结果表明LISA响应可在时频域中被精确且高效地建模，为端到端时频LISA分析框架（end-to-end time-frequency LISA analysis framework）提供了重要组成部分。

Abstract: Time-frequency methods are a promising approach to LISA data analysis. This paper presents a model for the LISA response directly in the time-frequency domain using the Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer (WDM) transformation. The implementation is vectorized using JAX and can efficiently evaluate the response for batches of signals, achieving white-noise mismatches of $\sim 10^{-9}$ compared to existing implementations of the LISA one-arm response. Our results demonstrate that the LISA response can be modeled accurately and efficiently in the time-frequency domain, providing an important component of an end-to-end time-frequency LISA analysis framework.

</details>


### [219] [RIFT for the O4 era: operating points, calibration marginalization, and cross-code validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33239)
*R. O'Shaughnessy,A. Jan,J. Lange,R. Mechum,N. Malagon*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍RIFT（Rapid Iterative FiTting）引力波参数推断算法在O4观测准备与分析期间（版本0.0.17.3–0.0.18.0）的改进，扩展了其单事件推断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RIFT作为基于模拟推断（simulation-based inference）的高效、高度并行化引力波参数推断方法，需提升与当代仪器的兼容性、与其他推断代码的可比性，并应对最响亮清晰信号及复杂相关参数的推断需求。

Method: 改进包括：增强校准边际化（calibration marginalization）能力；管理似然函数的内部、输出及时间插值表示；引入（横向）自旋与偏心平近点角的新坐标以提升弱相关参数推断的稳健性；针对最响亮信号调整运行点（operating point）选择；并识别与调和RIFT与其他生产级推断代码的似然差异，加入自动化波形对称性检查。

Result: 识别并调和了RIFT与其他生产代码之间最重要的正面似然差异来源；自动化波形对称性检查可在上游模型回归偏置推断之前将其检测出来；借助既有RIFT/asimov接口实现可复现设置，端到端演示了上述能力。

Conclusion: 所部署的改进拓宽了RIFT对单事件推断的适用性，提升了校准边际化、似然表示与参数坐标处理的稳健性及与其他代码的可比性，并通过可复现接口完成端到端验证。

Abstract: The Rapid Iterative FiTting (RIFT) parameter inference algorithm provides a simulation-based-inference approach to efficient, highly-parallelized parameter inference for GW sources. In this paper, we describe improvements deployed during preparations for and analysis of O4 observations (versions 0.0.17.3--0.0.18.0) that broaden RIFT's reach for single-event inference with contemporary instruments. We provide additional options to increase compatibility and comparability with other codes, notably including (a) enhancing RIFT's capabilities for calibration marginalization; (b) managing our likelihood's internal, output, and time-interpolated representation; (c) introducing new coordinates for (transverse) spin and eccentric mean anomaly, enabling more robust inference of parameters with subtle and complex correlations to others, and (d) revised operating point choices better suited to the loudest, clearest signals. We identify and reconcile the most substantial sources of head-to-head likelihood differences between RIFT and other production inference codes, and describe automated waveform symmetry checks that detect upstream model regressions before they bias inference. Using the previously-reported RIFT/asimov interface for reproducible settings consistent with past work, we demonstrate these capabilities end to end.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [220] [Spin superfluid transport of magnons in altermagnetic insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33294)
*Dele Li,Meng-Han Zhang,Dao-Xin Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究交变磁体（altermagnet）中Lieb与逆Lieb晶格的超流自旋输运，揭示易平面Néel序相位所载自旋角动量的相干传输机制，并给出可测的衰减长度与临界自旋流判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体兼具晶格交替的自旋劈裂与无净磁化特征，有望实现低耗散自旋输运；但Lieb型晶格上的自旋超流（spin superfluidity）行为及其可观测性尚待澄清，尤其是易平面各向异性与B₁g对称交换不平衡的协同作用。

Method: 采用连续场理论（continuum theory）结合Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert数值模拟，分析易平面Néel序的Goldstone模色散与稳定性边界，并考察界面热磁振子（thermal magnon）对临界自旋流的贡献。

Result: 在临界稳定性边界处Goldstone模变为二次色散；轴向相位梯度将相位通道谱权重转移至有能隙模，而对角流则不产生该特征；手性劈裂磁振子色散导致动量空间各向异性，强场与高温抑制相位相干；临界自旋流随注入角变化，衰减长度（decay length）与界面自旋混合电导（spin-mixing conductance）成比例。

Conclusion: 交变磁绝缘体（尤其是La₂O₃FeMnSe₂）可作为低耗散自旋输运的候选平台；自旋混合电导可通过自旋霍尔磁电阻（spin Hall magnetoresistance）与自旋塞贝克效应（spin Seebeck effect）实验测量，从而为理论预言提供检验途径。

Abstract: We study the spin superfluidity for Lieb and inverse Lieb lattices in altermagnets, which enables coherent transport of spin angular momentum carried by the phase of an easy-plane Néel order. The easy-plane anisotropy supplies the $U(1)$ platform, while the nonrelativistic spin splitting generates sublattice-alternating exchange imbalance with $B_{1g}$ symmetry. Via a continuum theory and Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert simulations, we find that the Goldstone mode becomes quadratic at the critical stability boundary. An axial phase gradient transfers phase-channel spectral weight to the gapped mode, whereas a diagonal flow leaves these signatures absent. The spin superfluid exhibits momentum-space anisotropy due to the chiral-splitting magnon dispersion, while strong fields or elevated temperatures suppress phase coherence. Accounting for the contribution of thermal magnons at the interface, we present that the critical spin flow varies with the injection angle under the altermagnetic crystal-field effects. The spin-superfluid decay length scales with the interfacial spin-mixing conductance, which can be measured by the spin Hall magnetoresistance and spin Seebeck effect. Our findings establish altermagnetic insulators, particularly $\mathrm{La_2O_3FeMnSe_2}$, as promising platforms for low-dissipation spin transport.

</details>


### [221] [Graphical Rules for Zero-energy Quantum-Interference Patterns in Bipartite Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33630)
*Xinhao Fan,Shunbo Hu,Jingzhe Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文将分子中量子干涉（quantum interference, QI）的研究推广到任意边界与孔洞的二分晶格（bipartite lattice）体系，提出一个三步图形化规则，用于预测以源点为固定、漏点变化所得到的电导分布图（conductance map），并可自然推广至无限晶格与空位缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子干涉决定分子电导，可产生随路径长度的奇偶振荡（even-odd oscillation）和破坏性干涉（destructive QI）导致的巨大开关比；已有研究多局限于单个分子，缺乏适用于广泛二维体系的抽象框架。

Method: 提出三步规则：先从输入网络中移除部分原子，构造单元胞复制（unit-cell-replicated）的二分子集，并用局域递推关系高效获得其格林函数图（Green's function map）；再将移除的原子与局域结构修饰作为局域自由度耦合回去，通过同一格林函数图生成零模向量（zero-mode vectors）；最后用有效耦合矩阵（effective coupling matrix）的投影算符筛选存活的零模组合并修正参考图，得到最终 QI 图案。

Result: 该规则可给出任意边界与孔洞的有限薄片（finite flakes）中的 QI 图案预测，并自然推广至无限晶格与空位缺陷情形。

Conclusion: 研究把 QI 分析从个体分子扩展到二分晶格系统，提供了一个可预测、可推广的图形化框架，为在广泛二维体系中探索量子干涉提供了抽象工具。

Abstract: Quantum interference(QI) plays an important role in electron transport in molecules, where conductance is determined by the coherent superposition of different quantum-walk paths connecting the source and drain sites. In some systems, the pronounced even-odd conductance oscillation with the walk length can be observed; meanwhile destructive QI can lead to a dramatic suppression of conductance, resulting in a huge on/off ratio. In this paper, we extend the investigation from individual molecules to bipartite lattice systems, providing an abstract framework for exploring QI in a broad range of two-dimensional systems. We propose a simple graphical rule for predicting the QI pattern-the conductance map obtained by varying the drain site while keeping the source site fixed-in bipartite structures with arbitrary boundaries and holes. The analysis can be completed in three steps. First, a unit-cell-replicated bipartite subset is constructed by removing a subset of atoms from the input network, and its Green's function map is efficiently obtained from a local recurrence relation. Second, the removed atoms and local structural modifications are coupled back as local degrees of freedom, generating zero-mode vectors through the same Green's function map. Finally, the surviving zero-mode combinations are selected by an effective coupling matrix, whose projectors correct the reference map to yield the final QI pattern. This rule applies to finite flakes with arbitrary boundaries and holes and extends naturally to infinite lattices and vacancy defects.

</details>


### [222] [Toward a quantum resistance standard above liquid nitrogen temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34069)
*Kaining Yang,Changwei Zhai,Naijie Ren,Kai Zhao,Chengyuan Li,Changhao Zhao,Yunfeng Lu,Yaning Wang,Jianting Zhao,Zheng Vitto Han*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究利用CrOCl与单层石墨烯（monolayer graphene）的界面电荷序耦合增强费米速度，形成稳固的朗道回旋能隙，从而在50 K、12 T条件下实现相对不确定度仅23 ppb的量子霍尔电阻（quantum Hall resistance）标准，并在100 K下仍保持2 ppm的相对不确定度，为液氮温度以上的下一代量子电阻标准提供平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子霍尔效应（quantum Hall effect）长期是国际单位制（SI）中实现量子霍尔电阻标准的独特途径，但在宽松条件下（尤其是温度高于30 K）实现高精度量子霍尔电阻此前一直无法做到，限制了其应用便利性。

Method: 采用CrOCl与单层石墨烯的界面电荷序耦合（interfacial charge-order coupling）以增强费米速度（Fermi velocity），构建稳固的朗道回旋能隙（Landau cyclotron gap）；并利用室温安装的定制直流比较仪（direct current comparator, DCC）对霍尔电阻进行精密测量。

Result: 在50 K、12 T外磁场和10 μA直流电流下，霍尔电阻相对R_K/2的偏差为(15 ± 23) nΩ Ω⁻¹（相对不确定度23 ppb）；在100 K下，系统表现出相对不确定度为2 ppm的量子霍尔电阻。

Conclusion: 结果表明该体系有望成为下一代量子电阻标准平台，可在液氮温度以上工作，并无需传统压缩机式制冷机。

Abstract: Quantum Hall effect (QHE) -- the pioneering demonstration of one-dimensional topologically-protected edge channels -- manifest a transversal resistance quantized solely by two universal constants $e$ and $h$ (the elementary charge and the Planck constant, respectively), referred to as the von Klitzing constant $R_\mathrm{K}=h/e^{2}$. For decades, this has enabled a unique route for realizing quantum Hall resistance (QHR) standard in the Système international d'unitès (SI), yet high precision QHR under relaxed conditions, especially temperature higher than 30 K, has not been possible. Here, we show that interfacial charge-order coupling inbetween CrOCl and monolayer graphene offers enhanced Fermi velocity, creating a robust Landau cyclotron gap. Using a customized direct current comparator (DCC, mounted at room temperature), we obtain relative deviation of the Hall resistance from $R_\mathrm{K}/2$ of (15 $\pm$ 23)\,n$Ω$ $Ω$$^{-1}$ (23 parts per billion of relative uncertainty) at 50 K, with an external magnetic field of 12 T and a dc current of 10 $μ$A. At 100 K, the system exhibits QHR with 2 parts per million of relative uncertainty. Our results therefore suggest a platform toward a next-generation quantum resistance standard that may work at above liquid nitrogen temperature and free of conventional compressor refrigerators.

</details>


### [223] [Tunable quantum metric driven nonlinear valley Hall effect in the PT-symmetric sliding antiferroelectric MoS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34116)
*Wen-Xin Jiang,Zhen-Hao Gong,Zhigang Gui,M. Umar Farooq,Li Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文设计四层MoS₂平台，结合滑动反铁电序（sliding antiferroelectric order）与应变诱导的C₃z破缺，在保持时间反演（T）与空间反演（P）对称性的条件下实现由量子度规（quantum metric）驱动的非线性谷霍尔效应（NVHE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常净量子度规效应需同时破缺时间反演（T）和空间反演（P）对称性；谷对比机制虽可在中心对称、非磁性体系中探测量子几何，但如何获得可调控、可实际实现的量子度规响应仍是问题。本文旨在揭示超越贝里曲率（Berry curvature）的量子几何signature并实现电控。

Method: 构建四层MoS₂体系，将滑动反铁电序（ABBA↔BAAB）与应变诱导的C₃z破缺相结合；采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）分析NVHE符号与大小，并利用可逆层间滑动实现非易失电控。

Result: 计算显示NVHE主要由外层主导（outer-layer-dominated），其符号和幅度可通过层间滑动调控；该谷响应在P与T对称性保持下可逆、非易失地被电学控制。

Conclusion: 该保持对称性、滑动可调的策略可在现实二维材料中激活隐藏的量子几何现象，为可调谷电子学（valleytronics）与量子信息器件提供一条清晰路径。

Abstract: The quantum metric, the real part of a quantum geometric tensor, plays an important role in fundamental transport and optical responses of quantum materials. While net quantum metric effects are typically realized by breaking both time-reversal (T) and inversion (P) symmetries, valley-contrast mechanisms enable the probing of quantum geometry in centrosymmetric, nonmagnetic systems, revealing physical signatures beyond Berry curvature. Here, we design a four-layer MoS$_2$ platform that combines sliding antiferroelectric order with strain-induced $C_{3z}$ breaking to realize a quantum metric driven nonlinear valley Hall effect (NVHE) under preserved P and T symmetries. Reversible interlayer sliding (ABBA $\leftrightarrow$ BAAB) provides nonvolatile electrical control of the valley response. First-principles calculations reveal an outer-layer-dominated NVHE with tunable sign and magnitude. This symmetry-preserving, sliding-tunable strategy activates hidden quantum geometric phenomena in realistic two-dimensional materials and opens a clean pathway for tunable valleytronics and quantum information devices.

</details>


### [224] [Intrinsically nonlinear nanomechanical resonator toward the single-phonon Kerr regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34345)
*S. Klingel,T. Althuon,C. Wassmer,C. Sürgers,T. Cubaynes,W. Wernsdorfer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究提出直接利用纳米机械谐振器（nanomechanical resonator）的强内禀机械非线性（intrinsic mechanical nonlinearity），而非引入外部非线性源，并通过弯曲模式耦合方法量化其非线性，为纯机械量子态制备提供途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单量子水平（single-quantum level）操控通常需要非谐能谱（anharmonic energy spectrum），但纳米机械谐振器在单声子区（single-phonon regime）普遍被视为线性对象，现有方案多依赖外部非线性源，代价是额外的弛豫与退相干通道（relaxation and decoherence channels）。

Method: 采用直接利用强内禀机械非线性的思路，建立基于弯曲模式（bending modes）耦合的新方法量化该非线性；并提出基于上翘弯曲纳米管谐振器（upward-buckled nanotube resonator）机械偏振角（mechanical polarization angle）的调谐机制以寻找最优工作点。

Result: 给出内禀机械双稳态（mechanical bistability）起始的上界为 34.0 ± 9.7 个声子；将内禀非线性水平外推至实际可实现的更短谐振器后，表明单声子克尔区（single-phonon Kerr regime）是可以达到的。

Conclusion: 内禀非线性足以支撑纯机械薛定谔猫态（Schrödinger-cat states）与高相干机械量子比特（mechanical qubits）的实现，为无需外部非线性源的机械量子器件提供可行路线。

Abstract: Manipulating a physical system at the single-quantum level typically requires an anharmonic energy spectrum. Nanomechanical resonators, however, are commonly admitted to be linear objects in the single-phonon regime, thus motivating most efforts to employ external sources of nonlinearity at the cost of added relaxation and decoherence channels. Here, we take a radically different approach by directly exploiting strong intrinsic mechanical nonlinearities. We quantify this nonlinearity using a novel method based on the coupling between bending modes. Using this technique, we establish an upper bound of 34.0 +/- 9.7 phonons for the onset of intrinsically mechanical bistability. In addition, we highlight a new tuning mechanism based on a mechanical polarization angle of an upward-buckled nanotube resonator allowing us to reach an optimal working point. Extrapolating this level of intrinsic nonlinearity to realistically shorter resonators shows that the single-phonon Kerr regime is within reach, allowing for the realization of purely mechanical Schrödinger-cat states and highly coherent mechanical qubits.

</details>


### [225] [Seebeck and Hall effects in charge-neutral ballistic graphene as probes of electron-electron interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34482)
*Z. Z. Alisultanov,A. V. Kavokin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在电荷中性石墨烯（charge-neutral graphene）中，库仑速度重整化（Coulomb velocity renormalization）的电子-空穴不对称性导致有限的塞贝克系数（Seebeck coefficient）与霍尔系数（Hall coefficient）。弹道区（ballistic regime）热电势仅约0.1–1 μV/K；当电子-电子散射长度短于样品长度时，热电势过渡到碰撞主导（collision-dominated）的4–10 μV/K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在解释电荷中性石墨烯中有限塞贝克与霍尔系数的来源，并寻找能够区分弹道输运与碰撞主导输运的热电判据。

Method: 基于库仑速度重整化的电子-空穴不对称性进行理论分析，比较弹道区与电子-电子散射长度低于样品长度两种情形下的热电势，并考虑 n_e = n_h 所要求的化学势移动。

Result: 弹道区热电势较小（0.1–1 μV/K），源于 n_e = n_h 所要求的化学势移动抵消了其主导项；当电子-电子散射长度低于样品长度后，热电势过渡到碰撞主导值 4–10 μV/K，约高一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该过渡是弹道输运（ballistic transport）失效的直接热电标记，并为识别狄拉克流体（Dirac fluid）提供基线。

Abstract: Electron-hole asymmetry of the Coulomb velocity renormalization is responsible for finite Seebeck and Hall coefficients in charge-neutral graphene. In the ballistic regime the thermopower is small, $0.1$--$1~μ$V/K, because the chemical-potential shift required by $n_e{=}n_h$ cancels its leading part. When the electron-electron scattering length falls below the sample length, the thermopower crosses over to a collision-dominated value of $4$--$10~μ$V/K, that is an order of magnitude larger. This crossover is a direct thermoelectric marker of the breakdown of ballistic transport and a baseline for identifying the Dirac fluid.

</details>


### [226] [Mode-resolved spin-wave spectroscopy by a single paramagnetic center](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34484)
*Clément Fourniols,Maki Umeda,Eric Clot,Eyub Yildiz,Isabelle Joumard,Benjamin Pigeau,Olivier Klein*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用(111)金刚石中单个氮-空位（nitrogen-vacancy, NV）色心的光学检测磁共振（optically detected magnetic resonance, ODMR），结合双频驱动经磁振子克尔效应（magnon Kerr effect）耦合磁化动力学，实现了模式分辨的铁磁共振（ferromagnetic resonance, FMR）谱学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FMR谱学缺乏原子尺度空间分辨率，难以分辨单个量子化自旋波模式；本文旨在提供一种兼具高灵敏与高空间分辨的线性FMR测量方案。

Method: 采用双频驱动通过磁振子克尔效应将磁化动力学耦合至NV自旋共振，由NV探测该效应产生的静态杂散场（stray field），灵敏度可低至进动锥角（precession cone angle）θ≈1°。

Result: 在法向磁化的钇铁石榴石（yttrium iron garnet, YIG）微盘上验证了该方案，分辨出其本征量子化自旋波模式；扫描探针获得模式的空间分布，与解析模型定量吻合。

Conclusion: 单NV色心可作为原子尺寸探针实现模式分辨的FMR谱学与模式空间成像，为纳米尺度磁化动力学研究提供通用方法。

Abstract: We demonstrate mode-resolved ferromagnetic resonance (FMR) spectroscopy using optically detected magnetic resonance (ODMR) of a single nitrogen-vacancy (NV) center in (111)-oriented diamond. A two-tone drive couples the magnetization dynamics to the NV spin resonance via the magnon Kerr effect. We show that the NV can detect the static stray field produced by this effect down to precession cone angles $θ\approx \SI{1}{\degree}$, providing a generic scheme to measure the linear FMR spectrum with the spatial resolution of an atomic-size probe. We validate the approach on a normally magnetized yttrium iron garnet (YIG) microdisk, resolving its intrinsic quantized spin-wave modes. Scanning the probe across the surface, we map the spatial profile of the modes and find quantitative agreement with analytical models.

</details>


### [227] [Localization of an Individual Charge Fluctuator in a Si/SiGe Spin Qubit Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34812)
*Leah Tom,John Nichol,Chris Anderson,Mark F. Gyure*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种空间三角定位（spatial triangulation）方法，结合栅极依赖与传感点化学势移动，在Si/SiGe量子点器件中联合约束单个双能级涨落体（two-level fluctuator, TLF）的三维位置与电偶极矩大小，将其定位在原子层沉积氧化铝（atomic layer deposited aluminum oxide）体内约31 nm × 1 nm × 4 nm的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体自旋量子比特（semiconductor spin qubits）中来自双能级涨落体的电荷噪声（charge noise）限制栅极保真度（gate fidelities），但器件内单个涨落体的定位仍具挑战性。

Method: 将TLF失谐（detuning）的栅极依赖性与其在传感点（sensing dot）中诱导的化学势移动相结合实现空间三角定位；应用于已报道的栅控Si/SiGe量子点器件测量数据，并借助静电模拟（electrostatic simulations）联合约束涨落体的三维位置与电偶极矩大小。

Result: 涨落体被定位于原子层沉积氧化铝体内横向31 nm × 1 nm、纵向4 nm的区域，从而识别出承载该单个电荷噪声源的材料；测量结果与垂直取向、幅度为3至4 eÅ的TLF一致。

Conclusion: 该方法为确定半导体自旋量子比特器件中哪些材料与界面承载活跃电荷涨落体提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Charge noise from two-level fluctuators (TLFs) limits gate fidelities in semiconductor spin qubits, yet locating individual fluctuators within a device remains challenging. Here, we demonstrate a spatial triangulation method that combines the gate dependence of a TLF's detuning with the chemical potential shifts it induces in a sensing dot. Applying this method to previously reported measurements from a gated Si/SiGe quantum dot device, we use electrostatic simulations to jointly constrain the fluctuator's three-dimensional position and the magnitude of its electric dipole moment. We localize the fluctuator to a region spanning 31 nm by 1 nm laterally and 4 nm vertically within the bulk of the atomic layer deposited aluminum oxide, identifying the material hosting this individual charge-noise source. The measurements are consistent with a vertically oriented TLF with a magnitude of 3 to 4 e$\mathring{A}$. This approach provides a route to determining which materials and interfaces host active charge fluctuators in semiconductor spin qubit devices.

</details>


### [228] [Brightening spin- and parity-forbidden interlayer excitons through rhombohedral stacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34900)
*Haodong Hu,Chih-En Hsu,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Hung-Chung Hsueh,Zhenglu Li,Ziliang Ye*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作表明，3R 堆垛的 MoS₂ 同质双层（homobilayer）可使两个对称性禁戒的层间激子（interlayer excitons）变亮，即自旋相反的 1s 激子与宇称奇异的 2p 激子，并据此揭示出隐藏的规范不变相位差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变亮的对称性禁戒激子（symmetry-forbidden excitons）可提供半导体电子结构与多体相互作用的关键信息，而层间原子配准（interlayer atomic registry）近期被视为调控原子级薄层材料基态与激发态的新手段，因此需要探索堆垛方式对层间激子的调控能力。

Method: 以 3R-MoS₂ 同质双层为对象，通过 g 因子（g-factor）表征及将层间激子调谐至与层内激子（intralayer excitons）共振时观测的杂化模式（hybridization patterns）来判定激子性质。

Result: 3R 堆垛使一个自旋相反（spin-unlike）的 1s 层间激子和一个宇称奇异（parity-odd）的 2p 层间激子变亮；杂化后的 2p 激子表现出所获跃迁偶极间的相长量子干涉（constructive quantum interference），揭示出组分电子态之间存在隐藏的规范不变相位差（gauge-invariant phase contrast）。

Conclusion: 堆垛调控（stacking control）可作为调制二维半导体中激子量子相（quantum phases of excitons）的有力途径。

Abstract: Brightened symmetry-forbidden excitons can provide critical information about the electronic structure and many-body interactions in semiconductors. Recently, interlayer atomic registry has emerged as a new way to control atomically thin layered materials in both ground and excited states. Here we show that the rhombohedral stacking in a 3R-MoS_2 homobilayer can brighten two symmetry-forbidden interlayer excitons: one is a spin-unlike 1s exciton and the other is a parity-odd 2p exciton. The nature of these interlayer excitons is revealed by their gfactors as well as the hybridization patterns observed when they are tuned to resonate with intralayer excitons. Surprisingly, the hybridized 2p exciton exhibits a constructive quantum interference between the acquired transition dipoles, revealing a hidden gauge-invariant phase contrast between the constituent electronic states. Our results establish stacking control as a powerful approach for modulating quantum phases of excitons in 2D semiconductors.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [Spontaneous Time-Reversal-Symmetry-Breaking State in Proximity-Induced Superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33288)
*Jonathan Schirmer,Wei Pan,Javad Shabani,Enrico Rossi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文提出一种在多带体系与常规s波超导体近邻耦合时自发破缺时间反演对称性的最小机制，并给出其存在条件、基态性质与相图，指出可出现重入式多次相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期关于s波超导体与狄拉克半金属构成的超导异质结构实验表明，即使各组分中无磁序也无非常规配对，超导态仍可能破缺时间反演对称性；本文旨在为这一现象提供不依赖磁性或非常规配对的最小理论机制。

Method: 构建多带体系（multiband system）与常规s波超导体近邻耦合的模型，推导时间反演对称性破缺超导态的出现条件，判定其是否为基态，并计算相图（phase diagram）。

Result: 该对称破缺态在存在之处即为基态；相图显示在某些情形下系统随参数连续变化可出现重入行为（reentrant behavior），即在时间反演对称态与自发破缺时间反演对称态之间发生多次转变。

Conclusion: 结果有助于识别可工程化设计、在无外磁场或磁化情形下产生非互易超导输运效应（nonreciprocal superconducting transport）的异质结构。

Abstract: Recent experiments on superconducting heterostructures formed by an $s$-wave superconductor and a Dirac semimetal suggest that in these systems the superconducting state can break time-reversal symmetry even though no magnetic order or unconventional pairing is present in any of the structure's components. We present a minimal mechanism for a time-reversal-symmetry-breaking superconducting state in multiband systems proximity-coupled to a conventional $s$-wave superconductor. We derive the conditions for this state, find that it is the ground state wherever it exists, and obtain the phase diagrams. We find that in some situations the phase diagrams describe reentrant behavior: by continuously varying a parameter the system can undergo multiple transitions between a time-reversal-symmetric state and a state that spontaneously breaks time-reversal symmetry. The results help identify heterostructures that can be engineered to obtain nonreciprocal superconducting transport effects even when no magnetic field or magnetization is present.

</details>


### [230] [Spectroscopic Signatures of Polaronic Density Wave Fluctuations in Superconducting Bismuthates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33422)
*Wojciech R. Pudełko,Muntaser Naamneh,Philip M. Dee,Hang Li,Eduardo B. Guedes,Dariusz J. Gawryluk,Anna Krztoń-Maziopa,Vahid Sazgari,Yona Soh,Ming Shi,Milan Radović,Johan Chang,Steven Johnston,Nicholas C. Plumb*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究在掺杂铋酸盐（bismuthate）Ba$_{1-x}$K$_x$BiO$_3$与BaPb$_{1-y}$Bi$y$O$_3$的费米面（Fermi surface）上观测到准粒子（quasiparticle）谱强度与散射率的周期性调制，其行为可由电子与短程相干、沿$(π,π,π)$方向波矢的涨落密度波（fluctuating density wave）耦合模型解释，支持双极化子（bipolaron）相关的双组分流体图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铋酸盐高温超导体（bismuthate high-temperature superconductors）被认为是潜在的双极化子（bipolaron）体系，其中空穴对与BiO$_6$八面体的局域呼吸畸变（breathing distortion）相结合；但对此类准粒子及其激发给出确定性证据仍具挑战性。

Method: 对掺杂铋酸盐Ba$_{1-x}$K$_x$BiO$_3$和BaPb$_{1-y}$Bi$y$O$_3$费米面上的准粒子谱强度与散射率进行周期性调制分析，并采用电子与沿$(π,π,π)$取向波矢、具短程相干性的涨落密度波耦合的模型进行描述。

Result: 观测到的周期性调制与铜氧化物超导体（cuprate superconductors）中报道的“热点”（hot spot）图样相似；该模型可良好描述这些特征，结果与存在双组分流体（two-component fluid）的情形一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明，未配对电子与由残余双极化子及其对BiO$_6$八面体的呼吸畸变所产生的涨落密度波相互作用，支持掺杂铋酸盐中双极化子相关物理的图像。

Abstract: The bismuthate high-temperature superconductors have been identified as potential bipolaron systems, where hole pairs are bound to local breathing distortions of the BiO$_6$ octahedra in their metallic phase. However, obtaining definitive evidence for these quasiparticles and their excitations remains challenging. Here, we report on periodic modulations of the spectral intensities and scattering rates of quasiparticles on the Fermi surfaces of doped bismuthates Ba$_{1-x}$K$_x$BiO$_3$ and BaPb$_{1-y}$Bi$_y$O$_3$, which are reminiscent of ``hot spot'' patterns that have been observed in cuprate superconductors. We show that these features can be well described by a model where electrons couple to fluctuating density wave modulations with short-range coherence along a $(π,π,π)$-oriented wavevector. The results are consistent with the existence of a two-component fluid where unpaired electrons interact with a fluctuating density wave created by residual bipolarons and their breathing distortions of the BiO$_6$ octahedra.

</details>


### [231] [Crystal-Symmetry-Dependent Field-Free Superconducting Diode Effect in CoPt/Nb Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33717)
*He Yu,Jiaxin Chen,Yi Zhang,Gan Zhao,Yizhen Shao,Jiaxu Li,Yu He,Qiusha Wang,Yuanliang Zhu,Bin Han,Junda Qu,Haochang Lyu,Bowen Dong,Jie Qi,Dapeng Zhu,Daoqian Zhu,Yunchi Zhao,Liang Liu,Shouguo Wang,Weisheng Zhao,Guang Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文在外延[111]取向A1-CoPt/Nb异质结中实现了零磁场下的超导二极管效应（superconducting diode effect）精确调控，其临界电流不对称性遵循磁点群（magnetic point group）对称性，并可用于电写磁态的超导读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导二极管效应通常依赖复杂器件结构与外加磁场来打破空间反演（spatial-inversion）和时间反演（time-reversal）对称性，限制了其在低温电子学与超导量子电路中的集成。

Method: 制备外延[111]取向A1-CoPt/Nb异质结，测量临界电流不对称性随晶向与磁化方向的变化，并开展电流与温度相关的二次谐波输运（second-harmonic transport）测量，结合A1-CoPt薄膜的零场电流诱导磁化翻转（current-induced magnetization switching）进行对照。

Result: 临界电流不对称性在低对称方向有限、沿磁镜轴（magnetic mirror axis）被抑制，并在60°面内旋转或CoPt磁化反转时改变符号；A1-CoPt薄膜的零场电流诱导磁化翻转呈现相同的三重对称依赖，二次谐波测量结果与由取向依赖界面边缘形貌引起的涡旋介导非互易性（vortex-mediated nonreciprocity）一致。

Conclusion: 晶体学对称性可作为调控超导二极管效应的有效自由度，拓宽了可选材料范围，并为零场超导电子学提供实用路径；同时利用磁化依赖的二极管极性实现了电写磁态的超导读出概念验证。

Abstract: Superconducting diodes convert nonreciprocal critical currents into current-polarity-selective switching between zero-resistance and resistive states. However, realizing the superconducting diode effect often requires complex device architectures and an external magnetic field to break spatial-inversion and time-reversal symmetry, substantially limiting its integration into cryogenic electronics and superconducting quantum circuits. Here, we demonstrate precise control of the field-free superconducting diode effect in epitaxial [111]-oriented A1-CoPt/Nb heterostructures. The critical-current asymmetry obeys the magnetic-point-group symmetry relation: it is finite along low-symmetry directions, suppressed along magnetic mirror axes, and reverses under either a 60° in-plane rotation or reversal of the CoPt magnetization. The same threefold symmetry dependence is observed in field-free current-induced magnetization switching in A1-CoPt films, indicating a common crystallographic origin for the superconducting and normal-state transport responses. Current- and temperature-dependent second-harmonic transport measurements are consistent with vortex-mediated nonreciprocity arising from an orientation-dependent interfacial edge landscape. We further exploit the magnetization-dependent diode polarity for proof-of-concept superconducting readout of electrically written magnetic states. These results establish crystallographic symmetry as an effective degree of freedom for engineering the superconducting diode effect, expanding the accessible materials space and providing a practical route toward field-free superconducting electronics.

</details>


### [232] [Superconductivity from the Berry connection from many-electron wave functions: $U(1)$ gauge fluctuation, gauge invariance, and superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33723)
*Hiroyasu Koizumi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文以多电子波函数的贝里联络（Berry connection）及其涨落构造一种超导理论，将无耗散量子态识别为超导态，并用以解释磁场中的可逆超导—正常相变与力平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标准超导理论（BCS理论）与实验之间存在两处根本性分歧，作者希望在不放弃BCS主要结论的前提下消除这些分歧。

Method: 采用多电子波函数的贝里联络（Berry connection）及其涨落所导致的量子态间概率性随机跃迁，将经典电动力学中的规范不变性（gauge invariance）理解为筛除受规范涨落影响部分的要求。

Result: 量子力学中存在不受规范涨落影响的耗散自由态，被识别为超导态；并据此解释了磁场中可逆的超导—正常相变，包括力平衡（force balance）。

Conclusion: 该理论在保留BCS理论主要结果的同时消解了理论与实验的两处根本分歧，因而可视为涵盖现有标准理论的超导理论。

Abstract: The electromagnetic $U(1)$ gauge invariance and superconductivity are examined by employing the Berry connection from many-electron wave functions and the fluctuations of it that give rise to the probabilistic random transitions among quantum states. The gauge invariance in the classical electrodynamics is regarded as the requirement that extracts unaffected parts by the gauge fluctuation. In quantum mechanics, dissipation-free states from the gauge fluctuation exist, and we identify them as superconducting states. We explain the reversible superconducting-normal phase transition in a magnetic field including the force balance. The present superconductivity theory lifts two fundamental disagreements between theory and experiment existing in the current standard theory with keeping the major results by the BCS theory. Thus, it may be considered as a superconductivity theory encompassing the current standard one.

</details>


### [233] [Superconductivity from a Cooper surface instability in a nodal Bose metal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33803)
*Jian-Jian Miao,Cui Ding*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在动量空间 \(t\)–\(J\) 模型中，欠掺杂铜氧化物的正常态被解析求解为具有四个单电子节点的节点型玻色金属；沿"库珀面"添加或移除自旋单态电子对不需能量，导致配对磁化率对数发散，从而使任意弱的配对转移吸引足以稳定节点 \(d\)-波超导体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠掺杂铜氧化物中，超导能隙在转变温度以上低能电子谱权重已耗尽处反而最大，这一反直觉现象引出核心问题：当单电子态密度（density of states）消失时，弱吸引如何驱动超导？

Method: 采用动量空间 \(t\)–\(J\) 模型，其正常态可精确求解，为含四个单电子节点的节点型玻色金属（nodal Bose metal）；分析沿两条"库珀面"（Cooper surfaces）添加或移除动量相反的自旋单态电子对的能量代价，并计算配对磁化率（pair susceptibility）。

Result: 沿库珀面配对无需能量代价，使配对磁化率呈对数发散（logarithmic divergence），因此任意弱的配对转移吸引（pair-transfer attraction）即可稳定一种关联节点 \(d\)-波超导体（correlated nodal d-wave superconductor）。

Conclusion: 该配对机制可由自旋分辨符合光电子发射（spin-resolved coincidence photoemission）检验：在转变温度以上分辨出两条零能配对移除脊，在其下方沿底层 Luttinger 面出现第三条脊。

Abstract: In underdoped cuprates, the superconducting gap is largest where low-energy electron spectral weight is already depleted above the transition. How can a weak attraction drive superconductivity when the one-electron density of states vanishes? In a momentum-space \(t\)--\(J\) model, the exactly solvable normal state is a nodal Bose metal with four one-electron nodes. Along two contours called Cooper surfaces, adding or removing a spin-singlet pair of electrons with opposite momenta costs no energy. The resulting logarithmic divergence of the pair susceptibility allows an arbitrarily weak pair-transfer attraction to stabilize a correlated nodal \(d\)-wave superconductor. Spin-resolved coincidence photoemission could test this pairing mechanism by resolving two zero-energy pair-removal ridges above the transition and a third along the underlying Luttinger surface below it.

</details>


### [234] [Structural Anisotropy in Bi$_2$Se$_3$/$λ$-(BETS)$_2$GaCl$_4$ Topological Insulator/Superconductor Bilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33869)
*Fathan M. Santana,Arifa D. Agustin,Aulia A. Firdaus,Malik A. Baqiya,Darminto Darminto,Muhammad K. Nuryadin,Takahiko Sasaki,Isao Watanabe,Takuya Kobayashi,Hiromi Taniguchi,Yasuyuki Ishii,Pasquale Marra,Paolo Mele,Dita P. Sari*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究考察了λ-(BETS)₂GaCl₄（λ-Ga）对Bi₂Se₃沉积响应的晶向依赖性，结合截面FIB成像、单晶X射线衍射、第一性原理计算与光学光谱，发现沉积4分钟时ab面结构修饰最大而ac面几乎不变，并对应先前识别的约110 nm中间沉积区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探究λ-Ga晶体对Bi₂Se₃沉积的响应是否依赖晶向，即沉积效应是否具有晶体学各向异性，并需确认沉积层与界面的形成及其厚度区间。

Method: 采用截面聚焦离子束（focused-ion-beam, FIB）成像确认Bi₂Se₃层及界面；用单晶X射线衍射（single-crystal X-ray diffraction, SC-XRD）表征结构变化；基于精修结构开展第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）评估电子结构修饰；并对Bi₂Se₃/SiO₂薄膜进行光学光谱（optical spectroscopy）测量。

Result: 覆盖后的λ-Ga晶体结构完整，但呈现强烈的晶向依赖且非单调的响应：沉积4分钟后ab面的修饰最大，ac面相对基本不变，该条件对应约110 nm Bi₂Se₃厚度的中间沉积区；计算显示同样条件下最大的晶向依赖电子修饰；Bi₂Se₃/SiO₂薄膜光谱呈随沉积时间演化的响应。

Conclusion: 结果表明λ-Ga对Bi₂Se₃沉积的结构响应与计算所得电子响应均具有显著的晶体学各向异性（crystallographic anisotropy）。

Abstract: We investigate the crystallographic-orientation dependence of the response of $λ$-(BETS)$_2$GaCl$_4$ ($λ$-Ga) to Bi$_2$Se$_3$ deposition. Cross-sectional focused-ion-beam (FIB) imaging confirms formation of a deposited Bi$_2$Se$_3$-containing layer and a distinct interface with $λ$-Ga. Single-crystal X-ray diffraction (SC-XRD) shows that the coated $λ$-Ga crystal remains structurally intact but exhibits a strongly orientation-dependent, non-monotonic response, with the largest modification for the $ab$-plane after 4 min of deposition, whereas the $ac$-plane remains comparatively unchanged. This condition corresponds to the intermediate-deposition regime previously identified at a Bi$_2$Se$_3$ thickness of approximately 110 nm. First-principles calculations based on the refined structures show the largest orientation-dependent electronic modification under the same conditions. Complementary optical spectroscopy of Bi$_2$Se$_3$/SiO$_2$ films shows deposition-time-dependent evolution of the film response. These results establish a pronounced crystallographic anisotropy in the structural and calculated electronic response of $λ$-Ga to Bi$_2$Se$_3$ deposition.

</details>
